Environment Duels: A Microcosm of LLM Co-Evolution
环境对决：大语言模型共演化的一个微观世界
Through a tangle of real-world interactions, from synthetic training pipelines to data shared on the open web, frontier LLMs absorb what their predecessors and competitors create. How well does each model play its role in this ecosystem, as a creator and as a solver? We introduce Environment Duels, a dynamic agentic benchmark that evaluates both capabilities on interactive environments. Each model writes environments, each with a privileged hint, aiming for tasks that capable solvers fail without the hint and pass with it. Across nine frontier LLMs, solving and authoring prove to be distinct skills: the strongest solvers are not the strongest authors, while smaller models learn more from how others played and write the hardest environments and the most useful hints. Beyond the scores, the pairwise interactions form a microcosm of how models affect one another. Head-to-head outcomes are transitive and so yield a stable ranking; independent authors converge on the same designs; and in a reinforcement-learning study with Qwen3.8-27B, the learner's hint lift on an author's environments tracks how much training on those environments improves it on held-out reasoning and agentic benchmarks, whether the environments are its own or another model's. Each of these patterns has a counterpart in how models shape one another outside the duel. By staging these interactions under controlled conditions, Environment Duels offers a window onto how LLMs teach, challenge and learn from one another. Code is available at https://github.com/LLM-Duels/EnvDuels.
中文翻译
通过复杂的现实交互，包括合成训练流程以及开放网络上的数据共享，前沿大语言模型吸收了前代模型和竞争对手所创造的内容。每个模型在这个生态系统中作为创造者和求解者，表现如何？我们引入了“环境对决”（Environment Duels），一种动态的智能体基准测试，用于评估模型在交互环境中的双重能力。每个模型自行编写环境，每个环境包含一个特权提示，目标是设计出只有具备强大求解能力的模型才能在获得提示后成功完成，而在没有提示时则失败的任务。在九个前沿大语言模型中，求解能力和创作能力被证明是两种不同的技能：最强的求解者并非最强的创作者；较小的模型更善于从他人行为中学习，并能编写最难的环境和最有用的提示。除了得分之外，成对交互形成了一种微型生态系统，展示了模型之间的相互影响。对决结果具有传递性，从而产生稳定的排名；独立作者会趋同于相同的设计；在对Qwen3.8-27B的强化学习研究中，学习者在作者环境上的提示增益，与该模型在未见推理和智能体基准上的性能提升高度相关，无论这些环境是自己生成还是其他模型生成的。这些模式在对决之外的模型相互塑造过程中均有对应。通过在受控条件下模拟这些互动，环境对决为我们提供了观察大语言模型如何相互教学、挑战与学习的窗口。代码已公开于 https://github.com/LLM-Duels/EnvDuels。
👤 Shibo Jin, Zhiqiu Xu, Yiling Zou, Shangyu Gong, Mayur Naik 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Quant-Arena: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Quantitative Trading Research
Quant-Arena：面向量化交易研究的大语言模型智能体基准测试
Quantitative research is an iterative loop: a researcher proposes a market hypothesis, implements it as an executable signal, tests it on historical data, and decides what to keep. Existing benchmarks score formulas, code, or individual trading decisions, leaving open whether large language models (LLMs) can complete this loop and produce strategies that work beyond the research period. We introduce Quant-Arena, a benchmark and public leaderboard for end-to-end quantitative research on real A-share market data. A model constructs features and stock-scoring code, receives feedback only on 2022--2023, and freezes one strategy for assessment on the sealed year 2024. A cash-account evaluator applies trading constraints and costs, including market impact estimated from a simulated order book. Scores are dominated by realized excess return and complemented by signal quality and the diversity of features used by the strategy. We evaluate nine frontier models, among which GPT-6 Astra scores highest; yet no model beats the equal-weight market in 2024. Quant-Arena is open to submissions from any model or agent harness.
中文翻译
量化研究是一个迭代循环：研究人员提出市场假设，将其转化为可执行信号，用历史数据测试，并决定保留哪些内容。现有基准仅评估公式、代码或单个交易决策，未能回答大语言模型是否能完整完成这一循环并生成在研究期后仍有效的策略。我们提出了Quant-Arena，一个面向真实A股市场数据的端到端量化研究基准及公开排行榜。模型需构建特征和股票评分代码，仅在2022–2023年接收反馈，并冻结一个策略用于对密封的2024年数据进行评估。一个现金账户评估器施加交易约束和成本，包括基于模拟订单簿估算的市场冲击。评分主要由实际超额收益主导，并辅以信号质量及策略所用特征的多样性。我们评估了九个前沿模型，其中GPT-6 Astra得分最高；然而，没有任何模型在2024年超越等权重市场。Quant-Arena对所有模型或智能体开放提交。
👤 Houde Qian, Zheng Jiang, Wenyi Li, Chenxi Xu, Xinjie Lin et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.30103]

Did the Problem Actually Get Harder? Testing LLMs on Paired P and NP-Complete Problems Hardened by Adversarial Multi-Agent Rewriting
问题真的变难了吗？基于对抗式多智能体重写的P与NP完全问题配对测试大语言模型
Many NP-complete (NPC) problems have a polynomial-time (P) twin that differs in a single structural detail, such as 3- versus 2-colouring a graph. A model that reasons rather than recalls should notice which side of this boundary a problem falls on, yet existing benchmarks either fix the wording of each task or ignore its complexity class, so they cannot separate the two. We introduce P2NPCBench, a benchmark of 12 problem families in which every NPC problem is paired with a P twin posed over the same surface. To remove the textbook cues that enable recall, we build it with AgentGAN, an adversarial multi-agent framework in which a generator rewrites each statement until a panel of frozen code models fails, while an executable validity gate keeps every rewrite answer- and class-preserving. The result is 2,114 hardened statements with hand-verified reference human solvers. Evaluating 12 proprietary and open-source models, we find that every model scores lower on NPC statements than on their P twins (35.4% vs. 63.8% pass@1), and that 95.1% of model-family pairs fail more often on the NPC side. A tenfold time limit raises NPC accuracy only from 28.5% to 31.9%, so failures stem from wrong or crashing programs rather than slow ones. Hardening also narrows the class gap from 24.8 to 14.6 points, showing that part of the apparent gap reflects familiarity with textbook phrasing rather than complexity class.
中文翻译
许多NP完全（NPC）问题都有一个多项式时间（P）对应的孪生问题，二者仅在单一结构细节上不同，例如图的三色着色与二色着色。一个真正具备推理能力而非依赖记忆的模型应能识别问题位于哪一侧边界，但现有基准要么固定任务表述，要么忽略复杂度类，因而无法区分两者。我们提出了P2NPCBench，一个包含12个问题族的基准，其中每个NPC问题都与一个在相同表面表述下的P类孪生问题配对。为消除可导致记忆的教科书线索，我们采用AgentGAN——一种对抗式多智能体框架，其中生成器不断重写陈述，直到一组冻结的代码模型无法解决；同时，一个可执行有效性门确保每次重写保持答案和复杂度类不变。最终得到2,114个经过人工验证参考人类解法的强化陈述。评估12个专有和开源模型发现，每个模型在NPC问题上的得分均低于其P类孪生问题（pass@1分别为35.4% vs. 63.8%），且95.1%的模型家族对在NPC侧失败频率更高。十倍时间限制仅使NPC准确率从28.5%提升至31.9%，说明失败源于错误或崩溃程序而非速度慢。强化还使类别差距从24.8分缩小至14.6分，表明部分看似的差距实则源于对教科书表述的熟悉度，而非复杂度本身。
👤 Nurul Labib Sayeedi, Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi, Zabir Al Nazi, Mahbub E Sobhani, Shubhashis Roy Dipta 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

What the Author Wrote, What the Agent Reads: Silent Cue Loss in Enterprise Document Ingestion
作者写了什么，代理读到了什么：企业文档摄入中的沉默线索损失
Enterprise agents act on documents. Before an agent can follow a policy, answer a compliance question or file a ticket, some ingestion layer must turn a Word file or PDF into tokens - and that layer is where an agent deployed in the wild silently loses information. We show that one layer is lost by every mainstream converter - the meaning an author encodes in how text looks - and that the loss makes agents confidently wrong rather than visibly incomplete. We build a task from 76 real policy documents: recover the requirements the author singled out, scored against the file's own OOXML properties by a deterministic matcher with no LLM judge. Formatting-blind ingestion collapses from micro F1 0.690 to 0.150 (docling) and 0.075 (plain text), a gap that replicates across six answering models from two vendors - five Claude models spanning the Opus, Sonnet and Haiku tiers, and OpenAI's GPT-5.6. An agent that cannot see the cue guesses, and docling's false-positive rate on requirements the author did not flag is four times ours. A span-level leave-one-out localises the loss to two cues - highlight (+0.302) and colour (+0.262), both Holm-adjusted p < 1e-4 - while bold, italic, monospace and strikethrough each sit within +/-0.003 of zero. The same asymmetry appears in PDF. Colour is also the cue no mainstream converter preserves and no parsing benchmark scores, and it is common: 32.8% of 6,460 documents carry a deliberate colour or highlight, 10.3% on an obligation. A pre-registered test shows that typing a cue for the agent adds +0.085 beyond merely preserving it (Holm-adjusted p = 0.049). We validate the labels against rendered page images through an independent modality (kappa = 0.72) and bound what wording alone can achieve (AUC 0.664). For agent builders the practical finding is that ingestion, not reasoning, is where this reliability is won or lost, and that the fix is cheap and measurable.
中文翻译
企业智能体依赖于文档执行任务。在智能体能够遵循策略、回答合规问题或提交工单之前，必须有一个摄入层将Word文件或PDF转换为标记——而正是这个层导致部署在真实环境中的智能体无声地丢失信息。我们发现，主流转换工具每一步都会丢失一个关键信息层：作者通过文本呈现方式编码的意义。这种信息丢失使智能体产生自信的错误而非明显的不完整。我们基于76份真实政策文档构建了一个任务：恢复作者特别标注的要求，并通过确定性匹配器根据文件自身的OOXML属性进行评分，无需大语言模型判断。忽略格式的摄入方式使微F1值从0.690降至0.150（docling）和0.075（纯文本），这一差距在来自两家供应商的六种问答模型中均复现——包括五个覆盖Opus、Sonnet和Haiku层级的Claude模型以及OpenAI的GPT-5.6。无法感知线索的智能体会猜测，而docling对作者未标记要求的误报率是我们的四倍。通过跨度级留一法分析，我们定位到两个关键线索——高亮（+0.302）和颜色（+0.262），两者均经Holm校正后p < 1e-4；而粗体、斜体、等宽和删除线的影响均在±0.003范围内。同样的不对称性也出现在PDF中。颜色是主流转换工具均不保留且无解析基准评估的线索，且使用频率较高：在6,460份文档中，有32.8%包含有意的颜色或高亮，其中10.3%涉及义务内容。一项预注册测试显示，为智能体输入线索可额外带来+0.085的提升（Holm校正后p = 0.049）。我们通过独立模态（渲染页面图像）验证标签一致性（kappa = 0.72），并界定了仅靠文字所能达到的上限（AUC 0.664）。对智能体开发者而言，实际启示在于：可靠性之争不在推理，而在摄入环节，而解决方案既廉价又可测量。
👤 Yikai Zhao, Saurabh Pandey, Pradeep Kumar Misra 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.30006]

Building Auditable AI Text Detectors with Sparse Autoencoders
基于稀疏自编码器构建可审计的AI文本检测器
AI-generated text detectors are increasingly deployed in consequential settings---peer review, education, admissions, journalism, and content moderation---where their outputs directly affect individuals. Yet the strongest detectors are fine-tuned neural classifiers whose decisions are opaque, while transparent alternatives (stylometric and linguistic-feature classifiers) underperform them by wide margins. We present a pipeline that converts a fine-tuned neural detector into a classifier that operates on human-readable concepts. We accomplish this by training sparse autoencoders (SAEs) directly on a detector's residual stream, assigning auto-interpretation labels to each SAE feature, and retaining only features whose generated descriptions pass a global fidelity threshold. We then train a lightweight classifier on just the interpretable features. We find that our SAEs recover latent concepts that correspond to highly specific and predictive linguistic "tells" of AI-generated text. Classifiers built on these extracted features are not only highly interpretable but exhibit stronger performance on out-of-distribution texts than either neural or stylometric alternatives.
中文翻译
AI生成文本检测器正被广泛应用于重要场景——同行评审、教育、招生、新闻报道和内容审核——其输出直接影响个人。然而，性能最强的检测器是经过微调的神经分类器，其决策过程不透明；而透明的替代方案（如风格特征和语言特征分类器）则在性能上与之存在巨大差距。我们提出一种管道方法，将微调后的神经检测器转化为基于人类可读概念的分类器。具体做法是在检测器的残差流上直接训练稀疏自编码器（SAEs），为每个SAE特征自动分配解释性标签，并仅保留那些生成描述通过全局保真度阈值的特征。随后，我们仅使用这些可解释特征训练一个轻量级分类器。结果表明，我们的SAEs成功恢复了对应于高度特定且预测性强的语言“迹象”的潜在概念。基于这些提取特征构建的分类器不仅具有高度可解释性，而且在分布外文本上的表现优于神经模型和风格特征模型。
👤 Liam Dugan, Callum McDougall, Matthew Denton, Philip Pham, Christine Kaeser-Chen et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29968]

CulturalRiddles: A Multicultural Benchmark of Riddles
CulturalRiddles：一个多元文化谜题基准
As LLMs expand their language coverage, evaluating how well they understand and represent diverse cultures becomes increasingly important. However, many benchmarks remain English-centric, with limited cultural coverage and a focus on factual knowledge over its application in real-world cultural contexts. We propose CulturalRiddles, a benchmark of original, culturally grounded riddles created entirely by humans through a participatory community effort, spanning 62 cultures (45 countries, 51 languages). These riddles are deeply rooted in their respective cultures and require abductive and compositional reasoning, alongside detailed factual and social knowledge, language skills, and an understanding of culturally specific associations. We benchmark 28 LLM configurations using both automatic and large-scale human evaluation. Even frontier models achieve at most 22% accuracy in some communities. We publicly release all artifacts alongside an open contribution process to expand the benchmark's linguistic and cultural coverage.
中文翻译
随着大语言模型扩展其语言覆盖范围，评估它们理解并表征多样化文化的能力变得日益重要。然而，许多现有基准仍以英语为中心，文化覆盖有限，且侧重于事实知识而非其在真实文化语境中的应用。我们提出CulturalRiddles，一个由人类通过参与式社区协作完全创作的原创、文化根基深厚的谜题集合，涵盖62个文化（45个国家，51种语言）。这些谜题深深植根于各自文化背景，要求具备溯因推理和组合推理能力，同时需要详尽的事实与社会知识、语言技能以及对文化特异性关联的理解。我们使用自动评估和大规模人工评估对28种LLM配置进行了基准测试。即使前沿模型在某些文化群体中的准确率也仅达22%左右。我们公开发布所有数据集及开放贡献流程，以持续扩展该基准的语言和文化覆盖面。
👤 My Chiffon Nguyen, Tianyi Hu, Vinod Anbalagan, Henry Gagnier, Pouya Sadeghi et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Towards English - Mizo Legal Machine Translation: Corpus Creation, Analysis, and Benchmarks
迈向英语-米佐语法律机器翻译：语料库构建、分析与基准测试
Indian court proceedings are held in English, so speakers of languages with limited translation technology have limited access to them. We present the first English Mizo parallel dataset of Indian court judgments. It contains 13,168 sentence pairs from the Gauhati High Court and the Supreme Court of India. Professional native Mizo translators translated complete documents; sentence pairs were manually aligned, and two independent reviewers checked the results. Our study finds that English words are borrowed in a very selective way: official and legal terms are almost always kept in English, but words for everyday things like actions, situations, and results are usually expressed in native Mizo. Mizo diacritics are also used inconsistently across the corpus. When we fine tune the NLLB 200 1.3B model on our training data, it scores 27.47 BLEU on a balanced legal test set of 1,000 sentence pairs, an improvement of 7.16 BLEU over the base model. However, it still uses English words about three times more often than a human translator does.
中文翻译
印度法庭程序使用英语进行，因此缺乏翻译技术的语言使用者难以获取相关信息。本文提出了首个印度法院判决的英语-米佐语平行语料库，包含来自高哈蒂高等法院和印度最高法院的13,168个句子对。专业母语米佐语译者完成了完整文档的翻译；句子对经过人工对齐，并由两名独立评审员核查结果。研究发现，英语词汇仅在特定情况下被借用：官方和法律术语几乎总是保留英文，而日常事物如动作、情境和结果则通常用本地米佐语表达。米佐语的变音符号在语料库中也使用不一致。当我们在训练数据上微调NLLB 200 1.3B模型时，在包含1,000个句子对的平衡法律测试集上得分达到27.47 BLEU，较基础模型提升7.16 BLEU。然而，其仍比人类译者多使用约三倍的英语词汇。
👤 Telem Joyson Singh, Sanabam Boyshana, Sanasam Ranbir Singh, Priyankoo Sarmah 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29926]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Task-Preserving Multilingual Instruction Tuning for Open-Ended QA in WMT 2026 MIST
面向WMT 2026 MIST开放式问答任务的多语言指令微调
This paper describes our submission to the WMT 2026 Multilingual Instruction Shared Task. We adapt Qwen3-8B with LoRA using organiser-provided data, EuroAlpaca, a multilingual instruction dataset created through task-preserving localisation, and CrossEuroAlpaca, a cross-lingual augmentation that assigns different source and target languages to instructions, contexts and responses. Our system targets open-ended question answering (QA-OEG) in ten shared-task languages represented in the auxiliary data. Under matched decoding, adaptation improves the mean Gemma-4 judge score on target-language QA-OEG by 7.8% relative to the base model. On an additional evaluation using the Aya Evaluation Suite across all shared-task languages, it yields relative gains of 41.5% in macro-averaged ROUGE-L and 2.9% in BERTScore-F1, with larger improvements in target languages. However, these gains are task- and language-specific. Gemma-4 scores improve for QA-OEG and summarisation in the target languages but decline for context-grounded QA there and across all three tasks in the non-target languages.
中文翻译
本文介绍了我们对WMT 2026多语言指令共享任务的提交方案。我们采用LoRA方法对Qwen3-8B进行适配，使用组织方提供的数据EuroAlpaca——一个通过任务保真本地化创建的多语言指令数据集，以及CrossEuroAlpaca——一种跨语言增强方法，为指令、上下文和响应分配不同的源语言和目标语言。我们的系统针对辅助数据中涵盖的十种共享任务语言的开放式问答（QA-OEG）任务。在匹配解码条件下，相较于基础模型，该系统在目标语言的QA-OEG任务上使Gemma-4裁判评分平均提升了7.8%。在额外使用Aya评估套件对所有共享任务语言进行评估时，其宏平均ROUGE-L相对提升41.5%，BERTScore-F1提升2.9%，且在目标语言中提升更显著。然而，这些增益具有任务和语言特异性：Gemma-4在目标语言的QA-OEG和摘要任务中得分提升，但在目标语言的上下文依赖问答任务中下降，且在非目标语言中所有三项任务均出现下降。
👤 Aleix Sant, Jordi Luque, Carlos Escolano 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29922]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Unintended Side Effects of Value Training in LLMs
大型语言模型价值训练的意外副作用
As LLMs are increasingly deployed in value-sensitive contexts, aligning them with human values has emerged as a post-training objective in NLP, aimed at instilling additional values and guiding model behaviour. However, current evaluations remain narrow. Because values are interconnected, it is unclear how fine-tuning for specific values alters unrelated capabilities and behaviours. In this work, we adapt four instruction-tuned LLMs across individual Hofstede's value dimensions and evaluate them on both cross-value transfer and broader behaviours. We find that value-specific training produces unintended side effects on untrained values, risk preferences, social cognition, and safety traits. Consequently, we suggest that value alignment after post-training should incorporate broader behavioral evaluations, and we highlight the need to investigate where additional value training might best fit into the pipeline.
中文翻译

Benchmarking Large Language Models for Detecting Satirical Code-Mixed Sentences
检测讽刺性混合语句的大型语言模型基准测试
Code-switching is a natural linguistic phenomenon, yet its impact on high-level reasoning tasks remains largely unexplored in under-resourced languages like Romanian. Natural language processing tasks, such as satire detection, rely heavily on nuanced cultural and syntactic cues that large language models struggle to capture. In this paper, we investigate four methods (i.e., random replacement, phrase translation, dependency parsing, and large language models) for generating synthetic code-mixed Romanian sentences with English, German, French, and Italian. We start with an existing dataset for satire detection, compiled from Romanian news articles and annotated at the sentence level by humans, and augment it with over 600k samples, namely CS-SeLeRoSa. We provide an in-depth analysis of the dataset generation process. Then, we evaluate several multilingual large language models across zero-shot, chain-of-thought, and fine-tuning regimes on both the synthetic corpus and a human-adjudicated gold benchmark, namely CS-SeLeRoSa-Gold. While older open models suffer substantial degradation (dropping to 37%-55% macro-F1), newer models like Gemma 4 31B achieve 68.1% zero-shot, rivaling frontier models like GPT-5 mini (69.5%). Furthermore, fine-tuning significantly reduces formatting errors and improves the results, increasing Gemma 4 31B to 77.5% on CS-SeLeRoSa and 72.6% on the gold standard, outperforming frontier zero-shot baselines.

Evolving with Open Worlds: Towards Recursive Self-Improvement for Future Forecasting
与开放世界共进化：迈向未来预测的递归自我改进
Most contemporary artificial intelligence (AI) systems are released with frozen parameters and improve through external updates, whereas an ideal system would keep improving from its own experience, a capacity we call recursive self-improvement (RSI). Yet studying RSI requires an environment that is continuously changing, objectively verifiable, and naturally grounded, posing a fundamental challenge for agent evolution. Here we introduce DeepEvolve, a sustainable evolution agent system grounded in future event prediction, in which agents continuously interact with a dynamically evolving environment and improve by internalizing and externalizing the experience gained through these interactions. A living environment synthesizes verifiable tasks, and a recursive-evolution engine turns the reasoning trajectory behind each prediction back into capability: decoupling search from prediction adapts parameter-level evolution to the contamination-prone live environment, while distilling reusable skills lets the agent evolve from the limited live trajectories such an environment affords. On the external FutureX leaderboard, which evaluates predictions against real-world outcomes and is updated weekly, our self-improved forecaster ranked first across all systems over three consecutive weeks, achieving up to a 195% improvement in rating. On our synthesized evaluation, it ranked among the top three across four base LLMs, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 10.7 Bradley--Terry points. More broadly, these results suggest that a system built for self-improvement is best understood as an agent that co-evolves with a genuinely living, open-ended environment, pointing to a shift in AI from training static models on fixed data toward cultivating agents that grow stronger from their own experience, with such an open world as the indispensable basis of that growth.
中文翻译
当前大多数人工智能系统以参数冻结的形式发布，并通过外部更新进行改进，而理想系统应能从自身经验中持续改进，我们称之为递归自我改进（RSI）。然而，研究RSI需要一个持续变化、客观可验证且自然扎根的环境，这对智能体演化构成根本挑战。本文提出DeepEvolve——一个基于未来事件预测的可持续演化智能体系统，其中智能体持续与动态演化的环境互动，并通过内化和外化这些互动所获得的经验实现改进。一个活态环境生成可验证的任务，而递归演化引擎将每次预测背后的推理轨迹转化为能力：解耦搜索与预测，使参数级演化适应易受污染的实时环境；同时提炼可复用技能，使智能体能够从此类环境提供的有限实时轨迹中演化。在每周更新的外部FutureX排行榜上，我们的自改进预测器连续三周排名第一，评分最高提升达195%。在合成评估中，它在四个基础LLM上均位列前三，相比最强基线最高提升10.7 Bradley-Terry分。更广泛地，这些结果表明，一个为自我改进设计的系统最好理解为与真正活态、开放无界的环境共同进化的智能体，标志着AI正从在固定数据上训练静态模型，转向培育能从自身经验中不断成长的智能体，而开放世界是这一成长不可或缺的基础。

ALIAS: Evaluating Performance and Memorization in LLM-Written TLA+ Specifications
ALIAS：评估大语言模型编写TLA+规范中的性能与记忆现象
Memorization inflates the benchmark scores of large language models (LLMs), since a model can reproduce answers it saw in training. Researchers test for it with consistent identifier renaming (CIR), which gives every identifier in a task a new name, and treat a lower score on the renamed task as evidence of recall. We test this method on the Temporal Logic of Actions Plus (TLA+) language, whose model checker acts as an oracle by confirming whether a renamed task preserves the exact behaviors of the original. Across 111 TLA+-Bench tasks, renaming lowers the correctness of Claude Opus 4.8 from 28% to 18% and of GPT-5 from 9% to 7%. Most of this decrease comes from how the test grades, which requires exact names that most descriptions never state. Grading by behavior shrinks the decrease to about 3 percentage points for Opus 4.8 and to 0 for GPT-5. In two model families fine-tuned on disjoint halves of the tasks, renaming removes about half of the training gain in likelihood and costs 4.9 percentage points (Qwen) and 1.3 percentage points (Gemma) on unseen tasks. A lower score after renaming therefore mixes memorization, name-based grading, and the cost of new names.
中文翻译
记忆现象会夸大大型语言模型（LLMs）在基准测试中的得分，因为模型可以复现训练中见过的答案。研究人员使用一致标识符重命名（CIR）方法检测记忆现象，即为任务中的每个标识符赋予新名称，若重命名后得分降低，则视为回忆证据。我们在时序动作逻辑加（TLA+）语言上测试该方法，其模型检查器作为判别器，可确认重命名任务是否保留原任务的精确行为。在111个TLA+-Bench任务中，重命名使Claude Opus 4.8的正确率从28%降至18%，GPT-5从9%降至7%。大部分下降源于评分机制要求精确名称，而多数描述并未提供。若按行为评分，Opus 4.8的下降仅约3个百分点，GPT-5则无下降。在对两个不相交任务子集进行微调的模型族中，重命名消除了约一半的训练增益，导致未见任务上的得分分别下降4.9个百分点（Qwen）和1.3个百分点（Gemma）。因此，重命名后的低分混合了记忆、基于名称的评分以及新名称带来的成本。
👤 Arslan Bisharat, Mujtaba Nazari, Khushboo Bhadauria, Brian Ortiz, Eric Spencer et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Bridging Foundational and Agentic Capabilities of LLMs
弥合大语言模型的基础能力与代理能力之间的鸿沟
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as agents, yet it remains unclear how their foundational capabilities translate into agentic capabilities. End-to-end scores leave unresolved whether low benchmark capability or weak deployment drives a failure, limiting actionable diagnosis. We bridge foundational and agentic capabilities through an operation-level measurement framework. We represent each model with a foundational profile derived from capability benchmarks and an operation profile derived from interaction trajectories, and define capability realization gaps as deviations of observed operation performance from predictions based on foundational capabilities. Experiments on fifteen model services in $τ^2$-Bench show that foundational profiles only partially predict agentic operations. Models with comparable profiles can differ substantially in execution, and these gaps remain consistent across task subsets. Operation profiles add information and substantially improve task-score prediction. The framework locates execution departures at the operation level to inform model development and agent evaluation. We will release our code and analysis artifacts.

👤 Wei Guo, Yuanjian Xu, Jianing Hao, Yongyao Duan, Ran Chen et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Retrieval Augmented Generation Compositionality
检索增强生成的组合性
Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds large language models in external knowledge. Modern pipelines combine embedding, chunking, retrieval, reranking, query augmentation, context compression, and agentic verification, yet these techniques are typically evaluated one at a time. It remains unclear whether their isolated gains survive composition, whether retrieval quality and answer faithfulness favor the same design choices, and whether conclusions from small corpora hold at large scale. We pre-declare three hypotheses and introduce LASAGNA, an open-source framework for controlled RAG evaluation. We test 450 configurations across eight benchmarks and corpora ranging from 5K to 200K documents. Three findings emerge. First, individually strong techniques from different phases of a RAG pipeline do not necessarily compose: three retrieval techniques predict a +47% combined improvement but deliver only +17% when used together. Second, component gains stack differently for retrieval and faithfulness, and optimizing one can hurt the other. Third, the best composition changes as the corpus grows: the combination that wins on a small corpus may not remain best at large scale. These findings show that gains measured in isolation are not reliable predictors of realistic performance, and that new RAG techniques should be tested with the full surrounding stack. We call on researchers to do so and supply LASAGNA to simplify these experiments.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）将大型语言模型与外部知识相结合。现代流水线结合了嵌入、分块、检索、重排序、查询增强、上下文压缩和代理验证等技术，但这些技术通常被单独评估。目前尚不清楚它们在组合使用时的增益是否依然有效，检索质量与答案忠实度是否偏好相同的设计选择，以及小语料库得出的结论在大规模场景下是否仍然成立。我们预先声明了三个假设，并引入LASAGNA——一个用于受控RAG评估的开源框架。我们在涵盖8个基准和语料库（从5K到200K文档）的450种配置上进行了测试。研究发现三个关键点：第一，来自RAG流水线不同阶段的单独表现优异的技术并不一定具有组合效应：三种检索技术预测可带来+47%的联合提升，但实际仅实现+17%；第二，组件增益在检索和忠实度上的叠加方式不同，优化其中一个可能损害另一个；第三，最佳组合随语料库规模增长而变化：在小语料库上表现最优的组合在大规模下未必仍最优。这些发现表明，孤立测量的增益无法可靠预测真实性能，新RAG技术应在其完整堆栈环境中进行测试。我们呼吁研究人员这样做，并提供LASAGNA以简化此类实验。

Keeping Time vs. Understanding Time: Neither Alone Explains Temporal Reasoning in LLMs
时间的保持 vs. 时间的理解：单独两者均无法解释大语言模型中的时间推理
Temporal reasoning is widely assumed to decompose into two modes: temporal arithmetic (date and time calculations), and temporal semantics (understanding temporal and causal relations). This dichotomy shapes how benchmarks are designed, how models are trained, and how progress is measured, yet its empirical validity has never been directly tested. We provide two lines of evidence that challenge it. First, we test whether tasks labelled as arithmetic or semantic by the benchmarks support transferable gains within their purported mode. Finetuning on these subsets yields predominantly dataset-specific gains ($+6.8$ to $+23.1$ percentage points), while transfer to a different dataset in the same mode ranges from $-3.2$ to $+1.1$ percentage points. Across the two benchmark families used in the transfer analysis, this pattern provides little evidence of coherent, transferable temporal abilities. Second, using cognitive-demand-based model evaluation, we examine which capabilities actually drive performance on these benchmarks. We find that quantitative and logical reasoning, the assumed proxies for temporal arithmetic and temporal semantics, are not the primary drivers; knowledge is. Together, these findings show that current temporal reasoning benchmarks do not cleanly realise the arithmetic-semantics distinction and instead largely reward models for remembering how time typically works.

TenetBench: A Clause-Anchored Russian Corpus for Evaluating Language Models against Professional Codes of Ethics
TenetBench：用于评估语言模型遵守专业伦理规范的条款锚定俄语语料库
Codes of professional ethics guide and restrict specialists, who increasingly work with assistants built on large language models. Such an assistant can breach the user's code, or help the user breach it, without the user noticing. Evaluations reporting broad categories of harm cannot show how often this happens or which provision is breached. TenetBench pairs 3,433 requests conflicting with clauses of fifteen Russian-language professional codes with benign twins that differ only in the legitimacy of what is asked. An automated pipeline generates the tasks from the text of a code, so the corpus grows with every code added and a provision yields new tasks on every run. It also produced tasks and human-validated verdicts from two English codes of other jurisdictions. In human validation of the frozen pipeline, specialists rated 0.995 of the conflicting tasks plausible, and the model judge agreed with the adjudicated label at Gwet's AC1 of at least 0.894 on 1,860 elements. We evaluated fourteen model and reasoning-mode combinations on whether they produce the conflicting text, decline or warn. Eleven of the fourteen produce conflicting content on most such requests, mostly without a warning, and nearly three warnings in four accompany conflicting content. The models that conform more do so by declining while still serving the legitimate twin, and enabling reasoning halved the refusals of two models. We hope the dataset and pipeline will help evaluate how language models observe professional norms and let diagnostic datasets be built for particular professions and organisations.
中文翻译
专业伦理规范指导并约束专业人士，而他们越来越多地使用基于大语言模型的助手。此类助手可能在用户未察觉的情况下违反用户的伦理规范，或协助用户违反。现有评估报告仅涵盖广义伤害类别，无法揭示此类违规发生的频率或具体违反哪一条款。TenetBench将3,433个与十五种俄语专业伦理条款冲突的请求，与仅在合法性上不同的良性对照请求配对。自动化流水线从伦理文本生成任务，因此语料库随新增条款动态扩展，每次运行均可产生新任务。该流程还为两个其他司法管辖区的英文伦理规范生成了任务和经人工验证的判决结果。在冻结流水线的人工验证中，专家评定99.5%的冲突任务具有合理性，模型裁判在1,860个样本上的Gwet's AC1值至少达到0.894。我们在十四种模型与推理模式组合上评估其是否生成冲突内容、拒绝或警告。其中十一组在大多数此类请求中生成了冲突内容，大多未发出警告，且约四分之三的警告伴随冲突内容出现。更合规的模型通过拒绝请求但仍服务合法对照请求实现合规，而启用推理机制使两个模型的拒绝率减半。我们希望该数据集与流水线能帮助评估语言模型如何遵守专业规范，并推动特定职业与组织诊断数据集的构建。
👤 Yegor Anichkov, Valery Vinogradov, Andrey Furov, Marina Polukoshko, Sergey Bolovtsov 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

OmniSimulator: Aligning Small Language Models for Authentic Heterogeneous Behavior Modeling
OmniSimulator：对齐小型语言模型以实现真实异构行为建模
Large language models (LLMs) have shown great potential as general-purpose user simulators for interactive systems. Prior studies have demonstrated that training on high-quality data can substantially improve model reasoning and downstream task performance. However, the upper bound of user simulation is still constrained by the model's reasoning paradigm: native chain-of-thought often degenerates into shallow pattern matching and fails to adequately model the behavioral factors underlying real user decisions. In this paper, we propose OmniSimulator, a novel training framework designed to improve the predictive ability of small LLMs for realistic user simulation on short-video platform environments. Specifically, OmniSimulator organizes behavior-oriented reasoning through a structured schema with five dimensions, carefully selects data from real user-behavior scenarios, and introduces a teacher model to generate high-quality reasoning traces under the proposed schema, helping small models learn behavior-relevant reasoning patterns rather than relying on superficial behavioral correlations. Based on this process, we construct 9,881 high-quality training instances from three months of historical interaction trajectories of 200 real users, averaging about 50 distilled examples per user. Experimental results show that OmniSimulator improves the average score of Qwen3-8B by 34.33%, achieves performance competitive with Claude-Sonnet-4.5, and supports smaller-scale, self-hosted deployment. These results suggest that learning structured behavior-oriented reasoning patterns is a promising direction towards scalable and high-fidelity LLM-based user simulation.

Omnilingual-MsASR: Benchmarking Multimodal, Multilingual, Long-Form Multi-Speaker ASR of Omni Large Language Models
Omnilingual-MsASR：面向全能大语言模型的多模态、多语言、长时程多人语音识别基准评测
Omni-modal large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in audio and video understanding. However, existing models struggle with long-form, multi-speaker videos, especially in understanding the entire video and recognizing who says what and when. To characterize the capability limit of current omni LLMs and guide future model development, we introduce Omnilingual-MsASR, a multilingual, multimodal benchmark for long-form Multi-speaker Automatic Speech Recognition (MsASR) covering six languages, recordings up to 30 minutes, and scenarios with up to 20 speakers. Omnilingual-MsASR provides parallel audio and video inputs to evaluate models' cross-modal consistency and their ability to exploit complementary cues across modalities. Extensive evaluations of leading omni-modal LLMs reveal substantial performance bottlenecks, while their tcpWERs frequently exceed 100%. These findings highlight fine-grained temporal localization, speaker disambiguation, and cross-modal synergy as key challenges for future omni-modal foundation models. Our work is available at https://github.com/QwenLM/Omnilingua-Bench/tree/main/Omnilingua-MSpeaker.

DynaWeave: Learning to Adapt through Dynamic Scenario Synthesis
DynaWeave：通过动态场景合成实现自适应学习
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use tools to complete tasks in stateful environments. However, many training environments remain largely static apart from changes caused by the agent's own actions, whereas external events introduced by other users, services, or sudden contingencies in real environments can invalidate ongoing plans. To address this gap, we introduce DynaWeave, an automated synthesis framework that transforms existing static tasks into verifiable dynamic scenarios. The framework generates exogenous events relevant to each task, covering changes in task requirements, environment state, and external information. It uses directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) to represent dependencies between events and an execution engine to activate them during agent interaction. A dynamic verifier revises only the evaluation checks affected by events that actually occur and retains all other checks. Using DynaWeave, we collect 2,095 verified interaction trajectories across 36 environments and use them for supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5 models. Experiments on Gaia2, CostBench, and ToolMaze show that DynaWeave achieves consistent improvements in overall performance across all three evaluated model sizes, increasing the task completion rate on CostBench by 67.34 percentage points and even exceeding the performance of Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview on ToolMaze. These results support dynamic scenario synthesis as a practical approach to training agents to adapt to changing environments. Our code is available at https://github.com/Miaow-Lab/DynaWeave.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）代理在有状态环境中越来越多地使用工具来完成任务。然而，许多训练环境除了代理自身行为引起的改变外，其余部分仍保持高度静态；而真实环境中由其他用户、服务或突发情况引入的外部事件可能使正在进行的计划失效。为解决这一差距，我们提出了DynaWeave，一个自动场景合成框架，可将现有静态任务转化为可验证的动态场景。该框架生成与每个任务相关的外生事件，涵盖任务需求、环境状态和外部信息的变化。它使用有向无环图（DAG）表示事件间的依赖关系，并通过执行引擎在代理交互过程中激活这些事件。动态验证器仅修改实际发生事件所影响的评估检查，保留其余所有检查。利用DynaWeave，我们在36个环境中收集了2,095条经验证的交互轨迹，并用于Qwen3.5模型的监督微调。在Gaia2、CostBench和ToolMaze上的实验表明，DynaWeave在所有三个评估模型尺寸上均实现了稳定的性能提升，在CostBench上的任务完成率提高了67.34个百分点，甚至超过了Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview在ToolMaze上的表现。这些结果支持动态场景合成作为训练代理适应变化环境的一种实用方法。我们的代码可在https://github.com/Miaow-Lab/DynaWeave获取。

Signals and Queries: Token-Efficient Agentic Context Discovery
信号与查询：面向代理的高效上下文发现
User-facing LLM agents must identify which behavioral policies apply at each turn, but evaluating every policy with an LLM is costly. We introduce Signals and Queries (S&Q), a lightweight first-stage relevance filter developed for Parlant 4.0 that removes at least 30% of candidate policies before downstream evaluation while preserving high recall. For each policy, an LLM generates activating examples and hard negatives offline. An independent logistic classifier trained on their embeddings then predicts relevance using embeddings and vector dot products, with no per-turn LLM calls. This shifts expensive relevance evaluation to a smaller candidate set, while allowing each policy's classifier to be updated independently. Evaluated against 9,144 LLM-judge relevance labels covering 254 $τ^2$-bench conversation prefixes and 36 airline policies, S&Q achieves higher precision than top-K and cosine-threshold retrieval at every evaluated recall level, with consistent results across two embedding providers. These findings support inexpensive, per-policy classification as a practical first stage for reducing downstream evaluation workload in customer-facing agents.
中文翻译
面向用户的LLM代理必须在每一轮中识别适用的行为策略，但使用LLM评估每个策略成本高昂。我们提出信号与查询（S&Q），一种为Parlant 4.0设计的轻量级第一阶段相关性过滤机制，可在下游评估前移除至少30%的候选策略，同时保持高召回率。对于每个策略，LLM离线生成激活示例和硬负样本。随后，一个独立训练的逻辑回归分类器基于其嵌入向量和向量点积预测相关性，无需每轮调用LLM。这将昂贵的相关性评估转移到更小的候选集上，同时允许每个策略的分类器独立更新。在包含254个τ²-bench对话前缀和36个航空公司策略的9,144个LLM裁判相关性标签上评估，S&Q在所有召回率水平下均优于Top-K和余弦阈值检索，且在两个嵌入提供商间结果一致。这些发现支持以低成本、按策略分类作为客户导向代理中降低下游评估工作量的实际第一阶段。

OSynth: A Longitudinal, Context-Sensitive Evaluation of Meeting Understanding
OSynth：面向会议理解的纵向、上下文敏感评估
Meeting notetakers are increasingly deployed in settings where longitudinal context is available including calendar metadata, prior meetings, emails, slides, and speaker enrollments; however, meeting corpora remain limited in terms of size, domain coverage, and available context. We present OSynth, a large-scale synthetic dataset of simulated organizations comprising 570 hours of meeting audio together with associated artifacts and a latent ground-truth state. In a pairwise comparison, human raters distinguish OSynth meetings from real meeting transcripts with only 56% accuracy based on excerpts of 150-300 words. Across state-of-the-art LLMs, additional context yields significant gains on several entity-sensitive dimensions, demonstrating limitations of contemporary approaches and the potential usefulness of OSynth in developing better-contextualized meeting-understanding systems.

Asking What a Director Sees Can Change What Vision-Language Models Choose
询问导演所见能改变视觉-语言模型的选择
Vision-language models are now routinely tested on theory of mind: whether they can work out what another person sees, knows, and means. A common design asks the model to report that perspective and then to act for the person, and reads a correct report beside a wrong action as a failure to use the perspective. That reading rests on a quiet assumption: that asking for the report leaves the action unchanged. We show that it does not. In a rendered Director Task, a partition hides the largest block from a director who asks for "the largest block". Asking first which blocks the director can see raises the share of replies that choose this hidden block by 12 to 88 percentage points on six GPT models and by 17 on Qwen3.8-27B, even though the models mostly report correctly which blocks the director sees. Reasoning traces and prompt rewordings suggest why: the question shifts how the request is read, from the director's view toward the model's own. When the prompt states what the director knows and refers to, fewer than 1% of replies pair a correct report with a hidden-block choice. Any evaluation that elicits a perspective report before the action it scores needs a baseline without the question, and we deliver one: paired prompts that differ only in that question, a design any perspective-taking benchmark can adopt.
中文翻译
视觉-语言模型如今常被用于测试心智理论：即它们能否推断出另一个人所见、所知和所指。常见设计是要求模型报告该视角，然后代表该人行动，并将正确报告与错误行动视为未能使用该视角。这一判断建立在一个隐含假设之上：询问报告不会改变后续行动。我们证明这一假设不成立。在渲染的导演任务中，一个隔板遮挡了最大块，导演要求“最大的块”。若先询问导演能看到哪些块，则六种GPT模型选择隐藏块的比例上升12至88个百分点，Qwen3.8-27B则上升17个百分点，尽管模型大多能正确报告导演可见的块。推理轨迹和提示重述表明原因：问题改变了请求的理解方式，从导演视角转向模型自身视角。当提示明确说明导演所知内容并引用时，少于1%的回复将正确报告与选择隐藏块配对。任何在行动前获取视角报告的评估，都需设置无该问题的基线，我们提供了这样的基线：仅在该问题上不同的配对提示，任何心智理论基准均可采用此设计。

Credit Where Credit Is Due: Contrastive Attribution Topology Search for Multi-Agent Systems
功过分明：多智能体系统的对比归因拓扑搜索
An LLM multi-agent system (MAS) is only as good as the agents it chooses to trust: its accuracy and cost hinge on its topology, meaning which agents are included and how they communicate. A single task score, however, does not reveal which agents help or hinder performance. We propose Contrastive Attribution Topology Search (CATS), which searches for compact teams within a fixed supergraph of specialized agents. An LLM attributer compares execution traces across candidate topologies and assigns credit to individual agents. CATS uses these credits to update supergraph edges while progressively pruning weak edges and agents, yielding a small team for deployment. With small open-weight models and matched candidate evaluations, CATS improves mean accuracy on BigCodeBench by 5.3 percentage points over score-only heuristic search while using 54\% fewer deployment tokens. On MBPP, CATS matches the strongest baselines with fewer tokens than the other searches. In a HotpotQA prompt-poisoning stress test, CATS excludes all adversarially prompted agents in all three runs, whereas random search retains some at comparable accuracy. Per-agent credit thus supports both team optimization and the pruning of potentially harmful agents.
中文翻译
一个LLM多智能体系统（MAS）的性能取决于其所信任的智能体：其准确性和成本取决于其拓扑结构，即包含哪些智能体以及如何通信。然而，单一任务得分无法揭示哪些智能体有助于性能，哪些会阻碍。我们提出对比归因拓扑搜索（CATS），在固定的专业智能体超图内搜索紧凑团队。一个LLM归因器比较不同候选拓扑的执行轨迹，并为个体智能体分配贡献度。CATS利用这些贡献度更新超图边，逐步剪枝弱边和智能体，最终生成一个小型部署团队。使用小型开源权重模型和匹配的候选评估，CATS在BigCodeBench上的平均准确率比仅基于得分的启发式搜索提高5.3个百分点，同时部署token减少54%。在MBPP上，CATS达到最强基线表现，且使用的token更少。在HotpotQA提示污染压力测试中，CATS在三次运行中均排除了所有恶意提示智能体，而随机搜索仍保留部分，且准确率相当。因此，个体智能体的贡献度既支持团队优化，也支持剔除潜在有害智能体。

Let Agents Design How They Search: Automatic Interface Optimization for Agentic Search
让智能体设计它们的搜索方式：面向代理搜索的自动界面优化
Agentic search depends on the interface through which a language-model agent accesses and explores a corpus. Existing interface designs are fixed by hand and applied unchanged to every corpus, although effective designs may depend on the corpus. We introduce SearchWeaver, a framework for automatic search interface optimization guided by downstream search performance. A proposer uses execution trajectories and task rewards to jointly revise corpus artifacts, executable tools, and usage instructions. This pro cess optimizes what information is prepared in advance, which operations tools perform, and which decisions remain with the search agent. With GPT-5.4-nano, the optimized interfaces improve answer accuracy from 71.6% to 78.4% on BrowseComp-Plus and average nDCG@10 from 45.7 to 55.0 across four BRIGHT subsets over Direct Corpus Interaction. These findings highlight the value of jointly optimizing corpus artifacts, executable tools, and usage instructions to develop effective search interfaces.

MOSAIC: An Omni-Modal Benchmark for Long-Term Memory and Cross-Modal Reasoning in Conversational Agents
MOSAIC：对话智能体长期记忆与跨模态推理的全模态基准
Omni-modal assistants must remember not only what users say, but also what they show and convey. Existing long-term memory benchmarks are mostly text-and-image or report a single end-to-end score, so they cannot locate whether evidence is lost when it is represented, retrieved, delivered, or read. We introduce MOSAIC, a benchmark of 923 questions over persona-grounded histories of 11-24 sessions spanning text, image, video, and audio. MOSAIC constructs targeted spatial, temporal, and prosodic signals rather than decorative media, with the strongest causal validation for spatial information and selector-level validation for annotated prosody, and pairs them with a matched diagnostic protocol that freezes the question, reader, prompt, and budget while intervening on representation, retrieval, and delivery. Matched decomposition shows that representation loss is modality-dependent, residual reading dominates even under gold evidence, and abstention remains weak on these controls: strong answerable scores coexist with failures to refuse. MOSAIC thus turns omni-modal memory evaluation from a leaderboard into a diagnosis of write, retrieve, deliver, and read failures.
中文翻译
全模态助手不仅需要记住用户说了什么，还需记住他们展示和传达的内容。现有长期记忆基准大多仅限于文本与图像，或仅报告单一端到端分数，无法定位证据是在表示、检索、传递还是阅读阶段丢失的。我们提出MOSAIC，一个包含923个问题的基准，覆盖11至24轮会话的基于人物设定的历史记录，涵盖文本、图像、视频和音频多种模态。MOSAIC构建的是有针对性的空间、时间与语调信号，而非装饰性媒体，其中空间信息具有最强因果验证，语调标注具备选择器级别的验证，并配以匹配的诊断协议——冻结问题、读者、提示和预算，仅干预表示、检索与传递环节。匹配分解显示，表示损失具有模态依赖性，即使在黄金证据下，残余阅读仍占主导地位，拒绝行为依然薄弱：高可回答分数与拒绝失败并存。因此，MOSAIC将全模态记忆评估从排行榜转变为对表示、检索、传递与阅读失败的诊断。

Beyond Binary Labels: Diagnostic Evaluation of LLM Safety with Prompt-Level Rubrics
超越二元标签：基于提示级评分标准的LLM安全诊断评估
Current LLM safety benchmarks predominantly rely on binary safe or unsafe labels, lacking explanatory depth and suffering from low consistency across automated judges. To address this, we introduce DiagSafe, a diagnostic evaluation framework that moves beyond binary classification by employing prompt-level rubrics. Grounded in globally applicable AI safety policies, each rubric provides explicit criteria along two dimensions: safety, which targets concrete harms with specific exemptions to suppress false positives, and usefulness, which independently evaluates instruction fulfillment. To ensure a robust and realistic evaluation, we construct a bilingual (English-Chinese) benchmark grounded in a comprehensive 84-label safety taxonomy, consisting of two subsets: DiagBase, which covers single- and multi-turn prompts with direct harmful queries and template-based jailbreaks, and DiagAttack, which embeds harmful intents into complex instruction-following tasks to form challenging instruction attacks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DiagSafe significantly enhances the reliability of LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, achieving 95.34% agreement with human annotations -- substantially higher than the 69%-87% achieved by the baselines -- and high inter-judge consistency (Cohen's $κ$ = 0.864). Furthermore, applying our framework reveals that leading LLMs differ markedly in their unsafe rates, and each model also exhibits unique vulnerabilities along different dimensions. In addition, we observe that models are particularly susceptible in Chinese and multi-turn contexts. Ultimately, DiagSafe provides a reliable and explainable tool for auditing and optimizing LLM alignment.
中文翻译
当前的大语言模型（LLM）安全基准主要依赖二元的安全或不安全标签，缺乏解释深度，且在自动化评判中一致性较低。为解决此问题，我们提出DiagSafe，一种超越二元分类的诊断评估框架，采用提示级评分标准。基于普遍适用的人工智能安全政策，每个评分标准提供两个维度的明确标准：安全性，针对具体危害并设置特定豁免以减少误报；实用性，独立评估指令完成情况。为确保评估的稳健性和真实性，我们构建了一个双语（英文-中文）基准，基于全面的84标签安全分类体系，包含两个子集：DiagBase涵盖单轮与多轮提示，包括直接有害查询和基于模板的越狱攻击；DiagAttack则将有害意图嵌入复杂指令遵循任务中，形成具有挑战性的指令攻击。大量实验表明，DiagSafe显著提升了LLM作为评判者的可靠性，与人工标注的一致性达到95.34%，远高于基线的69%-87%；且评判者间一致性高（Cohen's κ = 0.864）。此外，应用该框架揭示出主流LLM在不安全率上存在显著差异，且各模型在不同维度表现出独特脆弱性。我们还发现模型在中文和多轮上下文中尤为易受攻击。最终，DiagSafe提供了一种可靠且可解释的工具，用于审计和优化LLM对齐。

norregel: a preregistered benchmark of dated numeric values in Norwegian law
norregel：挪威法律中日期化数值的预注册基准测试
norregel asks language models for numeric values in Norwegian statutes, regulations and parliamentary decisions (NOK amounts, percentages, multiples of the National Insurance basic amount (G) and age limits), each at a stated date. The 916 items were extracted mechanically from Lovdata's open data, and each carries the dated history of its provision, so an answer can be classed as correct, outdated, wrong or uninterpretable. We report a first and a second preregistered run on the same items. In the first run (five models, listed publicly only after local analysis), H1, that outdated answers are more frequent for values that took effect after a model's cutoff, could not be tested in substance, and H2 and H3 were inconclusive; Opus 5.5 and Sonnet 4.6 answered 46.7% and 40.2% of the 880 primary items correctly. The second run was preregistered and published before any of its calls, and eight models were analysed. Its pooled primary test supported H1 under the preregistered decision rule (d = 17.8 percentage points, 95% interval [7.7, 28.9], p = 0.0001). In the preregistered sensitivity analysis S8, which counts as outdated only the most recent value that differs from the key, d was 3.9 [-3.6, 12.7] (p = 0.1754), an interval that contains 0. H1 was supported in every other preregistered sensitivity analysis, but S8 is the only one that gives both groups one target value, so we do not regard the support for H1 as robust. The share of outdated answers equal to that most recent value (H2) was 56.1% [46.6, 65.0], an interval that contains 0.5, and the pooled test of a lower share correct in Nynorsk than in Bokmål (H3) was inconclusive. Several models, among them Haiku 4.5, gave mostly uninterpretable answers. Items and keys are withheld; item IDs, per-answer classes, code and results are on Zenodo.
中文翻译
norregel要求语言模型提取挪威法律、法规及议会决议（如金额、百分比、国家保险基本金额G的倍数、年龄限制）中的数值，并指定具体日期。916个条目由Lovdata开放数据机械提取，每个条目均带有其条款的日期历史记录，因此答案可被归类为正确、过时、错误或不可解释。我们报告了对相同条目的两次预注册运行。第一次运行（五种模型，仅在本地分析后公开）中，H1（模型截止日期后生效的数值更易出现过时答案）因实质上无法检验而未被验证，H2和H3结果不确定；Opus 5.5和Sonnet 4.6分别正确回答了880个主条目的46.7%和40.2%。第二次运行是预注册且在任何调用前发布，分析了八种模型。其综合主测试在预注册决策规则下支持H1（d = 17.8个百分点，95%置信区间[7.7, 28.9]，p = 0.0001）。在预注册敏感性分析S8中（仅将最近年份且不同于关键值的视为过时），d为3.9 [-3.6, 12.7]（p = 0.1754），该区间包含0，因此不具稳健支持。H1在其他所有预注册敏感性分析中均成立，但S8是唯一使两组各有一个目标值的情形，故我们认为H1的支持不够稳健。过时答案等于最近年份值的比例（H2）为56.1% [46.6, 65.0]，该区间包含0.5；关于Nynorsk比Bokmål正确率更低的综合测试（H3）结果亦不明确。多个模型（包括Haiku 4.5）给出大量不可解释的答案。条目与答案密钥暂不公开；条目ID、每答类别、代码与结果已上传至Zenodo。

Our Own Jev: Typed Decisions in One Forward Pass, from an In-House Judge to Open LoRA Models
我们自己的Jev：从内部裁判到开放LoRA模型的单次前向传递类型化决策
A typed decision maps evidence, a question and a fixed set of options to a probability for every option, computed in one forward pass, by reading a language model's logits or a small head over its hidden states, instead of generating and parsing text. The commercial system Jev popularised this System~1 interface, and open reproductions have followed. We report two uses of the pattern. \emph{Part I} describes an in-house System~1 judge that triages about 148K agentic coding session records for a training corpus. It replaces a frontier LLM judge that cost on the order of \$1,000 per pass and whose stored labels matched a fresh two-judge consensus on only 26.9\% of the sessions where the two judges agreed; a self-hosted frozen backbone with a rank-16 LoRA adapter and a pointer head answers three typed questions in one forward pass, judges 1,000 sessions in about three minutes of one GPU, about \$0.06 at a reference rate and roughly 1\% of the frontier judge's cost, and keeps every session inside the organisation. \emph{Part II} asks whether a readout that adds no parameters can be competitive and whether training one helps. Applying the public Eikos recipe and corpus of about 25K decisions to three further backbones (1B, 2B and 35B), we read the frozen output layer of a LoRA-adapted decoder. On the 111 public hard items of JevBench our single-seed 35B, developed with those items in view, scores 79.3\%, not significantly different from the commercial reference (73.0\%, unadjusted $p=0.12$) and ahead of five open systems after Holm--Bonferroni correction; our 2B is ahead of all four kev checkpoints on the leaderboard. A single-seed ablation finds no detectable benefit from training the 26 letter rows of the output layer, and a retrain on a later corpus release moves the 2B's hard-tier accuracy by 11.7 points, which shows how little small evaluations resolve.
中文翻译
类型化决策将证据、问题和一组固定选项映射为每个选项的概率，通过读取语言模型的logits或其隐藏状态上的小型头部，在一次前向传递中完成，而非生成并解析文本。商业系统Jev推广了这种System~1接口，随后出现了多个开源复现。我们报告了该模式的两种应用。第一部分描述了一个内部System~1裁判，用于筛选约14.8万条智能体编码会话记录以构建训练语料库。它取代了一个成本约为每轮1000美元的前沿LLM裁判，且其存储标签仅在26.9%的双方裁判一致会话中与新双裁判共识匹配；使用自托管冻结主干网络搭配rank-16 LoRA适配器和指针头，可在一次前向传递中回答三个类型化问题，3分钟内完成1000个会话的判断（单GPU，约0.06美元，仅为前沿裁判成本的1%），且所有会话均保留在组织内部。第二部分探讨了不增加参数的读出是否具有竞争力，以及训练是否有助于提升性能。基于公开的Eikos方法和约2.5万条决策语料，对三个额外主干（1B、2B和35B）进行测试，读取LoRA适配解码器的冻结输出层。在JevBench的111个公开难题上，单种子35B模型得分79.3%，与商业参考值（73.0%，未调整p=0.12）无显著差异，并在Holm-Bonferroni校正后优于五个开源系统；2B模型在排行榜上领先所有四个kev检查点。单种子消融实验表明，训练输出层的26个字母行无明显收益；在后续语料版本上重新训练使2B模型难题准确率提升11.7个百分点，说明小规模评估难以揭示真实性能。

Scan or Index? large Evidence Coverage, Answer Quality, and Cost in Long-Document RAG
扫描还是索引？长文档RAG中的大范围证据覆盖、答案质量与成本权衡
Scanning every passage in a long document seems thorough, but does it preserve more useful evidence or produce better answers? We compare 11 retrieval configurations spanning lexical and dense search, hybrid reranking, and full-document scanning with Jev, OpenAI Decisions, and Cloudflare Clef. Our evaluation uses 396 QASPER questions and 80 questions from eight complete textbooks, with common passages, a five-passage limit, and a fixed answer generator. On books, Hybrid + Jev achieves the highest observed evidence coverage (94.1%), compared with 88.7% for Jev full scan and 81.3\% for OpenAI dense retrieval. Full scans examine every passage initially, yet later reduction steps can discard relevant evidence. Higher evidence coverage also does not consistently produce better answers: Hybrid + Decisions achieves the highest observed book Answer Accuracy among retrieval methods (0.892), despite recovering less reference evidence. Full scanning incurs substantially higher latency on books under the tested deployments, while reusable dense retrieval has lower projected API cost than paid decision-based methods as queries accumulate. Most QASPER articles already fit within the hybrid candidate pool, limiting the benefit of scanning. These results highlight evidence retention, answer quality, and reuse cost as complementary criteria for choosing a long-document retrieval strategy.

对长文档中每一句话进行扫描看似全面，但是否能保留更多有用证据或产生更好答案？我们比较了11种检索配置，涵盖词法与密集搜索、混合重排序及全文扫描，使用Jev、OpenAI Decisions和Cloudflare Clef进行评估。评估基于396个QASPER问题和来自八本完整教科书的80个问题，采用公共段落、五段限制和固定答案生成器。在教科书中，Hybrid + Jev实现了最高的证据覆盖率（94.1%），高于Jev全扫描的88.7%和OpenAI密集检索的81.3%。全扫描虽初始检查所有段落，但后续缩减步骤可能丢弃相关证据。更高的证据覆盖率并未始终带来更好的答案：Hybrid + Decisions在检索方法中实现最高书籍答案准确率（0.892），尽管其恢复的参考证据较少。全扫描在测试部署下对教科书造成显著更高的延迟，而可重复使用的密集检索相比付费决策型方法具有更低的API成本。大多数QASPER文章已包含在混合候选池中，限制了扫描的收益。这些结果强调了证据保留、答案质量和可复用成本是选择长文档检索策略时的互补标准。

Building AI Research Teams for Autonomous Discovery of Linear Attention Architectures
构建用于自主发现线性注意力架构的AI研究团队
Autonomous agents can increasingly propose ideas, modify code, and run experiments, creating an opportunity to accelerate scientific discovery using teams of collaborative AI researchers. To escape local minima in the discovery, we propose to encourage orthogonal exploration by constructing the team using agents with varying expertise. However, realizing this design requires more than diverse backgrounds. When experiments are costly, a team can test only a limited number of ideas, thereby creating the need to balance exploration and exploitation when selecting a subset of researchers to propose and execute new experiments. Furthermore, each result should shape what the team tries next. We therefore ask how AI researchers with different scientific backgrounds can build on one another's findings and sustain progress under a limited experimental budget. To address these challenges, we build a team of persistent AI researchers, each grounded in a different discipline, who keep private research histories, share experimental evidence, and receive research opportunities through adaptive allocation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this design by deploying the team to search for linear attention architectures, where it outperforms GDN2 at 1.3B scale on language modeling, common-sense reasoning, and synthetic and real-world retrieval tasks. In controlled comparisons, removing any single organizational component from our design yields markedly weaker discoveries: without disciplinary backgrounds, the search plateaus early; without private histories, researchers make less progress despite identical shared evidence; and uniform selection falls short of adaptive allocation within the same budget. These results suggest that sustained autonomous discovery depends not only on stronger agents, but also on who explores, what they remember, and how research effort is allocated.
中文翻译
自主智能体能够越来越多地提出想法、修改代码并运行实验，这为利用协作AI研究团队加速科学发现提供了机会。为避免发现过程陷入局部最优，我们提出通过构建具备不同专长的团队来鼓励正交探索。然而，实现这一设计不仅需要多样化的背景。当实验成本高昂时，团队只能测试有限数量的想法，从而需要在探索与利用之间权衡以选择研究人员子集来提出并执行新实验。此外，每次结果都应影响团队下一步的尝试。因此，我们探讨了具有不同科学背景的AI研究者如何基于彼此成果持续推进，且在有限实验预算下保持进展。为此，我们构建了一个由持久性AI研究者组成的团队，每位研究者基于不同学科，保留私有研究历史，共享实验证据，并通过自适应分配获得研究机会。我们通过部署该团队搜索线性注意力架构，证明了该设计的有效性：在1.3B规模下，其性能优于GDN2，在语言建模、常识推理以及合成与真实世界检索任务中均表现更优。在受控对比中，移除设计中的任一组织组件都会导致发现能力显著下降：缺乏学科背景时，搜索过早停滞；缺乏私有历史时，尽管共享证据相同，研究者进步更少；均匀选择也逊于自适应分配。这些结果表明，持续的自主发现不仅依赖更强的智能体，还取决于谁在探索、他们记得什么，以及研究资源如何分配。

SANE: Automated Scorer-Specific Metamorphic Testing for LLM Judges
SANE：针对LLM裁判的自动化评分器特定变异测试
LLM judges increasingly mediate benchmark evaluation, model selection, data curation, and training. Their decisions are useful only when each judge responds reliably to changes affecting its scoring criterion. Existing methods for reliability testing perturb inputs with known expected effects on judge scores. Prominent LLM-judge testing harnesses combine human-authored perturbation recipes with synthetic test generation, leaving scorer-specific recipe ideation outside the automated pipeline. This limits their use to fixed perturbations and pre-assumed scorer types. We introduce SANE, an agent-based framework that encodes behavioral requirements for stochastic evaluators as reusable agent instructions. Given a scorer specification, implementation, and representative inputs, the pipeline first identifies scorer-specific capabilities, then automatically derives perturbation recipes and expected score relations for each. SANE also defines a scorer-independent validation framework for generated judge tests. It determines whether each recipe agrees with scorer intent and whether each instantiated case realizes the recipe and supports its expected relation. Because SANE generates a recipe inventory tailored to each scorer, the number of aligned recipes grows as scorers are added. Across five domains, SANE produces 3.7 times as many validated test families as the Judge Reliability Harness (JRH) at comparable end-to-end validity and detects scorer deficiencies in every domain, including professionalism and toxicity, where JRH detects none. On faithfulness, SANE achieves strong human alignment in judge ranking: its test-suite scores rank 11 judge models consistently with human-labeled FaithBench performance (Spearman rho = 0.852), while JRH does not (rho = 0.100).
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）裁判日益参与基准评估、模型选择、数据整理和训练。其决策只有在每个裁判能可靠响应影响其评分标准的变化时才有效。现有可靠性测试方法通过已知预期影响裁判评分的输入扰动进行测试。主流的LLM裁判测试结合人工编写的扰动配方与合成测试生成，但评分器特定的配方构思仍不在自动化流程内。这限制了其仅适用于固定扰动和预设评分器类型。我们引入SANE，一种基于智能体的框架，将随机评估者的行為要求编码为可重用的智能体指令。给定评分器规格、实现和代表性输入，该流程首先识别评分器特定能力，然后自动推导每种评分器的扰动配方和预期分数关系。SANE还定义了一个评分器无关的验证框架，用于生成的裁判测试。它判断每条配方是否符合评分器意图，以及每个实例是否实现该配方并支持预期关系。由于SANE为每个评分器生成定制的配方库，随着评分器增加，对齐配方数量也随之增长。在五个领域中，SANE生成的经验证测试族数量是Judge Reliability Harness（JRH）的3.7倍，且端到端有效性相当，同时在每个领域中检测出评分器缺陷，包括专业性和毒性问题，而JRH未检测到任何问题。在忠实度方面，SANE在裁判排名上表现出强人类一致性：其测试套件得分与人类标注的FaithBench性能排名高度一致（Spearman rho = 0.852），而JRH则无此关联（rho = 0.100）。
👤 Ethan Ludwick, Yu-Chung Hsiao 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28819]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
LIFT: Scoped Constraints for Evaluating and Training Instruction Following
LIFT：用于评估与训练指令遵循的范围化约束
Common instruction following (IF) benchmarks, such as IFEval, evaluate the ability of language models (LMs) to follow simple constraints that can easily be verified programmatically. These same constraints, in turn, are used as reward signals for reinforcement learning. This design may lead to reward hacking of constraints, pushing models away from the main task. Recent attempts to address this resulted in benchmarks with richer constraints, but required expert labor, remained closed-access, and cannot be easily extended. We present Lift-Bench, an IF benchmark that asks a model to draft a document given a topic and a structured outline. Constraints are scoped to certain document sections, providing finer control over constraint density. Lift-Bench includes both verifiable and judged constraints, as well as a training counterpart, Lift-Train. Importantly, Lift-Bench provides both main task and instruction-following constraints, mitigating shallow reward hacking. On experiments with multiple models, we find that frontier models struggle with the harder subsets of our benchmark and analyze model performance along multiple axes. We also find that training on Lift-Train is much less susceptible to reward hacking than training on the common IFTrain. Lift-Bench and Lift-Train are publicly available, as well as the data generating pipeline.
中文翻译
常见的指令遵循（IF）基准测试，如IFEval，评估语言模型（LMs）遵循简单可程序化验证的约束的能力。这些约束同样被用作强化学习中的奖励信号。这种设计可能导致约束的奖励劫持，使模型偏离主要任务。近期尝试通过引入更丰富的约束来解决此问题，但需要专家劳动，仍为封闭访问，且难以扩展。我们提出了Lift-Bench，一个要求模型根据主题和结构化大纲起草文档的IF基准。约束被限定在特定文档部分，提供对约束密度的更精细控制。Lift-Bench包含可验证和需人工判断的约束，以及对应的训练版本Lift-Train。重要的是，Lift-Bench同时包含主任务约束和指令遵循约束，从而缓解浅层奖励劫持问题。在多个模型的实验中，我们发现前沿模型在我们基准的较难子集上表现不佳，并从多个维度分析了模型性能。我们还发现，使用Lift-Train进行训练比使用常见IFTrain更不易受到奖励劫持的影响。Lift-Bench和Lift-Train均已公开，包括数据生成管道。
👤 Eran Goldstein, Yuval Belfer, Yonatan Belinkov, Daniel Gissin 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28787]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Is Anyone Enforcing the Cartel? What Reinforcement Learning Teaches LLM Agents in Markets
是否有人在执行卡特尔？强化学习如何教会LLM代理在市场中的行为
Evaluations of large language model (LLM) agents report that pricing agents collude at others' expense. Yet collusion is defined by enforcement: a cartel, in which competitors share monopoly profits, holds only if a member who cheats is punished. We introduce an audit that tests enforcement by forcing a member to cheat in counterfactual copies of a market, and use it to study what reinforcement learning (RL) teaches agents in pricing and trading. Small LLMs trained by RL learn what their training rivals reward, but only what they readily try. In self-play, they learn fixed rules ignoring their rivals; against a punishing rival, to be deterred; against a cheating rival, to match its cuts, never the costly price war that would deter it, which they rarely try. A war forced into training is learned, but mostly only where it was forced. Against their own copies, deterred and matching agents can earn the same cartel profits, but only matching ones can enforce them, and then thinly: as a by-product and only against large cuts. Q-learners enforce collusion only in pricing. Prompted LLMs reach cartel-like outcomes mostly through rules that ignore the market. Outcome measures cannot tell these cases apart; the audit can.
中文翻译
对大型语言模型（LLM）代理的评估报告称，定价代理会以他人利益为代价进行合谋。然而，合谋的定义依赖于执行：只有当背叛者受到惩罚时，卡特尔（即竞争者分享垄断利润）才能成立。我们引入了一种审计机制，通过强制一个成员在反事实的市场副本中背叛，来测试执行情况，并利用它研究强化学习（RL）如何影响LLM代理在定价和交易中的行为。由RL训练的小型LLM学会了其训练对手所奖励的内容，但仅限于它们容易尝试的行为。在自对弈中，它们学会固定规则而忽略对手；面对惩罚性对手时，会受到威慑；面对背叛对手时，会匹配其降价行为，但从不采取代价高昂的价格战来威慑对方——因为它们很少尝试这种行为。只有在训练中被迫经历价格战时，才会学习相关策略，且主要局限于被强迫的情境。面对自身副本时，受到威慑和匹配的代理可以获得相同的卡特尔利润，但只有匹配的代理能执行这些利润，且执行力度很弱：作为副产品，且仅针对大幅降价。Q-学习者仅在定价任务中能执行合谋。提示型LLM大多通过忽略市场的规则达成类似卡特尔的结果。结果度量无法区分这些情况；而审计机制可以。

SkillEval: Learning Interpretable Ability Profiles of LLMs via Cognitive Diagnosis Models
SkillEval：通过认知诊断模型学习大语言模型的可解释能力画像
Current evaluation of LLMs on benchmarks typically aggregates performance over individual examples into a single scalar score. This approach is overly coarse and ignores the diverse scope of skills covered by benchmark datasets. To address this limitation and better exploit benchmark data, we propose SkillEval, a new evaluation framework that performs cross-model, skill-level analysis. The framework integrates advances from two areas: (1) it leverages the meta-cognitive capabilities of LLMs to automatically discover interpretable skills from questions, and (2) it applies cognitive diagnosis models (CDMs) from psychometrics to estimate per-skill abilities of LLMs. We conduct experiments on 3,811 LLMs evaluated on 9,523 items across five benchmarks spanning diverse domains (MATH, BBH, GPQA, MuSR, IFEval). Compared to the traditional average-based evaluation paradigm, SkillEval produces reliable, more calibrated and informative yet concise model profiles that more accurately predict model behavior on unseen items. We also demonstrate the utility of SkillEval in two downstream tasks: (1) LLM routing, which dynamically assigns each query to a capable but efficient model, reducing inference cost by over 60% while achieving improved performance, and (2) Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT), where an unseen model can be efficiently evaluated using a trained CDM. Furthermore, we show that these skill profiles provide useful insights, for example, how benchmarks share skills and how instruction-tuned models differ from their base models.
中文翻译
当前对大语言模型（LLMs）在基准测试上的评估通常将单个样本的表现汇总为单一标量分数，这种方法过于粗略，忽略了基准数据集涵盖的多样化技能范围。为解决这一局限并更充分地利用基准数据，我们提出了SkillEval——一种新的评估框架，实现跨模型、技能级别的分析。该框架结合了两个领域的进展：(1) 利用大语言模型的元认知能力自动从问题中发现可解释的技能；(2) 应用心理测量学中的认知诊断模型（CDMs）来估算大语言模型在各项技能上的能力水平。我们在5个涵盖不同领域（MATH、BBH、GPQA、MuSR、IFEval）的基准上，对3,811个大语言模型在9,523个题目上的表现进行了实验。相比传统的平均值评估范式，SkillEval生成了更可靠、更校准、更具信息量且简洁的模型画像，能更准确预测模型在未见题项上的行为。我们还展示了SkillEval在两项下游任务中的实用性：(1) 大语言模型路由，即动态将每个查询分配给能力强但高效的模型，使推理成本降低超过60%的同时性能提升；(2) 计算机化自适应测试（CAT），可通过训练好的CDM高效评估未知模型。此外，我们还发现这些技能画像提供了有价值的洞见，例如基准之间共享哪些技能，以及指令微调模型与其基础模型之间的差异。

VX-One: Typed, Calibrated Decision Heads for Real-Time Perimeter Triage - An empirical study of unknown-attack honesty, cascade gray zones, and calibration under drift
VX-One：面向实时边界筛查的类型化、校准决策头——未知攻击诚实性、级联灰区与漂移下的校准性实证研究
Perimeter triage faces a latency/semantics trade-off: signature engines are fast and format-safe but blind to unseen attacks, while generative models are semantic but slow and produce unparseable output. In late 2026 a class of commercial System One decision models (TypeSafe Jev; Microsoft-Decision-1) began selling typed decision primitives - binary gates, closed-set choices, ordinal scores - as product APIs, with no academic treatment. We present VX-One: a small multi-head network (100K-10M parameters) whose outputs are these primitives - a calibrated gate, a choice over a closed candidate set that always includes an explicit unknown option, and an ordinal score, trained multi-task, temperature-calibrated per head (~0.7-1.7 ms p50 on CPU). We evaluate it on UNSW-NB15, CIC-IDS2017, EMBER2024 against calibrated XGBoost, Random-Forest, MLP, Kitsune, and measure a two-phase cascade (Suricata with 52,674 Emerging-Threats rules -> VX-One) over five days of CIC-IDS2017. Findings: (i) VX-One wins detection (AUROC/FPR@95%TPR) on UNSW-NB15 and under temporal drift on CIC-IDS2017 (0.711 vs 0.637), but XGBoost wins multi-class attribution on all three benchmarks; (ii) leave-one-family-out gate detects never-seen families on 9/9 UNSW folds yet drops below chance on CIC recon family, and abstention threshold transfers poorly across benchmarks; (iii) on payload-less reconstructions signature engines resolve ~0.1% of flows - gray zone ~99.9% of traffic, recovered by Phase 2 (combined recall >=0.978); (iv) calibration collapses under temporal drift (ECE 0.04->0.40) for every method while ranking partially survives. We provide a measured account of where typed calibrated decisions help perimeter triage, and where they fail.

边界筛查面临延迟/语义权衡问题：签名引擎速度快且格式安全，但对未知攻击无感知；生成模型具备语义理解能力，却速度慢且输出难以解析。2026年末，一类商用System One决策模型（TypeSafe Jev；Microsoft-Decision-1）开始将二元门、闭集选择、序数评分等类型化决策原语作为产品API销售，尚未有学术研究。我们提出VX-One：一个小型多头网络（参数量10万至1000万），其输出为这些原语——一个校准后的决策门、一个包含显式未知选项的闭集选择、以及一个序数评分，采用多任务训练并按头进行温度校准（CPU上p50延迟约0.7-1.7毫秒）。我们在UNSW-NB15、CIC-IDS2017、EMBER2024数据集上评估了该模型，并与校准后的XGBoost、随机森林、MLP、Kitsune对比。实验结果表明：（i）在UNSW-NB15上VX-One检测性能更优（AUROC/FPR@95%TPR），在CIC-IDS2017的时间漂移下表现也优于XGBoost（0.711 vs 0.637），但XGBoost在三组基准上的多分类归因任务中表现更佳；（ii）留一类别门在UNSW所有折叠中可检测从未见过的攻击家族（9/9），但在CIC重构家族上性能低于随机猜测，且弃权阈值在不同基准间迁移效果差；（iii）在无载荷重构场景中，签名引擎仅能处理约0.1%流量，其余约99.9%处于“灰区”，由第二阶段（Suricata + VX-One）恢复，联合召回率≥0.978；（iv）所有方法在时间漂移下校准性均崩溃（ECE从0.04升至0.40），但排序性能部分保留。本文提供了对类型化校准决策在边界筛查中有效与失效场景的量化分析。

Agent Behavior Maps: Locating Agents by What They Would Do, Without Running Them
智能体行为图：无需运行即可定位智能体的行为特征
LLM agents with similar success rates can follow different action sequences. An Agent Behavior Map represents LLM-harness combinations using centered action log-likelihoods on shared reference trajectories, without requiring new environment rollouts from the target agent. We derive error bounds relating squared coordinate distances to trajectory KL under explicit assumptions, and across eight cross-scored models the distances track the directly measured trajectory KL in rank order. Across 100 LLMs and four environments, ALFWorld-based coordinates support performance prediction and identify behavioral similarities. In ALFWorld, these similarities persist after controlling for success rates and model size, including across model families. As an extension, harness-component displacements approximately compose, with accuracy varying across components and environments.

Commit or Abstain? System One Decision Models, LLMs, and Missing Legal Authority
承诺还是放弃？System One决策模型、LLMs与缺失法律权威问题
Given the enormous number of claims to be checked, verifiable legal AI requires scalable, cost-effective models that answer the practical needs of law. System One decision models are built on large language models but, instead of generating text, return a calibrated probability for each option. This suits the verification of legal authority, where the question is closed: a given holding, or none of them. We test whether such models abstain when the correct authority is missing. We extend CaseHOLD with an abstention condition in which the correct holding is replaced in 1,670 of 5,314 items, and compare four decision models with six generative LLMs. When no option is correct, generative LLMs still pick one in 38-86% of items; the most cautious decision model does so in 30%. On standard CaseHOLD, a decision model matches the best generative LLM (88.1% vs. 88.2%) at 1/55 of the cost. Accepting that LLM's answer only when a decision model agrees halves its wrong citations, and distilling a decision model's probabilities into Qwen3-8B, without labels, yields a well-calibrated local model. Legal AI should be judged by its wrong citations, not by accuracy alone.

面对海量待核查主张，可验证的法律AI需要可扩展、低成本的模型以满足实际法律需求。System One决策模型基于大语言模型，但不生成文本，而是返回每个选项的校准概率。这适用于法律权威验证，因为问题为封闭式：给定判例或无任何判例。我们测试此类模型在正确权威缺失时是否会放弃回答。我们将CaseHOLD扩展为包含弃权条件，其中1670/5314项的正确判例被替换，并比较四种决策模型与六种生成式LLM。当无正确选项时，生成式LLM仍会在38%-86%的项目中做出选择；最谨慎的决策模型也仅在30%的情况下放弃。在标准CaseHOLD上，决策模型以1/55的成本达到了与最佳生成式LLM相当的表现（88.1% vs 88.2%）。只有当决策模型与LLM意见一致时才接受LLM答案，可使错误引用减少一半；将决策模型的概率蒸馏至Qwen3-8B（无需标签），可生成校准良好的本地模型。法律AI应以错误引用数量为评判标准，而不仅仅是准确率。

Your Benchmark Is an Empirical Measure Over Difficulty
你的基准测试是一种难度的实证度量
A benchmark is not merely a collection of test questions; it naturally induces an empirical measure over difficulty. In this work, we develop this measure-based view of benchmark evaluation. This perspective allows us to identify and formalize two central failure modes: measure mismatch, where the benchmark difficulty distribution differs from the intended difficulty distribution, and coverage gaps, where uncovered regions along the difficulty axis prevent benchmarks from detecting meaningful localized training progress. We propose two diagnostic metrics that capture these two failure modes and provide rigorous theoretical guarantees. Empirically, we analyze 25 popular benchmarks spanning 7 domains using carefully estimated item difficulties. Our results show that many benchmarks implicitly overweight certain difficulty regions, so their leaderboard conclusions should be interpreted with caution; others leave substantial portions of the difficulty axis uncovered, limiting their ability to monitor the model improvement process. Finally, we turn diagnosis into action by introducing BenchFill, a simple difficulty-targeted item rewriting algorithm that effectively reduces local coverage gaps.
中文翻译
基准测试不仅仅是测试题的集合；它自然地引出了对难度的实证度量。本文发展了基于度量的基准评估视角，识别并形式化了两种核心失败模式：度量不匹配（基准难度分布与预期分布不同）和覆盖缺口（难度轴上未覆盖区域导致无法检测到有意义的局部训练进展）。我们提出了两个诊断指标来捕捉这两种失败模式，并提供了严格的理论保证。实证上，我们使用精心估算的题目难度分析了涵盖7个领域的25个流行基准。结果表明，许多基准隐性地高估了某些难度区域，因此其排行榜结论应谨慎解读；另一些基准则在难度轴上留下大量未覆盖区域，限制了其监控模型改进过程的能力。最后，我们将诊断转化为行动，引入BenchFill——一种简单的难度目标导向的题目重写算法，有效减少了局部覆盖缺口。

Real PMIDs, Wrong Papers: Harness Design for Biomedical Claim-Verification Agents
真实的PMID，错误的论文：生物医学声明验证智能体的Harness设计
Literature-searching language agents are becoming a standard component of scientific assistants for the life sciences, yet there is little controlled evidence on which parts of their harness matter. We run a controlled ablation on biomedical claim verification (SciFact) with the live PubMed API as the only tool. Holding the backbone and output format fixed, we compare four harnesses: closed-book answering, single-shot retrieval using the claim as the query, a ReAct-style agent that writes its own PubMed queries, and an oracle given the gold abstracts, each with two efficient frontier backbones. Naive single-shot retrieval finds the gold paper for only 6% of claims and turns overconfident errors into abstentions without improving accuracy. Letting the model formulate its own queries triples gold recall and yields the best verdicts (62% accuracy vs. 49-59% for the other realistic harnesses), although these differences are not significant at our sample size and agents use fewer than half of their search budget. With gold abstracts, the same models reach 79-86% accuracy, a significant 17-24 point gap showing that retrieval, not reasoning, is the main bottleneck. Finally, a citation audit shows that closed-book models cite PubMed IDs that almost always exist (327/335) but are almost never relevant: a cross-family LLM judge rates only 3 of 327 existing citations as relevant to the claim, versus 67% for citations produced by the search agent. Existence checks are therefore insufficient safeguards for scientific agents. We release code, prompts, and all cached tool calls at https://github.com/saqlain2204/real-pmids-wrong-papers.
中文翻译
文献检索语言智能体正成为生命科学领域科学助手的标准组件，但关于其Harness中哪些部分起作用的受控证据仍很少。我们在生物医学声明验证（SciFact）任务上进行了受控消融实验，仅使用实时PubMed API作为工具。保持主干网络和输出格式固定，比较四种Harness：闭卷回答、以声明为查询的单次检索、采用ReAct风格自动生成PubMed查询的智能体，以及给定黄金摘要的“全知”代理，每种搭配两种高效前沿主干网络。朴素的单次检索仅能为6%的声明找到黄金论文，且将过度自信的错误转为回避，但并未提升准确率。允许模型自主生成查询使黄金召回率提升三倍，并取得最佳判断结果（62%准确率，高于其他现实Harness的49-59%），尽管在当前样本量下差异不显著，且智能体使用的搜索预算不足一半。当提供黄金摘要时，相同模型达到79-86%准确率，相比前者的17-24个百分点差距显著，表明检索而非推理是主要瓶颈。最后的引用审计显示，闭卷模型引用的PMID几乎总是存在（327/335），但几乎从不相关：跨家族LLM裁判仅认为3个存在的引用与声明相关，而搜索代理生成的引用有67%相关。因此，存在性检查不足以保障科学智能体的安全性。代码、提示和所有缓存工具调用已发布于https://github.com/saqlain2204/real-pmids-wrong-papers。

Conflicted Records: Typed Adversarial Contracts for Auditable LLM Output Quality Enforcement
冲突记录：用于可审计LLM输出质量强制的类型化对抗合约
LLM-generated records increasingly populate production information systems where they are treated as data assets subject to the same quality expectations as records from operational databases. A single such record can simultaneously satisfy and violate a quality rule: a grounded fact adjacent to a hallucination, a useful answer adjacent to a stereotype. Classical data cleaning cannot resolve this because the two judgments are incompatible over the same span; a single LLM judge cannot either, since it shares the training biases that produced the conflict. Existing interventions such as prompt engineering, chain-of-thought, and RLHF reduce surface error rates but provide no audit trail and cannot distinguish genuine remediation from blanket suppression. The question is whether a deterministic check over structured inter-agent records can catch the defects that referential checks alone miss. We present BiasGuard-D: two adversarial roles drawn from distinct model families exchange typed claim documents, a three-model quorum adjudicates by majority vote, and a combined referential-plus-semantic invariant validates the verdict. The semantic component fires on verdicts referential checks miss; removing the iterative loop drops invariant satisfaction sharply. The deliberation layer transfers from stereotype to hallucination remediation on HaluEval QA with only role-framing changes, raising right-answer recovery by 49 percentage points; the triage gate required a cross-family second auditor to maintain recall on objective hallucination. Every claim, vote, and instruction is stored as a typed record giving operators a provenance trail auditable under emerging AI regulations.
中文翻译
LLM生成的记录越来越多地进入生产信息系统，被视为与操作数据库记录同等质量要求的数据资产。一条记录可能同时满足和违反质量规则：一个基于事实的部分紧邻着一个幻觉，一个有用的回答紧邻着一个刻板印象。传统数据清洗无法解决此问题，因为两种判断在同一跨度上不兼容；单一LLM裁判也无法解决，因为它共享生成冲突的训练偏见。现有干预手段如提示工程、思维链和RLHF虽降低表面错误率，但无审计轨迹，无法区分真正的修复与全面压制。问题是：能否通过结构化跨智能体记录的确定性检查，捕获仅靠参照检查遗漏的缺陷？我们提出BiasGuard-D：来自不同模型家族的两个对抗角色交换类型化的声明文档，三模型共识组通过多数投票裁决，结合参照+语义不变性共同验证判决。语义组件在参照检查遗漏的判决上触发；移除迭代循环会导致不变性满足度急剧下降。决策层仅通过角色框架变化即可实现从刻板印象到幻觉修复的转移，在HaluEval QA上使正确答案恢复率提升49个百分点；分诊门限需要跨家族第二名审计员以维持客观幻觉的召回率。每个声明、投票和指令均以类型化记录形式存储，为运营者提供符合新兴AI监管要求的可审计溯源路径。
👤 Kardelen Subasi, Jarosław A. Chudziak 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

OACap: Caption-Guided Active Observation for Detailed Video Captioning
OACap：基于字幕引导的主动观察用于详细视频字幕生成
Unlike question answering, where a query specifies a perceptual target, open-ended detailed captioning must determine what evidence to acquire and how to integrate it into a coherent global description. We propose Observation-Adaptive Video Captioner (OACap), a caption-centered agent that turns unsupported or missing information into targeted observation requests, uses spatiotemporal tools to acquire evidence, and iteratively verifies and revises the caption. OACap captions consistently improve performance across general video-understanding benchmarks using a fixed text-only reader. To better diagnose the information they preserve, we introduce Factual-Semantic Captioning Bench (FSCap-Bench). Its Core-Support Factuality Score (CSFS) combines claim precision with importance-aware fact coverage and responds as expected to four controlled perturbation types on 93.0-98.3% of valid caption pairs. Its Cross-Modal Semantic Round-Trip Score (CSRS) measures semantic preservation through a video-caption-video-caption round trip by matching atomic facts from source and recovered captions. Together, they connect downstream textual-memory utility with factual and semantic preservation.

LLM-Based Exogenous World Modeling for Dynamic Scheduling
基于大语言模型的外生世界建模用于动态调度
Dynamic scheduling requires dispatching online arrivals while anticipating future jobs, as poor decisions can compound downstream costs under nonlinear tardiness objectives. We show that a pretrained large language model (LLM) can serve as the exogenous component of a structured world model, forecasting uncertain future job arrivals. Specifically, we exploit an afterstate decomposition, which separates known deterministic shop transitions from uncertain exogenous job arrivals, enabling the LLM to forecast only the latter through a compact belief representation. Building on this, we develop Afterstate World-Model Planning (AWMP), which combines an evolved, belief-conditioned dispatching strategy with short scenario-based rollouts. A surprise-triggered refresh mechanism queries the LLM only when newly observed arrivals deviate sufficiently from previous forecast, and online residual self-calibration further conditions the LLM on its recent forecast errors to handle nonstationary arrivals. Across diverse arrival distributions, AWMP reduces weighted tardiness by 6-21% on dynamic job-shop and 22-46% on flexible job-shop benchmarks, relative to the best of eight classical priority-dispatching rules.
中文翻译
动态调度需要在处理在线到达任务的同时预测未来任务，因为糟糕的决策可能在非线性延迟目标下累积下游成本。我们表明，预训练的大语言模型（LLM）可作为结构化世界模型的外生组件，用于预测不确定的未来任务到达。具体而言，我们采用事后状态分解方法，将已知的确定性车间状态转移与不确定的外生任务到达分离，使LLM仅需通过紧凑的信念表示预测后者。在此基础上，我们开发了事后状态世界模型规划（AWMP），结合演化后的信念条件调度策略与短时情景滚动模拟。当新观测到的任务到达显著偏离先前预测时，会触发意外刷新机制，仅此时调用LLM；在线残差自校准进一步根据LLM近期预测误差对其条件化，以应对非平稳到达。在多种到达分布下，AWMP相较于八种经典优先级调度规则中的最优者，分别在动态作业车间和柔性作业车间基准上降低了6%-21%和22%-46%的加权延迟。
