Same Probabilities, Different Advice: Gain-Loss Framing Effects in High-Stakes LLM Decision Support
相同概率，不同建议：高风险LLM决策支持中的得失框架效应
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support consequential decisions, yet their reliability under equivalent representations of uncertainty remains unclear. Existing work has documented prompt sensitivity, cognitive biases, and confidence miscalibration, but it is still unknown whether high-stakes advice changes when the same probability is expressed through complementary gain and loss frames. We introduce FrameBench-HPS, a controlled benchmark of 2,880 prompts derived from 240 high-stakes scenarios across healthcare, finance, education/career, and legal/consumer-rights domains. Across seven LLMs, gain-loss framing changes the recommendation category in 32.4% of complete groups, with most changes involving uncertainty rather than direct reversals. Human evaluation confirms corresponding shifts in perceived risk and directiveness. Robustness experiments show that framing effects persist under conversational paraphrases and exceed ordinary repeated-generation variability. Probability canonicalization substantially reduces framing sensitivity, identifying probability representation as a practical target for improving consistency in high-stakes LLM decision support.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地用于支持重大决策，但其在不确定性等价表达下的可靠性仍不明确。现有研究记录了提示敏感性、认知偏差和置信度误标等问题，但尚不清楚当同一概率通过互补的收益和损失框架表达时，高风险建议是否会改变。我们引入FrameBench-HPS，一个由240个高风险场景衍生出的2,880个提示组成的受控基准，涵盖医疗、金融、教育/职业、法律/消费者权益领域。在七个LLMs中，得失框架导致32.4%的完整组别推荐类别发生变化，多数变化涉及不确定性而非直接反转。人类评估确认了感知风险和指导性的相应转变。稳健性实验显示，框架效应在对话改写下依然存在，且超过普通重复生成的变异性。概率规范化显著降低了框架敏感性，识别出概率表示是提升高风险LLM决策支持一致性的一个实际优化目标。

Computer Vision and Knowledge-Grounded Language Models for Intelligent Beekeeper Assistance
计算机视觉与知识增强型语言模型在智能养蜂人辅助中的应用
Managed and wild bees sustain roughly a third of the global food products, yet colonies face mounting stress from climate-change including extreme weather patterns, altered parasite and disease patterns. Timely assessment of colony condition such as understanding colony strength, brood area, honey and pollen storage, comb usage, is essential to adaptive management. However, manual hive frame inspection is slow, costly, and quality depends on scarce expertise. We present an artificial targeted intelligent system framework focusing on edge deployable pipeline that turns hive frame photographs into interpretable and action-oriented inspection report. The pipeline includes an object detector to detect biologically-meaningful comb-cell classes; detections are distilled into structured fact schema; facts are converted into constrained factual text; the text triggers a grounded knowledge-base for interpretations and recommendations. Knowledge-base entries are required to be triggerable by evidence expressed in the fact schema. We built our system based on open source FAIRHiveFrames-1K dataset and favour reproducible, edge-deployable models over large general purpose frontier models. We additionally release FAIRHiveText-1K dataset that turns FAIRHiveFrames-1K dataset into interchangeable facts and text interpretation of the facts, making it easy for further LLM-based research into this area. We also propose a pathway to develop the grounded knowledge-base that will be helpful in providing interpretable and actionable reports from the facts.

Preference Is Not Permission: Personalizing the Auto-Purchase Line in Agentic Commerce
偏好不等于授权：在智能体电商中个性化自动购买边界
Personalized shopping benchmarks ask which item fits the user; we ask how far an agent may commit on that item, and pose learning the individual's auto-purchase line as a task. The boundary is evidence-conditioned and three-valued: whether committing is appropriate depends on the sufficiency of product information (warrant), reversibility, price against the user's norm, and urgency, not only on fit; and the natural output is don't buy, confirm first, or buy automatically. Across sixteen instruction-tuned models, autonomous commitment is misaligned with a human authorization reference in which fifteen annotators, role-playing the delegating principal from a given profile, choose the furthest action they would allow without confirmation: models commit even when fit is low and when their own evidence flag reports the warrant missing. This is overreach. The line is individual: these raters disagree, yet the disagreement is structured and partly predictable from a person's own other labels. A shallow learner on a few dozen of a person's own labels predicts their held-out auto-purchase line at a median of 0.79 by 30-40 labels, outperforming every evaluated prompt$\times$model configuration, including a per-annotator oracle over 80 configurations; the learned boundary does not transfer across domains. We release the counterfactual benchmark, human authorization reference, and annotation protocol after acceptance.

个性化购物基准测试关注哪个商品适合用户；我们则关注智能体在该商品上可自主承诺的程度，并将学习个体的自动购买边界作为一项任务。该边界是基于证据的三值判断：是否应进行承诺取决于产品信息充分性（担保）、可逆性、价格与用户标准的对比以及紧迫性，而不仅仅是商品匹配度；自然输出应为‘不买’、‘先确认’或‘自动购买’。在十六个指令微调模型中，自主承诺行为与人类授权参考存在偏差，其中十五名标注者以特定身份角色扮演委托人，选择他们无需确认即可允许的最远行动：模型即使在匹配度低且自身证据标记显示担保缺失时仍会承诺。这属于过度承诺。该边界具有个体差异性：这些标注者意见不一，但分歧具有结构且部分可由个人其他标签预测。仅用几十个个人标签的浅层学习模型即可在30-40个标签下以中位数0.79的准确率预测其未见的自动购买边界，优于所有评估的提示×模型配置，包括每标注者80种配置的最优oracle；但该学习边界无法跨领域迁移。我们将在论文接受后发布反事实基准、人类授权参考及标注协议。

VeeReview: Measuring Build-Verify Pairs in Agent-Authored Pull Requests from a V-Model Perspective
VeeReview：从V模型视角测量智能体撰写的拉取请求中的构建-验证配对
Context: Reviewing a pull request requires verification evidence that addresses the proposed change. Coding agent benchmarks and studies of pull request outcomes do not directly assess this relationship. Objective: We test whether the V-Model's build and verify pairs can measure verification evidence in pull requests and support comparisons between agent-authored and human-authored changes. Method: VeeReview classifies four build and verify pairs using repository signals and a language-model judge. Three pairs use model judgements constrained by deterministic signals, and a rule over the diff and repository tree decides Module Design-Unit Test. We evaluated VeeReview against a 25-pull request reference set labelled by two raters and applied it to 1,454 matched pairs of agent and human pull requests from 741 repositories. Results: VeeReview assigned lower build-only rates to agent-authored pull requests on all four pairs, contrary to the preregistered hypothesis (mixed-effects odds ratios 0.52 to 0.86). Architecture-Integration was not significant. Standardization for linked issues, shipped tests and size reduced matched-rate differences by 34% to 60%, and the rule-decided pair's standardized interval included zero. The instrument did not meet its preregistered calibration criterion: agreement with adjudicated labels was 4% to 60% against a required 80%, and inter-rater agreement was weak (kappa = 0.154). Conclusions: The rule-decided pair shows that a process measure judged by a language model partly reflects how a pull request is presented rather than the verification work behind it. We report the cohort differences as observations made with this instrument, and we argue that such measures need a mechanically decided component and a validated human reference before they can support comparisons of verification practice.

背景：审查拉取请求需要能说明所提变更的验证证据。现有的代码智能体基准测试和拉取请求结果研究并未直接评估这一关系。目标：我们检验V模型中的构建与验证配对是否可用于衡量拉取请求中的验证证据，并支持智能体撰写与人工撰写变更之间的比较。方法：VeeReview使用仓库信号和语言模型裁判对四类构建-验证配对进行分类。其中三类配对使用受确定性信号约束的语言模型判断，而第四个配对（模块设计-单元测试）由diff和仓库树结构的规则决定。我们在由两名评审员标注的25个拉取请求参考集上评估VeeReview，并将其应用于来自741个仓库的1,454对匹配的智能体与人工拉取请求。结果：VeeReview在所有四类配对中均对智能体撰写的拉取请求分配了更低的仅构建率，与预先注册的假设相反（混合效应优势比为0.52至0.86）。架构-集成配对不显著。对关联问题、已发布测试和大小进行标准化后，匹配率差异减少了34%至60%，且规则决定配对的标准化区间包含零。该工具未达到预先注册的校准标准：与裁定标签的一致性仅为4%至60%（要求80%），且评审者间一致性较弱（kappa = 0.154）。结论：规则决定的配对表明，由语言模型判断的过程指标部分反映的是拉取请求的呈现方式，而非其背后的验证工作。我们报告了使用该工具观察到的群体差异，并认为此类度量需包含机械决定组件和经验证的人类参考，才能支持对验证实践的比较。

VeriEvolve: Evaluator-First Evolution for Tool-Using Agents
VeriEvolve：面向工具使用智能体的评估者优先进化
A central obstacle to self-improving agents is the absence of a reliable fitness signal for realistic stateful tasks. We introduce \textsc{VeriEvolve}, an evaluator-first evolution framework that constructs this missing evaluation layer before optimizing the agent. From task instruction, VeriEvolve synthesizes instruction-grounded challenge states and an executable evaluator that checks them. During each round of workflow evolution, the evaluator is frozen and serves as the fitness signal. Between rounds, VeriEvolve revises the evaluator by converting the end states of strong and weak runs into a pair of counterfactual test cases. Across all 30 MCPMark Filesystem tasks, VeriEvolve constructs 30 evaluators with 141 challenge states, achieving 141/141 agreement on the generated suites and 113/122 agreement on held-out states produced by executor agents. Evaluator-guided evolution increases externally verified success from 6/30 to 18/30 for GLM-5.2 and from 27/30 to 30/30 for Claude; refinement removes all three false positives that the weak executor exposes. Cross-environment studies on PostgreSQL, Playwright, Notion, and GitHub further expose complementary limits from observability, state representation, and search/execution capacity. These results show that executable evaluators can be synthesized and used to guide search when task success is observable and an external verifier is available for calibration.

Where Should a Stuck Agent Spend Its Next Tokens? Causal Attribution of Inference-Time Resources
卡住的智能体下一步应如何分配其下一个token？推理时资源的因果归因
Inference-time scaling is often treated as a token-budget problem, but a failed coding agent can seek qualitatively different resources: computation, information, or feedback. We introduce Banzhaf Resource Attribution (BRA), a controlled factorial framework that measures each resource's marginal contribution to repair and its interactions while holding the failed state and revision protocol fixed. We evaluate six models on 194 function-level failures from MBPP+ and HumanEval+ and 126 repository-level failures from SWE-bench Verified. Task-relevant specifications consistently improve repair, whereas public-test feedback provides small gains and retrieved code little to none. The value of additional computation declines with model capability, while information remains useful; resource channels largely exhibit diminishing returns, although complementary information can reinforce itself. Yet three of four frontier models select feedback in 95-100% of cases and obtain lower gains than uniform random resource selection. BRA thus exposes a gap between the resources agents prefer and those that measurably improve repair.

Measure First, Then Allocate: Calibrated Rollout Budgets for MCP Agents in Reinforcement Learning
先度量，再分配：面向MCP智能体在强化学习中的校准滚动预算
Training tool-using language models requires costly interaction, yet identical rewards within a rollout group provide no group-relative learning signal. We use reward disagreement to guide harness evaluation and the allocation of rollouts for policy training in Model Context Protocol (MCP) environments. We first introduce Discord, which tests harness edits using exact paired inference and decision-preserving early stopping: only disagreeing paired outcomes favor one harness over another. This insight motivates prospective allocation in BRACE (Bank-calibrated Rollout Allocation under Correlated Episodes). BRACE combines historical task evidence, uncertainty in task success rates, and a correlated reward model to forecast mixed-reward groups before allocating generation budget. Controlled harness experiments favor Discord, while live MCP experiments expose a retention-cost trade-off. At three Qwen3-VL model scales, BRACE exceeds the strongest plotted baseline peaks with fewer new training rollouts; historical-bank collection is counted separately. It also leads the compared methods on BFCL and NESTFUL without further training. Ablations probe the bank, estimator, and allocation score; prospective evaluation checks forecast calibration. These results support forecasting reward contrast before generation.
中文翻译
训练使用工具的语言模型需要大量交互，但同一组滚动内的相同奖励无法提供组间相对学习信号。我们利用奖励分歧来指导Harnes评估以及在Model Context Protocol（MCP）环境中策略训练的滚动分配。我们首先引入Discord，该方法通过精确配对推理和保持决策的早期停止来测试Harnes修改：只有在配对结果不一致时，才认为一个Harnes优于另一个。这一洞见促使BRACE（Bank-calibrated Rollout Allocation under Correlated Episodes）采用前瞻性分配策略。BRACE结合历史任务证据、任务成功率的不确定性以及相关奖励模型，提前预测混合奖励组，再分配生成预算。受控Harnes实验支持Discord，而真实MCP实验揭示了保留成本与收益之间的权衡。在三个Qwen3-VL模型规模下，BRACE在更少的新训练滚动次数内超过了最强基准峰值；历史银行数据收集单独计数。它还在BFCL和NESTFUL上领先于对比方法，且无需进一步训练。消融实验探究了银行、估计器和分配评分；前瞻性评估检查了预测校准性。这些结果支持在生成前预测奖励差异。

Automating ML for Science: Can Frontier Agents Climb Scientific Hills in the Wild?
自动化机器学习用于科学：前沿智能体能否在真实环境中攀登科学高峰？
Frontier coding agents are increasingly framed as end-to-end automated researchers, and ML for science offers a demanding test of that promise: improving a scientific model often requires domain insight beyond routine ML engineering. We introduce Agent4Sci, a long-horizon testbed for measuring how far today's agents can improve the published best models on scientific ML benchmarks, spanning 20 benchmarks across 11 domains. Rather than ranking agents on a leaderboard, Agent4Sci asks what kinds of progress agents can make on these tasks under in-the-wild conditions, and where they fall short. Empirically, today's frontier agents advance the published best on every benchmark, with Claude Opus reaching 10.4% mean relative improvement and no run flagged for academic misconduct. These gains, however, come almost entirely from a bag of ML-engineering tricks rather than research-level insight. Trajectory analysis surfaces three recurring limitations in this setting: novelty miscalibration, resource under-utilization, and rapid collapse into local engineering sweeps. Lightweight plug-in skills for research ideation, experiment management, and scheduled checklists raise Claude Opus from 10.4% to 20.1% and Codex from 4.4% to 8.1%, while yielding more diverse, domain-relevant interventions. Agent4Sci gives the community a way to track and push frontier agents from ML engineering toward domain insight on real-world ML-for-science problems. Code and data are available on GitHub (https://github.com/maszhongming/agent4sci) and Hugging Face (https://huggingface.co/datasets/MingZhong/agent4sci).
中文翻译
前沿编码智能体被日益视为端到端的自动化研究者，而机器学习用于科学则对这一承诺提出了严峻考验：改进科学模型通常需要超越常规机器学习工程的领域洞察。我们提出Agent4Sci，一个长周期测试平台，用于衡量当前智能体在20个跨11个领域的科学机器学习基准上，能将已发表的最佳模型推进多远。与排行榜排名不同，Agent4Sci关注智能体在真实环境条件下能实现何种进展，以及其短板所在。实证结果显示，当前前沿智能体在所有基准上均实现了改进，Claude Opus平均相对提升达10.4%，且无任何运行被标记为学术不端。然而，这些提升几乎完全来自一系列机器学习工程技巧，而非研究级的洞察。轨迹分析揭示了该场景下的三个反复出现的局限：新颖性校准偏差、资源利用不足，以及迅速陷入局部工程优化循环。引入轻量级插件技能（如研究构思、实验管理、定时清单）后，Claude Opus的性能从10.4%提升至20.1%，Codex从4.4%提升至8.1%，同时产生更多样化、更符合领域背景的干预措施。Agent4Sci为社区提供了一种追踪并推动智能体从机器学习工程向真实世界机器学习科学问题中的领域洞察演进的方法。代码和数据可在GitHub（https://github.com/maszhongming/agent4sci）和Hugging Face（https://huggingface.co/datasets/MingZhong/agent4sci）获取。
👤 Ming Zhong, Stacy Li, Nicholas Carlini, Matthew Jagielski 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

What the Author Wrote, What the Agent Reads: Silent Cue Loss in Enterprise Document Ingestion
作者写了什么，代理读到了什么：企业文档摄入中的沉默线索损失
Enterprise agents act on documents. Before an agent can follow a policy, answer a compliance question or file a ticket, some ingestion layer must turn a Word file or PDF into tokens - and that layer is where an agent deployed in the wild silently loses information. We show that one layer is lost by every mainstream converter - the meaning an author encodes in how text looks - and that the loss makes agents confidently wrong rather than visibly incomplete. We build a task from 76 real policy documents: recover the requirements the author singled out, scored against the file's own OOXML properties by a deterministic matcher with no LLM judge. Formatting-blind ingestion collapses from micro F1 0.690 to 0.150 (docling) and 0.075 (plain text), a gap that replicates across six answering models from two vendors - five Claude models spanning the Opus, Sonnet and Haiku tiers, and OpenAI's GPT-5.6. An agent that cannot see the cue guesses, and docling's false-positive rate on requirements the author did not flag is four times ours. A span-level leave-one-out localises the loss to two cues - highlight (+0.302) and colour (+0.262), both Holm-adjusted p < 1e-4 - while bold, italic, monospace and strikethrough each sit within +/-0.003 of zero. The same asymmetry appears in PDF. Colour is also the cue no mainstream converter preserves and no parsing benchmark scores, and it is common: 32.8% of 6,460 documents carry a deliberate colour or highlight, 10.3% on an obligation. A pre-registered test shows that typing a cue for the agent adds +0.085 beyond merely preserving it (Holm-adjusted p = 0.049). We validate the labels against rendered page images through an independent modality (kappa = 0.72) and bound what wording alone can achieve (AUC 0.664). For agent builders the practical finding is that ingestion, not reasoning, is where this reliability is won or lost, and that the fix is cheap and measurable.
中文翻译
企业智能体依赖于文档执行任务。在智能体能够遵循策略、回答合规问题或提交工单之前，必须有一个摄入层将Word文件或PDF转换为标记——而正是这个层导致部署在真实环境中的智能体无声地丢失信息。我们发现，主流转换工具每一步都会丢失一个关键信息层：作者通过文本呈现方式编码的意义。这种信息丢失使智能体产生自信的错误而非明显的不完整。我们基于76份真实政策文档构建了一个任务：恢复作者特别标注的要求，并通过确定性匹配器根据文件自身的OOXML属性进行评分，无需大语言模型判断。忽略格式的摄入方式使微F1值从0.690降至0.150（docling）和0.075（纯文本），这一差距在来自两家供应商的六种问答模型中均复现——包括五个覆盖Opus、Sonnet和Haiku层级的Claude模型以及OpenAI的GPT-5.6。无法感知线索的智能体会猜测，而docling对作者未标记要求的误报率是我们的四倍。通过跨度级留一法分析，我们定位到两个关键线索——高亮（+0.302）和颜色（+0.262），两者均经Holm校正后p < 1e-4；而粗体、斜体、等宽和删除线的影响均在±0.003范围内。同样的不对称性也出现在PDF中。颜色是主流转换工具均不保留且无解析基准评估的线索，且使用频率较高：在6,460份文档中，有32.8%包含有意的颜色或高亮，其中10.3%涉及义务内容。一项预注册测试显示，为智能体输入线索可额外带来+0.085的提升（Holm校正后p = 0.049）。我们通过独立模态（渲染页面图像）验证标签一致性（kappa = 0.72），并界定了仅靠文字所能达到的上限（AUC 0.664）。对智能体开发者而言，实际启示在于：可靠性之争不在推理，而在摄入环节，而解决方案既廉价又可测量。
👤 Yikai Zhao, Saurabh Pandey, Pradeep Kumar Misra 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Remembering Through Change: Provenance-Preserving Hypergraph Memory for Multimodal Agents
通过变化记住：用于多模态智能体的溯源保持超图记忆
Long-term memory for multimodal agents remains challenging, as it must preserve fine-grained visual evidence while tracking entity evolution across sessions. Existing abstraction-based memories compress images into text, often discarding visually discriminative details, whereas pixel-preserving memories retain raw observations but struggle to reliably associate diverse observations with persistent entity identities and track their evolving states. To address these limitations, we propose MHG-Mem, a multimodal hypergraph memory that jointly organizes entity identity, state evolution, and provenance to visual evidence. Specifically, MHG-Mem represents distinct entity mentions and their observed facts as individual nodes, grouping identity-consistent mentions and facts through occurrence hyperedges. It then synthesizes aspect-specific evolution states from these grouped observations and links them to supporting entities, facts, and source evidence through evolutionary and evidential hyperedges. To navigate this structured memory, we introduce hypergraph-based agentic search, which adaptively selects an entry layer, traverses typed hyperedges across memory levels, and inspects source observations when fine-grained verification is required. These designs support both detailed visual verification and reasoning over cross-session state changes. Extensive experiments across three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of MHG-Mem, improving performance by 8.20-9.98% over the strongest baseline.
中文翻译
多模态智能体的长期记忆仍然面临挑战，因为它必须在跨会话中保留细粒度视觉证据的同时追踪实体的演化。现有的基于抽象的记忆将图像压缩为文本，常丢弃具有视觉区分性的细节；而像素保持型记忆虽保留原始观测，却难以可靠地将多样化的观测与持久的实体身份关联，并跟踪其状态演变。为解决这些局限，我们提出了MHG-Mem，一种多模态超图记忆结构，它联合组织实体身份、状态演化和视觉证据的溯源信息。具体而言，MHG-Mem将不同的实体提及及其观察事实表示为独立节点，通过出现超边将身份一致的提及和事实分组。然后，从这些分组的观测中合成特定方面的演化状态，并通过演化超边和证据超边将其与支持的实体、事实和源证据相连接。为导航此结构化记忆，我们引入基于超图的智能体搜索机制，该机制自适应选择入口层，跨记忆层级遍历类型化超边，并在需要细粒度验证时检查源观测。这些设计支持详细的视觉验证以及跨会话状态变化的推理。在三个基准上的大量实验表明，MHG-Mem相比最强基线性能提升8.20%-9.98%。
👤 Jielong Tang, Ling-An Zeng, Xujie Yuan, Xiao Dong, Zhiqian Xia et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

CulturalRiddles: A Multicultural Benchmark of Riddles
CulturalRiddles：一个多元文化谜题基准
As LLMs expand their language coverage, evaluating how well they understand and represent diverse cultures becomes increasingly important. However, many benchmarks remain English-centric, with limited cultural coverage and a focus on factual knowledge over its application in real-world cultural contexts. We propose CulturalRiddles, a benchmark of original, culturally grounded riddles created entirely by humans through a participatory community effort, spanning 62 cultures (45 countries, 51 languages). These riddles are deeply rooted in their respective cultures and require abductive and compositional reasoning, alongside detailed factual and social knowledge, language skills, and an understanding of culturally specific associations. We benchmark 28 LLM configurations using both automatic and large-scale human evaluation. Even frontier models achieve at most 22% accuracy in some communities. We publicly release all artifacts alongside an open contribution process to expand the benchmark's linguistic and cultural coverage.
中文翻译
随着大语言模型扩展其语言覆盖范围，评估它们理解并表征多样化文化的能力变得日益重要。然而，许多现有基准仍以英语为中心，文化覆盖有限，且侧重于事实知识而非其在真实文化语境中的应用。我们提出CulturalRiddles，一个由人类通过参与式社区协作完全创作的原创、文化根基深厚的谜题集合，涵盖62个文化（45个国家，51种语言）。这些谜题深深植根于各自文化背景，要求具备溯因推理和组合推理能力，同时需要详尽的事实与社会知识、语言技能以及对文化特异性关联的理解。我们使用自动评估和大规模人工评估对28种LLM配置进行了基准测试。即使前沿模型在某些文化群体中的准确率也仅达22%左右。我们公开发布所有数据集及开放贡献流程，以持续扩展该基准的语言和文化覆盖面。
👤 My Chiffon Nguyen, Tianyi Hu, Vinod Anbalagan, Henry Gagnier, Pouya Sadeghi et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29933]

Graph Topology Meets Energy-Driven Pathfinding: Preserving Contextual Consistency in Retrieval-Augmented Generation
图拓扑遇上能量驱动路径规划：在检索增强生成中保持上下文一致性
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) frequently integrates knowledge graphs (KGs) for complex multi-hop question answering. However, their performance is increasingly bottlenecked by the knowledge structuring and reasoning processes. (i) Severe semantic loss: Distilling continuous texts into discrete symbols strips away contextual nuances and narrative coherence. (ii) Pronounced modality mismatch: Fragmented graph structures clash with the continuous texts LLMs are pre-trained on, while rewriting them into natural language introduces generative noise and omits information. To address these limitations, we propose ThreadRAG, a semantic-topology-based RAG framework for context-preserving knowledge structuring and modality-native evidence reasoning. ThreadRAG introduces a Semantic Topology Mapper (STM), which organizes retrieved documents into context-intact Semantic Nodes and constructs a Semantic Topology Graph (STG) through narrative, semantic, and lexical edges. It further incorporates Energy-Driven Pathfinding (EDP), which identifies query-relevant subgraphs via energy diffusion and deterministically linearizes them into coherent textual evidence paths. Experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that ThreadRAG consistently outperforms strong baselines. Notably, it achieves a 14.4% EM improvement over text-centric methods on HotpotQA and surpasses the best baseline by 5.8% EM on 2WikiMultihop, while reducing token cost to 23.3% of the strongest baseline.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）常结合知识图谱（KG）以应对复杂的多跳问答任务。然而，其性能日益受限于知识结构化与推理过程。（i）严重的语义损失：将连续文本提炼为离散符号会丢失上下文细微差别和叙事连贯性。（ii）明显的模态不匹配：碎片化的图结构与大语言模型预训练所依赖的连续文本不兼容，而将其重写为自然语言又引入生成噪声并遗漏信息。为解决这些局限，我们提出ThreadRAG，一种基于语义拓扑的RAG框架，用于保持上下文一致性的知识结构化与模态原生的证据推理。ThreadRAG引入语义拓扑映射器（STM），将检索到的文档组织为上下文完整的语义节点，并通过叙事、语义和词汇边构建语义拓扑图（STG）。同时引入能量驱动路径规划（EDP），通过能量扩散识别与查询相关的子图，并确定性地将其线性化为连贯的文本证据路径。在三个多跳问答基准上的实验表明，ThreadRAG始终优于强基线。尤其在HotpotQA上相比文本中心方法提升14.4% EM，在2WikiMultihop上超越最佳基线5.8% EM，同时将token成本降低至最强基线的23.3%。
👤 Yufeng Li, Zaijing Li, Fuchen Zhang, Zongzong Wu, Ming Zhao et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Unintended Side Effects of Value Training in LLMs
大型语言模型价值训练的意外副作用
As LLMs are increasingly deployed in value-sensitive contexts, aligning them with human values has emerged as a post-training objective in NLP, aimed at instilling additional values and guiding model behaviour. However, current evaluations remain narrow. Because values are interconnected, it is unclear how fine-tuning for specific values alters unrelated capabilities and behaviours. In this work, we adapt four instruction-tuned LLMs across individual Hofstede's value dimensions and evaluate them on both cross-value transfer and broader behaviours. We find that value-specific training produces unintended side effects on untrained values, risk preferences, social cognition, and safety traits. Consequently, we suggest that value alignment after post-training should incorporate broader behavioral evaluations, and we highlight the need to investigate where additional value training might best fit into the pipeline.
中文翻译
随着大型语言模型越来越多地部署于价值敏感场景，将其与人类价值观对齐已成为自然语言处理中的后训练目标，旨在赋予模型额外价值观并引导其行为。然而，当前评估仍较为狭窄。由于价值观相互关联，尚不清楚针对特定价值观的微调如何影响无关能力与行为。本研究在四个指令微调的大语言模型上，分别沿霍夫斯泰德价值观维度进行调整，并评估其在跨价值观迁移及更广泛行为表现上的效果。我们发现，针对特定价值观的训练会在未训练的价值观、风险偏好、社会认知和安全特性上产生意外副作用。因此，我们建议在后训练阶段进行价值对齐时应纳入更广泛的行為评估，并强调需探究额外价值训练最适宜融入流水线的位置。
👤 Siddhesh Pawar, Chen Cecilia Liu, Anna Korhonen, Isabelle Augenstein 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29823]

MemoryTax: The Bitter Lesson of Agent Memory
MemoryTax：智能体记忆的苦涩教训
Agent memory systems such as Mem0 and Zep decide what to keep before any question arrives; coding agents keep the raw history and let the model search it with its own tools. We ask whether designed memory still helps once the model is strong enough to search for itself. On one LoCoMo conversation (419 turns, 152 questions), we run four memory methods (full context, BM25, Mem0, Zep) and three coding agents (Pi, Codex, Claude Code), each with GPT-4o-mini, GPT-6 Luna and Claude Opus 5.5, and record what every rollout costs. This is a short empirical study, with one conversation and one run per cell. Agent memory is a costly patch for a weak model: Mem0 beats full context only with GPT-4o-mini, and its build alone costs more than a whole full-context rollout. Agent memory also has a ceiling, set by the model that writes it: a stronger reader does not recover what a weaker writer left out. With Claude Opus 5.5, Pi tops the cost-accuracy frontier, scoring 99% for less than half the cost of full context; its one miss is a wrong answer key. Part of that lead comes from photo captions that only the agents could read: on the 88 questions whose evidence has no photo, Pi and full context differ by one answer.
中文翻译
智能体记忆系统如Mem0和Zep在任何问题出现前就决定保留什么内容；编码型智能体则保留原始历史记录，并让模型使用自身工具进行搜索。我们探讨当模型足够强大时，预先设计的记忆是否仍然有效。在一次LoCoMo对话（419轮，152个问题）中，我们测试了四种记忆方法（完整上下文、BM25、Mem0、Zep）和三种编码智能体（Pi、Codex、Claude Code），每种搭配GPT-4o-mini、GPT-6 Luna和Claude Opus 5.5，并记录每次运行的成本。这是一项简短的实证研究，每个单元仅进行一次对话和一次运行。结果显示，智能体记忆是弱模型的一种高成本补丁：只有在使用GPT-4o-mini时，Mem0才优于完整上下文，且其构建成本超过一次完整上下文运行的总成本。智能体记忆也存在上限，由撰写记忆的模型决定：更强的读取器无法恢复较弱写入者遗漏的内容。使用Claude Opus 5.5时，Pi在成本-准确率前沿表现最优，以不到完整上下文一半的成本达到99%准确率；其唯一失误是答案键错误。部分优势来自仅智能体可读的照片描述：在88个无照片证据的问题中，Pi与完整上下文仅相差一个答案。
👤 Zedong Peng 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Evolving with Open Worlds: Towards Recursive Self-Improvement for Future Forecasting
与开放世界共进化：迈向未来预测的递归自我改进
Most contemporary artificial intelligence (AI) systems are released with frozen parameters and improve through external updates, whereas an ideal system would keep improving from its own experience, a capacity we call recursive self-improvement (RSI). Yet studying RSI requires an environment that is continuously changing, objectively verifiable, and naturally grounded, posing a fundamental challenge for agent evolution. Here we introduce DeepEvolve, a sustainable evolution agent system grounded in future event prediction, in which agents continuously interact with a dynamically evolving environment and improve by internalizing and externalizing the experience gained through these interactions. A living environment synthesizes verifiable tasks, and a recursive-evolution engine turns the reasoning trajectory behind each prediction back into capability: decoupling search from prediction adapts parameter-level evolution to the contamination-prone live environment, while distilling reusable skills lets the agent evolve from the limited live trajectories such an environment affords. On the external FutureX leaderboard, which evaluates predictions against real-world outcomes and is updated weekly, our self-improved forecaster ranked first across all systems over three consecutive weeks, achieving up to a 195% improvement in rating. On our synthesized evaluation, it ranked among the top three across four base LLMs, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 10.7 Bradley--Terry points. More broadly, these results suggest that a system built for self-improvement is best understood as an agent that co-evolves with a genuinely living, open-ended environment, pointing to a shift in AI from training static models on fixed data toward cultivating agents that grow stronger from their own experience, with such an open world as the indispensable basis of that growth.
中文翻译
当前大多数人工智能系统以参数冻结的形式发布，并通过外部更新进行改进，而理想系统应能从自身经验中持续改进，我们称之为递归自我改进（RSI）。然而，研究RSI需要一个持续变化、客观可验证且自然扎根的环境，这对智能体演化构成根本挑战。本文提出DeepEvolve——一个基于未来事件预测的可持续演化智能体系统，其中智能体持续与动态演化的环境互动，并通过内化和外化这些互动所获得的经验实现改进。一个活态环境生成可验证的任务，而递归演化引擎将每次预测背后的推理轨迹转化为能力：解耦搜索与预测，使参数级演化适应易受污染的实时环境；同时提炼可复用技能，使智能体能够从此类环境提供的有限实时轨迹中演化。在每周更新的外部FutureX排行榜上，我们的自改进预测器连续三周排名第一，评分最高提升达195%。在合成评估中，它在四个基础LLM上均位列前三，相比最强基线最高提升10.7 Bradley-Terry分。更广泛地，这些结果表明，一个为自我改进设计的系统最好理解为与真正活态、开放无界的环境共同进化的智能体，标志着AI正从在固定数据上训练静态模型，转向培育能从自身经验中不断成长的智能体，而开放世界是这一成长不可或缺的基础。
👤 Zhipeng Chen, Yunsen Liang, Yanxi Chen, Hongkang Zhao, Lingyun Feng et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

ALIAS: Evaluating Performance and Memorization in LLM-Written TLA+ Specifications
ALIAS：评估大语言模型编写TLA+规范中的性能与记忆现象
Memorization inflates the benchmark scores of large language models (LLMs), since a model can reproduce answers it saw in training. Researchers test for it with consistent identifier renaming (CIR), which gives every identifier in a task a new name, and treat a lower score on the renamed task as evidence of recall. We test this method on the Temporal Logic of Actions Plus (TLA+) language, whose model checker acts as an oracle by confirming whether a renamed task preserves the exact behaviors of the original. Across 111 TLA+-Bench tasks, renaming lowers the correctness of Claude Opus 4.8 from 28% to 18% and of GPT-5 from 9% to 7%. Most of this decrease comes from how the test grades, which requires exact names that most descriptions never state. Grading by behavior shrinks the decrease to about 3 percentage points for Opus 4.8 and to 0 for GPT-5. In two model families fine-tuned on disjoint halves of the tasks, renaming removes about half of the training gain in likelihood and costs 4.9 percentage points (Qwen) and 1.3 percentage points (Gemma) on unseen tasks. A lower score after renaming therefore mixes memorization, name-based grading, and the cost of new names.
中文翻译
记忆现象会夸大大型语言模型（LLMs）在基准测试中的得分，因为模型可以复现训练中见过的答案。研究人员使用一致标识符重命名（CIR）方法检测记忆现象，即为任务中的每个标识符赋予新名称，若重命名后得分降低，则视为回忆证据。我们在时序动作逻辑加（TLA+）语言上测试该方法，其模型检查器作为判别器，可确认重命名任务是否保留原任务的精确行为。在111个TLA+-Bench任务中，重命名使Claude Opus 4.8的正确率从28%降至18%，GPT-5从9%降至7%。大部分下降源于评分机制要求精确名称，而多数描述并未提供。若按行为评分，Opus 4.8的下降仅约3个百分点，GPT-5则无下降。在对两个不相交任务子集进行微调的模型族中，重命名消除了约一半的训练增益，导致未见任务上的得分分别下降4.9个百分点（Qwen）和1.3个百分点（Gemma）。因此，重命名后的低分混合了记忆、基于名称的评分以及新名称带来的成本。

Bridging Foundational and Agentic Capabilities of LLMs
弥合大语言模型的基础能力与代理能力之间的鸿沟
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as agents, yet it remains unclear how their foundational capabilities translate into agentic capabilities. End-to-end scores leave unresolved whether low benchmark capability or weak deployment drives a failure, limiting actionable diagnosis. We bridge foundational and agentic capabilities through an operation-level measurement framework. We represent each model with a foundational profile derived from capability benchmarks and an operation profile derived from interaction trajectories, and define capability realization gaps as deviations of observed operation performance from predictions based on foundational capabilities. Experiments on fifteen model services in $τ^2$-Bench show that foundational profiles only partially predict agentic operations. Models with comparable profiles can differ substantially in execution, and these gaps remain consistent across task subsets. Operation profiles add information and substantially improve task-score prediction. The framework locates execution departures at the operation level to inform model development and agent evaluation. We will release our code and analysis artifacts.

大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被部署为智能体，但其基础能力如何转化为代理能力仍不清晰。端到端评分无法区分是基础能力不足还是部署薄弱导致失败，限制了可操作的诊断。我们通过操作层面的测量框架，弥合基础能力与代理能力之间的差距。我们将每个模型表示为基于能力基准的基础能力画像，以及基于交互轨迹的操作能力画像，并将能力实现差距定义为实际操作表现与基于基础能力预测值之间的偏差。在$τ^2$-Bench的十五个模型服务上的实验表明，基础画像只能部分预测代理操作。具有相似画像的模型在执行上可能差异显著，且这些差距在不同任务子集中保持一致。操作画像提供了额外信息，并显著提升了任务得分预测能力。该框架可在操作层面定位执行偏差，为模型开发与代理评估提供指导。代码与分析工具将公开发布。

Accept or Repair: Preserving Passage-Supported Answers in Contrastive Decoding
接受或修复：在对比解码中保留有段落支持的答案
Large language models often answer from knowledge learned during training, even when a provided passage contradicts that knowledge. To address this problem, contrastive decoding contrasts the predictions under the full and a modified prompt at every token, but it also alters correct answers that the passage supports. We show that checking answers against the passage removes this trade-off. Based on this observation, we propose Accept-or-Repair (AoR), a training-free procedure. AoR keeps a sampled answer if the passage supports it. Otherwise, it tries up to two replacements with contrastive decoding, returning one as soon as it passes this check. It rejects replacements that repeat the answer generated without the passage when the passage favors another answer. Our experiments show that AoR raises the score over sampling by 12.5 points on counterfactual passages and 2.3 on ordinary ones, surpassing eight contrastive and prompting baselines on both, including when each receives the same rejection rule. Replacing only initial answers that the passage does not support improves the average score of every decoder. These results suggest that models can follow passages that contradict their knowledge while preserving accuracy on passages that agree with it. Code will be released at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/AoR.
中文翻译
大型语言模型经常根据训练中学到的知识作答，即使提供的段落与其矛盾。为解决此问题，对比解码在每个词元处对比完整提示与修改后提示下的预测，但也会改变段落支持的正确答案。我们发现，将答案与段落进行比对可消除这一权衡。基于此观察，我们提出无需训练的Accept-or-Repair（AoR）方法。AoR若采样答案被段落支持则保留；否则尝试最多两次替换，使用对比解码生成，一旦通过段落验证即返回。若替换重复了无段落支持时生成的答案，而段落支持另一答案，则拒绝该替换。实验表明，AoR在反事实段落上比采样提升12.5分，在普通段落上提升2.3分，超越八种对比解码与提示基线，包括每种均采用相同拒绝规则的情况。仅修复段落不支持的初始答案即可提升所有解码器的平均得分。结果表明，模型可以在段落与自身知识冲突时遵循段落，同时保留段落与知识一致时的准确性。代码将在https://github.com/js-lee-AI/AoR发布。
👤 Jungseob Lee, Seungyoon Lee, Hyeonseok Moon, Seongtae Hong, Suhyune Son et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Uni-Mem: Learning a Long-Horizon Agentic Memory Manager with Unified Online Policy Optimization
Uni-Mem：基于统一在线策略优化的长周期智能体记忆管理器学习
Long-term agent memory is shifting from predefined workflows toward learnable policies for memory construction and retrieval. Existing learning-based methods either optimize construction alone or combine retrieval-side online reinforcement learning (RL) with offline construction supervision, limiting co-adaptation between the two stages. In this paper, we explore end-to-end online RL for a unified memory manager (Uni-Mem) that learns both capabilities under a shared policy. However, joint optimization with existing RL algorithms such as GRPO faces confounded group comparisons and sparse feedback over long memory-management trajectories. To this end, we propose Stage-Decoupled GRPO, which alternates construction and retrieval optimization while controlling variation from the other stage within each rollout group, yielding stage-specific learning signals. We further introduce Event-Wise Credit Assignment (ECA), which localizes feedback to responsible events and propagates event-level advantages to their constituent operations. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show that Uni-Mem outperforms the strongest RL-based memory, demonstrating the effectiveness of jointly learning memory construction and retrieval via Stage-Decoupled GRPO and ECA.
中文翻译
长期智能体记忆正从预定义工作流转向可学习的记忆构建与检索策略。现有基于学习的方法要么仅优化记忆构建，要么将检索端的在线强化学习（RL）与离线构建监督结合，限制了两个阶段之间的协同适应。本文探索一种端到端的在线强化学习方法，用于统一记忆管理器（Uni-Mem），使其在共享策略下同时学习构建与检索能力。然而，使用现有RL算法（如GRPO）进行联合优化时，面临群体比较混淆和长记忆管理轨迹中反馈稀疏的问题。为此，我们提出分阶段解耦GRPO，通过交替优化构建与检索阶段，并在每次滚动更新中控制另一阶段的变量，从而获得阶段特定的学习信号。我们还引入事件级信用分配（ECA），将反馈定位至相关事件，并将事件级优势传播至其构成操作。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上的实验表明，Uni-Mem优于最强的基于RL的记忆系统，证明了通过分阶段解耦GRPO和ECA联合学习记忆构建与检索的有效性。

A Strong MoE Is All You Need for Process Supervision
一个强大的MoE就足以实现过程监督
Process reward models (PRMs) provide fine-grained supervision for multi-step reasoning, yet obtaining reliable process labels remains a central bottleneck. Existing approaches typically rely on human annotation, rollout-based estimation, or auxiliary verification. In this work, we explore a different source of process supervision natively exposed by Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models: expert routing. We show that simple routing statistics provide an intrinsic process-confidence signal that is competitive with conventional output-logit confidence. Building on this observation, we construct routing confidence by combining overall routing mass, head routing mass, and within-step routing variation, and introduce RoCo-Rethink, which identifies the least confident reasoning step and selectively regenerates the subsequent trajectory. We further introduce RoCo-PRM, which uses a strong MoE to automatically annotate reasoning trajectories with routing-derived step scores and distills them into a standalone PRM. Across multiple MoE architectures and diverse reasoning tasks, both RoCo-Rethink and RoCo-PRM consistently outperform strong baselines, while RoCo-PRM transfers effectively across both model architectures and tasks. Our findings suggest that expert routing is more than a computational dispatch mechanism: it provides an architecture-native source of process supervision for reasoning.

过程奖励模型（PRMs）为多步推理提供细粒度监督，但获取可靠的过程标签仍是核心瓶颈。现有方法通常依赖人工标注、基于回放的估计或辅助验证。本文探索了一种由混合专家（MoE）模型天然暴露的过程监督来源：专家路由。我们发现，简单的路由统计量可提供一种与传统输出logit置信度相当的内在过程置信信号。基于此观察，我们通过结合整体路由质量、头路由质量及步骤内路由变化构建路由置信度，并提出RoCo-Rethink，该方法识别出最不自信的推理步骤并选择性地重生成后续轨迹。此外，我们引入RoCo-PRM，利用强MoE自动为推理轨迹标注路由衍生的步骤得分，并将其提炼为独立的PRM。在多种MoE架构和多样推理任务中，RoCo-Rethink和RoCo-PRM均持续优于强基线，且RoCo-PRM在模型架构和任务间具有良好的迁移能力。研究结果表明，专家路由不仅是计算调度机制，更是一种原生的、可用于推理过程监督的架构级资源。

OmniSimulator: Aligning Small Language Models for Authentic Heterogeneous Behavior Modeling
OmniSimulator：对齐小型语言模型以实现真实异构行为建模
Large language models (LLMs) have shown great potential as general-purpose user simulators for interactive systems. Prior studies have demonstrated that training on high-quality data can substantially improve model reasoning and downstream task performance. However, the upper bound of user simulation is still constrained by the model's reasoning paradigm: native chain-of-thought often degenerates into shallow pattern matching and fails to adequately model the behavioral factors underlying real user decisions. In this paper, we propose OmniSimulator, a novel training framework designed to improve the predictive ability of small LLMs for realistic user simulation on short-video platform environments. Specifically, OmniSimulator organizes behavior-oriented reasoning through a structured schema with five dimensions, carefully selects data from real user-behavior scenarios, and introduces a teacher model to generate high-quality reasoning traces under the proposed schema, helping small models learn behavior-relevant reasoning patterns rather than relying on superficial behavioral correlations. Based on this process, we construct 9,881 high-quality training instances from three months of historical interaction trajectories of 200 real users, averaging about 50 distilled examples per user. Experimental results show that OmniSimulator improves the average score of Qwen3-8B by 34.33%, achieves performance competitive with Claude-Sonnet-4.5, and supports smaller-scale, self-hosted deployment. These results suggest that learning structured behavior-oriented reasoning patterns is a promising direction towards scalable and high-fidelity LLM-based user simulation.

Memory-Centric Continual Learning for AI Agents
以记忆为中心的持续学习用于AI智能体
Autonomous agents powered by foundation models can reason, plan, and use tools, but their fixed weights and context limits hinder sustained learning from experience over long-term deployment. While external memory systems attempt to bridge this gap, they mostly act as passive retrieval caches, evaluated primarily by whether they can recall stored facts or past interactions. Whether memory can drive genuine continual learning remains an open question. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Centric Continual Learning (MCCL), arguing that memory should be judged by whether it improves future actions, not merely what it retrieves. MCCL has three main parts. First, a three-axis taxonomy organizes memory by where state is stored (substrates), what form it takes (assets), and how it is managed (policies). This defines a two-loop lifecycle that updates memory state in an inner loop while refining management policies in an outer loop based on task feedback. Second, an evaluation protocol measures retention under interference, adaptation to change, and task transfer to isolate genuine behavioral gains from simple context expansion. Third, a memory scaling framework models how agent performance scales with experience, storage capacity, and compute budgets across non-parametric and parametric memory. Together, these components reframe agent memory from passive lookup to continuous learning, offering a principled route toward self-improving autonomous agents.
中文翻译
由基础模型驱动的自主智能体能够推理、规划并使用工具，但其固定的权重和上下文限制阻碍了在长期部署中从经验中持续学习。尽管外部记忆系统试图弥合这一差距，但它们大多仅作为被动检索缓存，主要通过能否回忆存储的事实或过往交互来评估。记忆是否能真正推动持续学习仍是未解之谜。本文提出以记忆为中心的持续学习（MCCL），主张应根据记忆是否改善未来行为而非仅其检索内容来评判记忆。MCCL包含三个核心部分：首先，一个三轴分类法按状态存储位置（基质）、形式（资产）和管理方式（策略）组织记忆，定义了一个双循环生命周期，内层循环更新记忆状态，外层循环基于任务反馈优化管理策略；其次，提出一种评估协议，通过干扰下的保留能力、对变化的适应性以及任务迁移能力，分离出真正的行为提升与简单的上下文扩展；第三，构建一个记忆扩展框架，建模智能体性能随经验、存储容量和计算预算的变化规律，涵盖非参数化与参数化记忆。这些组件共同将智能体记忆从被动查询重构为持续学习机制，为自改进的自主智能体提供了系统性路径。

ChronoSynth: Constraint-Aware Question Synthesis over Temporal Knowledge Graphs
ChronoSynth：面向时间知识图谱的约束感知问题生成
Knowledge graph question generation (KGQG) generates natural-language questions from structured graph evidence and a target answer. When the input graph is temporal, a valid question must also preserve the answer field, time condition, reference event, and temporal order that determine the answer set. Existing KGQG methods and prompting baselines can produce fluent questions that alter these conditions, causing temporal semantic drift. This paper studies temporal KGQG as a constraint-preserving graph-to-question problem and proposes ChronoSynth, a memory-augmented and constraint-aware generation framework. ChronoSynth first constructs a Temporal Intent State to make the intended question semantics explicit. It then retrieves compatible typed question patterns from an indexed pattern memory, adapts them to the input graph, and validates the generated question. Validation feedback guides revisions when a supported inconsistency is detected. Experiments on CRONQUESTIONS and MultiTQ demonstrate state-of-the-art generation performance, with ablations supporting the contributions of its components.

Seeing the Forest for the Trees: Emergent Agreement from Individual Conversations
见林不见树：个体对话中涌现的一致性
As LLM agents interact in the wild and shape each other's decisions, understanding how they converge toward agreement becomes important. We study this in multi-turn conversations where two LLMs discuss subjective matters, can agree or disagree, and may change their stances. Using a linear classifier on individual conversation activations, we identify an internal representation of agreement. We find that it varies substantially across turns and conversations and does not reliably track external agreement at the individual conversation level. We therefore move beyond the representation of agreement in individual conversations, by aggregating the agreement representation across conversations. Externally, this aggregate view reveals a simple structure: when pooled across conversations, agreement can be predicted well with a Markov process, with agreement at each turn being a direct function of agreement at the previous turn. Similarly, when internal agreement is aggregated, it couples with the external Markov agreement through a power-law mapping, exposing Markovian dynamics in internal representations. Additionally, we find that after a change of stance, models express agreement externally before their internal representations reflect the same shift. These results reveal a joint structure between internal and external agreement that emerges only when internal features are aggregated across conversations. Code available at https://github.com/technion-cs-nlp/SeeingTheForestForTheTrees.
中文翻译
当大型语言模型（LLM）代理在真实环境中交互并相互影响决策时，理解它们如何趋向一致变得至关重要。我们研究了多轮对话中两个LLM讨论主观议题、可能达成一致或分歧，并可能改变立场的情况。通过分析单个对话激活值的线性分类器，我们识别出一种内部一致性表征。发现该表征在不同轮次和对话间变化显著，且在个体对话层面无法可靠追踪外部一致性。因此，我们超越了单一对话中的一致性表征，转而对跨对话的一致性表征进行聚合。外部观察显示，这种聚合视图具有简单结构：当跨对话汇总时，一致性可由马尔可夫过程良好预测，每一轮的一致性直接依赖于前一轮的一致性。类似地，当内部一致性被聚合时，它与外部马尔可夫一致性通过幂律映射耦合，揭示了内部表征中的马尔可夫动态。此外，我们发现，在立场转变后，模型会先在外部表现出一致性，其内部表征才反映这一变化。这些结果揭示了只有在跨对话聚合内部特征时才会出现的内外一致性联合结构。代码已公开于 https://github.com/technion-cs-nlp/SeeingTheForestForTheTrees。
👤 Adi Simhi, Yaniv Nikankin, Jonathan Herzig, Idan Szpektor, Yonatan Belinkov et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29326]

Signals and Queries: Token-Efficient Agentic Context Discovery
信号与查询：面向代理的高效上下文发现
User-facing LLM agents must identify which behavioral policies apply at each turn, but evaluating every policy with an LLM is costly. We introduce Signals and Queries (S&Q), a lightweight first-stage relevance filter developed for Parlant 4.0 that removes at least 30% of candidate policies before downstream evaluation while preserving high recall. For each policy, an LLM generates activating examples and hard negatives offline. An independent logistic classifier trained on their embeddings then predicts relevance using embeddings and vector dot products, with no per-turn LLM calls. This shifts expensive relevance evaluation to a smaller candidate set, while allowing each policy's classifier to be updated independently. Evaluated against 9,144 LLM-judge relevance labels covering 254 $τ^2$-bench conversation prefixes and 36 airline policies, S&Q achieves higher precision than top-K and cosine-threshold retrieval at every evaluated recall level, with consistent results across two embedding providers. These findings support inexpensive, per-policy classification as a practical first stage for reducing downstream evaluation workload in customer-facing agents.
中文翻译
面向用户的LLM代理必须在每一轮中识别适用的行为策略，但使用LLM评估每个策略成本高昂。我们提出信号与查询（S&Q），一种为Parlant 4.0设计的轻量级第一阶段相关性过滤机制，可在下游评估前移除至少30%的候选策略，同时保持高召回率。对于每个策略，LLM离线生成激活示例和硬负样本。随后，一个独立训练的逻辑回归分类器基于其嵌入向量和向量点积预测相关性，无需每轮调用LLM。这将昂贵的相关性评估转移到更小的候选集上，同时允许每个策略的分类器独立更新。在包含254个τ²-bench对话前缀和36个航空公司策略的9,144个LLM裁判相关性标签上评估，S&Q在所有召回率水平下均优于Top-K和余弦阈值检索，且在两个嵌入提供商间结果一致。这些发现支持以低成本、按策略分类作为客户导向代理中降低下游评估工作量的实际第一阶段。

OSynth: A Longitudinal, Context-Sensitive Evaluation of Meeting Understanding
OSynth：面向会议理解的纵向、上下文敏感评估
Meeting notetakers are increasingly deployed in settings where longitudinal context is available including calendar metadata, prior meetings, emails, slides, and speaker enrollments; however, meeting corpora remain limited in terms of size, domain coverage, and available context. We present OSynth, a large-scale synthetic dataset of simulated organizations comprising 570 hours of meeting audio together with associated artifacts and a latent ground-truth state. In a pairwise comparison, human raters distinguish OSynth meetings from real meeting transcripts with only 56% accuracy based on excerpts of 150-300 words. Across state-of-the-art LLMs, additional context yields significant gains on several entity-sensitive dimensions, demonstrating limitations of contemporary approaches and the potential usefulness of OSynth in developing better-contextualized meeting-understanding systems.

Let Agents Design How They Search: Automatic Interface Optimization for Agentic Search
让智能体设计它们的搜索方式：面向代理搜索的自动界面优化
Agentic search depends on the interface through which a language-model agent accesses and explores a corpus. Existing interface designs are fixed by hand and applied unchanged to every corpus, although effective designs may depend on the corpus. We introduce SearchWeaver, a framework for automatic search interface optimization guided by downstream search performance. A proposer uses execution trajectories and task rewards to jointly revise corpus artifacts, executable tools, and usage instructions. This pro cess optimizes what information is prepared in advance, which operations tools perform, and which decisions remain with the search agent. With GPT-5.4-nano, the optimized interfaces improve answer accuracy from 71.6% to 78.4% on BrowseComp-Plus and average nDCG@10 from 45.7 to 55.0 across four BRIGHT subsets over Direct Corpus Interaction. These findings highlight the value of jointly optimizing corpus artifacts, executable tools, and usage instructions to develop effective search interfaces.

MOSAIC: An Omni-Modal Benchmark for Long-Term Memory and Cross-Modal Reasoning in Conversational Agents
MOSAIC：对话智能体长期记忆与跨模态推理的全模态基准
Omni-modal assistants must remember not only what users say, but also what they show and convey. Existing long-term memory benchmarks are mostly text-and-image or report a single end-to-end score, so they cannot locate whether evidence is lost when it is represented, retrieved, delivered, or read. We introduce MOSAIC, a benchmark of 923 questions over persona-grounded histories of 11-24 sessions spanning text, image, video, and audio. MOSAIC constructs targeted spatial, temporal, and prosodic signals rather than decorative media, with the strongest causal validation for spatial information and selector-level validation for annotated prosody, and pairs them with a matched diagnostic protocol that freezes the question, reader, prompt, and budget while intervening on representation, retrieval, and delivery. Matched decomposition shows that representation loss is modality-dependent, residual reading dominates even under gold evidence, and abstention remains weak on these controls: strong answerable scores coexist with failures to refuse. MOSAIC thus turns omni-modal memory evaluation from a leaderboard into a diagnosis of write, retrieve, deliver, and read failures.
中文翻译
全模态助手不仅需要记住用户说了什么，还需记住他们展示和传达的内容。现有长期记忆基准大多仅限于文本与图像，或仅报告单一端到端分数，无法定位证据是在表示、检索、传递还是阅读阶段丢失的。我们提出MOSAIC，一个包含923个问题的基准，覆盖11至24轮会话的基于人物设定的历史记录，涵盖文本、图像、视频和音频多种模态。MOSAIC构建的是有针对性的空间、时间与语调信号，而非装饰性媒体，其中空间信息具有最强因果验证，语调标注具备选择器级别的验证，并配以匹配的诊断协议——冻结问题、读者、提示和预算，仅干预表示、检索与传递环节。匹配分解显示，表示损失具有模态依赖性，即使在黄金证据下，残余阅读仍占主导地位，拒绝行为依然薄弱：高可回答分数与拒绝失败并存。因此，MOSAIC将全模态记忆评估从排行榜转变为对表示、检索、传递与阅读失败的诊断。

C$^2$U in RAG: Compositional Concept Unlearning in Retrieval-Augmented Generation
RAG中的C$^2$U：检索增强生成中的组合概念消解
Retrieval-augmented generation can expose unwanted concepts through retrieved evidence even when the generator is moderated. We study query-side concept suppression with a fixed encoder and document index. Compositional Concept Unlearning (C$^2$U) routes a profile-derived direction through an augmented atom bank, adding only a bounded edited-minus-original delta to the raw query. Across five datasets, four encoders, and three seeds, the validation-selected configuration reduces pooled binary target Hit@5 by 4.25 percentage points with non-positive control degradation and target-exposure changes. Under the evaluated query transforms, it suppresses target density without increasing budgeted control-query exposure. Separately, ArXiv four-concept composition improves over an averaged-direction comparator after independent validation gates and first-use test evaluation.

LTEM: LLM-Friendly Table-Enhanced Markdown for Table Understanding and Generation
LTEM：面向大语言模型的表格增强型Markdown格式用于表格理解与生成
Tables in real-world documents, including scientific papers, financial reports, and statistical summaries, frequently use merged cells, multi-level headers, and sectioned layouts. Yet the lightweight formats LLMs handle best (Markdown, CSV) cannot express these structures, while expressive formats (HTML, LaTeX) impose excessive token cost and parsing complexity. To enable systematic analysis of this trade-off, we introduce a taxonomy decomposing table structure into six atomic features (F1-F6) and corresponding complexity levels (L1-L6), and construct STB (Synthetic Table Benchmark), a controlled benchmark with 1,089 tables systematically distributed across complexity levels. Using this framework, we propose LTEM (LLM-friendly Table-Enhanced Markdown), a formally specified, minimal extension of Markdown that explicitly encodes spans and hierarchical headers. Experiments across five state-of-the-art LLMs show that LTEM achieves accuracy comparable to HTML, offers a favorable accuracy-token trade-off relative to HTML and LaTeX, and produces substantially fewer invalid responses than LaTeX; for table generation, LTEM is comparable to MultiMarkdown though HTML is best. We release our taxonomy, benchmark, and format specification to support future research on LLM-table interaction.

[2610.28992]
agent-memory
Language Impairment as a Lens onto Large Language Models
语言障碍作为理解大型语言模型的透镜
Although large language models (LLMs) encode language in highly distributed spaces, they remain vulnerable to focal disruptions---a phenomenon strikingly similar to how localized brain damage causes clinical aphasia in humans. To investigate this theoretical tension, we ask whether LLMs possess a functional structure analogous to the human language system, by introducing a novel mechanistic interpretability framework inspired by aphasia. Through two complementary approaches, classifier-guided lesioning and continuous activation steering, we systematically induce aphasia-like breakdowns in LLMs. We find that language functions can be successfully isolated to discrete structural circuits and continuous representational vectors, yet these functions remain densely entangled with significant circuit overlap and vector similarities across distinct aphasic profiles. Furthermore, while steering generalizes robustly across downstream tasks, structural lesioning remains asymmetrically constrained. By bridging neuropsychology and mechanistic interpretability, we demonstrate that LLMs, functionally analogous to biological brains, rely on a densely entangled architecture that can be systematically mapped through clinically-informed interventions.

Scan or Index? large Evidence Coverage, Answer Quality, and Cost in Long-Document RAG
扫描还是索引？长文档RAG中的大范围证据覆盖、答案质量与成本权衡
Scanning every passage in a long document seems thorough, but does it preserve more useful evidence or produce better answers? We compare 11 retrieval configurations spanning lexical and dense search, hybrid reranking, and full-document scanning with Jev, OpenAI Decisions, and Cloudflare Clef. Our evaluation uses 396 QASPER questions and 80 questions from eight complete textbooks, with common passages, a five-passage limit, and a fixed answer generator. On books, Hybrid + Jev achieves the highest observed evidence coverage (94.1%), compared with 88.7% for Jev full scan and 81.3\% for OpenAI dense retrieval. Full scans examine every passage initially, yet later reduction steps can discard relevant evidence. Higher evidence coverage also does not consistently produce better answers: Hybrid + Decisions achieves the highest observed book Answer Accuracy among retrieval methods (0.892), despite recovering less reference evidence. Full scanning incurs substantially higher latency on books under the tested deployments, while reusable dense retrieval has lower projected API cost than paid decision-based methods as queries accumulate. Most QASPER articles already fit within the hybrid candidate pool, limiting the benefit of scanning. These results highlight evidence retention, answer quality, and reuse cost as complementary criteria for choosing a long-document retrieval strategy.

Building AI Research Teams for Autonomous Discovery of Linear Attention Architectures
构建用于自主发现线性注意力架构的AI研究团队
Autonomous agents can increasingly propose ideas, modify code, and run experiments, creating an opportunity to accelerate scientific discovery using teams of collaborative AI researchers. To escape local minima in the discovery, we propose to encourage orthogonal exploration by constructing the team using agents with varying expertise. However, realizing this design requires more than diverse backgrounds. When experiments are costly, a team can test only a limited number of ideas, thereby creating the need to balance exploration and exploitation when selecting a subset of researchers to propose and execute new experiments. Furthermore, each result should shape what the team tries next. We therefore ask how AI researchers with different scientific backgrounds can build on one another's findings and sustain progress under a limited experimental budget. To address these challenges, we build a team of persistent AI researchers, each grounded in a different discipline, who keep private research histories, share experimental evidence, and receive research opportunities through adaptive allocation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this design by deploying the team to search for linear attention architectures, where it outperforms GDN2 at 1.3B scale on language modeling, common-sense reasoning, and synthetic and real-world retrieval tasks. In controlled comparisons, removing any single organizational component from our design yields markedly weaker discoveries: without disciplinary backgrounds, the search plateaus early; without private histories, researchers make less progress despite identical shared evidence; and uniform selection falls short of adaptive allocation within the same budget. These results suggest that sustained autonomous discovery depends not only on stronger agents, but also on who explores, what they remember, and how research effort is allocated.
中文翻译
自主智能体能够越来越多地提出想法、修改代码并运行实验，这为利用协作AI研究团队加速科学发现提供了机会。为避免发现过程陷入局部最优，我们提出通过构建具备不同专长的团队来鼓励正交探索。然而，实现这一设计不仅需要多样化的背景。当实验成本高昂时，团队只能测试有限数量的想法，从而需要在探索与利用之间权衡以选择研究人员子集来提出并执行新实验。此外，每次结果都应影响团队下一步的尝试。因此，我们探讨了具有不同科学背景的AI研究者如何基于彼此成果持续推进，且在有限实验预算下保持进展。为此，我们构建了一个由持久性AI研究者组成的团队，每位研究者基于不同学科，保留私有研究历史，共享实验证据，并通过自适应分配获得研究机会。我们通过部署该团队搜索线性注意力架构，证明了该设计的有效性：在1.3B规模下，其性能优于GDN2，在语言建模、常识推理以及合成与真实世界检索任务中均表现更优。在受控对比中，移除设计中的任一组织组件都会导致发现能力显著下降：缺乏学科背景时，搜索过早停滞；缺乏私有历史时，尽管共享证据相同，研究者进步更少；均匀选择也逊于自适应分配。这些结果表明，持续的自主发现不仅依赖更强的智能体，还取决于谁在探索、他们记得什么，以及研究资源如何分配。

智能体行为图：无需运行即可定位智能体的行为特征
LLM agents with similar success rates can follow different action sequences. An Agent Behavior Map represents LLM-harness combinations using centered action log-likelihoods on shared reference trajectories, without requiring new environment rollouts from the target agent. We derive error bounds relating squared coordinate distances to trajectory KL under explicit assumptions, and across eight cross-scored models the distances track the directly measured trajectory KL in rank order. HiddenLens: A Multi-Layer LLM Framework for Detecting and Resolving Hidden Assumptions in Software Requirements
HiddenLens：一种用于检测与解决软件需求中隐藏假设的多层LLM框架
Natural-language software requirements often omit assumptions about performance, operating conditions, user behaviour, and domain constraints, affecting architecture, testing, and deployment. If undiscovered until implementation, resolving them is far more costly than addressing them early. Although ambiguity and vagueness are widely studied, less attention has been given to assumptions embedded in requirements that appear clear and reasonable. We propose HiddenLens, a novel six-layer BERT-LLM pipeline and, to our knowledge, the first framework engineered to detect, classify, question, and resolve hidden assumptions rather than merely flag ambiguity. Layer 1 uses BERT attention entropy to localise unresolved spans; Layer 2 uses CoT-prompted LLM reasoning to articulate each assumption and its failure risk; Layer 3 uses fine-tuned BERT to classify assumptions into six non-functional requirement categories: performance, security, scalability, usability, reliability, and domain; Layer 4 generates ranked clarification options using FS LLM prompting; Layer 5 enables human validation through confirmation, editing, or rephrasing; and Layer 6 rewrites the requirement using the confirmed resolution, producing an explicit, testable, and traceable requirement. We also introduce the HiddenLens Corpus (HLC), comprising 312 requirements annotated with 847 hidden assumptions across six NFR categories. Inspired by PROMISE and PURE, HLC addresses the lack of annotated data for this task and is, to our knowledge, the first dataset developed specifically for it. HiddenLens outperforms keyword-based and QuARS-Adapted baselines by 71% and 61%, respectively, achieving an end-to-end F1-score of 0.79; its BERT-based NFR classifier achieves an F1-score of 0.847. These results indicate that HiddenLens can help analysts resolve hidden assumptions before they propagate through software development.
中文翻译
自然语言编写的软件需求常遗漏关于性能、运行条件、用户行为及领域约束的假设，影响架构设计、测试与部署。若在实现阶段才发现，修复成本远高于早期发现。尽管模糊性和歧义性已被广泛研究，但对看似清晰合理的需求数中嵌入的隐藏假设关注较少。我们提出HiddenLens，一种新颖的六层BERT-LLM流水线，据我们所知是首个专门设计用于检测、分类、质疑并解决隐藏假设而非仅标记歧义的框架。第一层使用BERT注意力熵定位未解决片段；第二层利用CoT提示的LLM推理阐明每个假设及其失败风险；第三层使用微调BERT将假设分类为六大非功能性需求类别：性能、安全、可扩展性、可用性、可靠性与领域；第四层通过FS LLM提示生成排序后的澄清选项；第五层支持人工验证，包括确认、编辑或重述；第六层基于确认的解决方案重写需求，生成明确、可测试、可追溯的需求。我们还引入HiddenLens语料库（HLC），包含312条需求，标注了847个隐藏假设，覆盖六个NFR类别。受PROMISE和PURE启发，HLC解决了该任务缺乏标注数据的问题，据我们所知是首个专为此任务构建的数据集。HiddenLens在端到端F1分数上比关键词基线和QuARS-Adapted基线分别高出71%和61%，达到0.79；其基于BERT的NFR分类器F1分数达0.847。结果表明，HiddenLens可在软件开发过程中帮助分析师提前化解隐藏假设。

Commit or Abstain? System One Decision Models, LLMs, and Missing Legal Authority
承诺还是放弃？System One决策模型、LLMs与缺失法律权威问题
Given the enormous number of claims to be checked, verifiable legal AI requires scalable, cost-effective models that answer the practical needs of law. System One decision models are built on large language models but, instead of generating text, return a calibrated probability for each option. This suits the verification of legal authority, where the question is closed: a given holding, or none of them. We test whether such models abstain when the correct authority is missing. We extend CaseHOLD with an abstention condition in which the correct holding is replaced in 1,670 of 5,314 items, and compare four decision models with six generative LLMs. When no option is correct, generative LLMs still pick one in 38-86% of items; the most cautious decision model does so in 30%. On standard CaseHOLD, a decision model matches the best generative LLM (88.1% vs. 88.2%) at 1/55 of the cost. Accepting that LLM's answer only when a decision model agrees halves its wrong citations, and distilling a decision model's probabilities into Qwen3-8B, without labels, yields a well-calibrated local model. Legal AI should be judged by its wrong citations, not by accuracy alone.
中文翻译
面对海量待核查主张，可验证的法律AI需要可扩展、低成本的模型以满足实际法律需求。System One决策模型基于大语言模型，但不生成文本，而是返回每个选项的校准概率。这适用于法律权威验证，因为问题为封闭式：给定判例或无任何判例。我们测试此类模型在正确权威缺失时是否会放弃回答。我们将CaseHOLD扩展为包含弃权条件，其中1670/5314项的正确判例被替换，并比较四种决策模型与六种生成式LLM。当无正确选项时，生成式LLM仍会在38%-86%的项目中做出选择；最谨慎的决策模型也仅在30%的情况下放弃。在标准CaseHOLD上，决策模型以1/55的成本达到了与最佳生成式LLM相当的表现（88.1% vs 88.2%）。只有当决策模型与LLM意见一致时才接受LLM答案，可使错误引用减少一半；将决策模型的概率蒸馏至Qwen3-8B（无需标签），可生成校准良好的本地模型。法律AI应以错误引用数量为评判标准，而不仅仅是准确率。

ToM2Vec: Continuous Theory-of-Mind Tokens for Multi-Agent Coordination
ToM2Vec：用于多智能体协作的连续心智理论令牌
Forming an accurate theory-of-mind (ToM) is key to successful multi-agent collaboration and requires reasoning under uncertainty to capture multiple possible partner states. This is hard to do in the text representation space native to large language model (LLM) agents, which more easily describe a single partner state. To address this challenge, we introduce ToM2Vec, which learns to form abstract theories-of-mind in a rich latent space via a fixed budget of continuous reasoning tokens. During training, supervision for these tokens comes from the partner's actual thoughts, which the ToM tokens are trained to abstract and match. At test-time, ToM tokens are generated by each agent and act as conditioners for that agent's thoughts and actions. Across several environments covering collaborative, competitive, and mixed-motive settings (Overcooked-AI, multiparty negotiation, Decrypto, Hanabi, and Collab Escape/Capture), we evaluate ToM2Vec's ability to support multi-agent interactions and collaboration, finding consistent improvements over the base model and a variety of competitive trained and prompted baselines. We show that latent reasoning is critical, with performance dropping when reasoning is expressed in text. Moreover, we show that ToM2Vec training generalizes to partner model families not seen at train-time, including a proprietary model (GPT-5.4). In our analysis, we provide evidence that the latent tokens encode a ToM, rather than task-specific information: ToM2Vec's tokens improve the agent's ability to directly answer questions about partner beliefs, desires, and next actions.
中文翻译
形成准确的心智理论（ToM）是多智能体协作成功的关键，需要在不确定性下推理以捕捉多个可能的伙伴状态。这在大型语言模型（LLM）智能体原生的文本表示空间中难以实现，因为它们更容易描述单一伙伴状态。为应对这一挑战，我们提出ToM2Vec，它通过固定预算的连续推理令牌，在丰富的潜在空间中学习形成抽象的心智理论。训练期间，这些令牌的监督信号来自伙伴的真实想法，ToM令牌被训练为抽象并匹配这些想法。测试时，每个智能体生成ToM令牌，并作为其思想与行动的调节器。我们在多个涵盖协作、竞争与混合动机场景的环境中（Overcooked-AI、多方谈判、Decrypto、Hanabi、Collab Escape/Capture）评估ToM2Vec支持多智能体交互与协作的能力，结果一致优于基础模型及多种竞争性训练与提示基线。我们证明潜空间推理至关重要，当推理表达为文本时性能显著下降。此外，我们展示了ToM2Vec训练可泛化至训练时未见的伙伴模型家族，包括专有模型（GPT-5.4）。分析表明，潜在令牌编码的是ToM而非特定任务信息：ToM2Vec的令牌提升了智能体直接回答关于伙伴信念、欲望和下一步行动问题的能力。

Conflicted Records: Typed Adversarial Contracts for Auditable LLM Output Quality Enforcement
冲突记录：用于可审计LLM输出质量强制的类型化对抗合约
LLM-generated records increasingly populate production information systems where they are treated as data assets subject to the same quality expectations as records from operational databases. A single such record can simultaneously satisfy and violate a quality rule: a grounded fact adjacent to a hallucination, a useful answer adjacent to a stereotype. Classical data cleaning cannot resolve this because the two judgments are incompatible over the same span; a single LLM judge cannot either, since it shares the training biases that produced the conflict. Existing interventions such as prompt engineering, chain-of-thought, and RLHF reduce surface error rates but provide no audit trail and cannot distinguish genuine remediation from blanket suppression. The question is whether a deterministic check over structured inter-agent records can catch the defects that referential checks alone miss. We present BiasGuard-D: two adversarial roles drawn from distinct model families exchange typed claim documents, a three-model quorum adjudicates by majority vote, and a combined referential-plus-semantic invariant validates the verdict. The semantic component fires on verdicts referential checks miss; removing the iterative loop drops invariant satisfaction sharply. The deliberation layer transfers from stereotype to hallucination remediation on HaluEval QA with only role-framing changes, raising right-answer recovery by 49 percentage points; the triage gate required a cross-family second auditor to maintain recall on objective hallucination. Every claim, vote, and instruction is stored as a typed record giving operators a provenance trail auditable under emerging AI regulations.
中文翻译
LLM生成的记录越来越多地进入生产信息系统，被视为与操作数据库记录同等质量要求的数据资产。一条记录可能同时满足和违反质量规则：一个基于事实的部分紧邻着一个幻觉，一个有用的回答紧邻着一个刻板印象。传统数据清洗无法解决此问题，因为两种判断在同一跨度上不兼容；单一LLM裁判也无法解决，因为它共享生成冲突的训练偏见。现有干预手段如提示工程、思维链和RLHF虽降低表面错误率，但无审计轨迹，无法区分真正的修复与全面压制。问题是：能否通过结构化跨智能体记录的确定性检查，捕获仅靠参照检查遗漏的缺陷？我们提出BiasGuard-D：来自不同模型家族的两个对抗角色交换类型化的声明文档，三模型共识组通过多数投票裁决，结合参照+语义不变性共同验证判决。语义组件在参照检查遗漏的判决上触发；移除迭代循环会导致不变性满足度急剧下降。决策层仅通过角色框架变化即可实现从刻板印象到幻觉修复的转移，在HaluEval QA上使正确答案恢复率提升49个百分点；分诊门限需要跨家族第二名审计员以维持客观幻觉的召回率。每个声明、投票和指令均以类型化记录形式存储，为运营者提供符合新兴AI监管要求的可审计溯源路径。

OACap: Caption-Guided Active Observation for Detailed Video Captioning
OACap：基于字幕引导的主动观察用于详细视频字幕生成
Unlike question answering, where a query specifies a perceptual target, open-ended detailed captioning must determine what evidence to acquire and how to integrate it into a coherent global description. We propose Observation-Adaptive Video Captioner (OACap), a caption-centered agent that turns unsupported or missing information into targeted observation requests, uses spatiotemporal tools to acquire evidence, and iteratively verifies and revises the caption. OACap captions consistently improve performance across general video-understanding benchmarks using a fixed text-only reader. To better diagnose the information they preserve, we introduce Factual-Semantic Captioning Bench (FSCap-Bench). Its Core-Support Factuality Score (CSFS) combines claim precision with importance-aware fact coverage and responds as expected to four controlled perturbation types on 93.0-98.3% of valid caption pairs. Its Cross-Modal Semantic Round-Trip Score (CSRS) measures semantic preservation through a video-caption-video-caption round trip by matching atomic facts from source and recovered captions. Together, they connect downstream textual-memory utility with factual and semantic preservation.

ExpEvolve: Structuring and Reusing Experience for Open-Ended Discovery
ExpEvolve：结构化与重用经验以实现开放式发现
Evolutionary coding agents such as AlphaEvolve are one of the notable successes in using Large Language Models (LLMs) to build AI Scientists. These agents tackle open-ended scientific problems by iteratively improving and evolving programs, leveraging the prior knowledge and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Despite their success, mutations over programs are mostly low-level upon surface heuristics, without a persistent unit of control. In contrast, science compounds through ideas. In this work, we propose ExpEvolve, which injects idea-level extraction and control into evolution: it discovers and leverages the high-level factors from programs that explain scoring differences among different programs. We characterize theoretical conditions on when discovery with idea factors improves sample efficiency. We also build a practical framework to show that the extracted high-level idea factors are helpful for guiding exploitation from experience and exploration when discovery comes to a plateau. Across 11 open-ended mathematical tasks, ExpEvolve significantly improves the final performance and sample efficiency up to 5.0x. The improvements also transfer to 4 other LLMs and to 6 machine learning tasks from MLS-Bench. Analysis shows that ExpEvolve can discover idea-level factors to provide better exploration and exploitation for evolutionary discovery.

Computer Vision and Knowledge-Grounded Language Models for Intelligent Beekeeper Assistance

Managed and wild bees sustain roughly a third of the global food products, yet colonies face mounting stress from climate-change including extreme weather patterns, altered parasite and disease patterns. Timely assessment of colony condition such as understanding colony strength, brood area, honey and pollen storage, comb usage, is essential to adaptive management. However, manual hive frame inspection is slow, costly, and quality depends on scarce expertise. We present an artificial targeted intelligent system framework focusing on edge deployable pipeline that turns hive frame photographs into interpretable and action-oriented inspection report. The pipeline includes an object detector to detect biologically-meaningful comb-cell classes; detections are distilled into structured fact schema; facts are converted into constrained factual text; the text triggers a grounded knowledge-base for interpretations and recommendations. Knowledge-base entries are required to be triggerable by evidence expressed in the fact schema. We built our system based on open source FAIRHiveFrames-1K dataset and favour reproducible, edge-deployable models over large general purpose frontier models. We additionally release FAIRHiveText-1K dataset that turns FAIRHiveFrames-1K dataset into interchangeable facts and text interpretation of the facts, making it easy for further LLM-based research into this area. We also propose a pathway to develop the grounded knowledge-base that will be helpful in providing interpretable and actionable reports from the facts.

Reasoning-Search-Augmented LLMs: An Operational Taxonomy, Benchmark Analysis and Controlled Evidence Synthesis
推理-搜索增强型大语言模型：操作分类、基准分析与受控证据整合
Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive emergent reasoning capabilities, yet they remain constrained by fixed model parameters and finite context windows. Even when augmented with static retrieval, they can produce statements that are unsupported by, or even contradictory to, retrieved evidence. This leads to hallucinations, difficulty incorporating up-to-date information, and failures to correctly interpret or reconcile sources. Recent work addresses these limitations by tightly coupling step-by-step reasoning with interactive search, treating retrieval as an action within an iterative agentic loop rather than a one-shot preprocessing step. This study reviews frameworks that implement such reasoning-search loops by interleaving planning, querying, retrieval, integration, verification, and stopping. We analyze these systems through four architectural axes: retrieval coupling, controller design, knowledge source, and grounding strictness. We separately treat the learning signal as a cross-cutting dimension because prompting, supervision, preference optimization, and reinforcement learning can instantiate the same architecture while producing materially different search, stopping, and grounding behavior. We further distinguish benchmark difficulty from RSA-loop demand and synthesize controlled within-study ablations and quality-cost deltas, avoiding invalid pooling across heterogeneous model backbones, retrievers, corpora, and evaluation protocols. Our goal is to clarify the landscape of reasoning-search-augmented LLMs and outline a unified perspective on the design choices shaping future research.

大语言模型（LLMs）展现出令人印象深刻的涌现推理能力，但仍受限于固定的模型参数和有限的上下文窗口。即使通过静态检索增强，它们仍可能生成与检索证据不符甚至矛盾的陈述，导致幻觉、难以融入最新信息，以及正确解读或调和来源的失败。近期工作通过将逐步推理与交互式搜索紧密结合，解决这些问题，将检索视为迭代智能体循环中的一个动作，而非一次性预处理步骤。本研究综述了实现此类推理-搜索循环的框架，其通过规划、查询、检索、整合、验证和停止等步骤的交错执行来运作。我们从四个架构维度分析这些系统：检索耦合方式、控制器设计、知识源和接地严格性。我们还将学习信号视为一个交叉维度，因为提示、监督、偏好优化和强化学习可在相同架构下产生显著不同的搜索、停止和接地行为。我们进一步区分基准难度与RSA循环需求，并合成受控的内部消融实验与质量-成本差异，避免在异构模型主干、检索器、语料库和评估协议之间无效合并。我们的目标是厘清推理-搜索增强型LLMs的研究格局，并为未来研究的设计选择提供统一视角。

TeachBack: Learning to Teach via Bidirectional Preference Optimization for On-Policy Self-Distillation
TeachBack：通过双向偏好优化实现策略内自蒸馏中的教学学习
On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) enables a language model to learn from a Teacher instantiated from the same model but given access to privileged information, such as a reference solution. We find that access to sufficient privileged information allows the Teacher to generate highly accurate trajectories; however, accuracy alone does not guarantee that these trajectories are suitable for Student learning. Direct access to a complete solution may encourage shortened, reference-conditioned derivations that omit the exploration, verification, and self-correction required to solve problems without privileged information. We introduce TeachBack, a bidirectional preference-learning framework for on-policy self-distillation that learns not only from the Teacher but also how the Teacher should teach. TeachBack shapes the Teacher's generation behavior by controlling the length of the reference-reasoning suffix injected into the prompt and by varying the prompt style. While maintaining comparable Teacher accuracy, it encourages the Teacher to explore and re-solve problems, thereby reducing its reliance on the reference solution. At each rollout refresh, the shared policy generates both a privileged Teacher trajectory and a problem-only Student trajectory. A pairwise preference objective uses privileged Teacher trajectories to improve Student learning, while the reversed preference uses the Student's behavior to shape future Teacher supervision under the privileged condition. Experiments on mathematical and logical reasoning tasks show that TeachBack consistently improves Student performance over existing OPSD methods and related baselines.
中文翻译
策略内自蒸馏（OPSD）使语言模型能够从一个教师模型中学习，该教师模型由同一模型实例生成，但可访问特权信息（如参考解法）。我们发现，充足的特权信息使教师能够生成高度准确的轨迹；然而，准确性本身并不能保证这些轨迹适合学生学习。直接访问完整解法可能促使教师生成简化的、依赖参考的推导路径，从而省略在无特权信息条件下解决问题所需的探索、验证和自我修正过程。为此，我们提出TeachBack，一种用于策略内自蒸馏的双向偏好学习框架，不仅学习教师的输出，还学习教师应该如何教学。TeachBack通过控制注入提示中的参考推理后缀长度以及调整提示风格来塑造教师的生成行为。在保持教师准确率相当的同时，鼓励教师探索并重新求解问题，从而减少对参考解法的依赖。在每次滚动刷新时，共享策略同时生成一个带有特权信息的教师轨迹和一个仅含问题的学生轨迹。成对偏好目标利用特权教师轨迹改进学生学习，而反向偏好则利用学生的行为主动塑造未来在特权条件下的教师监督。在数学和逻辑推理任务上的实验表明，TeachBack在学生性能上持续优于现有OPSD方法及相关基线。

VeeReview: Measuring Build-Verify Pairs in Agent-Authored Pull Requests from a V-Model Perspective
VeeReview：从V模型视角测量智能体撰写的拉取请求中的构建-验证配对
Context: Reviewing a pull request requires verification evidence that addresses the proposed change. Coding agent benchmarks and studies of pull request outcomes do not directly assess this relationship. Objective: We test whether the V-Model's build and verify pairs can measure verification evidence in pull requests and support comparisons between agent-authored and human-authored changes. Method: VeeReview classifies four build and verify pairs using repository signals and a language-model judge. Three pairs use model judgements constrained by deterministic signals, and a rule over the diff and repository tree decides Module Design-Unit Test. We evaluated VeeReview against a 25-pull request reference set labelled by two raters and applied it to 1,454 matched pairs of agent and human pull requests from 741 repositories. Results: VeeReview assigned lower build-only rates to agent-authored pull requests on all four pairs, contrary to the preregistered hypothesis (mixed-effects odds ratios 0.52 to 0.86). Architecture-Integration was not significant. Standardization for linked issues, shipped tests and size reduced matched-rate differences by 34% to 60%, and the rule-decided pair's standardized interval included zero. The instrument did not meet its preregistered calibration criterion: agreement with adjudicated labels was 4% to 60% against a required 80%, and inter-rater agreement was weak (kappa = 0.154). Conclusions: The rule-decided pair shows that a process measure judged by a language model partly reflects how a pull request is presented rather than the verification work behind it. We report the cohort differences as observations made with this instrument, and we argue that such measures need a mechanically decided component and a validated human reference before they can support comparisons of verification practice.

背景：审查拉取请求需要能说明所提变更的验证证据。现有的代码智能体基准测试和拉取请求结果研究并未直接评估这一关系。目标：我们检验V模型中的构建与验证配对是否可用于衡量拉取请求中的验证证据，并支持智能体撰写与人工撰写变更之间的比较。方法：VeeReview使用仓库信号和语言模型裁判对四类构建-验证配对进行分类。其中三类配对使用受确定性信号约束的语言模型判断，而第四个配对（模块设计-单元测试）由diff和仓库树结构的规则决定。我们在由两名评审员标注的25个拉取请求参考集上评估VeeReview，并将其应用于来自741个仓库的1,454对匹配的智能体与人工拉取请求。结果：VeeReview在所有四类配对中均对智能体撰写的拉取请求分配了更低的仅构建率，与预先注册的假设相反（混合效应优势比为0.52至0.86）。架构-集成配对不显著。对关联问题、已发布测试和大小进行标准化后，匹配率差异减少了34%至60%，且规则决定配对的标准化区间包含零。该工具未达到预先注册的校准标准：与裁定标签的一致性仅为4%至60%（要求80%），且评审者间一致性较弱（kappa = 0.154）。结论：规则决定的配对表明，由语言模型判断的过程指标部分反映的是拉取请求的呈现方式，而非其背后的验证工作。我们报告了使用该工具观察到的群体差异，并认为此类度量需包含机械决定组件和经验证的人类参考，才能支持对验证实践的比较。
