Combining Long-Term Memory and LLMs Comes at a High Cost: From Prompt Injection to Persistent Compromise
长期记忆与大语言模型结合代价高昂：从提示注入到持久性妥协
The widespread adoption of generative artificial intelligence in recent years has brought a parallel rise in prompt injection and jailbreak attacks. By embedding malicious instructions, these exploits manipulate language models to bypass intended behavior and operational boundaries. In traditional chatbots, such attacks are confined to a single session because state memory is not preserved across conversations. However, persistent memory features in modern conversational interfaces enable cross-session attack propagation, establishing a threat vector known as memory injection. This work explores this emerging vulnerability in leading LLM-as-a-service platforms, highlighting its real-world severity and the need for countermeasures. While existing literature focuses on violations tailored to specific platform features, we present a general threat model. We demonstrate that all NIST attack goals are collectively achievable across ChatGPT Instant 5.3, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Gemini 3 Flash under this model. We also show that memory injections can exploit multiple attack vectors, including personal memories, projects, skills, organization instructions, and Model Context Protocol (MCP)-connected resources. Our findings reveal that all evaluated services can be manipulated to persistently spread disinformation, generate harmful content, or simulate offline unresponsiveness, even in fresh sessions. In the particular case of Claude, shared-memory features allow an attacker to compromise multiple users within an organization and also exfiltrate user prompts. To address these vulnerabilities, we derive defense mechanisms from our proposed threat model and foundational cybersecurity principles. Our analysis indicates that nearly half of these mitigations remain unimplemented across the target services. Datasets and attack artifacts are available at https://github.com/PedroBarcha/Memory-injection.
中文翻译
近年来生成式人工智能的广泛应用带来了提示注入和越狱攻击的同步上升。通过嵌入恶意指令，这些攻击操纵语言模型绕过预期行为和操作边界。在传统聊天机器人中，此类攻击局限于单次会话，因为状态记忆不会跨对话保留。然而，现代对话界面中的持久记忆功能使得攻击可在会话间传播，形成一种称为记忆注入的威胁向量。本文探讨了主流LLM即服务平台中这一新兴漏洞，强调其现实严重性及应对措施的必要性。现有文献多聚焦于针对特定平台特性的攻击，我们提出一个通用威胁模型。我们证明，在该模型下，所有NIST攻击目标均可在ChatGPT Instant 5.3、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Gemini 3 Flash上共同实现。我们还表明，记忆注入可利用多种攻击向量，包括个人记忆、项目、技能、组织指令以及Model Context Protocol（MCP）连接资源。研究发现，所有被评估的服务均可能被操控以持久传播虚假信息、生成有害内容或模拟离线不响应，即使在全新会话中也是如此。特别是对于Claude，共享记忆功能允许攻击者在一个组织内影响多个用户，并窃取用户提示。为应对这些漏洞，我们基于所提出的威胁模型和基础网络安全原则推导出防御机制。分析表明，近一半的缓解措施在目标服务中仍未实现。数据集和攻击工件可在https://github.com/PedroBarcha/Memory-injection获取。

TAGR: Task-Adaptive Geo-Semantic Retrieval across Heterogeneous Urban Delivery Tasks
TAGR：跨异构城市配送任务的任务自适应地理语义检索
As urban residents demand higher-quality instant delivery services, demand forecasting, estimated time of arrival prediction, and next-stop prediction have become critical tasks in urban delivery systems. However, the three tasks rely on historical evidence with substantially different structures and granularities. Meanwhile, their prediction targets differ in output form, making it difficult to integrate retrieved evidence through a single fusion mechanism without causing mismatch or negative transfer. These issues can increase prediction errors and subsequently lead to imbalanced resource allocation, where some regions experience unmet demand. In contrast, others suffer from underutilization, ultimately degrading overall efficiency and service quality. To address these challenges, we propose TAGR, a Task-Adaptive Geo-Semantic Retrieval framework across heterogeneous urban delivery tasks. TAGR constructs an LLM-based geo-semantic shared prior and task-specific retrieval banks to share regional knowledge while preserving heterogeneous task-specific evidence. The LLM-derived regional representations encode geographic context and semantic relations among regions, providing transferable knowledge for all three tasks without requiring online LLM inference. It further introduces a task-adaptive retrieval augmentation module to accommodate heterogeneous output structures, thereby mitigating information mismatch and negative transfer caused by a uniform retrieval-fusion strategy. Experiments on three city datasets demonstrate that TAGR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across multiple tasks.

随着城市居民对高质量即时配送服务的需求上升，需求预测、预计到达时间预测和下一停靠点预测已成为城市配送系统中的关键任务。然而，这三个任务依赖的历史证据具有显著不同的结构和粒度。同时，它们的预测目标在输出形式上也存在差异，使得仅通过单一融合机制整合检索证据变得困难，容易引发不匹配或负迁移。这些问题会增加预测误差，进而导致资源配置失衡，某些区域出现未满足需求，而其他区域则面临资源闲置，最终降低整体效率和服务质量。为应对这些挑战，我们提出TAGR，一种跨异构城市配送任务的任务自适应地理语义检索框架。TAGR构建基于大语言模型的地理语义共享先验和任务特定的检索库，以共享区域知识的同时保留异构任务特有的证据。由大语言模型生成的区域表征编码了地理上下文和区域间的语义关系，为所有三项任务提供可迁移的知识，且无需在线大语言模型推理。此外，引入任务自适应检索增强模块以适配异构输出结构，从而缓解统一检索-融合策略引起的资讯不匹配和负迁移。在三个城市数据集上的实验表明，TAGR在多个任务上持续优于现有最先进基线。

LongZero: Enhancing Long-Context Reasoning in LLMs from Zero Data
LongZero：从零数据增强大语言模型的长上下文推理能力
Reinforcement learning (RL) is an effective way to strengthen the long-context reasoning of large language models (LLMs), but it needs training data that are long, diverse, challenging, and verifiable. Human annotation of such data is expensive, naturally occurring data are too scarce to sustain RL, and existing synthesis methods depend on external corpora or stronger teacher models, which become harder to obtain as the trained models get stronger. Recently, RL from zero data, in which a model learns from tasks it proposes itself, has achieved excellent performance, but existing methods target short-context tasks. Therefore, we propose $\textbf{LongZero}$, a method that lets an LLM improve its own long-context reasoning without any external data. Instead of writing questions and answers over existing documents, LongZero first has the model synthesize the question and answer with its supporting evidence in a compact structured form, and only then separately expands the evidence into a long multi-document context. LongZero combines these self-written multi-hop reasoning tasks with self-written multi-round retrieval tasks and trains the model with GRPO. We evaluate LongZero on two LLMs across five long-context reasoning benchmarks and compare it with RL on data synthesized from external corpora. Experimental results show that LongZero can generalize to real tasks, and outperforms both baselines, suggesting a viable and promising path to improving long-context reasoning of LLMs.
中文翻译
强化学习（RL）是提升大语言模型（LLMs）长上下文推理能力的有效方法，但需要长、多样、具有挑战性且可验证的训练数据。人工标注此类数据成本高昂，自然生成的数据过于稀少，无法持续支持RL训练；而现有的合成方法依赖外部语料库或更强的教师模型，随着被训练模型能力的提升，这些资源也愈发难以获取。最近，基于零数据的强化学习方法让模型通过自我提出任务进行学习，取得了优异性能，但现有方法主要针对短上下文任务。因此，我们提出了$ extbf{LongZero}$，一种无需任何外部数据即可让LLM自主提升其长上下文推理能力的方法。LongZero不直接在已有文档上编写问题和答案，而是先将问题、答案及其支持证据以紧凑的结构化形式合成，再单独将证据扩展为长篇多文档上下文。LongZero结合自动生成的多跳推理任务与多轮检索任务，并使用GRPO进行模型训练。我们在两个LLM上对五个长上下文推理基准进行了评估，并与基于外部语料库合成数据的RL方法进行对比。实验结果表明，LongZero能够泛化到真实任务，且优于两种基线方法，显示出改善LLM长上下文推理能力的一条可行且有前景的路径。

Environment Duels: A Microcosm of LLM Co-Evolution
环境对决：大语言模型共演化的一个微观世界
Through a tangle of real-world interactions, from synthetic training pipelines to data shared on the open web, frontier LLMs absorb what their predecessors and competitors create. How well does each model play its role in this ecosystem, as a creator and as a solver? We introduce Environment Duels, a dynamic agentic benchmark that evaluates both capabilities on interactive environments. Each model writes environments, each with a privileged hint, aiming for tasks that capable solvers fail without the hint and pass with it. Across nine frontier LLMs, solving and authoring prove to be distinct skills: the strongest solvers are not the strongest authors, while smaller models learn more from how others played and write the hardest environments and the most useful hints. Beyond the scores, the pairwise interactions form a microcosm of how models affect one another. Head-to-head outcomes are transitive and so yield a stable ranking; independent authors converge on the same designs; and in a reinforcement-learning study with Qwen3.8-27B, the learner's hint lift on an author's environments tracks how much training on those environments improves it on held-out reasoning and agentic benchmarks, whether the environments are its own or another model's. Each of these patterns has a counterpart in how models shape one another outside the duel. By staging these interactions under controlled conditions, Environment Duels offers a window onto how LLMs teach, challenge and learn from one another. Code is available at https://github.com/LLM-Duels/EnvDuels.
中文翻译
通过复杂的现实交互，包括合成训练流程以及开放网络上的数据共享，前沿大语言模型吸收了前代模型和竞争对手所创造的内容。每个模型在这个生态系统中作为创造者和求解者，表现如何？我们引入了“环境对决”（Environment Duels），一种动态的智能体基准测试，用于评估模型在交互环境中的双重能力。每个模型自行编写环境，每个环境包含一个特权提示，目标是设计出只有具备强大求解能力的模型才能在获得提示后成功完成，而在没有提示时则失败的任务。在九个前沿大语言模型中，求解能力和创作能力被证明是两种不同的技能：最强的求解者并非最强的创作者；较小的模型更善于从他人行为中学习，并能编写最难的环境和最有用的提示。除了得分之外，成对交互形成了一种微型生态系统，展示了模型之间的相互影响。对决结果具有传递性，从而产生稳定的排名；独立作者会趋同于相同的设计；在对Qwen3.8-27B的强化学习研究中，学习者在作者环境上的提示增益，与该模型在未见推理和智能体基准上的性能提升高度相关，无论这些环境是自己生成还是其他模型生成的。这些模式在对决之外的模型相互塑造过程中均有对应。通过在受控条件下模拟这些互动，环境对决为我们提供了观察大语言模型如何相互教学、挑战与学习的窗口。代码已公开于 https://github.com/LLM-Duels/EnvDuels。

Quant-Arena: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Quantitative Trading Research
Quant-Arena：面向量化交易研究的大语言模型智能体基准测试
Quantitative research is an iterative loop: a researcher proposes a market hypothesis, implements it as an executable signal, tests it on historical data, and decides what to keep. Existing benchmarks score formulas, code, or individual trading decisions, leaving open whether large language models (LLMs) can complete this loop and produce strategies that work beyond the research period. We introduce Quant-Arena, a benchmark and public leaderboard for end-to-end quantitative research on real A-share market data. A model constructs features and stock-scoring code, receives feedback only on 2022--2023, and freezes one strategy for assessment on the sealed year 2024. A cash-account evaluator applies trading constraints and costs, including market impact estimated from a simulated order book. Scores are dominated by realized excess return and complemented by signal quality and the diversity of features used by the strategy. We evaluate nine frontier models, among which GPT-6 Astra scores highest; yet no model beats the equal-weight market in 2024. Quant-Arena is open to submissions from any model or agent harness.
中文翻译
量化研究是一个迭代循环：研究人员提出市场假设，将其转化为可执行信号，用历史数据测试，并决定保留哪些内容。现有基准仅评估公式、代码或单个交易决策，未能回答大语言模型是否能完整完成这一循环并生成在研究期后仍有效的策略。我们提出了Quant-Arena，一个面向真实A股市场数据的端到端量化研究基准及公开排行榜。模型需构建特征和股票评分代码，仅在2022–2023年接收反馈，并冻结一个策略用于对密封的2024年数据进行评估。一个现金账户评估器施加交易约束和成本，包括基于模拟订单簿估算的市场冲击。评分主要由实际超额收益主导，并辅以信号质量及策略所用特征的多样性。我们评估了九个前沿模型，其中GPT-6 Astra得分最高；然而，没有任何模型在2024年超越等权重市场。Quant-Arena对所有模型或智能体开放提交。
👤 Houde Qian, Zheng Jiang, Wenyi Li, Chenxi Xu, Xinjie Lin et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

agentic-rl
Looped Uniform Diffusion Language Models
循环均匀扩散语言模型
Uniform diffusion language models (UDLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising sequences of ordinary vocabulary tokens, any of which may be corrupted and subsequently revised. During training, UDLMs must denoise from a context that may itself be unreliable, whereas masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) always condition on a clean context. This challenging training objective makes UDLMs more FLOP-intensive to train than MDLMs, but it yields stronger downstream reasoning performance. Recently, selective looping, which recursively applies a few early Transformer layers within each denoising step, has been shown to improve training efficiency in MDLMs under an iso-FLOP budget. We investigate looping in UDLMs while keeping the training objective and sampler unchanged. We find that looping significantly improves training efficiency: looped UDLMs reach the validation NLL of a non-looped UDLM trained on about 0.5 trillion tokens with up to 2.8x fewer training FLOPs on OpenWebText and 3.3x fewer on FineWeb-Edu. We also find that, at a matched NFE budget, looped UDLMs reach up to 9.8% lower generation perplexity than the non-looped UDLM, with most gains achieved at moderate loop counts. Finally, we study different loop-count distributions and find that sampling smaller loop counts more frequently during training improves inference efficiency: on downstream tasks, this strategy uses 12% fewer loops than uniform loop-count sampling without degrading accuracy.
中文翻译
均匀扩散语言模型（UDLM）通过迭代去噪普通词汇标记序列来生成文本，其中任何标记都可能被破坏并随后修正。在训练过程中，UDLM必须从可能不可靠的上下文中进行去噪，而掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）始终基于干净的上下文进行条件建模。这种具有挑战性的训练目标使得UDLM比MDLM需要更多的计算量（FLOPs），但能带来更强的下游推理性能。最近的研究表明，在保持相同FLOP预算的前提下，选择性循环机制——即在每个去噪步骤中递归应用少量早期Transformer层——可以提升MDLM的训练效率。本文研究了在保持训练目标和采样器不变的情况下，UDLM中的循环机制。我们发现，循环显著提升了训练效率：在OpenWebText上，循环UDLM仅需非循环UDLM训练约0.5万亿token所用FLOPs的2.8倍以下，而在FineWeb-Edu上则只需3.3倍以下。此外，在匹配的NFE预算下，循环UDLM的生成困惑度比非循环UDLM低最多9.8%，且大部分性能提升出现在中等循环次数时。最后，我们研究了不同的循环次数分布策略，发现训练期间更频繁地采样较小的循环次数可提高推理效率：在下游任务中，该策略比均匀采样循环次数减少12%的循环次数，同时不降低准确性。
👤 Saiteja Utpala, Chieh-Hsin Lai, Yuki Mitsufuji, Praneeth Vepakomma 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Remembering Through Change: Provenance-Preserving Hypergraph Memory for Multimodal Agents
通过变化记住：用于多模态智能体的溯源保持超图记忆
Long-term memory for multimodal agents remains challenging, as it must preserve fine-grained visual evidence while tracking entity evolution across sessions. Existing abstraction-based memories compress images into text, often discarding visually discriminative details, whereas pixel-preserving memories retain raw observations but struggle to reliably associate diverse observations with persistent entity identities and track their evolving states. To address these limitations, we propose MHG-Mem, a multimodal hypergraph memory that jointly organizes entity identity, state evolution, and provenance to visual evidence. Specifically, MHG-Mem represents distinct entity mentions and their observed facts as individual nodes, grouping identity-consistent mentions and facts through occurrence hyperedges. It then synthesizes aspect-specific evolution states from these grouped observations and links them to supporting entities, facts, and source evidence through evolutionary and evidential hyperedges. To navigate this structured memory, we introduce hypergraph-based agentic search, which adaptively selects an entry layer, traverses typed hyperedges across memory levels, and inspects source observations when fine-grained verification is required. These designs support both detailed visual verification and reasoning over cross-session state changes. Extensive experiments across three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of MHG-Mem, improving performance by 8.20-9.98% over the strongest baseline.
中文翻译
多模态智能体的长期记忆仍然面临挑战，因为它必须在跨会话中保留细粒度视觉证据的同时追踪实体的演化。现有的基于抽象的记忆将图像压缩为文本，常丢弃具有视觉区分性的细节；而像素保持型记忆虽保留原始观测，却难以可靠地将多样化的观测与持久的实体身份关联，并跟踪其状态演变。为解决这些局限，我们提出了MHG-Mem，一种多模态超图记忆结构，它联合组织实体身份、状态演化和视觉证据的溯源信息。具体而言，MHG-Mem将不同的实体提及及其观察事实表示为独立节点，通过出现超边将身份一致的提及和事实分组。然后，从这些分组的观测中合成特定方面的演化状态，并通过演化超边和证据超边将其与支持的实体、事实和源证据相连接。为导航此结构化记忆，我们引入基于超图的智能体搜索机制，该机制自适应选择入口层，跨记忆层级遍历类型化超边，并在需要细粒度验证时检查源观测。这些设计支持详细的视觉验证以及跨会话状态变化的推理。在三个基准上的大量实验表明，MHG-Mem相比最强基线性能提升8.20%-9.98%。

Graph Topology Meets Energy-Driven Pathfinding: Preserving Contextual Consistency in Retrieval-Augmented Generation
图拓扑遇上能量驱动路径规划：在检索增强生成中保持上下文一致性
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) frequently integrates knowledge graphs (KGs) for complex multi-hop question answering. However, their performance is increasingly bottlenecked by the knowledge structuring and reasoning processes. (i) Severe semantic loss: Distilling continuous texts into discrete symbols strips away contextual nuances and narrative coherence. (ii) Pronounced modality mismatch: Fragmented graph structures clash with the continuous texts LLMs are pre-trained on, while rewriting them into natural language introduces generative noise and omits information. To address these limitations, we propose ThreadRAG, a semantic-topology-based RAG framework for context-preserving knowledge structuring and modality-native evidence reasoning. ThreadRAG introduces a Semantic Topology Mapper (STM), which organizes retrieved documents into context-intact Semantic Nodes and constructs a Semantic Topology Graph (STG) through narrative, semantic, and lexical edges. It further incorporates Energy-Driven Pathfinding (EDP), which identifies query-relevant subgraphs via energy diffusion and deterministically linearizes them into coherent textual evidence paths. Experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that ThreadRAG consistently outperforms strong baselines. Notably, it achieves a 14.4% EM improvement over text-centric methods on HotpotQA and surpasses the best baseline by 5.8% EM on 2WikiMultihop, while reducing token cost to 23.3% of the strongest baseline.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）常结合知识图谱（KG）以应对复杂的多跳问答任务。然而，其性能日益受限于知识结构化与推理过程。（i）严重的语义损失：将连续文本提炼为离散符号会丢失上下文细微差别和叙事连贯性。（ii）明显的模态不匹配：碎片化的图结构与大语言模型预训练所依赖的连续文本不兼容，而将其重写为自然语言又引入生成噪声并遗漏信息。为解决这些局限，我们提出ThreadRAG，一种基于语义拓扑的RAG框架，用于保持上下文一致性的知识结构化与模态原生的证据推理。ThreadRAG引入语义拓扑映射器（STM），将检索到的文档组织为上下文完整的语义节点，并通过叙事、语义和词汇边构建语义拓扑图（STG）。同时引入能量驱动路径规划（EDP），通过能量扩散识别与查询相关的子图，并确定性地将其线性化为连贯的文本证据路径。在三个多跳问答基准上的实验表明，ThreadRAG始终优于强基线。尤其在HotpotQA上相比文本中心方法提升14.4% EM，在2WikiMultihop上超越最佳基线5.8% EM，同时将token成本降低至最强基线的23.3%。

Task-Preserving Multilingual Instruction Tuning for Open-Ended QA in WMT 2026 MIST
面向WMT 2026 MIST开放式问答任务的多语言指令微调
This paper describes our submission to the WMT 2026 Multilingual Instruction Shared Task. We adapt Qwen3-8B with LoRA using organiser-provided data, EuroAlpaca, a multilingual instruction dataset created through task-preserving localisation, and CrossEuroAlpaca, a cross-lingual augmentation that assigns different source and target languages to instructions, contexts and responses. Our system targets open-ended question answering (QA-OEG) in ten shared-task languages represented in the auxiliary data. Under matched decoding, adaptation improves the mean Gemma-4 judge score on target-language QA-OEG by 7.8% relative to the base model. On an additional evaluation using the Aya Evaluation Suite across all shared-task languages, it yields relative gains of 41.5% in macro-averaged ROUGE-L and 2.9% in BERTScore-F1, with larger improvements in target languages. However, these gains are task- and language-specific. Gemma-4 scores improve for QA-OEG and summarisation in the target languages but decline for context-grounded QA there and across all three tasks in the non-target languages.
中文翻译
本文介绍了我们对WMT 2026多语言指令共享任务的提交方案。我们采用LoRA方法对Qwen3-8B进行适配，使用组织方提供的数据EuroAlpaca——一个通过任务保真本地化创建的多语言指令数据集，以及CrossEuroAlpaca——一种跨语言增强方法，为指令、上下文和响应分配不同的源语言和目标语言。我们的系统针对辅助数据中涵盖的十种共享任务语言的开放式问答（QA-OEG）任务。在匹配解码条件下，相较于基础模型，该系统在目标语言的QA-OEG任务上使Gemma-4裁判评分平均提升了7.8%。在额外使用Aya评估套件对所有共享任务语言进行评估时，其宏平均ROUGE-L相对提升41.5%，BERTScore-F1提升2.9%，且在目标语言中提升更显著。然而，这些增益具有任务和语言特异性：Gemma-4在目标语言的QA-OEG和摘要任务中得分提升，但在目标语言的上下文依赖问答任务中下降，且在非目标语言中所有三项任务均出现下降。

Benchmarking Large Language Models for Detecting Satirical Code-Mixed Sentences
检测讽刺性混合语句的大型语言模型基准测试
Code-switching is a natural linguistic phenomenon, yet its impact on high-level reasoning tasks remains largely unexplored in under-resourced languages like Romanian. Natural language processing tasks, such as satire detection, rely heavily on nuanced cultural and syntactic cues that large language models struggle to capture. In this paper, we investigate four methods (i.e., random replacement, phrase translation, dependency parsing, and large language models) for generating synthetic code-mixed Romanian sentences with English, German, French, and Italian. We start with an existing dataset for satire detection, compiled from Romanian news articles and annotated at the sentence level by humans, and augment it with over 600k samples, namely CS-SeLeRoSa. We provide an in-depth analysis of the dataset generation process. Then, we evaluate several multilingual large language models across zero-shot, chain-of-thought, and fine-tuning regimes on both the synthetic corpus and a human-adjudicated gold benchmark, namely CS-SeLeRoSa-Gold. While older open models suffer substantial degradation (dropping to 37%-55% macro-F1), newer models like Gemma 4 31B achieve 68.1% zero-shot, rivaling frontier models like GPT-5 mini (69.5%). Furthermore, fine-tuning significantly reduces formatting errors and improves the results, increasing Gemma 4 31B to 77.5% on CS-SeLeRoSa and 72.6% on the gold standard, outperforming frontier zero-shot baselines.
中文翻译
代码切换是一种自然的语言现象，但其对资源匮乏语言（如罗马尼亚语）中高级推理任务的影响仍鲜有研究。自然语言处理任务（如讽刺检测）高度依赖微妙的文化和句法线索，而大型语言模型难以捕捉这些特征。本文研究了四种生成合成混合语罗马尼亚语句子的方法（即随机替换、短语翻译、依存句法分析和大型语言模型），混合英语、德语、法语和意大利语。我们以现有讽刺检测数据集为基础（源自罗马尼亚新闻文章，经人工逐句标注），并扩充超过60万样本，形成CS-SeLeRoSa数据集。我们提供了数据集生成过程的深入分析。随后，我们在合成语料库和人工裁定的黄金基准CS-SeLeRoSa-Gold上，评估多个多语言大型语言模型在零样本、思维链和微调三种范式下的表现。较旧的开源模型性能大幅下降（宏F1降至37%-55%），而新模型如Gemma 4 31B在零样本下达到68.1%，接近前沿模型GPT-5 mini（69.5%）。此外，微调显著减少了格式错误并提升了结果，使Gemma 4 31B在CS-SeLeRoSa上达到77.5%，在黄金标准上达到72.6%，优于前沿零样本基线。

MAPS: Macro-Adaptive Parallel Reasoning for Regulating Search Momentum in Retrieval-Augmented Generation
MAPS：用于调控检索增强生成中搜索动量的宏观自适应并行推理
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) by retrieving external knowledge. Recently, some RAG methods interleave reasoning and retrieval, enabling LLMs to progressively generate sub-queries and retrieve external knowledge. However, these methods may suffer from redundant or ineffective sub-queries and insufficiently diverse query exploration. Moreover, due to the tightly interleaved nature of reasoning and retrieval, it remains unclear how the evolving reasoning process influences query intent. To characterize the evolution of query intent during reasoning, we introduce search momentum, which captures the accumulated retrieval tendency induced by historical changes in generated sub-queries along the reasoning trajectory. Through momentum-based analysis, we find that the query generation process tends to continuously evolve along existing retrieval trajectories. Based on this observation, we propose MAPS, a Macro-Adaptive Parallel Reasoning framework for regulating search momentum and mitigating query-intent drift. MAPS first retains the retrieved knowledge and a refined reasoning state, thereby reducing the influence of historical reasoning trajectories on subsequent sub-query generation. Subsequently, MAPS extends the conventional sequential reasoning process into a parallel reasoning paradigm. At each iteration, MAPS identifies complementary retrieval directions, generates corresponding sub-queries for parallel retrieval, and consolidates the retrieved evidence to update the knowledge and reasoning states. Experiments on six question-answering benchmarks demonstrate that MAPS consistently outperforms representative RAG methods across different backbone models. All code and resources are available at https://github.com/NEUIR/MAPS.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）通过检索外部知识来提升大型语言模型（LLMs）的性能。最近一些RAG方法将推理与检索交错进行，使LLMs能够逐步生成子查询并检索外部知识。然而，这些方法可能面临冗余或无效子查询以及查询探索不足的问题。此外，由于推理与检索紧密交织，推理过程的演变如何影响查询意图仍不清晰。为刻画推理过程中查询意图的演化，我们引入“搜索动量”概念，捕捉由推理轨迹上生成子查询的历史变化所引发的累积检索倾向。基于动量分析，我们发现查询生成过程倾向于沿着既有的检索轨迹持续演化。基于此观察，我们提出MAPS——一种用于调控搜索动量并缓解查询意图漂移的宏观自适应并行推理框架。MAPS首先保留已检索的知识和精炼的推理状态，从而降低历史推理轨迹对后续子查询生成的影响。随后，MAPS将传统的顺序推理过程扩展为并行推理范式。在每一轮迭代中，MAPS识别互补的检索方向，生成相应的子查询进行并行检索，并整合检索到的证据以更新知识和推理状态。在六个问答基准上的实验表明，MAPS在不同主干模型下均持续优于代表性RAG方法。所有代码和资源已在 https://github.com/NEUIR/MAPS 公开。
👤 Xinze Li, Dingwen Zhang, Zhenghao Liu, Yukun Yan, Shuo Wang et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Where Do LLMs Learn to Recommend AI?
大语言模型从何处学会推荐人工智能？
Asked what to study, which career to pursue, or what product to build, large language models (LLMs) often recommend AI-related options. We ask where this tendency comes from by comparing models before and after fine-tuning and by varying how questions are asked. First, pretrained models already include explicit AI/ML terms in the recommendations of 27% of their responses, and their released instruction-tuned versions do so in 59%. Second, fine-tuning models ourselves shows that this rise is not inevitable: general instruction tuning leaves the rate unchanged, but removing the 7% of instruction examples that concern AI cuts it by 14 percentage points, below the level of the pretrained models it starts from. Data rewarding helpfulness raise it by 9 points, while data ranked by the preferences of diverse simulated users lower it by 9. Finally, with models held fixed, asking for the "best" option or for what to do "right now" raises the rate by 8 and 18 points, whereas telling the model it is an AI advisor adds 4. Overall, the tendency starts in pretraining, and what models learn afterward, together with how a question is phrased, decides how strongly it appears.
中文翻译
当被问及该学习什么、选择何种职业或开发何种产品时，大型语言模型（LLMs）常常推荐与人工智能相关的选择。我们通过比较微调前后模型的表现以及改变提问方式，探究这种倾向的来源。首先，预训练模型在27%的回答中已明确包含AI/ML术语，而其发布的指令微调版本则在59%的回答中如此。其次，我们自行微调模型发现，这种上升趋势并非不可避免：通用指令微调使比例保持不变，但移除涉及AI的7%指令示例可使其下降14个百分点，低于原始预训练模型水平。奖励有用性的数据使比例上升9个百分点，而基于多样化模拟用户偏好的数据则使其下降9个百分点。最后，在模型固定的情况下，要求选择“最佳”选项或“现在”应采取的行动会使比例分别上升8和18个百分点，而告知模型其为AI顾问则增加4个百分点。总体而言，这种倾向始于预训练阶段，后续学习内容与问题表述方式共同决定了其强度。
👤 Benaya Trabelsi, Jonathan Shaki, Sarit Kraus 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Evolving with Open Worlds: Towards Recursive Self-Improvement for Future Forecasting
与开放世界共进化：迈向未来预测的递归自我改进
Most contemporary artificial intelligence (AI) systems are released with frozen parameters and improve through external updates, whereas an ideal system would keep improving from its own experience, a capacity we call recursive self-improvement (RSI). Yet studying RSI requires an environment that is continuously changing, objectively verifiable, and naturally grounded, posing a fundamental challenge for agent evolution. Here we introduce DeepEvolve, a sustainable evolution agent system grounded in future event prediction, in which agents continuously interact with a dynamically evolving environment and improve by internalizing and externalizing the experience gained through these interactions. A living environment synthesizes verifiable tasks, and a recursive-evolution engine turns the reasoning trajectory behind each prediction back into capability: decoupling search from prediction adapts parameter-level evolution to the contamination-prone live environment, while distilling reusable skills lets the agent evolve from the limited live trajectories such an environment affords. On the external FutureX leaderboard, which evaluates predictions against real-world outcomes and is updated weekly, our self-improved forecaster ranked first across all systems over three consecutive weeks, achieving up to a 195% improvement in rating. On our synthesized evaluation, it ranked among the top three across four base LLMs, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 10.7 Bradley--Terry points. More broadly, these results suggest that a system built for self-improvement is best understood as an agent that co-evolves with a genuinely living, open-ended environment, pointing to a shift in AI from training static models on fixed data toward cultivating agents that grow stronger from their own experience, with such an open world as the indispensable basis of that growth.
中文翻译
当前大多数人工智能系统以参数冻结的形式发布，并通过外部更新进行改进，而理想系统应能从自身经验中持续改进，我们称之为递归自我改进（RSI）。然而，研究RSI需要一个持续变化、客观可验证且自然扎根的环境，这对智能体演化构成根本挑战。本文提出DeepEvolve——一个基于未来事件预测的可持续演化智能体系统，其中智能体持续与动态演化的环境互动，并通过内化和外化这些互动所获得的经验实现改进。一个活态环境生成可验证的任务，而递归演化引擎将每次预测背后的推理轨迹转化为能力：解耦搜索与预测，使参数级演化适应易受污染的实时环境；同时提炼可复用技能，使智能体能够从此类环境提供的有限实时轨迹中演化。在每周更新的外部FutureX排行榜上，我们的自改进预测器连续三周排名第一，评分最高提升达195%。在合成评估中，它在四个基础LLM上均位列前三，相比最强基线最高提升10.7 Bradley-Terry分。更广泛地，这些结果表明，一个为自我改进设计的系统最好理解为与真正活态、开放无界的环境共同进化的智能体，标志着AI正从在固定数据上训练静态模型，转向培育能从自身经验中不断成长的智能体，而开放世界是这一成长不可或缺的基础。

Bridging Foundational and Agentic Capabilities of LLMs
弥合大语言模型的基础能力与代理能力之间的鸿沟
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as agents, yet it remains unclear how their foundational capabilities translate into agentic capabilities. End-to-end scores leave unresolved whether low benchmark capability or weak deployment drives a failure, limiting actionable diagnosis. We bridge foundational and agentic capabilities through an operation-level measurement framework. We represent each model with a foundational profile derived from capability benchmarks and an operation profile derived from interaction trajectories, and define capability realization gaps as deviations of observed operation performance from predictions based on foundational capabilities. Experiments on fifteen model services in $τ^2$-Bench show that foundational profiles only partially predict agentic operations. Models with comparable profiles can differ substantially in execution, and these gaps remain consistent across task subsets. Operation profiles add information and substantially improve task-score prediction. The framework locates execution departures at the operation level to inform model development and agent evaluation. We will release our code and analysis artifacts.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被部署为智能体，但其基础能力如何转化为代理能力仍不清晰。端到端评分无法区分是基础能力不足还是部署薄弱导致失败，限制了可操作的诊断。我们通过操作层面的测量框架，弥合基础能力与代理能力之间的差距。我们将每个模型表示为基于能力基准的基础能力画像，以及基于交互轨迹的操作能力画像，并将能力实现差距定义为实际操作表现与基于基础能力预测值之间的偏差。在$τ^2$-Bench的十五个模型服务上的实验表明，基础画像只能部分预测代理操作。具有相似画像的模型在执行上可能差异显著，且这些差距在不同任务子集中保持一致。操作画像提供了额外信息，并显著提升了任务得分预测能力。该框架可在操作层面定位执行偏差，为模型开发与代理评估提供指导。代码与分析工具将公开发布。
👤 Wei Guo, Yuanjian Xu, Jianing Hao, Yongyao Duan, Ran Chen et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Accept or Repair: Preserving Passage-Supported Answers in Contrastive Decoding
接受或修复：在对比解码中保留有段落支持的答案
Large language models often answer from knowledge learned during training, even when a provided passage contradicts that knowledge. To address this problem, contrastive decoding contrasts the predictions under the full and a modified prompt at every token, but it also alters correct answers that the passage supports. We show that checking answers against the passage removes this trade-off. Based on this observation, we propose Accept-or-Repair (AoR), a training-free procedure. AoR keeps a sampled answer if the passage supports it. Otherwise, it tries up to two replacements with contrastive decoding, returning one as soon as it passes this check. It rejects replacements that repeat the answer generated without the passage when the passage favors another answer. Our experiments show that AoR raises the score over sampling by 12.5 points on counterfactual passages and 2.3 on ordinary ones, surpassing eight contrastive and prompting baselines on both, including when each receives the same rejection rule. Replacing only initial answers that the passage does not support improves the average score of every decoder. These results suggest that models can follow passages that contradict their knowledge while preserving accuracy on passages that agree with it. Code will be released at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/AoR.
中文翻译
大型语言模型经常根据训练中学到的知识作答，即使提供的段落与其矛盾。为解决此问题，对比解码在每个词元处对比完整提示与修改后提示下的预测，但也会改变段落支持的正确答案。我们发现，将答案与段落进行比对可消除这一权衡。基于此观察，我们提出无需训练的Accept-or-Repair（AoR）方法。AoR若采样答案被段落支持则保留；否则尝试最多两次替换，使用对比解码生成，一旦通过段落验证即返回。若替换重复了无段落支持时生成的答案，而段落支持另一答案，则拒绝该替换。实验表明，AoR在反事实段落上比采样提升12.5分，在普通段落上提升2.3分，超越八种对比解码与提示基线，包括每种均采用相同拒绝规则的情况。仅修复段落不支持的初始答案即可提升所有解码器的平均得分。结果表明，模型可以在段落与自身知识冲突时遵循段落，同时保留段落与知识一致时的准确性。代码将在https://github.com/js-lee-AI/AoR发布。
👤 Jungseob Lee, Seungyoon Lee, Hyeonseok Moon, Seongtae Hong, Suhyune Son et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29722]

Retrieval Augmented Generation Compositionality
检索增强生成的组合性
Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds large language models in external knowledge. Modern pipelines combine embedding, chunking, retrieval, reranking, query augmentation, context compression, and agentic verification, yet these techniques are typically evaluated one at a time. It remains unclear whether their isolated gains survive composition, whether retrieval quality and answer faithfulness favor the same design choices, and whether conclusions from small corpora hold at large scale. We pre-declare three hypotheses and introduce LASAGNA, an open-source framework for controlled RAG evaluation. We test 450 configurations across eight benchmarks and corpora ranging from 5K to 200K documents. Three findings emerge. First, individually strong techniques from different phases of a RAG pipeline do not necessarily compose: three retrieval techniques predict a +47% combined improvement but deliver only +17% when used together. Second, component gains stack differently for retrieval and faithfulness, and optimizing one can hurt the other. Third, the best composition changes as the corpus grows: the combination that wins on a small corpus may not remain best at large scale. These findings show that gains measured in isolation are not reliable predictors of realistic performance, and that new RAG techniques should be tested with the full surrounding stack. We call on researchers to do so and supply LASAGNA to simplify these experiments.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）将大型语言模型与外部知识相结合。现代流水线结合了嵌入、分块、检索、重排序、查询增强、上下文压缩和代理验证等技术，但这些技术通常被单独评估。目前尚不清楚它们在组合使用时的增益是否依然有效，检索质量与答案忠实度是否偏好相同的设计选择，以及小语料库得出的结论在大规模场景下是否仍然成立。我们预先声明了三个假设，并引入LASAGNA——一个用于受控RAG评估的开源框架。我们在涵盖8个基准和语料库（从5K到200K文档）的450种配置上进行了测试。研究发现三个关键点：第一，来自RAG流水线不同阶段的单独表现优异的技术并不一定具有组合效应：三种检索技术预测可带来+47%的联合提升，但实际仅实现+17%；第二，组件增益在检索和忠实度上的叠加方式不同，优化其中一个可能损害另一个；第三，最佳组合随语料库规模增长而变化：在小语料库上表现最优的组合在大规模下未必仍最优。这些发现表明，孤立测量的增益无法可靠预测真实性能，新RAG技术应在其完整堆栈环境中进行测试。我们呼吁研究人员这样做，并提供LASAGNA以简化此类实验。

Uni-Mem: Learning a Long-Horizon Agentic Memory Manager with Unified Online Policy Optimization
Uni-Mem：基于统一在线策略优化的长周期智能体记忆管理器学习
Long-term agent memory is shifting from predefined workflows toward learnable policies for memory construction and retrieval. Existing learning-based methods either optimize construction alone or combine retrieval-side online reinforcement learning (RL) with offline construction supervision, limiting co-adaptation between the two stages. In this paper, we explore end-to-end online RL for a unified memory manager (Uni-Mem) that learns both capabilities under a shared policy. However, joint optimization with existing RL algorithms such as GRPO faces confounded group comparisons and sparse feedback over long memory-management trajectories. To this end, we propose Stage-Decoupled GRPO, which alternates construction and retrieval optimization while controlling variation from the other stage within each rollout group, yielding stage-specific learning signals. We further introduce Event-Wise Credit Assignment (ECA), which localizes feedback to responsible events and propagates event-level advantages to their constituent operations. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show that Uni-Mem outperforms the strongest RL-based memory, demonstrating the effectiveness of jointly learning memory construction and retrieval via Stage-Decoupled GRPO and ECA.
中文翻译
长期智能体记忆正从预定义工作流转向可学习的记忆构建与检索策略。现有基于学习的方法要么仅优化记忆构建，要么将检索端的在线强化学习（RL）与离线构建监督结合，限制了两个阶段之间的协同适应。本文探索一种端到端的在线强化学习方法，用于统一记忆管理器（Uni-Mem），使其在共享策略下同时学习构建与检索能力。然而，使用现有RL算法（如GRPO）进行联合优化时，面临群体比较混淆和长记忆管理轨迹中反馈稀疏的问题。为此，我们提出分阶段解耦GRPO，通过交替优化构建与检索阶段，并在每次滚动更新中控制另一阶段的变量，从而获得阶段特定的学习信号。我们还引入事件级信用分配（ECA），将反馈定位至相关事件，并将事件级优势传播至其构成操作。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上的实验表明，Uni-Mem优于最强的基于RL的记忆系统，证明了通过分阶段解耦GRPO和ECA联合学习记忆构建与检索的有效性。

RCSD: Reinforcement and Continual Learning for Domain-Specific Speculative Decoding
RCSD：面向领域特定推测解码的强化与持续学习
Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by employing a compact draft model to propose candidate tokens that are verified in parallel by a target model. Conventional training relies on soft-label distillation, which forces the draft model to mimic the target's full output distribution, including high-entropy regions that degrade acceptance rates and increase early rejection risk. We propose RCSD (Reinforcement and Continual Learning for Speculative Decoding), a training framework that aligns draft model optimization with the system-level objective of maximizing acceptance length. RCSD leverages naturally occurring accepted and rejected sequences during speculative inference to construct implicit preference pairs, with masked loss to avoid penalizing target-aligned tokens. Evaluated across multiple benchmarks, RCSD improves average acceptance length by 35% over HASS and achieves 1.35x faster end-to-end inference than HASS on practical workloads, without compromising generation quality.
中文翻译
推测解码通过使用紧凑的草稿模型提出候选标记，并由目标模型并行验证，从而加速大语言模型的推理过程。传统训练依赖软标签蒸馏，迫使草稿模型模仿目标模型的完整输出分布，包括高熵区域，这会降低接受率并增加早期拒绝风险。我们提出 RCSD（用于推测解码的强化与持续学习），一种将草稿模型优化与系统级目标——最大化接受长度——对齐的训练框架。RCSD 利用推测解码过程中自然产生的接受和拒绝序列构建隐式偏好对，采用掩码损失避免惩罚与目标一致的标记。在多个基准测试中评估表明，RCSD 相较于 HASS 平均接受长度提升 35%，并在实际工作负载下实现 1.35 倍更快的端到端推理速度，且未牺牲生成质量。

A Strong MoE Is All You Need for Process Supervision
一个强大的MoE就足以实现过程监督
Process reward models (PRMs) provide fine-grained supervision for multi-step reasoning, yet obtaining reliable process labels remains a central bottleneck. Existing approaches typically rely on human annotation, rollout-based estimation, or auxiliary verification. In this work, we explore a different source of process supervision natively exposed by Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models: expert routing. We show that simple routing statistics provide an intrinsic process-confidence signal that is competitive with conventional output-logit confidence. Building on this observation, we construct routing confidence by combining overall routing mass, head routing mass, and within-step routing variation, and introduce RoCo-Rethink, which identifies the least confident reasoning step and selectively regenerates the subsequent trajectory. We further introduce RoCo-PRM, which uses a strong MoE to automatically annotate reasoning trajectories with routing-derived step scores and distills them into a standalone PRM. Across multiple MoE architectures and diverse reasoning tasks, both RoCo-Rethink and RoCo-PRM consistently outperform strong baselines, while RoCo-PRM transfers effectively across both model architectures and tasks. Our findings suggest that expert routing is more than a computational dispatch mechanism: it provides an architecture-native source of process supervision for reasoning.

过程奖励模型（PRMs）为多步推理提供细粒度监督，但获取可靠的过程标签仍是核心瓶颈。现有方法通常依赖人工标注、基于回放的估计或辅助验证。本文探索了一种由混合专家（MoE）模型天然暴露的过程监督来源：专家路由。我们发现，简单的路由统计量可提供一种与传统输出logit置信度相当的内在过程置信信号。基于此观察，我们通过结合整体路由质量、头路由质量及步骤内路由变化构建路由置信度，并提出RoCo-Rethink，该方法识别出最不自信的推理步骤并选择性地重生成后续轨迹。此外，我们引入RoCo-PRM，利用强MoE自动为推理轨迹标注路由衍生的步骤得分，并将其提炼为独立的PRM。在多种MoE架构和多样推理任务中，RoCo-Rethink和RoCo-PRM均持续优于强基线，且RoCo-PRM在模型架构和任务间具有良好的迁移能力。研究结果表明，专家路由不仅是计算调度机制，更是一种原生的、可用于推理过程监督的架构级资源。

OmniSimulator: Aligning Small Language Models for Authentic Heterogeneous Behavior Modeling
OmniSimulator：对齐小型语言模型以实现真实异构行为建模
Large language models (LLMs) have shown great potential as general-purpose user simulators for interactive systems. Prior studies have demonstrated that training on high-quality data can substantially improve model reasoning and downstream task performance. However, the upper bound of user simulation is still constrained by the model's reasoning paradigm: native chain-of-thought often degenerates into shallow pattern matching and fails to adequately model the behavioral factors underlying real user decisions. In this paper, we propose OmniSimulator, a novel training framework designed to improve the predictive ability of small LLMs for realistic user simulation on short-video platform environments. Specifically, OmniSimulator organizes behavior-oriented reasoning through a structured schema with five dimensions, carefully selects data from real user-behavior scenarios, and introduces a teacher model to generate high-quality reasoning traces under the proposed schema, helping small models learn behavior-relevant reasoning patterns rather than relying on superficial behavioral correlations. Based on this process, we construct 9,881 high-quality training instances from three months of historical interaction trajectories of 200 real users, averaging about 50 distilled examples per user. Experimental results show that OmniSimulator improves the average score of Qwen3-8B by 34.33%, achieves performance competitive with Claude-Sonnet-4.5, and supports smaller-scale, self-hosted deployment. These results suggest that learning structured behavior-oriented reasoning patterns is a promising direction towards scalable and high-fidelity LLM-based user simulation.

DynaWeave: Learning to Adapt through Dynamic Scenario Synthesis
DynaWeave：通过动态场景合成实现自适应学习
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use tools to complete tasks in stateful environments. However, many training environments remain largely static apart from changes caused by the agent's own actions, whereas external events introduced by other users, services, or sudden contingencies in real environments can invalidate ongoing plans. To address this gap, we introduce DynaWeave, an automated synthesis framework that transforms existing static tasks into verifiable dynamic scenarios. The framework generates exogenous events relevant to each task, covering changes in task requirements, environment state, and external information. It uses directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) to represent dependencies between events and an execution engine to activate them during agent interaction. A dynamic verifier revises only the evaluation checks affected by events that actually occur and retains all other checks. Using DynaWeave, we collect 2,095 verified interaction trajectories across 36 environments and use them for supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5 models. Experiments on Gaia2, CostBench, and ToolMaze show that DynaWeave achieves consistent improvements in overall performance across all three evaluated model sizes, increasing the task completion rate on CostBench by 67.34 percentage points and even exceeding the performance of Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview on ToolMaze. These results support dynamic scenario synthesis as a practical approach to training agents to adapt to changing environments. Our code is available at https://github.com/Miaow-Lab/DynaWeave.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）代理在有状态环境中越来越多地使用工具来完成任务。然而，许多训练环境除了代理自身行为引起的改变外，其余部分仍保持高度静态；而真实环境中由其他用户、服务或突发情况引入的外部事件可能使正在进行的计划失效。为解决这一差距，我们提出了DynaWeave，一个自动场景合成框架，可将现有静态任务转化为可验证的动态场景。该框架生成与每个任务相关的外生事件，涵盖任务需求、环境状态和外部信息的变化。它使用有向无环图（DAG）表示事件间的依赖关系，并通过执行引擎在代理交互过程中激活这些事件。动态验证器仅修改实际发生事件所影响的评估检查，保留其余所有检查。利用DynaWeave，我们在36个环境中收集了2,095条经验证的交互轨迹，并用于Qwen3.5模型的监督微调。在Gaia2、CostBench和ToolMaze上的实验表明，DynaWeave在所有三个评估模型尺寸上均实现了稳定的性能提升，在CostBench上的任务完成率提高了67.34个百分点，甚至超过了Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview在ToolMaze上的表现。这些结果支持动态场景合成作为训练代理适应变化环境的一种实用方法。我们的代码可在https://github.com/Miaow-Lab/DynaWeave获取。

Right On Q: Recovering Reasoning under Quantization via Self-Generated Trajectories
Right On Q：通过自生成轨迹恢复量化下的推理能力
Quantization-aware training (QAT) aims to recover reasoning capabilities in low-bit large language models (LLMs). Existing recipes typically supervise the quantized student with the original full-precision model on fixed response sequences. However, the resulting low-bit quantized models still struggle on long-horizon reasoning tasks and often fail to terminate within a token budget that suffices for the full-precision model. We identify two challenges in closing this gap. First, fixed-response supervision leaves a mismatch between training prefixes and those encountered during student generation, and this trajectory divergence grows as responses lengthen. Second, on its own prefixes, the student underpredicts some next-token candidates and overpredicts others, and this distributional misalignment becomes more frequent at high-entropy prefixes. We present OnQ, a low-bit recovery pipeline that combines on-policy distillation and candidate-wise Dynamic KL. Starting from block-wise calibration, the quantized student generates responses and receives full-precision teacher supervision on its own prefixes. Dynamic KL weights forward and reverse KL by each candidate's teacher-student probability ratio to balance correction of under- and overprediction. Across Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B at 2- and 3-bit precision, OnQ achieves the highest nine-benchmark average, with the largest gains on long-horizon reasoning benchmarks. At 2 bits, it outperforms every baseline on every benchmark at every model size, improving the Qwen3-8B average by 10.26 points over the strongest baseline. It also produces shorter responses with fewer truncations and repetitive errors.
中文翻译
量化感知训练（QAT）旨在恢复低比特大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力。现有方法通常以固定响应序列对量化学生模型进行全精度教师模型的监督。然而，由此产生的低比特量化模型在长时程推理任务上仍表现不佳，且常常无法在足以支撑全精度模型的token预算内终止。我们识别出两个关键挑战：第一，固定响应监督导致训练前缀与学生生成过程中遇到的前缀之间存在不匹配，且随着响应长度增加，这种轨迹偏差不断放大；第二，学生模型在其自身前缀上会低估某些下一个词候选，高估另一些候选，且在高熵前缀中，这种分布错配现象更加频繁。我们提出OnQ，一个低比特恢复管道，结合了策略内蒸馏与候选级动态KL。从块级校准开始，量化学生模型生成响应，并接收其自身前缀的全精度教师监督。动态KL根据每个候选的教师-学生概率比值，加权正向与反向KL，以平衡低估与高估的修正。在Qwen3-1.7B、4B和8B模型于2比特和3比特精度下的实验中，OnQ在九项基准测试平均得分上达到最高，尤其在长时程推理基准上提升显著。在2比特下，它在所有模型规模和所有基准上均优于所有基线，使Qwen3-8B的平均得分比最强基线高出10.26分。此外，它还能生成更短的响应，减少截断和重复错误。
👤 Boyuan Zheng, Jing Xiong, Chenwei Wu, Hui Shen, Zesen Zhao et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29357]

Memory-Centric Continual Learning for AI Agents
以记忆为中心的持续学习用于AI智能体
Autonomous agents powered by foundation models can reason, plan, and use tools, but their fixed weights and context limits hinder sustained learning from experience over long-term deployment. While external memory systems attempt to bridge this gap, they mostly act as passive retrieval caches, evaluated primarily by whether they can recall stored facts or past interactions. Whether memory can drive genuine continual learning remains an open question. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Centric Continual Learning (MCCL), arguing that memory should be judged by whether it improves future actions, not merely what it retrieves. MCCL has three main parts. First, a three-axis taxonomy organizes memory by where state is stored (substrates), what form it takes (assets), and how it is managed (policies). This defines a two-loop lifecycle that updates memory state in an inner loop while refining management policies in an outer loop based on task feedback. Second, an evaluation protocol measures retention under interference, adaptation to change, and task transfer to isolate genuine behavioral gains from simple context expansion. Third, a memory scaling framework models how agent performance scales with experience, storage capacity, and compute budgets across non-parametric and parametric memory. Together, these components reframe agent memory from passive lookup to continuous learning, offering a principled route toward self-improving autonomous agents.
中文翻译
由基础模型驱动的自主智能体能够推理、规划并使用工具，但其固定的权重和上下文限制阻碍了在长期部署中从经验中持续学习。尽管外部记忆系统试图弥合这一差距，但它们大多仅作为被动检索缓存，主要通过能否回忆存储的事实或过往交互来评估。记忆是否能真正推动持续学习仍是未解之谜。本文提出以记忆为中心的持续学习（MCCL），主张应根据记忆是否改善未来行为而非仅其检索内容来评判记忆。MCCL包含三个核心部分：首先，一个三轴分类法按状态存储位置（基质）、形式（资产）和管理方式（策略）组织记忆，定义了一个双循环生命周期，内层循环更新记忆状态，外层循环基于任务反馈优化管理策略；其次，提出一种评估协议，通过干扰下的保留能力、对变化的适应性以及任务迁移能力，分离出真正的行为提升与简单的上下文扩展；第三，构建一个记忆扩展框架，建模智能体性能随经验、存储容量和计算预算的变化规律，涵盖非参数化与参数化记忆。这些组件共同将智能体记忆从被动查询重构为持续学习机制，为自改进的自主智能体提供了系统性路径。

Don't Stop Long-Context Continual Pre-Training Too Early: An Empirical Scaling Law
不要过早停止长上下文持续预训练：一项实证缩放定律研究
Long-context continual pre-training (LCCP) is widely used to improve long-context capability, but how its benefits scale with the number of tokens used for LCCP remains unclear. We investigate this question through modeling, monitoring, and validating the benefits of scaling LCCP. First, we establish an empirical additive power law relating long-context performance to model size and the respective token budgets of short-context pre-training and LCCP. Experiments across five model scales and more than one hundred short- and long-context budget configurations show that the fitted law reproduces the observed trends and extrapolates well to held-out model scales. Second, we introduce \textsc{Constant-Last4}, a training and observation protocol that averages the four most recent consecutive checkpoints from a constant-learning-rate run to enable stable monitoring along a single extensible training trajectory. Under this protocol, the same scaling-law form fits well and accurately predicts the LCCP performance trajectory of a 1.22B-parameter model trained on approximately 100B LCCP tokens. Finally, downstream evaluations along this trajectory show continued improvements in long-context performance, with no clear sign of saturation within the evaluated range. These gains persist after both 128K context extension and supervised fine-tuning, with benefits extending to long-context understanding and agentic tasks. Together, these results argue for continuing to scale LCCP rather than stopping early: its gains are stably observable along an extensible trajectory, quantitatively predictable, and transferable to downstream tasks.
中文翻译
长上下文持续预训练（LCCP）被广泛用于提升长上下文能力，但其收益随LCCP所用token数量的增加如何变化仍不明确。本文通过建模、监控和验证LCCP的收益来探究该问题。首先，我们建立了一个经验性的加性幂律关系，将长上下文性能与模型规模及短上下文预训练和LCCP各自的token预算关联起来。在五个模型规模和一百多个短/长上下文预算配置的实验中，拟合的幂律能准确再现观测趋势，并良好外推至未测试的模型规模。其次，我们引入 extsc{Constant-Last4}训练与观测协议，通过平均恒定学习率运行中最近四个连续检查点，实现单一可扩展训练轨迹上的稳定监控。在此协议下，相同缩放定律形式表现良好，并准确预测了一个12.2亿参数模型在约1000亿LCCP token上训练的LCCP性能轨迹。最后，沿该轨迹的下游评估显示长上下文性能持续提升，在评估范围内无明显饱和迹象。这些增益在128K上下文扩展和监督微调后依然存在，且延伸至长上下文理解与代理任务。综合结果表明，应继续扩大LCCP而非过早停止：其收益可在可扩展轨迹上稳定观测、定量预测，并传递到下游任务。

Seeing the Forest for the Trees: Emergent Agreement from Individual Conversations
见林不见树：个体对话中涌现的一致性
As LLM agents interact in the wild and shape each other's decisions, understanding how they converge toward agreement becomes important. We study this in multi-turn conversations where two LLMs discuss subjective matters, can agree or disagree, and may change their stances. Using a linear classifier on individual conversation activations, we identify an internal representation of agreement. We find that it varies substantially across turns and conversations and does not reliably track external agreement at the individual conversation level. We therefore move beyond the representation of agreement in individual conversations, by aggregating the agreement representation across conversations. Externally, this aggregate view reveals a simple structure: when pooled across conversations, agreement can be predicted well with a Markov process, with agreement at each turn being a direct function of agreement at the previous turn. Similarly, when internal agreement is aggregated, it couples with the external Markov agreement through a power-law mapping, exposing Markovian dynamics in internal representations. Additionally, we find that after a change of stance, models express agreement externally before their internal representations reflect the same shift. These results reveal a joint structure between internal and external agreement that emerges only when internal features are aggregated across conversations. Code available at https://github.com/technion-cs-nlp/SeeingTheForestForTheTrees.
中文翻译
当大型语言模型（LLM）代理在真实环境中交互并相互影响决策时，理解它们如何趋向一致变得至关重要。我们研究了多轮对话中两个LLM讨论主观议题、可能达成一致或分歧，并可能改变立场的情况。通过分析单个对话激活值的线性分类器，我们识别出一种内部一致性表征。发现该表征在不同轮次和对话间变化显著，且在个体对话层面无法可靠追踪外部一致性。因此，我们超越了单一对话中的一致性表征，转而对跨对话的一致性表征进行聚合。外部观察显示，这种聚合视图具有简单结构：当跨对话汇总时，一致性可由马尔可夫过程良好预测，每一轮的一致性直接依赖于前一轮的一致性。类似地，当内部一致性被聚合时，它与外部马尔可夫一致性通过幂律映射耦合，揭示了内部表征中的马尔可夫动态。此外，我们发现，在立场转变后，模型会先在外部表现出一致性，其内部表征才反映这一变化。这些结果揭示了只有在跨对话聚合内部特征时才会出现的内外一致性联合结构。代码已公开于 https://github.com/technion-cs-nlp/SeeingTheForestForTheTrees。

Asking What a Director Sees Can Change What Vision-Language Models Choose
询问导演所见能改变视觉-语言模型的选择
Vision-language models are now routinely tested on theory of mind: whether they can work out what another person sees, knows, and means. A common design asks the model to report that perspective and then to act for the person, and reads a correct report beside a wrong action as a failure to use the perspective. That reading rests on a quiet assumption: that asking for the report leaves the action unchanged. We show that it does not. In a rendered Director Task, a partition hides the largest block from a director who asks for "the largest block". Asking first which blocks the director can see raises the share of replies that choose this hidden block by 12 to 88 percentage points on six GPT models and by 17 on Qwen3.8-27B, even though the models mostly report correctly which blocks the director sees. Reasoning traces and prompt rewordings suggest why: the question shifts how the request is read, from the director's view toward the model's own. When the prompt states what the director knows and refers to, fewer than 1% of replies pair a correct report with a hidden-block choice. Any evaluation that elicits a perspective report before the action it scores needs a baseline without the question, and we deliver one: paired prompts that differ only in that question, a design any perspective-taking benchmark can adopt.
中文翻译
视觉-语言模型如今常被用于测试心智理论：即它们能否推断出另一个人所见、所知和所指。常见设计是要求模型报告该视角，然后代表该人行动，并将正确报告与错误行动视为未能使用该视角。这一判断建立在一个隐含假设之上：询问报告不会改变后续行动。我们证明这一假设不成立。在渲染的导演任务中，一个隔板遮挡了最大块，导演要求“最大的块”。若先询问导演能看到哪些块，则六种GPT模型选择隐藏块的比例上升12至88个百分点，Qwen3.8-27B则上升17个百分点，尽管模型大多能正确报告导演可见的块。推理轨迹和提示重述表明原因：问题改变了请求的理解方式，从导演视角转向模型自身视角。当提示明确说明导演所知内容并引用时，少于1%的回复将正确报告与选择隐藏块配对。任何在行动前获取视角报告的评估，都需设置无该问题的基线，我们提供了这样的基线：仅在该问题上不同的配对提示，任何心智理论基准均可采用此设计。

Credit Where Credit Is Due: Contrastive Attribution Topology Search for Multi-Agent Systems
功过分明：多智能体系统的对比归因拓扑搜索
An LLM multi-agent system (MAS) is only as good as the agents it chooses to trust: its accuracy and cost hinge on its topology, meaning which agents are included and how they communicate. A single task score, however, does not reveal which agents help or hinder performance. We propose Contrastive Attribution Topology Search (CATS), which searches for compact teams within a fixed supergraph of specialized agents. An LLM attributer compares execution traces across candidate topologies and assigns credit to individual agents. CATS uses these credits to update supergraph edges while progressively pruning weak edges and agents, yielding a small team for deployment. With small open-weight models and matched candidate evaluations, CATS improves mean accuracy on BigCodeBench by 5.3 percentage points over score-only heuristic search while using 54\% fewer deployment tokens. On MBPP, CATS matches the strongest baselines with fewer tokens than the other searches. In a HotpotQA prompt-poisoning stress test, CATS excludes all adversarially prompted agents in all three runs, whereas random search retains some at comparable accuracy. Per-agent credit thus supports both team optimization and the pruning of potentially harmful agents.
中文翻译
一个LLM多智能体系统（MAS）的性能取决于其所信任的智能体：其准确性和成本取决于其拓扑结构，即包含哪些智能体以及如何通信。然而，单一任务得分无法揭示哪些智能体有助于性能，哪些会阻碍。我们提出对比归因拓扑搜索（CATS），在固定的专业智能体超图内搜索紧凑团队。一个LLM归因器比较不同候选拓扑的执行轨迹，并为个体智能体分配贡献度。CATS利用这些贡献度更新超图边，逐步剪枝弱边和智能体，最终生成一个小型部署团队。使用小型开源权重模型和匹配的候选评估，CATS在BigCodeBench上的平均准确率比仅基于得分的启发式搜索提高5.3个百分点，同时部署token减少54%。在MBPP上，CATS达到最强基线表现，且使用的token更少。在HotpotQA提示污染压力测试中，CATS在三次运行中均排除了所有恶意提示智能体，而随机搜索仍保留部分，且准确率相当。因此，个体智能体的贡献度既支持团队优化，也支持剔除潜在有害智能体。
👤 Fabian Tritthart, Tom Zehle 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Hierarchical Masked Diffusion Language Models
分层掩码扩散语言模型
Masked diffusion language models have emerged as an alternative to autoregressive models, enabling parallel token generation. However, standard masked diffusion language models use a shared noise schedule across positions without explicitly encoding which positions should be resolved first. Many tasks exhibit a natural priority among positions: document titles and section headings can guide the generation of body text, while Sudoku cells determined directly from the initial clues can help resolve more difficult cells. To incorporate this notion of priority (or hierarchy) among token positions, we introduce the Hierarchical Masked Diffusion Language Model (HMDLM), which augments each token position with a hierarchy level available as part of the training data, and requires zero or minimal additional annotations. It factors generation into two passes: the first predicts a hierarchy distribution over masked positions, the second predicts token distributions conditioned on it. Giving each hierarchy level its own noise schedule induces a soft, controllable preference for revealing higher-priority positions earlier while keeping decoding fully parallel. Our formulation is mathematically grounded, and we re-derive the forward and reverse processes and the NELBO for this factorization. Our evaluation shows that for the task of language generation on LM1B, HMDLM reduces perplexity by more than 1.5 points over Anchored Diffusion Language Model (ADLM), a recently proposed strong diffusion baseline; on WikiHow, we see a drop in perplexity of up to 4.4 pts on ADLM and up to 7 pts against MDLM. An LLM judge on WikiHow prefers HMDLM generations (the two variants combined) more than $2\times$ as often as the baseline. On the task of solving Sudoku puzzles requiring multi-step reasoning, it improves solution accuracy by up to $8.6\%$ absolute points over existing diffusion baselines.
中文翻译
掩码扩散语言模型作为一种替代自回归模型的方法，能够实现并行标记生成。然而，标准的掩码扩散语言模型在所有位置使用相同的噪声调度，未显式编码哪些位置应优先解决。许多任务中存在自然的位置优先级：文档标题和章节标题可引导正文生成，而根据初始线索直接确定的数独格子有助于解决更困难的格子。为引入这种位置优先级（或层次结构）的概念，我们提出了分层掩码扩散语言模型（HMDLM），该模型在训练数据中为每个标记位置附加一个层次级别，且几乎不需要额外标注。它将生成过程分为两步：第一步预测被掩码位置的层次分布，第二步在该分布条件下预测标记分布。为每个层次级别分配独立的噪声调度，从而在保持解码完全并行的同时，对优先级更高的位置产生软性、可控的提前揭示偏好。我们的公式具有数学基础，并重新推导了该分解的前向与反向过程及NELBO。评估结果显示，在LM1B语言生成任务上，HMDLM相比近期提出的强基线模型Anchored Diffusion Language Model（ADLM）将困惑度降低了超过1.5点；在WikiHow任务上，相对于ADLM困惑度下降最多达4.4点，相对于MDLM下降最多达7点。由大语言模型判断，HMDLM生成内容被偏好程度是基线的两倍以上。在需要多步推理的数独求解任务中，其解决方案准确率相比现有扩散基线最高提升8.6个百分点。

Let Agents Design How They Search: Automatic Interface Optimization for Agentic Search
让智能体设计它们的搜索方式：面向代理搜索的自动界面优化
Agentic search depends on the interface through which a language-model agent accesses and explores a corpus. Existing interface designs are fixed by hand and applied unchanged to every corpus, although effective designs may depend on the corpus. We introduce SearchWeaver, a framework for automatic search interface optimization guided by downstream search performance. A proposer uses execution trajectories and task rewards to jointly revise corpus artifacts, executable tools, and usage instructions. This pro cess optimizes what information is prepared in advance, which operations tools perform, and which decisions remain with the search agent. With GPT-5.4-nano, the optimized interfaces improve answer accuracy from 71.6% to 78.4% on BrowseComp-Plus and average nDCG@10 from 45.7 to 55.0 across four BRIGHT subsets over Direct Corpus Interaction. These findings highlight the value of jointly optimizing corpus artifacts, executable tools, and usage instructions to develop effective search interfaces.
中文翻译
代理搜索依赖于语言模型代理访问和探索语料库的接口。现有接口设计由人工固定，且对所有语料库采用相同方案，尽管有效设计可能依赖于具体语料库。我们提出SearchWeaver框架，一种基于下游搜索性能的自动搜索接口优化方法。提议者利用执行轨迹和任务奖励，联合优化语料库资源、可执行工具和使用说明。该过程优化了预先准备的信息、工具执行的操作，以及仍需由搜索代理自主决策的部分。使用GPT-5.4-nano，优化后的接口使BrowseComp-Plus上的答案准确率从71.6%提升至78.4%，在四个BRIGHT子集上平均nDCG@10从45.7提升至55.0，相较于直接语料库交互显著提高。这些发现凸显了联合优化语料库资源、可执行工具和使用说明对于构建高效搜索接口的价值。

Beyond Binary Labels: Diagnostic Evaluation of LLM Safety with Prompt-Level Rubrics
超越二元标签：基于提示级评分标准的LLM安全诊断评估
Current LLM safety benchmarks predominantly rely on binary safe or unsafe labels, lacking explanatory depth and suffering from low consistency across automated judges. To address this, we introduce DiagSafe, a diagnostic evaluation framework that moves beyond binary classification by employing prompt-level rubrics. Grounded in globally applicable AI safety policies, each rubric provides explicit criteria along two dimensions: safety, which targets concrete harms with specific exemptions to suppress false positives, and usefulness, which independently evaluates instruction fulfillment. To ensure a robust and realistic evaluation, we construct a bilingual (English-Chinese) benchmark grounded in a comprehensive 84-label safety taxonomy, consisting of two subsets: DiagBase, which covers single- and multi-turn prompts with direct harmful queries and template-based jailbreaks, and DiagAttack, which embeds harmful intents into complex instruction-following tasks to form challenging instruction attacks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DiagSafe significantly enhances the reliability of LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, achieving 95.34% agreement with human annotations -- substantially higher than the 69%-87% achieved by the baselines -- and high inter-judge consistency (Cohen's $κ$ = 0.864). Furthermore, applying our framework reveals that leading LLMs differ markedly in their unsafe rates, and each model also exhibits unique vulnerabilities along different dimensions. In addition, we observe that models are particularly susceptible in Chinese and multi-turn contexts. Ultimately, DiagSafe provides a reliable and explainable tool for auditing and optimizing LLM alignment.
中文翻译
当前的大语言模型（LLM）安全基准主要依赖二元的安全或不安全标签，缺乏解释深度，且在自动化评判中一致性较低。为解决此问题，我们提出DiagSafe，一种超越二元分类的诊断评估框架，采用提示级评分标准。基于普遍适用的人工智能安全政策，每个评分标准提供两个维度的明确标准：安全性，针对具体危害并设置特定豁免以减少误报；实用性，独立评估指令完成情况。为确保评估的稳健性和真实性，我们构建了一个双语（英文-中文）基准，基于全面的84标签安全分类体系，包含两个子集：DiagBase涵盖单轮与多轮提示，包括直接有害查询和基于模板的越狱攻击；DiagAttack则将有害意图嵌入复杂指令遵循任务中，形成具有挑战性的指令攻击。大量实验表明，DiagSafe显著提升了LLM作为评判者的可靠性，与人工标注的一致性达到95.34%，远高于基线的69%-87%；且评判者间一致性高（Cohen's κ = 0.864）。此外，应用该框架揭示出主流LLM在不安全率上存在显著差异，且各模型在不同维度表现出独特脆弱性。我们还发现模型在中文和多轮上下文中尤为易受攻击。最终，DiagSafe提供了一种可靠且可解释的工具，用于审计和优化LLM对齐。

C$^2$U in RAG: Compositional Concept Unlearning in Retrieval-Augmented Generation
RAG中的C$^2$U：检索增强生成中的组合概念消解
Retrieval-augmented generation can expose unwanted concepts through retrieved evidence even when the generator is moderated. We study query-side concept suppression with a fixed encoder and document index. Compositional Concept Unlearning (C$^2$U) routes a profile-derived direction through an augmented atom bank, adding only a bounded edited-minus-original delta to the raw query. Across five datasets, four encoders, and three seeds, the validation-selected configuration reduces pooled binary target Hit@5 by 4.25 percentage points with non-positive control degradation and target-exposure changes. Under the evaluated query transforms, it suppresses target density without increasing budgeted control-query exposure. Separately, ArXiv four-concept composition improves over an averaged-direction comparator after independent validation gates and first-use test evaluation.

检索增强生成即使在生成器受到控制的情况下，仍可能通过检索到的证据暴露不需要的概念。我们研究了在固定编码器和文档索引下，查询端的概念抑制方法。组合概念消解（C$^2$U）通过一个增强的原子库路由由用户画像推导出的方向，仅对原始查询添加一个有界编辑前后差值。在五个数据集、四个编码器和三个随机种子下，经过验证选择的配置在不产生正向控制退化或目标暴露变化的前提下，使合并后的二分类目标Hit@5降低了4.25个百分点。在评估的查询变换下，该方法在不增加预算控制查询暴露的情况下有效抑制了目标密度。此外，在独立验证门控和首次使用测试评估后，ArXiv四概念组合优于平均方向比较器。

Understanding Target Probability Reuse in Speculative Decoding
理解推测解码中的目标概率复用
Speculative decoding uses a target model to verify a tree of draft tokens in parallel. We study whether the resulting next-token distributions can help select the next tree. For unrestricted target models, we show that guaranteed exact reuse requires a distribution computed at the next round's complete context. We characterize when retaining this distribution requires sacrificing accepted tokens and establish conditions under which this loss persists across decoding rounds. Across six targets, we find that reranking first-token branches with a separately computed distribution at the current context improves expected acceptance over draft ranking, especially with small tree budgets. Drawing the first token from this distribution before allocating the tree further improves expected acceptance over pruning a preselected tree, even at equal mean tree size. In our experiments, both selection rules also decode faster than draft ranking when the separate target pass overlaps standalone drafting. Our findings show that making target probabilities available before tree allocation can improve the efficiency of speculative decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/TargetRows.
中文翻译
推测解码使用目标模型并行验证草稿标记的树结构。我们研究由此产生的下一个标记分布是否有助于选择下一个树结构。对于无限制的目标模型，我们表明保证精确复用需要在下一回合完整上下文下计算的分布。我们刻画了保留该分布时需牺牲已接受标记的情况，并建立了该损失在解码轮次间持续存在的条件。在六个目标上，我们发现使用当前上下文中单独计算的分布重新排序首个标记分支，可提高预期接受率，尤其在小树预算下效果显著。在分配树之前从该分布中抽取首个标记，进一步提升了预期接受率，即使在平均树大小相等的情况下也优于预选树剪枝。实验中，当独立目标遍历与独立草稿重叠时，这两种选择规则的解码速度也快于草稿排序。我们的发现表明，在树分配前使目标概率可用，可以提升推测解码的效率。代码已公开于https://github.com/js-lee-AI/TargetRows。
👤 Jungseob Lee, Giyoon Jang, Chailin Nam, Jiwoo Kum, Sihyeon Lee et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

TrainBridge: Agentic NPU Training Optimization Guided by GPU Compilation Evidence
TrainBridge：基于GPU编译证据的智能体式NPU训练优化
We study how to improve training efficiency on Ascend NPUs. In the NPU training programs we study, framework-level compilation optimizations such as operator fusion are only partially applied, leaving fragmented computation and avoidable execution overheads. Profiling-guided tuning can address these inefficiencies, but it often requires costly trial and error. To make training on NPUs more efficient, we propose TrainBridge, an agentic approach for transferring GPU optimization guidance to NPU training. TrainBridge uses the highly optimized execution produced by mature GPU compilation stacks as a task-specific reference for NPU tuning. It extracts optimization intents from the GPU training execution to guide NPU program rewrites and further refines the rewritten program through iterative profiling. Our GPU-to-NPU migration experiments show that TrainBridge achieves an average 1.7x speedup across four workloads covering LLM pretraining, image classification, and vision-language training, and an average speedup of nearly 2.0x across 27 Modded-NanoGPT speedrun records with highly optimized GPU implementations.
中文翻译
我们研究如何提升Ascend NPU上的训练效率。在所研究的NPU训练程序中，框架级编译优化（如算子融合）仅部分应用，导致计算碎片化和不必要的执行开销。基于性能剖析的调优虽可解决这些问题，但通常需要代价高昂的试错过程。为提升NPU训练效率，我们提出TrainBridge，一种将GPU优化指导迁移至NPU训练的智能体方法。TrainBridge利用成熟GPU编译栈生成的高度优化执行作为NPU调优的任务特定参考。它从GPU训练执行中提取优化意图，指导NPU程序重写，并通过迭代剖析进一步优化重写后的程序。我们的GPU到NPU迁移实验显示，TrainBridge在涵盖LLM预训练、图像分类和视觉-语言训练的四个工作负载上实现了平均1.7倍加速；在27个经优化的GPU实现的Modded-NanoGPT速跑记录中，平均加速接近2.0倍。
👤 Yupeng Chen, Kunxiang Zhao, Xingjian Wang, Yue Zhao, Yijing Wu et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

A Large-Scale Evaluation of Role-Enhanced Zero-Shot Chain-of-Thought Prompting
角色增强型零样本思维链提示的大规模评估
This paper proposes Role-Enhanced Zero-Shot Chain-of-Thought (RE-ZS-CoT), a prompting framework that systematically integrates domain-specific expert roles to enhance reasoning in large language models (LLMs) without task-specific examples. We assess two versions of our approach. In the first version, static role selection, we evaluate eight predefined expert roles across arithmetic, commonsense, and reading comprehension benchmarks to determine their impact on reasoning performance. In the second version, dynamic role selection, each model autonomously selects the single most suitable role for each benchmark, enabling adaptive task-role alignment without manual specification. Across 20 LLMs and seven benchmarks, RE-ZS-CoT frequently surpasses zero-shot and zero-shot-CoT baselines, achieving performance improvements of up to 44 percentage points. The results reveal a strong correspondence between static and dynamic versions; the benchmarks where static roles yielded improvements were almost always those where dynamic roles also outperformed baselines, though static selection achieved peak performance. Ablation studies further show that unconventional roles degrade performance, underscoring the necessity of task-role alignment. The framework's scalability is validated across model sizes (2b-14b parameters), with smaller models like Granite3.3-8b achieving remarkable proficiency in arithmetic reasoning. RE-ZS-CoT advances zero-shot prompting by unifying role specialization with chain-of-thought reasoning, offering a practical, resource-efficient solution for improving LLM capabilities across diverse domains.
中文翻译
本文提出角色增强型零样本思维链（RE-ZS-CoT），一种系统性整合领域特定专家角色以增强大语言模型（LLMs）推理能力的提示框架，无需任务特定示例。我们评估了该方法的两个版本。第一版为静态角色选择，我们在算术、常识和阅读理解基准上评估八个预定义专家角色，以确定其对推理性能的影响。第二版为动态角色选择，每个模型自主选择每个基准中最合适的单一角色，实现无需人工指定的自适应任务-角色对齐。在20个LLMs和七个基准上，RE-ZS-CoT频繁超越零样本和零样本思维链基线，性能提升最高达44个百分点。结果表明，静态与动态版本之间存在强烈对应关系：静态角色带来提升的基准，几乎总是动态角色也优于基线，尽管静态选择能达到峰值性能。消融实验进一步显示，非传统角色会降低性能，凸显任务-角色对齐的必要性。该框架在模型规模（2b–14b参数）上均表现出可扩展性，较小模型如Granite3.3-8b在算术推理方面展现出惊人的能力。RE-ZS-CoT通过统一角色专业化与思维链推理，推进了零样本提示技术，为跨多样化领域提升LLM能力提供了一种实用且资源高效的解决方案。

Our Own Jev: Typed Decisions in One Forward Pass, from an In-House Judge to Open LoRA Models
我们自己的Jev：从内部裁判到开放LoRA模型的单次前向传递类型化决策
A typed decision maps evidence, a question and a fixed set of options to a probability for every option, computed in one forward pass, by reading a language model's logits or a small head over its hidden states, instead of generating and parsing text. The commercial system Jev popularised this System~1 interface, and open reproductions have followed. We report two uses of the pattern. \emph{Part I} describes an in-house System~1 judge that triages about 148K agentic coding session records for a training corpus. It replaces a frontier LLM judge that cost on the order of \$1,000 per pass and whose stored labels matched a fresh two-judge consensus on only 26.9\% of the sessions where the two judges agreed; a self-hosted frozen backbone with a rank-16 LoRA adapter and a pointer head answers three typed questions in one forward pass, judges 1,000 sessions in about three minutes of one GPU, about \$0.06 at a reference rate and roughly 1\% of the frontier judge's cost, and keeps every session inside the organisation. \emph{Part II} asks whether a readout that adds no parameters can be competitive and whether training one helps. Applying the public Eikos recipe and corpus of about 25K decisions to three further backbones (1B, 2B and 35B), we read the frozen output layer of a LoRA-adapted decoder. On the 111 public hard items of JevBench our single-seed 35B, developed with those items in view, scores 79.3\%, not significantly different from the commercial reference (73.0\%, unadjusted $p=0.12$) and ahead of five open systems after Holm--Bonferroni correction; our 2B is ahead of all four kev checkpoints on the leaderboard. A single-seed ablation finds no detectable benefit from training the 26 letter rows of the output layer, and a retrain on a later corpus release moves the 2B's hard-tier accuracy by 11.7 points, which shows how little small evaluations resolve.
中文翻译
类型化决策将证据、问题和一组固定选项映射为每个选项的概率，通过读取语言模型的logits或其隐藏状态上的小型头部，在一次前向传递中完成，而非生成并解析文本。商业系统Jev推广了这种System~1接口，随后出现了多个开源复现。我们报告了该模式的两种应用。第一部分描述了一个内部System~1裁判，用于筛选约14.8万条智能体编码会话记录以构建训练语料库。它取代了一个成本约为每轮1000美元的前沿LLM裁判，且其存储标签仅在26.9%的双方裁判一致会话中与新双裁判共识匹配；使用自托管冻结主干网络搭配rank-16 LoRA适配器和指针头，可在一次前向传递中回答三个类型化问题，3分钟内完成1000个会话的判断（单GPU，约0.06美元，仅为前沿裁判成本的1%），且所有会话均保留在组织内部。第二部分探讨了不增加参数的读出是否具有竞争力，以及训练是否有助于提升性能。基于公开的Eikos方法和约2.5万条决策语料，对三个额外主干（1B、2B和35B）进行测试，读取LoRA适配解码器的冻结输出层。在JevBench的111个公开难题上，单种子35B模型得分79.3%，与商业参考值（73.0%，未调整p=0.12）无显著差异，并在Holm-Bonferroni校正后优于五个开源系统；2B模型在排行榜上领先所有四个kev检查点。单种子消融实验表明，训练输出层的26个字母行无明显收益；在后续语料版本上重新训练使2B模型难题准确率提升11.7个百分点，说明小规模评估难以揭示真实性能。
👤 Yuchuan Weng, Chi Liu, Xin Chen, Srinivasan Manoharan 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Target Regeneration Shrinks the Gains of Draft-Training Objectives in Speculative Decoding
目标重生成削弱了推测解码目标在草稿训练中的增益
Speculative decoding accelerates large language models by verifying drafted tokens in parallel. New draft-training objectives are usually credited with the acceptance they add over cross-entropy on externally written responses, yet many pipelines let the target rewrite them. We ask how much of that gain survives. With a vision-language target and a block drafter, we train three objectives on four versions of one corpus: cross-entropy on corpus tokens, token-level distillation from the target, and distillation gated to positions that would survive verification. Distillation and the gate add large gains on external responses, but after greedy regeneration, distillation adds a smaller gain and the gate little or none. The shrinkage persists across data orders, position weights, verifiers, decoding temperatures and, for distillation, initializations and learning rates. Most of it remains at matched supervised length and at each objective's best measured training length. A second vision corpus shows the shrinkage, as does a text-only replication, for distillation also with an autoregressive drafter. Objective gains should therefore be measured against a target-regenerated baseline and across training lengths. Our code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/TargetRegeneration.
中文翻译
推测解码通过并行验证草稿标记来加速大型语言模型。新的草稿训练目标通常被归功于其相对于外部编写响应的交叉熵所增加的接受率，但许多流水线允许目标重写它们。我们探究这些增益中有多少能保留下来。使用视觉-语言目标和块草稿器，在一个语料库的四个版本上训练三种目标：基于语料库标记的交叉熵、来自目标的逐标记知识蒸馏，以及仅在可能通过验证的位置上启用门控的知识蒸馏。知识蒸馏和门控在外部响应上带来显著增益，但在贪婪重生成后，蒸馏带来的增益减小，门控几乎无增益。这种衰减在数据顺序、位置权重、验证器、解码温度以及蒸馏的初始化和学习率变化下持续存在。即使在匹配监督长度和各目标最佳训练长度下，大部分衰减仍保持不变。第二个视觉语料库和纯文本复现也显示出相同趋势，包括使用自回归草稿器的情况。因此，目标增益应基于目标重生成基线并在不同训练长度下进行衡量。代码已公开于https://github.com/js-lee-AI/TargetRegeneration。

Building AI Research Teams for Autonomous Discovery of Linear Attention Architectures
构建用于自主发现线性注意力架构的AI研究团队
Autonomous agents can increasingly propose ideas, modify code, and run experiments, creating an opportunity to accelerate scientific discovery using teams of collaborative AI researchers. To escape local minima in the discovery, we propose to encourage orthogonal exploration by constructing the team using agents with varying expertise. However, realizing this design requires more than diverse backgrounds. When experiments are costly, a team can test only a limited number of ideas, thereby creating the need to balance exploration and exploitation when selecting a subset of researchers to propose and execute new experiments. Furthermore, each result should shape what the team tries next. We therefore ask how AI researchers with different scientific backgrounds can build on one another's findings and sustain progress under a limited experimental budget. To address these challenges, we build a team of persistent AI researchers, each grounded in a different discipline, who keep private research histories, share experimental evidence, and receive research opportunities through adaptive allocation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this design by deploying the team to search for linear attention architectures, where it outperforms GDN2 at 1.3B scale on language modeling, common-sense reasoning, and synthetic and real-world retrieval tasks. In controlled comparisons, removing any single organizational component from our design yields markedly weaker discoveries: without disciplinary backgrounds, the search plateaus early; without private histories, researchers make less progress despite identical shared evidence; and uniform selection falls short of adaptive allocation within the same budget. These results suggest that sustained autonomous discovery depends not only on stronger agents, but also on who explores, what they remember, and how research effort is allocated.
中文翻译
自主智能体能够越来越多地提出想法、修改代码并运行实验，这为利用协作AI研究团队加速科学发现提供了机会。为避免发现过程陷入局部最优，我们提出通过构建具备不同专长的团队来鼓励正交探索。然而，实现这一设计不仅需要多样化的背景。当实验成本高昂时，团队只能测试有限数量的想法，从而需要在探索与利用之间权衡以选择研究人员子集来提出并执行新实验。此外，每次结果都应影响团队下一步的尝试。因此，我们探讨了具有不同科学背景的AI研究者如何基于彼此成果持续推进，且在有限实验预算下保持进展。为此，我们构建了一个由持久性AI研究者组成的团队，每位研究者基于不同学科，保留私有研究历史，共享实验证据，并通过自适应分配获得研究机会。我们通过部署该团队搜索线性注意力架构，证明了该设计的有效性：在1.3B规模下，其性能优于GDN2，在语言建模、常识推理以及合成与真实世界检索任务中均表现更优。在受控对比中，移除设计中的任一组织组件都会导致发现能力显著下降：缺乏学科背景时，搜索过早停滞；缺乏私有历史时，尽管共享证据相同，研究者进步更少；均匀选择也逊于自适应分配。这些结果表明，持续的自主发现不仅依赖更强的智能体，还取决于谁在探索、他们记得什么，以及研究资源如何分配。
