SANE: Automated Scorer-Specific Metamorphic Testing for LLM Judges
SANE：针对LLM裁判的自动化评分器特定变异测试
LLM judges increasingly mediate benchmark evaluation, model selection, data curation, and training. Their decisions are useful only when each judge responds reliably to changes affecting its scoring criterion. Existing methods for reliability testing perturb inputs with known expected effects on judge scores. Prominent LLM-judge testing harnesses combine human-authored perturbation recipes with synthetic test generation, leaving scorer-specific recipe ideation outside the automated pipeline. This limits their use to fixed perturbations and pre-assumed scorer types. We introduce SANE, an agent-based framework that encodes behavioral requirements for stochastic evaluators as reusable agent instructions. Given a scorer specification, implementation, and representative inputs, the pipeline first identifies scorer-specific capabilities, then automatically derives perturbation recipes and expected score relations for each. SANE also defines a scorer-independent validation framework for generated judge tests. It determines whether each recipe agrees with scorer intent and whether each instantiated case realizes the recipe and supports its expected relation. Because SANE generates a recipe inventory tailored to each scorer, the number of aligned recipes grows as scorers are added. Across five domains, SANE produces 3.7 times as many validated test families as the Judge Reliability Harness (JRH) at comparable end-to-end validity and detects scorer deficiencies in every domain, including professionalism and toxicity, where JRH detects none. On faithfulness, SANE achieves strong human alignment in judge ranking: its test-suite scores rank 11 judge models consistently with human-labeled FaithBench performance (Spearman rho = 0.852), while JRH does not (rho = 0.100).
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）裁判日益参与基准评估、模型选择、数据整理和训练。其决策只有在每个裁判能可靠响应影响其评分标准的变化时才有效。现有可靠性测试方法通过已知预期影响裁判评分的输入扰动进行测试。主流的LLM裁判测试结合人工编写的扰动配方与合成测试生成，但评分器特定的配方构思仍不在自动化流程内。这限制了其仅适用于固定扰动和预设评分器类型。我们引入SANE，一种基于智能体的框架，将随机评估者的行為要求编码为可重用的智能体指令。给定评分器规格、实现和代表性输入，该流程首先识别评分器特定能力，然后自动推导每种评分器的扰动配方和预期分数关系。SANE还定义了一个评分器无关的验证框架，用于生成的裁判测试。它判断每条配方是否符合评分器意图，以及每个实例是否实现该配方并支持预期关系。由于SANE为每个评分器生成定制的配方库，随着评分器增加，对齐配方数量也随之增长。在五个领域中，SANE生成的经验证测试族数量是Judge Reliability Harness（JRH）的3.7倍，且端到端有效性相当，同时在每个领域中检测出评分器缺陷，包括专业性和毒性问题，而JRH未检测到任何问题。在忠实度方面，SANE在裁判排名上表现出强人类一致性：其测试套件得分与人类标注的FaithBench性能排名高度一致（Spearman rho = 0.852），而JRH则无此关联（rho = 0.100）。
👤 Ethan Ludwick, Yu-Chung Hsiao 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28819]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
LIFT: Scoped Constraints for Evaluating and Training Instruction Following
LIFT：用于评估与训练指令遵循的范围化约束
Common instruction following (IF) benchmarks, such as IFEval, evaluate the ability of language models (LMs) to follow simple constraints that can easily be verified programmatically. These same constraints, in turn, are used as reward signals for reinforcement learning. This design may lead to reward hacking of constraints, pushing models away from the main task. Recent attempts to address this resulted in benchmarks with richer constraints, but required expert labor, remained closed-access, and cannot be easily extended. We present Lift-Bench, an IF benchmark that asks a model to draft a document given a topic and a structured outline. Constraints are scoped to certain document sections, providing finer control over constraint density. Lift-Bench includes both verifiable and judged constraints, as well as a training counterpart, Lift-Train. Importantly, Lift-Bench provides both main task and instruction-following constraints, mitigating shallow reward hacking. On experiments with multiple models, we find that frontier models struggle with the harder subsets of our benchmark and analyze model performance along multiple axes. We also find that training on Lift-Train is much less susceptible to reward hacking than training on the common IFTrain. Lift-Bench and Lift-Train are publicly available, as well as the data generating pipeline.
中文翻译
常见的指令遵循（IF）基准测试，如IFEval，评估语言模型（LMs）遵循简单可程序化验证的约束的能力。这些约束同样被用作强化学习中的奖励信号。这种设计可能导致约束的奖励劫持，使模型偏离主要任务。近期尝试通过引入更丰富的约束来解决此问题，但需要专家劳动，仍为封闭访问，且难以扩展。我们提出了Lift-Bench，一个要求模型根据主题和结构化大纲起草文档的IF基准。约束被限定在特定文档部分，提供对约束密度的更精细控制。Lift-Bench包含可验证和需人工判断的约束，以及对应的训练版本Lift-Train。重要的是，Lift-Bench同时包含主任务约束和指令遵循约束，从而缓解浅层奖励劫持问题。在多个模型的实验中，我们发现前沿模型在我们基准的较难子集上表现不佳，并从多个维度分析了模型性能。我们还发现，使用Lift-Train进行训练比使用常见IFTrain更不易受到奖励劫持的影响。Lift-Bench和Lift-Train均已公开，包括数据生成管道。
👤 Eran Goldstein, Yuval Belfer, Yonatan Belinkov, Daniel Gissin 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28787]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Is Anyone Enforcing the Cartel? What Reinforcement Learning Teaches LLM Agents in Markets
是否有人在执行卡特尔？强化学习如何教会LLM代理在市场中的行为
Evaluations of large language model (LLM) agents report that pricing agents collude at others' expense. Yet collusion is defined by enforcement: a cartel, in which competitors share monopoly profits, holds only if a member who cheats is punished. We introduce an audit that tests enforcement by forcing a member to cheat in counterfactual copies of a market, and use it to study what reinforcement learning (RL) teaches agents in pricing and trading. Small LLMs trained by RL learn what their training rivals reward, but only what they readily try. In self-play, they learn fixed rules ignoring their rivals; against a punishing rival, to be deterred; against a cheating rival, to match its cuts, never the costly price war that would deter it, which they rarely try. A war forced into training is learned, but mostly only where it was forced. Against their own copies, deterred and matching agents can earn the same cartel profits, but only matching ones can enforce them, and then thinly: as a by-product and only against large cuts. Q-learners enforce collusion only in pricing. Prompted LLMs reach cartel-like outcomes mostly through rules that ignore the market. Outcome measures cannot tell these cases apart; the audit can.
中文翻译
对大型语言模型（LLM）代理的评估报告称，定价代理会以他人利益为代价进行合谋。然而，合谋的定义依赖于执行：只有当背叛者受到惩罚时，卡特尔（即竞争者分享垄断利润）才能成立。我们引入了一种审计机制，通过强制一个成员在反事实的市场副本中背叛，来测试执行情况，并利用它研究强化学习（RL）如何影响LLM代理在定价和交易中的行为。由RL训练的小型LLM学会了其训练对手所奖励的内容，但仅限于它们容易尝试的行为。在自对弈中，它们学会固定规则而忽略对手；面对惩罚性对手时，会受到威慑；面对背叛对手时，会匹配其降价行为，但从不采取代价高昂的价格战来威慑对方——因为它们很少尝试这种行为。只有在训练中被迫经历价格战时，才会学习相关策略，且主要局限于被强迫的情境。面对自身副本时，受到威慑和匹配的代理可以获得相同的卡特尔利润，但只有匹配的代理能执行这些利润，且执行力度很弱：作为副产品，且仅针对大幅降价。Q-学习者仅在定价任务中能执行合谋。提示型LLM大多通过忽略市场的规则达成类似卡特尔的结果。结果度量无法区分这些情况；而审计机制可以。

Think Harder Where It Matters: Item-Level Adaptive Recursion for Sequential Recommendation
在关键处更深入思考：面向序列推荐的项目级自适应递归
Existing sequential recommenders usually allocate a fixed computational budget to every item in a user's interaction history, regardless of the item's contribution to the final prediction. We challenge this de facto design with two diagnostic findings. (1) Non-uniform computational need: items within the same sequence vary drastically in how much computation they require. Most converge after shallow processing while a critical minority only stabilizes at full depth. (2) Suboptimality of fixed allocation: given oracle access to the target item, a non-uniform depth assignment consistently outranks the fixed one, indicating that existing uniform computation both wastes capacity on already-converged items and sacrifices overall recommendation quality. These observations motivate a shift from fixed-depth computation to item-level adaptive reasoning. To this end, we propose LoopRec. LoopRec recursively applies a single weight-shared block and introduces a lightweight gate at each recursion boundary that makes a binary exit-or-continue decision for every item independently. Items whose representations have sufficiently matured exit early, while those requiring deeper reasoning continue to subsequent recursions. This design converts the fixed global depth into a learned, per-item property without auxiliary depth labels, and is readily adaptable to diverse backbone architectures. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks with four backbone families demonstrate that LoopRec consistently outperforms matched fixed-depth counterparts. Further analysis reveals that LoopRec's learned allocation exhibits interpretable patterns aligned with our post-hoc oracle analysis: lower-frequency items and more recent interactions naturally receive deeper computation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Elpmis117/LoopRec.
中文翻译
现有的序列推荐系统通常对用户交互历史中的每个项目分配固定的计算预算，而不论其对最终预测的贡献程度。我们通过两项诊断性发现挑战这一默认设计。(1) 非均匀的计算需求：同一序列内的项目在所需计算量上差异巨大，大多数项目经过浅层处理即可收敛，而少数关键项目需完整深度处理才能稳定。(2) 固定分配的次优性：在拥有目标项目的理想访问权限下，非均匀深度分配始终优于固定分配，表明现有均匀计算既浪费了已收敛项目的算力，也牺牲了整体推荐质量。这些观察促使我们从固定深度计算转向项目级自适应推理。为此，我们提出LoopRec。LoopRec递归应用单一共享权重模块，并在每次递归边界引入轻量级门控机制，对每个项目独立做出是否退出或继续的二元决策。表示已充分成熟的项目提前退出，而需要更深层推理的项目则进入后续递归。该设计将固定的全局深度转化为无需辅助深度标签的可学习、项目级属性，且易于适配多种骨干架构。在四个骨干网络家族的公共基准上的大量实验表明，LoopRec始终优于对应的固定深度模型。进一步分析显示，LoopRec所学习到的分配模式与我们事后的理想分析一致：低频项目和较近的交互自然获得更深的计算。代码已公开于https://github.com/Elpmis117/LoopRec。
👤 Zhanshuo Cao, Jiakai Tang, Yuqi Zhou, Sunhao Dai, Jun Xu 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

VX-One: Typed, Calibrated Decision Heads for Real-Time Perimeter Triage - An empirical study of unknown-attack honesty, cascade gray zones, and calibration under drift
VX-One：面向实时边界筛查的类型化、校准决策头——未知攻击诚实性、级联灰区与漂移下的校准性实证研究
Perimeter triage faces a latency/semantics trade-off: signature engines are fast and format-safe but blind to unseen attacks, while generative models are semantic but slow and produce unparseable output. In late 2026 a class of commercial System One decision models (TypeSafe Jev; Microsoft-Decision-1) began selling typed decision primitives - binary gates, closed-set choices, ordinal scores - as product APIs, with no academic treatment. We present VX-One: a small multi-head network (100K-10M parameters) whose outputs are these primitives - a calibrated gate, a choice over a closed candidate set that always includes an explicit unknown option, and an ordinal score, trained multi-task, temperature-calibrated per head (~0.7-1.7 ms p50 on CPU). We evaluate it on UNSW-NB15, CIC-IDS2017, EMBER2024 against calibrated XGBoost, Random-Forest, MLP, Kitsune, and measure a two-phase cascade (Suricata with 52,674 Emerging-Threats rules -> VX-One) over five days of CIC-IDS2017. Findings: (i) VX-One wins detection (AUROC/FPR@95%TPR) on UNSW-NB15 and under temporal drift on CIC-IDS2017 (0.711 vs 0.637), but XGBoost wins multi-class attribution on all three benchmarks; (ii) leave-one-family-out gate detects never-seen families on 9/9 UNSW folds yet drops below chance on CIC recon family, and abstention threshold transfers poorly across benchmarks; (iii) on payload-less reconstructions signature engines resolve ~0.1% of flows - gray zone ~99.9% of traffic, recovered by Phase 2 (combined recall >=0.978); (iv) calibration collapses under temporal drift (ECE 0.04->0.40) for every method while ranking partially survives. We provide a measured account of where typed calibrated decisions help perimeter triage, and where they fail.
中文翻译
边界筛查面临延迟/语义权衡问题：签名引擎速度快且格式安全，但对未知攻击无感知；生成模型具备语义理解能力，却速度慢且输出难以解析。2026年末，一类商用System One决策模型（TypeSafe Jev；Microsoft-Decision-1）开始将二元门、闭集选择、序数评分等类型化决策原语作为产品API销售，尚未有学术研究。我们提出VX-One：一个小型多头网络（参数量10万至1000万），其输出为这些原语——一个校准后的决策门、一个包含显式未知选项的闭集选择、以及一个序数评分，采用多任务训练并按头进行温度校准（CPU上p50延迟约0.7-1.7毫秒）。我们在UNSW-NB15、CIC-IDS2017、EMBER2024数据集上评估了该模型，并与校准后的XGBoost、随机森林、MLP、Kitsune对比。实验结果表明：（i）在UNSW-NB15上VX-One检测性能更优（AUROC/FPR@95%TPR），在CIC-IDS2017的时间漂移下表现也优于XGBoost（0.711 vs 0.637），但XGBoost在三组基准上的多分类归因任务中表现更佳；（ii）留一类别门在UNSW所有折叠中可检测从未见过的攻击家族（9/9），但在CIC重构家族上性能低于随机猜测，且弃权阈值在不同基准间迁移效果差；（iii）在无载荷重构场景中，签名引擎仅能处理约0.1%流量，其余约99.9%处于“灰区”，由第二阶段（Suricata + VX-One）恢复，联合召回率≥0.978；（iv）所有方法在时间漂移下校准性均崩溃（ECE从0.04升至0.40），但排序性能部分保留。本文提供了对类型化校准决策在边界筛查中有效与失效场景的量化分析。

Commit or Abstain? System One Decision Models, LLMs, and Missing Legal Authority
承诺还是放弃？System One决策模型、LLMs与缺失法律权威问题
Given the enormous number of claims to be checked, verifiable legal AI requires scalable, cost-effective models that answer the practical needs of law. System One decision models are built on large language models but, instead of generating text, return a calibrated probability for each option. This suits the verification of legal authority, where the question is closed: a given holding, or none of them. We test whether such models abstain when the correct authority is missing. We extend CaseHOLD with an abstention condition in which the correct holding is replaced in 1,670 of 5,314 items, and compare four decision models with six generative LLMs. When no option is correct, generative LLMs still pick one in 38-86% of items; the most cautious decision model does so in 30%. On standard CaseHOLD, a decision model matches the best generative LLM (88.1% vs. 88.2%) at 1/55 of the cost. Accepting that LLM's answer only when a decision model agrees halves its wrong citations, and distilling a decision model's probabilities into Qwen3-8B, without labels, yields a well-calibrated local model. Legal AI should be judged by its wrong citations, not by accuracy alone.

面对海量待核查主张，可验证的法律AI需要可扩展、低成本的模型以满足实际法律需求。System One决策模型基于大语言模型，但不生成文本，而是返回每个选项的校准概率。这适用于法律权威验证，因为问题为封闭式：给定判例或无任何判例。我们测试此类模型在正确权威缺失时是否会放弃回答。我们将CaseHOLD扩展为包含弃权条件，其中1670/5314项的正确判例被替换，并比较四种决策模型与六种生成式LLM。当无正确选项时，生成式LLM仍会在38%-86%的项目中做出选择；最谨慎的决策模型也仅在30%的情况下放弃。在标准CaseHOLD上，决策模型以1/55的成本达到了与最佳生成式LLM相当的表现（88.1% vs 88.2%）。只有当决策模型与LLM意见一致时才接受LLM答案，可使错误引用减少一半；将决策模型的概率蒸馏至Qwen3-8B（无需标签），可生成校准良好的本地模型。法律AI应以错误引用数量为评判标准，而不仅仅是准确率。

ToM2Vec: Continuous Theory-of-Mind Tokens for Multi-Agent Coordination
ToM2Vec：用于多智能体协作的连续心智理论令牌
Forming an accurate theory-of-mind (ToM) is key to successful multi-agent collaboration and requires reasoning under uncertainty to capture multiple possible partner states. This is hard to do in the text representation space native to large language model (LLM) agents, which more easily describe a single partner state. To address this challenge, we introduce ToM2Vec, which learns to form abstract theories-of-mind in a rich latent space via a fixed budget of continuous reasoning tokens. During training, supervision for these tokens comes from the partner's actual thoughts, which the ToM tokens are trained to abstract and match. At test-time, ToM tokens are generated by each agent and act as conditioners for that agent's thoughts and actions. Across several environments covering collaborative, competitive, and mixed-motive settings (Overcooked-AI, multiparty negotiation, Decrypto, Hanabi, and Collab Escape/Capture), we evaluate ToM2Vec's ability to support multi-agent interactions and collaboration, finding consistent improvements over the base model and a variety of competitive trained and prompted baselines. We show that latent reasoning is critical, with performance dropping when reasoning is expressed in text. Moreover, we show that ToM2Vec training generalizes to partner model families not seen at train-time, including a proprietary model (GPT-5.4). In our analysis, we provide evidence that the latent tokens encode a ToM, rather than task-specific information: ToM2Vec's tokens improve the agent's ability to directly answer questions about partner beliefs, desires, and next actions.

Real PMIDs, Wrong Papers: Harness Design for Biomedical Claim-Verification Agents
真实的PMID，错误的论文：生物医学声明验证智能体的Harness设计
Literature-searching language agents are becoming a standard component of scientific assistants for the life sciences, yet there is little controlled evidence on which parts of their harness matter. We run a controlled ablation on biomedical claim verification (SciFact) with the live PubMed API as the only tool. Holding the backbone and output format fixed, we compare four harnesses: closed-book answering, single-shot retrieval using the claim as the query, a ReAct-style agent that writes its own PubMed queries, and an oracle given the gold abstracts, each with two efficient frontier backbones. Naive single-shot retrieval finds the gold paper for only 6% of claims and turns overconfident errors into abstentions without improving accuracy. Letting the model formulate its own queries triples gold recall and yields the best verdicts (62% accuracy vs. 49-59% for the other realistic harnesses), although these differences are not significant at our sample size and agents use fewer than half of their search budget. With gold abstracts, the same models reach 79-86% accuracy, a significant 17-24 point gap showing that retrieval, not reasoning, is the main bottleneck. Finally, a citation audit shows that closed-book models cite PubMed IDs that almost always exist (327/335) but are almost never relevant: a cross-family LLM judge rates only 3 of 327 existing citations as relevant to the claim, versus 67% for citations produced by the search agent. Existence checks are therefore insufficient safeguards for scientific agents. We release code, prompts, and all cached tool calls at https://github.com/saqlain2204/real-pmids-wrong-papers.
中文翻译
文献检索语言智能体正成为生命科学领域科学助手的标准组件，但关于其Harness中哪些部分起作用的受控证据仍很少。我们在生物医学声明验证（SciFact）任务上进行了受控消融实验，仅使用实时PubMed API作为工具。保持主干网络和输出格式固定，比较四种Harness：闭卷回答、以声明为查询的单次检索、采用ReAct风格自动生成PubMed查询的智能体，以及给定黄金摘要的“全知”代理，每种搭配两种高效前沿主干网络。朴素的单次检索仅能为6%的声明找到黄金论文，且将过度自信的错误转为回避，但并未提升准确率。允许模型自主生成查询使黄金召回率提升三倍，并取得最佳判断结果（62%准确率，高于其他现实Harness的49-59%），尽管在当前样本量下差异不显著，且智能体使用的搜索预算不足一半。当提供黄金摘要时，相同模型达到79-86%准确率，相比前者的17-24个百分点差距显著，表明检索而非推理是主要瓶颈。最后的引用审计显示，闭卷模型引用的PMID几乎总是存在（327/335），但几乎从不相关：跨家族LLM裁判仅认为3个存在的引用与声明相关，而搜索代理生成的引用有67%相关。因此，存在性检查不足以保障科学智能体的安全性。代码、提示和所有缓存工具调用已发布于https://github.com/saqlain2204/real-pmids-wrong-papers。

SPARE: Sparsity Allocation via Routing Entropy for Efficient MoEs
SPARE：基于路由熵的稀疏性分配以实现高效MoE
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale model capacity efficiently, yet their substantial memory footprint poses a major barrier to deployment. Expert pruning reduces this footprint, but most methods focus on which experts to remove and prune every layer equally. We propose SPARE (Sparsity Allocation via Routing Entropy), a training-free, selector-agnostic method that decides how many experts to remove from each layer using routing entropy, the entropy of tokens' normalized Top-K gate weights on calibration data. When a token's gate weight is spread evenly, removing one active expert discards little of it, so SPARE prunes layers with more diffuse routing more aggressively. A perturbation bound under random pruning formalizes this intuition, and controlled interventions with a fixed selector link concentrated routing to larger output changes. With the same selector and budget, SPARE improves average accuracy over uniform allocation on Mixtral, Qwen3, and DeepSeek models, especially at high pruning ratios. At 50% expert pruning, it raises the eight-task average of Qwen3-30B-A3B from 57.2% to 63.8%, and the math, code, and science average of DeepSeek-R1 from 80.1 to 82.8.
中文翻译
混合专家（MoE）架构能高效扩展模型容量，但其巨大的内存开销仍是部署的主要障碍。专家剪枝可减少内存占用，但大多数方法仅关注剪掉哪些专家，且对每一层进行同等程度的剪枝。我们提出SPARE（基于路由熵的稀疏性分配），一种无需训练、与选择器无关的方法，通过校准数据上的路由熵（即token的归一化Top-K门控权重的熵）决定每层应剪掉多少专家。当token的门控权重分布均匀时，移除一个活跃专家所损失的信息极少，因此SPARE对路由更分散的层进行更激进的剪枝。随机剪枝下的扰动界形式化了这一直觉，且固定选择器的控制干预表明集中路由会带来更大的输出变化。在相同选择器和预算下，SPARE在Mixtral、Qwen3和DeepSeek模型上优于均匀分配的平均准确率，尤其在高剪枝比例下表现更优。在50%专家剪枝时，Qwen3-30B-A3B在八项任务上的平均得分从57.2%提升至63.8%，DeepSeek-R1在数学、代码和科学任务上的平均得分从80.1提升至82.8。

WRDO: Weighted Refusal Direction Optimization in Aligned Language Models
WRDO：对齐语言模型中的加权拒绝方向优化
Aligned large language models are trained to comply with benign requests while refusing harmful ones. However, recent work shows that this refusal behavior is fragile and can often be disrupted by simple activation- or weight-space interventions. In particular, refusal has been shown to be largely mediated by a single residual-stream direction, typically computed as a difference-of-means vector between harmful and harmless prompts. However, this standard construction aggregates heterogeneous harmful prompts into one uniform average, potentially conflating distinct refusal-related phenomena. In this work, we study whether refusal interventions can be strengthened by learning how to combine prompt-specific refusal directions. We propose Weighted Refusal Direction Optimization (WRDO), a procedure that constructs a composite refusal direction from a bank of per-prompt directions using learned aggregation weights. This approach allows different harmful prompts to contribute unequally to the final intervention direction. We evaluate WRDO against prior single-direction methods on three open-weight language models using three guard judges and HarmBench as an external evaluator. We further measure the effect of the intervention on general model capability through standard utility benchmarks. Finally, we investigate a defense-oriented setting in which the same optimization machinery is used in reverse to restore refusal behavior in an abliterated model checkpoint. Our results show that weighted aggregation provides a more effective and flexible mechanism for manipulating refusal representations.
中文翻译
对齐的大语言模型被训练为响应良性请求，拒绝有害请求。然而，近期研究显示，这种拒绝行为脆弱，常被简单的激活或权重空间干预破坏。特别是，拒绝行为主要由单个残差流方向决定，通常通过有害与无害提示的均值差向量计算得出。但这种标准构造将异质的有害提示聚合为单一平均值，可能混淆不同的拒绝相关现象。本文研究是否可通过学习如何组合特定提示的拒绝方向来增强拒绝干预。我们提出加权拒绝方向优化（WRDO），一种从每提示方向库中使用学习聚合权重构建复合拒绝方向的过程。该方法允许不同有害提示对最终干预方向贡献不等。我们在三个开源权重语言模型上，使用三个守卫裁判和HarmBench作为外部评估者，对比WRDO与先前单方向方法。我们还通过标准效用基准衡量干预对模型通用能力的影响。最后，我们探讨防御性场景：同一优化机制反向使用，以恢复被消除的模型检查点中的拒绝行为。结果表明，加权聚合提供了一种更有效且灵活的操纵拒绝表示的机制。
👤 Grigoriy Evseev, Vladimir Solodkin, Dmitry Bylinkin, Daniil Medyakov, Vladimir Aletov et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

Harness Scaling for Generalizable Agentic Reinforcement Learning
利用规模化实现可泛化的智能体强化学习
Agent harnesses have become a central component of modern agentic systems, shaping how models use tools, manage context, and coordinate long-horizon interaction. Realizing the benefits of these harnesses depends on how effectively the underlying model can make use of them, motivating reinforcement learning directly through the harness. However, existing approaches typically train through a single fixed harness, which can lead to specialization and limited transfer across harness configurations. To address this, we introduce \textbf{Harness Scaling}, a new scaling dimension for agentic RL that extends training from a single harness to diverse execution configurations. Concretely, we construct a factorized harness space, select a compact training subset with balanced factorial coverage, and organize cross-harness training to combine global coverage with controlled variation for the same task. Across multiple benchmarks, model scales, and RL algorithms, Harness Scaling consistently improves generalization to unseen white-box configurations. Moreover, models trained through structured white-box variation transfer effectively to unseen external black-box harnesses, outperforming models trained through a single complex black-box harness. These results establish harness diversity as a meaningful scaling dimension for agentic RL and highlight the importance of how this diversity is organized for cross-harness generalization.
中文翻译
智能体 harness 已成为现代智能体系统的核心组件，影响模型如何使用工具、管理上下文以及协调长周期交互。充分发挥这些 harness 的优势取决于底层模型能否有效利用它们，从而推动通过 harness 直接进行强化学习。然而，现有方法通常通过单一固定 harness 进行训练，容易导致模型特化，并限制在不同 harness 配置间的迁移能力。为此，我们提出 extbf{Harness Scaling}，一种新的智能体强化学习规模化维度，将训练从单一 harness 扩展到多样化的执行配置。具体而言，我们构建了一个因子分解的 harness 空间，选取具有均衡因子覆盖的紧凑训练子集，并组织跨 harness 训练，以在同一任务上实现全局覆盖与受控变化的结合。在多个基准、模型规模和 RL 算法上，Harness Scaling 均一致提升了对未见白盒配置的泛化能力。此外，通过结构化白盒变化训练的模型能有效迁移到未见的外部黑盒 harness 上，优于通过单一复杂黑盒 harness 训练的模型。这些结果确立了 harness 多样性作为智能体强化学习中一个有意义的规模化维度，并强调了多样性组织方式对跨 harness 泛化的重要性。

BIRD-RL: Scaling Agentic Reinforcement Learning over Stateful Data-Centric Environments
BIRD-RL：面向状态化数据中心环境的智能体强化学习规模化
While agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for training small language models (SLMs), existing frameworks are predominantly tool-centric and struggle with data-centric environments. Specifically, applying tool-centric frameworks to stateful tasks like SQL debugging introduces severe spatial inefficiencies since proper environment isolation typically requires a separate database container per rollout. Naively reusing databases without tracking evolving states across multi-turn interactions causes trajectory contamination and cross-trajectory interference, which severely destabilizes policy updates. To overcome these bottlenecks, we introduce BIRD-RL, a novel four-stage agentic RL curriculum that alternately enhances foundational agent reasoning and trajectory-adaptive on-policy exploration to circumvent the instability inherently associated with direct end-to-end learning. Crucially, to support this curriculum, BIRD-RL is driven by a Trajectory-scoped Persistent Execution Infrastructure, which enables safe database replica reuse while preserving trajectory isolation. This design improves spatial RL efficiency by 12.8x, successfully enabling scalable agentic RL for complex data-centric tasks without needs of Kubernettes. Empirical evaluations on the BIRD-CRITIC-SQLite benchmark demonstrate that our 7B and 14B models achieve Success Rates of 44.40% and 48.00%, respectively, showing competitive performance against strong proprietary model-based agents such as Claude-Opus-4.6 Agent (48.20%). Furthermore, BIRD-RL successfully trains unified SLMs capable of both advanced SQL debugging and generation, highlighting its broader potential for multi-objective optimization across heterogeneous, real-world data-centric tasks.
中文翻译
尽管智能体强化学习（RL）已成为训练小型语言模型（SLMs）的一种有前景范式，但现有框架主要聚焦于工具导向，难以应对数据导向环境。具体而言，将工具导向框架应用于如SQL调试等状态化任务时，会引入严重的空间效率问题，因为正确的环境隔离通常需要为每次推演单独部署数据库容器。若不追踪多轮交互中的状态演变而直接复用数据库，则会导致轨迹污染和跨轨迹干扰，严重 destabilize 策略更新。为克服这些瓶颈，我们提出了 BIRD-RL，一种新颖的四阶段智能体RL课程，交替增强基础智能体推理能力和轨迹自适应的在线探索，以规避直接端到端学习固有的不稳定性。关键在于，为支持该课程，BIRD-RL 采用了一种轨迹范围持久执行基础设施，可在保留轨迹隔离的前提下安全复用数据库副本。该设计将空间RL效率提升了12.8倍，成功实现了复杂数据导向任务的可扩展智能体RL，无需Kubernetes。在BIRD-CRITIC-SQLite基准上的实证评估表明，我们的7B和14B模型分别取得了44.40%和48.00%的成功率，表现出与Claude-Opus-4.6 Agent等强大专有模型基线相当的性能（48.20%）。此外，BIRD-RL成功训练出可同时处理高级SQL调试与生成的统一SLM，凸显其在异构、真实世界数据导向任务中实现多目标优化的广泛潜力。

OACap: Caption-Guided Active Observation for Detailed Video Captioning
OACap：基于字幕引导的主动观察用于详细视频字幕生成
Unlike question answering, where a query specifies a perceptual target, open-ended detailed captioning must determine what evidence to acquire and how to integrate it into a coherent global description. We propose Observation-Adaptive Video Captioner (OACap), a caption-centered agent that turns unsupported or missing information into targeted observation requests, uses spatiotemporal tools to acquire evidence, and iteratively verifies and revises the caption. OACap captions consistently improve performance across general video-understanding benchmarks using a fixed text-only reader. To better diagnose the information they preserve, we introduce Factual-Semantic Captioning Bench (FSCap-Bench). Its Core-Support Factuality Score (CSFS) combines claim precision with importance-aware fact coverage and responds as expected to four controlled perturbation types on 93.0-98.3% of valid caption pairs. Its Cross-Modal Semantic Round-Trip Score (CSRS) measures semantic preservation through a video-caption-video-caption round trip by matching atomic facts from source and recovered captions. Together, they connect downstream textual-memory utility with factual and semantic preservation.
中文翻译
与需要查询指定感知目标的问题回答不同，开放式详细字幕生成必须自主决定获取哪些证据，并如何将其整合为连贯的整体描述。我们提出了观察自适应视频字幕生成器（OACap），这是一种以字幕为中心的智能体，能将缺失或不支持的信息转化为有针对性的观察请求，利用时空工具获取证据，并迭代验证和修正字幕。OACap在使用固定文本阅读器的情况下，在通用视频理解基准测试中持续提升性能。为更好地诊断其信息保留情况，我们引入了事实-语义字幕基准测试（FSCap-Bench）。其核心支持事实真实性评分（CSFS）结合了陈述精确性与重要性感知的事实覆盖率，对93.0%-98.3%的有效字幕对在四种受控扰动下表现出预期响应。其跨模态语义往返评分（CSRS）通过视频-字幕-视频-字幕的往返过程，匹配源字幕与恢复字幕中的原子事实来衡量语义保留。二者共同将下游文本记忆效用与事实性和语义保留联系起来。
👤 Hao Shi, Hong Yan, Xiaoyang Zhang, Qing Cui, Chao Huang 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

LLM-Based Exogenous World Modeling for Dynamic Scheduling
基于大语言模型的外生世界建模用于动态调度
Dynamic scheduling requires dispatching online arrivals while anticipating future jobs, as poor decisions can compound downstream costs under nonlinear tardiness objectives. We show that a pretrained large language model (LLM) can serve as the exogenous component of a structured world model, forecasting uncertain future job arrivals. Specifically, we exploit an afterstate decomposition, which separates known deterministic shop transitions from uncertain exogenous job arrivals, enabling the LLM to forecast only the latter through a compact belief representation. Building on this, we develop Afterstate World-Model Planning (AWMP), which combines an evolved, belief-conditioned dispatching strategy with short scenario-based rollouts. A surprise-triggered refresh mechanism queries the LLM only when newly observed arrivals deviate sufficiently from previous forecast, and online residual self-calibration further conditions the LLM on its recent forecast errors to handle nonstationary arrivals. Across diverse arrival distributions, AWMP reduces weighted tardiness by 6-21% on dynamic job-shop and 22-46% on flexible job-shop benchmarks, relative to the best of eight classical priority-dispatching rules.
中文翻译
动态调度需要在处理在线到达任务的同时预测未来任务，因为糟糕的决策可能在非线性延迟目标下累积下游成本。我们表明，预训练的大语言模型（LLM）可作为结构化世界模型的外生组件，用于预测不确定的未来任务到达。具体而言，我们采用事后状态分解方法，将已知的确定性车间状态转移与不确定的外生任务到达分离，使LLM仅需通过紧凑的信念表示预测后者。在此基础上，我们开发了事后状态世界模型规划（AWMP），结合演化后的信念条件调度策略与短时情景滚动模拟。当新观测到的任务到达显著偏离先前预测时，会触发意外刷新机制，仅此时调用LLM；在线残差自校准进一步根据LLM近期预测误差对其条件化，以应对非平稳到达。在多种到达分布下，AWMP相较于八种经典优先级调度规则中的最优者，分别在动态作业车间和柔性作业车间基准上降低了6%-21%和22%-46%的加权延迟。
👤 Ni Zhang, Haofeng Yuan, Jianan Zhou, Shiji Song, Zhiguang Cao 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

ExpEvolve: Structuring and Reusing Experience for Open-Ended Discovery
ExpEvolve：结构化与重用经验以实现开放式发现
Evolutionary coding agents such as AlphaEvolve are one of the notable successes in using Large Language Models (LLMs) to build AI Scientists. These agents tackle open-ended scientific problems by iteratively improving and evolving programs, leveraging the prior knowledge and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Despite their success, mutations over programs are mostly low-level upon surface heuristics, without a persistent unit of control. In contrast, science compounds through ideas. In this work, we propose ExpEvolve, which injects idea-level extraction and control into evolution: it discovers and leverages the high-level factors from programs that explain scoring differences among different programs. We characterize theoretical conditions on when discovery with idea factors improves sample efficiency. We also build a practical framework to show that the extracted high-level idea factors are helpful for guiding exploitation from experience and exploration when discovery comes to a plateau. Across 11 open-ended mathematical tasks, ExpEvolve significantly improves the final performance and sample efficiency up to 5.0x. The improvements also transfer to 4 other LLMs and to 6 machine learning tasks from MLS-Bench. Analysis shows that ExpEvolve can discover idea-level factors to provide better exploration and exploitation for evolutionary discovery.
中文翻译
如AlphaEvolve等进化编码智能体是利用大语言模型（LLMs）构建AI科学家的显著成功案例。这些智能体通过迭代改进和演化程序来解决开放式科学问题，利用LLM的先验知识和推理能力。尽管取得成功，但程序的突变大多基于表面启发式，缺乏持久的控制单元。相比之下，科学是通过思想不断积累的。在本工作中，我们提出ExpEvolve，将思想层面的提取与控制注入进化过程：它发现并利用解释不同程序评分差异的高层因素。我们刻画了在何种理论条件下，基于思想因子的发现能提升样本效率。我们还构建了一个实用框架，证明所提取的高层思想因子有助于指导经验驱动的利用以及在发现停滞时的探索。在11个开放式数学任务中，ExpEvolve显著提升了最终性能和样本效率，最高达5.0倍。该提升还迁移至另外4个LLM以及MLS-Bench中的6个机器学习任务。分析表明，ExpEvolve能够发现思想层面的因素，为进化发现提供更优的探索与利用策略。

Preference Is Not Permission: Personalizing the Auto-Purchase Line in Agentic Commerce
偏好不等于授权：在智能体电商中个性化自动购买边界
Personalized shopping benchmarks ask which item fits the user; we ask how far an agent may commit on that item, and pose learning the individual's auto-purchase line as a task. The boundary is evidence-conditioned and three-valued: whether committing is appropriate depends on the sufficiency of product information (warrant), reversibility, price against the user's norm, and urgency, not only on fit; and the natural output is don't buy, confirm first, or buy automatically. Across sixteen instruction-tuned models, autonomous commitment is misaligned with a human authorization reference in which fifteen annotators, role-playing the delegating principal from a given profile, choose the furthest action they would allow without confirmation: models commit even when fit is low and when their own evidence flag reports the warrant missing. This is overreach. The line is individual: these raters disagree, yet the disagreement is structured and partly predictable from a person's own other labels. A shallow learner on a few dozen of a person's own labels predicts their held-out auto-purchase line at a median of 0.79 by 30-40 labels, outperforming every evaluated prompt$\times$model configuration, including a per-annotator oracle over 80 configurations; the learned boundary does not transfer across domains. We release the counterfactual benchmark, human authorization reference, and annotation protocol after acceptance.
中文翻译
个性化购物基准测试关注哪个商品适合用户；我们则关注智能体在该商品上可自主承诺的程度，并将学习个体的自动购买边界作为一项任务。该边界是基于证据的三值判断：是否应进行承诺取决于产品信息充分性（担保）、可逆性、价格与用户标准的对比以及紧迫性，而不仅仅是商品匹配度；自然输出应为‘不买’、‘先确认’或‘自动购买’。在十六个指令微调模型中，自主承诺行为与人类授权参考存在偏差，其中十五名标注者以特定身份角色扮演委托人，选择他们无需确认即可允许的最远行动：模型即使在匹配度低且自身证据标记显示担保缺失时仍会承诺。这属于过度承诺。该边界具有个体差异性：这些标注者意见不一，但分歧具有结构且部分可由个人其他标签预测。仅用几十个个人标签的浅层学习模型即可在30-40个标签下以中位数0.79的准确率预测其未见的自动购买边界，优于所有评估的提示×模型配置，包括每标注者80种配置的最优oracle；但该学习边界无法跨领域迁移。我们将在论文接受后发布反事实基准、人类授权参考及标注协议。

Reasoning-Search-Augmented LLMs: An Operational Taxonomy, Benchmark Analysis and Controlled Evidence Synthesis
推理-搜索增强型大语言模型：操作分类、基准分析与受控证据整合
Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive emergent reasoning capabilities, yet they remain constrained by fixed model parameters and finite context windows. Even when augmented with static retrieval, they can produce statements that are unsupported by, or even contradictory to, retrieved evidence. This leads to hallucinations, difficulty incorporating up-to-date information, and failures to correctly interpret or reconcile sources. Recent work addresses these limitations by tightly coupling step-by-step reasoning with interactive search, treating retrieval as an action within an iterative agentic loop rather than a one-shot preprocessing step. This study reviews frameworks that implement such reasoning-search loops by interleaving planning, querying, retrieval, integration, verification, and stopping. We analyze these systems through four architectural axes: retrieval coupling, controller design, knowledge source, and grounding strictness. We separately treat the learning signal as a cross-cutting dimension because prompting, supervision, preference optimization, and reinforcement learning can instantiate the same architecture while producing materially different search, stopping, and grounding behavior. We further distinguish benchmark difficulty from RSA-loop demand and synthesize controlled within-study ablations and quality-cost deltas, avoiding invalid pooling across heterogeneous model backbones, retrievers, corpora, and evaluation protocols. Our goal is to clarify the landscape of reasoning-search-augmented LLMs and outline a unified perspective on the design choices shaping future research.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）展现出令人印象深刻的涌现推理能力，但仍受限于固定的模型参数和有限的上下文窗口。即使通过静态检索增强，它们仍可能生成与检索证据不符甚至矛盾的陈述，导致幻觉、难以融入最新信息，以及正确解读或调和来源的失败。近期工作通过将逐步推理与交互式搜索紧密结合，解决这些问题，将检索视为迭代智能体循环中的一个动作，而非一次性预处理步骤。本研究综述了实现此类推理-搜索循环的框架，其通过规划、查询、检索、整合、验证和停止等步骤的交错执行来运作。我们从四个架构维度分析这些系统：检索耦合方式、控制器设计、知识源和接地严格性。我们还将学习信号视为一个交叉维度，因为提示、监督、偏好优化和强化学习可在相同架构下产生显著不同的搜索、停止和接地行为。我们进一步区分基准难度与RSA循环需求，并合成受控的内部消融实验与质量-成本差异，避免在异构模型主干、检索器、语料库和评估协议之间无效合并。我们的目标是厘清推理-搜索增强型LLMs的研究格局，并为未来研究的设计选择提供统一视角。
👤 Biswas Poudel, Nilson Chapagain, Amit Kumar, Xianshun Jiang 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

TeachBack: Learning to Teach via Bidirectional Preference Optimization for On-Policy Self-Distillation
TeachBack：通过双向偏好优化实现策略内自蒸馏中的教学学习
On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) enables a language model to learn from a Teacher instantiated from the same model but given access to privileged information, such as a reference solution. We find that access to sufficient privileged information allows the Teacher to generate highly accurate trajectories; however, accuracy alone does not guarantee that these trajectories are suitable for Student learning. Direct access to a complete solution may encourage shortened, reference-conditioned derivations that omit the exploration, verification, and self-correction required to solve problems without privileged information. We introduce TeachBack, a bidirectional preference-learning framework for on-policy self-distillation that learns not only from the Teacher but also how the Teacher should teach. TeachBack shapes the Teacher's generation behavior by controlling the length of the reference-reasoning suffix injected into the prompt and by varying the prompt style. While maintaining comparable Teacher accuracy, it encourages the Teacher to explore and re-solve problems, thereby reducing its reliance on the reference solution. At each rollout refresh, the shared policy generates both a privileged Teacher trajectory and a problem-only Student trajectory. A pairwise preference objective uses privileged Teacher trajectories to improve Student learning, while the reversed preference uses the Student's behavior to shape future Teacher supervision under the privileged condition. Experiments on mathematical and logical reasoning tasks show that TeachBack consistently improves Student performance over existing OPSD methods and related baselines.
中文翻译
策略内自蒸馏（OPSD）使语言模型能够从一个教师模型中学习，该教师模型由同一模型实例生成，但可访问特权信息（如参考解法）。我们发现，充足的特权信息使教师能够生成高度准确的轨迹；然而，准确性本身并不能保证这些轨迹适合学生学习。直接访问完整解法可能促使教师生成简化的、依赖参考的推导路径，从而省略在无特权信息条件下解决问题所需的探索、验证和自我修正过程。为此，我们提出TeachBack，一种用于策略内自蒸馏的双向偏好学习框架，不仅学习教师的输出，还学习教师应该如何教学。TeachBack通过控制注入提示中的参考推理后缀长度以及调整提示风格来塑造教师的生成行为。在保持教师准确率相当的同时，鼓励教师探索并重新求解问题，从而减少对参考解法的依赖。在每次滚动刷新时，共享策略同时生成一个带有特权信息的教师轨迹和一个仅含问题的学生轨迹。成对偏好目标利用特权教师轨迹改进学生学习，而反向偏好则利用学生的行为主动塑造未来在特权条件下的教师监督。在数学和逻辑推理任务上的实验表明，TeachBack在学生性能上持续优于现有OPSD方法及相关基线。
👤 Linfei Ji, Jiayun Li, Haozhao Wang, Siyu Jiang, Zehao Wang et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv

VeriEvolve: Evaluator-First Evolution for Tool-Using Agents
VeriEvolve：面向工具使用智能体的评估者优先进化
A central obstacle to self-improving agents is the absence of a reliable fitness signal for realistic stateful tasks. We introduce \textsc{VeriEvolve}, an evaluator-first evolution framework that constructs this missing evaluation layer before optimizing the agent. From task instruction, VeriEvolve synthesizes instruction-grounded challenge states and an executable evaluator that checks them. During each round of workflow evolution, the evaluator is frozen and serves as the fitness signal. Between rounds, VeriEvolve revises the evaluator by converting the end states of strong and weak runs into a pair of counterfactual test cases. Across all 30 MCPMark Filesystem tasks, VeriEvolve constructs 30 evaluators with 141 challenge states, achieving 141/141 agreement on the generated suites and 113/122 agreement on held-out states produced by executor agents. Evaluator-guided evolution increases externally verified success from 6/30 to 18/30 for GLM-5.2 and from 27/30 to 30/30 for Claude; refinement removes all three false positives that the weak executor exposes. Cross-environment studies on PostgreSQL, Playwright, Notion, and GitHub further expose complementary limits from observability, state representation, and search/execution capacity. These results show that executable evaluators can be synthesized and used to guide search when task success is observable and an external verifier is available for calibration.
中文翻译

FlashNext-RL: A System Optimization Study for Long-Horizon Agentic RL of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next
FlashNext-RL：Qwen3.8-Flash-Next长时程智能体强化学习的系统优化研究
Long-horizon agentic reinforcement learning (RL) requires both efficient rollout generation and training on long sequences. However, the memory requirements and model-specific operators of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next make this difficult with existing open-source implementations. To resolve these limitations, we introduce FlashNext-RL, a model-specific RL training optimization framework based on prime-rl, with vLLM for software engineering (SWE) rollout generation. Our main focus is BF16 training of the full 48-layer model on a single 8xB200 node, with the first two layers, the n-gram table, the sparse-attention indexer and the multi-token prediction head frozen. We optimize host offload, gradient accumulation and the kernels for mixture-of-experts, sparse attention and the gated residual stream. In the full-model development study, non-padding training throughput increases from approximately 395 tokens/s/GPU in the initial 32K-token configuration to 4,438 tokens/s/GPU in the optimized 131K-token configuration, with hardware matmul throughput (aka without considering the cost of attention components) increasing from approximately 20 to 213 TFLOPs/GPU. A controlled cumulative study on an 8-layer model further quantifies the contributions of individual optimizations. Together with three inference nodes, the system supports a live SWE RL run with a target batch of 1,024 trainable rollouts and a 131,072-token context limit. Over steps 5-15, an iteration takes 22.4 minutes on average, with bounded entropy and trainer-sampler mismatch KL. These results highlight the importance of tailoring RL training to the model's architecture, memory layout and kernel requirements, rather than relying only on general-purpose RL infrastructure.

Where Should a Stuck Agent Spend Its Next Tokens? Causal Attribution of Inference-Time Resources
卡住的智能体下一步应如何分配其下一个token？推理时资源的因果归因
Inference-time scaling is often treated as a token-budget problem, but a failed coding agent can seek qualitatively different resources: computation, information, or feedback. We introduce Banzhaf Resource Attribution (BRA), a controlled factorial framework that measures each resource's marginal contribution to repair and its interactions while holding the failed state and revision protocol fixed. We evaluate six models on 194 function-level failures from MBPP+ and HumanEval+ and 126 repository-level failures from SWE-bench Verified. Task-relevant specifications consistently improve repair, whereas public-test feedback provides small gains and retrieved code little to none. The value of additional computation declines with model capability, while information remains useful; resource channels largely exhibit diminishing returns, although complementary information can reinforce itself. Yet three of four frontier models select feedback in 95-100% of cases and obtain lower gains than uniform random resource selection. BRA thus exposes a gap between the resources agents prefer and those that measurably improve repair.

Measure First, Then Allocate: Calibrated Rollout Budgets for MCP Agents in Reinforcement Learning
先度量，再分配：面向MCP智能体在强化学习中的校准滚动预算
Training tool-using language models requires costly interaction, yet identical rewards within a rollout group provide no group-relative learning signal. We use reward disagreement to guide harness evaluation and the allocation of rollouts for policy training in Model Context Protocol (MCP) environments. We first introduce Discord, which tests harness edits using exact paired inference and decision-preserving early stopping: only disagreeing paired outcomes favor one harness over another. This insight motivates prospective allocation in BRACE (Bank-calibrated Rollout Allocation under Correlated Episodes). BRACE combines historical task evidence, uncertainty in task success rates, and a correlated reward model to forecast mixed-reward groups before allocating generation budget. Controlled harness experiments favor Discord, while live MCP experiments expose a retention-cost trade-off. At three Qwen3-VL model scales, BRACE exceeds the strongest plotted baseline peaks with fewer new training rollouts; historical-bank collection is counted separately. It also leads the compared methods on BFCL and NESTFUL without further training. Ablations probe the bank, estimator, and allocation score; prospective evaluation checks forecast calibration. These results support forecasting reward contrast before generation.

Automating ML for Science: Can Frontier Agents Climb Scientific Hills in the Wild?
自动化机器学习用于科学：前沿智能体能否在真实环境中攀登科学高峰？
Frontier coding agents are increasingly framed as end-to-end automated researchers, and ML for science offers a demanding test of that promise: improving a scientific model often requires domain insight beyond routine ML engineering. We introduce Agent4Sci, a long-horizon testbed for measuring how far today's agents can improve the published best models on scientific ML benchmarks, spanning 20 benchmarks across 11 domains. Rather than ranking agents on a leaderboard, Agent4Sci asks what kinds of progress agents can make on these tasks under in-the-wild conditions, and where they fall short. Empirically, today's frontier agents advance the published best on every benchmark, with Claude Opus reaching 10.4% mean relative improvement and no run flagged for academic misconduct. These gains, however, come almost entirely from a bag of ML-engineering tricks rather than research-level insight. Trajectory analysis surfaces three recurring limitations in this setting: novelty miscalibration, resource under-utilization, and rapid collapse into local engineering sweeps. Lightweight plug-in skills for research ideation, experiment management, and scheduled checklists raise Claude Opus from 10.4% to 20.1% and Codex from 4.4% to 8.1%, while yielding more diverse, domain-relevant interventions. Agent4Sci gives the community a way to track and push frontier agents from ML engineering toward domain insight on real-world ML-for-science problems. Code and data are available on GitHub (https://github.com/maszhongming/agent4sci) and Hugging Face (https://huggingface.co/datasets/MingZhong/agent4sci).

TAGR: Task-Adaptive Geo-Semantic Retrieval across Heterogeneous Urban Delivery Tasks
TAGR：跨异构城市配送任务的任务自适应地理语义检索
As urban residents demand higher-quality instant delivery services, demand forecasting, estimated time of arrival prediction, and next-stop prediction have become critical tasks in urban delivery systems. However, the three tasks rely on historical evidence with substantially different structures and granularities. Meanwhile, their prediction targets differ in output form, making it difficult to integrate retrieved evidence through a single fusion mechanism without causing mismatch or negative transfer. These issues can increase prediction errors and subsequently lead to imbalanced resource allocation, where some regions experience unmet demand. In contrast, others suffer from underutilization, ultimately degrading overall efficiency and service quality. To address these challenges, we propose TAGR, a Task-Adaptive Geo-Semantic Retrieval framework across heterogeneous urban delivery tasks. TAGR constructs an LLM-based geo-semantic shared prior and task-specific retrieval banks to share regional knowledge while preserving heterogeneous task-specific evidence. The LLM-derived regional representations encode geographic context and semantic relations among regions, providing transferable knowledge for all three tasks without requiring online LLM inference. It further introduces a task-adaptive retrieval augmentation module to accommodate heterogeneous output structures, thereby mitigating information mismatch and negative transfer caused by a uniform retrieval-fusion strategy. Experiments on three city datasets demonstrate that TAGR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across multiple tasks.
中文翻译
随着城市居民对高质量即时配送服务的需求上升，需求预测、预计到达时间预测和下一停靠点预测已成为城市配送系统中的关键任务。然而，这三个任务依赖的历史证据具有显著不同的结构和粒度。同时，它们的预测目标在输出形式上也存在差异，使得仅通过单一融合机制整合检索证据变得困难，容易引发不匹配或负迁移。这些问题会增加预测误差，进而导致资源配置失衡，某些区域出现未满足需求，而其他区域则面临资源闲置，最终降低整体效率和服务质量。为应对这些挑战，我们提出TAGR，一种跨异构城市配送任务的任务自适应地理语义检索框架。TAGR构建基于大语言模型的地理语义共享先验和任务特定的检索库，以共享区域知识的同时保留异构任务特有的证据。由大语言模型生成的区域表征编码了地理上下文和区域间的语义关系，为所有三项任务提供可迁移的知识，且无需在线大语言模型推理。此外，引入任务自适应检索增强模块以适配异构输出结构，从而缓解统一检索-融合策略引起的资讯不匹配和负迁移。在三个城市数据集上的实验表明，TAGR在多个任务上持续优于现有最先进基线。

[2610.26542]

Breaking Diffusion: Parallel Reasoning with Draft Refinement
突破扩散：通过草稿精炼实现并行推理
Drafting an answer allows a reasoning model to reconsider each decision in the context of the others. Yet repeated diffusion denoising does not ensure that individually plausible token updates form a better answer together. We introduce Draft Refinement Language Models (DRLMs), which use a single denoiser to propose parallel revisions and evaluate a candidate draft. We train the denoiser to reconstruct every token from uniformly corrupted drafts, teaching both preservation and repair. Its predictions specify revisions directly, without a diffusion reverse transition. Evaluation requires a different use of these predictions: confidence in an existing token reflects both contextual support and evidence from observing the token itself. Using the known corruption process, we correct for this self-token evidence to estimate how well each token is supported by the prompt and the remaining draft. Aggregating these estimates yields a cavity energy computed in one forward pass, without an additional scoring model. Each round proposes a revision, reads the completed candidate in its revised context, and retains it only if its energy is lower. Across Countdown, Sudoku-Extreme, and Maze-Hard, our method outperforms autoregressive and discrete diffusion baselines. With 6M parameters, DRLM reaches 44.4% on Countdown-5 and 93.9% on Sudoku-Extreme. The results establish complete-draft parallel refinement as an effective alternative to reverse diffusion for complex reasoning.
中文翻译
草稿撰写允许推理模型在整体上下文中重新审视每个决策。然而，重复的扩散去噪并不能确保单个合理的词元更新组合后能形成更优的答案。我们引入了草稿精炼语言模型（DRLMs），利用单一去噪器同时提出并行修订方案，并评估候选草稿。我们训练去噪器从均匀损坏的草稿中重建每个词元，从而同时学习保留与修复能力。其预测直接指定修订内容，无需扩散的逆向过程。评估时需不同地使用这些预测：现有词元的置信度既反映上下文支持，也来自观察该词元自身的证据。利用已知的损坏过程，我们校正这种自词元证据，以估计每个词元由提示和剩余草稿支持的程度。聚合这些估计可在一个前向传播中计算出腔体能量，无需额外评分模型。每轮提出修订，以修订后的上下文读取完整候选答案，并仅在能量降低时保留。在Countdown、Sudoku-Extreme和Maze-Hard任务上，该方法优于自回归和离散扩散基线。参数量为600万的DRLM在Countdown-5上达到44.4%准确率，在Sudoku-Extreme上达到93.9%。结果表明，完整草稿的并行精炼是复杂推理中替代反向扩散的有效方法。
👤 Jaesang Yu, Seoyeon Byun, Yun Wang, Yan Lu, Juho Kim 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv

[2610.30067]
GoiBERT: Compact Vietnamese Language Models via Two-Stage Knowledge Distillation
GoiBERT：通过两阶段知识蒸馏构建紧凑的越南语语言模型
Vietnamese pre-trained encoders have improved rapidly, but the strongest ones are large, multilingual-vocabulary models or depend on external word segmentation, which makes them costly to deploy. We present GoiBERT, a family of two compact Vietnamese encoders with 26M and 49M parameters that operate directly on raw text. GoiBERT learns from two complementary teachers. A same-tokenizer Vietnamese encoder first teaches general-purpose representations. A much larger teacher then provides task-level guidance through soft labels that are generated without ever labeling its own training data. Across eight benchmarks covering text classification, aspect-based sentiment analysis, natural language inference, and named entity recognition, GoiBERT-large achieves the highest average score among non-teacher models. It does so with fewer than half the parameters of the strongest compact baseline and twice its throughput. GoiBERT-base retains 99% of that baseline's performance at a quarter of its size. Our analysis shows that compression is nearly lossless for sentiment and classification tasks but remains costly for inference and entity recognition, which points to clear directions for future work. Our code and models are available at: https://github.com/Nhantran65/GoiBERT.git
中文翻译
越南语预训练编码器发展迅速，但最强的模型通常是大型多语言词汇模型，或依赖外部词分割，导致部署成本高昂。我们提出了GoiBERT，一系列参数量为26M和49M的紧凑越南语编码器，可直接处理原始文本。GoiBERT从两个互补的教师模型中学习：一个同分词器的越南语编码器首先传授通用表示；一个更大规模的教师则通过从未标记自身训练数据的情况下生成的软标签提供任务级指导。在涵盖文本分类、基于方面的情感分析、自然语言推断和命名实体识别的八个基准上，GoiBERT-large在非教师模型中取得最高平均得分。其参数量不到最强紧凑基线的一半，吞吐量却达到其两倍。GoiBERT-base在四分之一大小下保留了基线99%的性能。我们的分析表明，压缩对情感和分类任务几乎无损，但在推理和实体识别任务上仍具成本，指明了未来工作的明确方向。代码与模型已发布于：https://github.com/Nhantran65/GoiBERT.git。
👤 Quang Anh Kiet Tran, Nam Anh Tran, Manh Hung Trinh, Thanh Danh Le, Khang Nhat Hoang Vo et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29896]
Investigating the Impact of Learner Errors on Social Bias in Large Language Models
探究学习者错误对大型语言模型中社会偏见的影响
Existing social bias benchmarks evaluate large language models mostly on fluent, well-formed English, reflecting native speaker usage and leaving non-native writers unrepresented. To address this gap, we investigate how grammatical errors by non-native writers affect social bias. We develop a controlled framework that injects common learner errors into prompts and apply it to three bias benchmarks covering stereotype alignment and demographic preference across gender, race, and religion. We find that grammatical errors increase bias, and the effect grows with the error rate. Among the four common error types we consider, preposition errors produce the largest and most consistent increase in stereotype alignment. Our analysis suggests that errors increase bias when they are more likely to interfere with comprehension. Finally, fine-tuning on erroneous texts reduces the bias increase caused by grammatical errors. Our findings show that bias evaluations based exclusively on well-formed prompts may underestimate the bias experienced by users whose English contains grammatical errors.
中文翻译
现有的社会偏见基准测试主要基于流畅、语法正确的英语，反映了母语者的使用习惯，导致非母语写作者未被充分代表。为弥补这一空白，我们研究了非母语写作者的语法错误如何影响社会偏见。我们构建了一个受控框架，将常见的学习者错误注入提示，并将其应用于三个涵盖性别、种族和宗教方面刻板印象一致性和人口偏好偏好的偏见基准。我们发现，语法错误会加剧偏见，且影响随错误率增加而增大。在我们考虑的四种常见错误类型中，介词错误导致刻板印象一致性最大且最稳定的提升。我们的分析表明，当错误更可能干扰理解时，偏见会增加。最后，对有错误文本进行微调可减少由语法错误引起的偏见增加。研究结果表明，仅基于语法正确提示的偏见评估可能会低估那些英语存在语法错误的用户所经历的偏见。

[2610.29843]
Every Part Has Its Role: Part-of-Speech Effects in LLM Post-Training
每个成分都有其作用：词性在大语言模型后训练中的影响
Large language models are typically trained with next-token prediction (NTP), which rewards predicting specific tokens rather than explicitly accounting for the semantic similarities between different words or expressions. Prior work has addressed this limitation by augmenting training data with minimally varying sentences that replace a target word with conceptually related alternatives, e.g., "The tired coach ran home" $\rightarrow$ {"The tired trainer ran home", "The tired instructor ran home", ...}. Such concept augmentation improves performance, but it remains unclear whether its effectiveness depends on where in the sentence the concept is expressed. We reproduce and extend prior work $\textit{beyond nouns}$ to verbs and adjectives, asking whether the part of speech (POS) carrying the concept signal affects perplexity and downstream performance. We find different patterns across evaluation settings: concept augmentation improves perplexity most consistently on news, independent of the POS used, whereas verbs provide the most reliable downstream gains. Despite these behavioral differences, representational analyses using CKA and linear probing show that the conditions yielding the largest downstream gains are not necessarily those that most alter the model's internal representations. Together, these results suggest that the effectiveness of concept augmentation depends on where and how conceptual information is introduced during training.
中文翻译
大型语言模型通常采用下一个词预测（NTP）进行训练，这奖励的是预测特定词元，而非显式考虑不同词语或表达之间的语义相似性。先前的研究通过在训练数据中加入最小变化的句子来补充，用概念相关的替代词替换目标词，例如“疲惫的教练跑回家”→{“疲惫的教练跑回家”，“疲惫的导师跑回家”，...}。这种概念增强能提升性能，但其有效性是否取决于概念在句子中的位置仍不明确。我们扩展先前工作，不仅限于名词，还涵盖了动词和形容词，探讨承载概念信号的词性（POS）是否影响困惑度和下游性能。我们发现不同评估设置下存在不同的模式：在新闻数据上，概念增强对困惑度的改善最为一致，且不受所用词性影响；而动词则带来最可靠的下游收益。尽管行为表现存在差异，但使用CKA和线性探测的表征分析显示，带来最大下游收益的条件并不一定是最显著改变模型内部表征的条件。这些结果共同表明，概念增强的有效性取决于概念信息在训练过程中引入的位置和方式。
👤 Laya Iyer, Chen Shani 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29832]
S-EDL: Eliciting Self-Evidence from Sequence Likelihoods for Semantic Calibration of LLMs
S-EDL：从序列似然中引出自证证据以实现大语言模型的语义校准
Calibrating large language models (LLMs) in open-ended generation is uniquely challenging because many distinct token sequences can express the same underlying meaning. Prior calibration techniques target either fixed-set classification confidence or the binary correctness of individual strings, making them inherently ill-suited for the dynamic, multi-string nature of open-ended semantic outcomes. To bridge this gap, we introduce S-EDL, a novel evidential method that natively optimizes LLMs for semantic calibration. By eliciting evidence from the sequence likelihoods of the model itself, S-EDL constructs a differentiable Dirichlet prior over prompt-specific semantic classes. Optimizing the evidential loss under this prior yields a calibration-aware training signal over variable semantic classes, completely bypassing the need for fixed multiple-choice options. Evaluations across four open-ended benchmarks and three models demonstrate that S-EDL substantially reduces semantic calibration error while improving accuracy. By aligning generative likelihoods with semantic correctness, this work establishes a framework for deploying trustworthy LLMs in complex, open-ended applications. Code is available at: https://github.com/sandylaker/s-edl.
中文翻译
在开放式生成中校准大型语言模型（LLMs）具有独特挑战性，因为许多不同的词元序列可以表达相同的潜在含义。以往的校准技术要么针对固定分类任务的置信度，要么针对单个字符串的二元正确性，因此本质上不适合开放式语义结果的动态多串特性。为弥合这一差距，我们提出S-EDL，一种原生优化LLM进行语义校准的新颖证据方法。通过从模型自身的序列似然中提取证据，S-EDL构建了一个针对特定提示的语义类别的可微分狄利克雷先验。在此先验下优化证据损失，产生一个面向可变语义类别的校准感知训练信号，完全无需固定多项选择选项。在四个开放式基准和三种模型上的评估表明，S-EDL显著降低了语义校准误差，同时提升了准确性。通过使生成似然与语义正确性对齐，本工作建立了一个在复杂开放式应用中部署可信LLM的框架。代码已公开：https://github.com/sandylaker/s-edl。
👤 Yawei Li, Jiazheng Li, David Rügamer, Bernd Bischl, Yulan He et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29681]
Scaling Multilingual Pretraining of Pixel Language Models
像素语言模型多语言预训练的扩展
Pixel language models process text as rendered images instead of a finite vocabulary, which gives them strong cross-lingual transfer abilities. However, not much is known about how these abilities are influenced by larger-scale pretraining. In this paper, we train a family of MPIXEL models along three axes of variation: more languages, more scripts, and larger model capacity under fixed per-language compute. In evaluations on five tasks covering 83 languages, we find that scaling acts locally: each axis improves the added languages and scripts, while increased capacity mitigates the curse of multilinguality from pretraining on more languages. Transfer to unseen scripts improves with multilingual pretraining, but does not further improve with scaling. On typologically distant low-resource languages, our models outperform subword and character-level baselines across many writing systems. We also show that larger pixel models reserve more layers for visual processing, and that cross-lingual alignment weakens as more languages are added. Overall, our findings characterize how multilingual scaling improves pixel language models.
中文翻译
像素语言模型将文本作为渲染图像处理，而非有限词汇表，因此具备强大的跨语言迁移能力。然而，这种能力如何受到更大规模预训练的影响仍不明确。本文沿三个维度扩展训练了一组MPIXEL模型：更多语言、更多书写系统，以及在每语言计算量固定下的更大模型容量。在覆盖83种语言的五个任务上进行评估，发现扩展呈局部效应：每个维度仅改善新增语言和书写系统的性能，而更大的模型容量则缓解了因多语言预训练带来的多语言诅咒问题。对未见书写系统的迁移能力随多语言预训练提升，但进一步扩展并未带来改进。在语言类型差异大且资源稀缺的语言上，我们的模型在多种书写系统中均优于子词和字符级基线。此外，我们还发现更大的像素模型会分配更多层用于视觉处理，且随着语言数量增加，跨语言对齐能力减弱。总体而言，我们的研究揭示了多语言扩展如何影响像素语言模型的性能。

[2610.29657]
Effects of Model Scale and Quantization on Social Bias and Abstention in Question Answering
模型规模与量化对问答中社会偏见与回避行为的影响
The deployment of small and quantized language models in resource-constrained settings raises an important fairness question: when a compressed model produces fewer biased responses, does this reflect fairer behavior or greater abstention? We study this problem in Question Answering (QA) using the BBQ benchmark across multiple model sizes and quantization settings from Qwen3, Gemma-3, and SmolLM2. We also examine whether the observed trends remain stable across alternative prompt formulations and when the ``Unknown'' option is removed in a forced-choice setting. Our results show that larger models achieved higher QA accuracy, but bias showed no simple scaling trend and quantization effects varied across configurations. Notably, 20 of the 26 configurations with fewer biased responses also show increased over-abstention, indicating that lower observed bias can partly reflect greater abstention. These findings show that lower observed bias can partly reflect greater abstention, and that fairness evaluation should consider biased responses, abstention, prompt formulation, and answer-option design jointly.
中文翻译
在资源受限环境下部署小型化和量化后的语言模型引发一个重要公平性问题：当压缩模型产生更少偏见响应时，这反映的是更公平的行为，还是更高的回避倾向？我们通过BBQ基准，在Qwen3、Gemma-3和SmolLM2等多个模型尺寸和量化设置下研究该问题。我们还考察了观察到的趋势在不同提示格式下是否稳定，以及在强制选择设置中移除“未知”选项后是否依然成立。结果表明，更大模型实现了更高的问答准确率，但偏见并无简单缩放趋势，量化影响因配置而异。值得注意的是，26个配置中有20个表现出更少偏见响应的同时也出现更高程度的过度回避，表明较低的观测偏见可能部分源于更高的回避行为。这些发现说明，较低的观测偏见可能反映更高的回避，公平性评估应综合考虑偏见响应、回避行为、提示设计和回答选项设置。

[2610.29645]
LLMs as survey respondents: Comparison to simple semantic baselines
大语言模型作为调查受访者：与简单语义基线的对比
Large language models (LLMs) have been explored as surrogates for human survey respondents. A more promising application is data augmentation, where LLMs predict new responses from partial human data. However, the semantic similarity between questionnaire items is a known source of correlation between answers. It is thus unclear whether LLMs actually infer meaningful relationships between question-response pairs, or use surface-level comparison between questions as a heuristic. To assess this, we evaluated how three LLMs can predict responses to held-out items in two surveys measuring attitudes toward automated or light-electric vehicles, based on varying proportions of known responses. Performance was compared to two baselines, where new responses were derived by maximizing embedding-similarity to a prior response. While LLMs mostly predicted human responses better than baselines, their performance consistently declined with a lower share of known responses, even aligning with the baseline in one survey. Our results show that LLMs' capacities for augmenting replies varied across surveys, and can revert to the same levels as simpler embedding similarity baselines when less data is available.
中文翻译

The evaluation results highlight both the potential of these approaches and the remaining challenges in patient attribution and phenotype normalization from full-text biomedical literature. The post-evaluation phase remains open at https://tianchi.aliyun.com/competition/entrance/532512.

A Diagnostic Study of Faithfulness in Diffusion Language Models for Table-to-Text Generation
扩散语言模型在表格到文本生成中忠实性的诊断研究
Diffusion language models generate by iteratively denoising a masked sequence under bidirectional attention, without the strict left-to-right commitment of autoregressive decoding. Whether this difference affects how reliably they remain grounded in structured inputs is largely unexplored. We study this question in table-to-text generation using ToTTo, where highlighted source cells make factual grounding directly testable. We compare the masked diffusion models MDLM and LLaDA with GPT-2 and GPT-Neo under an identical retrieval-based few-shot protocol, together with a QLoRA-adapted LLaDA, and we score reference similarity and source grounding as separate axes. The two pull apart. Within LLaDA, additional demonstrations raise BLEU from 11.3 to 22.0 while NLI entailment against the source table falls from 0.78 to 0.52; QLoRA reaches the best BLEU of the study, 24.15, and grounds no better for it. Manual annotation shows the failure modes differ in kind: the autoregressive baselines tend to wander off the table, while LLaDA-based models more often stay on topic and swap out individual entities. Reference-based metrics alone are insufficient to assess faithfulness in table-to-text generation.

[2610.29545]
PACE: Periodic and Channel-Aligned Encoding of Four-Bit Recurrent States
PACE：四比特循环状态的周期性与通道对齐编码
Linear-attention language models keep their context in a recurrent state of fixed size, where an inference system stores one copy of this state for every sequence it decodes at the same time. Storing the state at lower precision therefore lets more sequences share an accelerator. Existing state quantizers round the state after every generated token and report that four bits lose accuracy unless the bit width is raised or the model is fine-tuned.\par We introduce PACE, a post-hoc four-bit state quantizer. PACE rounds the state only once every few generated tokens, as chunked kernels already do once per chunk during prefill, and keeps the tokens generated in between in a small buffer. Rounding less often reduces the error that accumulates in the state. Additionally, we find that the large entries of the gated-delta state gather in columns, because some channels of the update written at each token remain large across many tokens. PACE therefore shares each quantization scale within one column. It also keeps the heads that retain rounding error longest at eight bits. On four hybrid models from 4B to 35B parameters, PACE with mixed four- and eight-bit heads is competitive with eight-bit DAMP on the three larger models at about two thirds of its memory, PACE with four-bit heads throughout scores higher than Q-Mamba at equal memory, and rounding every few tokens preserves the decoding speedup of the buffer.
中文翻译
线性注意力语言模型将上下文保存在一个固定大小的循环状态中，推理系统为同时解码的每个序列存储一份该状态副本。因此，降低状态存储精度可使更多序列共享加速器。现有状态量化器在每个生成标记后对状态进行舍入，且报告称四比特精度会导致准确性下降，除非提高位宽或微调模型。 我们提出 PACE，一种后处理的四比特状态量化器。PACE 每生成若干标记才进行一次舍入，如同分块内核在预填充阶段每块仅舍入一次，并将中间生成的标记暂存于小缓冲区。减少舍入频率可降低状态中累积的误差。此外，我们发现门控-增量状态的大值集中在列上，因为每个标记写入的更新中某些通道在多个标记间持续保持较大值。因此，PACE 在同一列内共享量化尺度，并保留舍入误差持续时间最长的头使用八比特。在四个参数规模从 4B 到 35B 的混合模型上，采用混合四比特和八比特头的 PACE 在三个较大模型上性能与八比特 DAMP 相当，内存仅为后者的约三分之二；全四比特头的 PACE 在相同内存下优于 Q-Mamba；每几轮舍入一次的策略也保持了缓冲区带来的解码加速优势。
👤 Patrick Haller, Filipe Laitenberger, Alan Akbik 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29518]
Mitigating Query Shortcuts in Chain-of-Thought with Context-Aware Decoding
通过上下文感知解码缓解思维链中的查询捷径
While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting improves the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs), recent studies have shown that final answers do not always depend on the generated rationales. In particular, LLMs may rely on query-specific shortcuts rather than the information provided by their reasoning steps. In this paper, we investigate whether context-aware decoding (CAD), an existing contrastive decoding method, can mitigate such shortcut behavior when applied to CoT reasoning. We study this application as Rationale Contrastive Decoding (RCD), which contrasts the next-token distributions conditioned on the query with and without its rationale, amplifying tokens whose likelihood increases due to the rationale. We provide a theoretical interpretation of this decoding rule via a KL-regularized reward-maximization objective. Experiments across five reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RCD improves answer accuracy over standard CoT. Furthermore, intervention analyses using ground-truth and random rationales reveal that RCD increases the behavioral dependence of final answers on the rationale content. Our findings suggest that contrastive decoding offers a simple, training-free approach to mitigating query shortcuts.
中文翻译
尽管思维链（CoT）提示能提升大语言模型（LLMs）的推理性能，但近期研究表明，最终答案并不总是依赖于生成的推理过程。具体而言，LLMs 可能依赖于查询本身的捷径，而非推理步骤提供的信息。本文研究现有对比解码方法——上下文感知解码（CAD）在 CoT 推理中是否能缓解此类捷径行为。我们将此应用称为推理对比解码（RCD），通过对比有无推理过程条件下的下一个标记分布，放大因推理过程而概率上升的标记。我们通过 KL 正则化的奖励最大化目标提供了该解码规则的理论解释。在五个推理基准上的实验表明，RCD 相较于标准 CoT 提升了答案准确率。此外，使用真实与随机推理过程的干预分析显示，RCD 增强了最终答案对推理内容的依赖性。我们的研究结果表明，对比解码提供了一种简单、无需训练的方法来缓解查询捷径问题。
👤 Shin'ya Yamaguchi 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29424]
A KAN-based Non-linear Factorization for Embedding Layer Compression
基于KAN的非线性分解用于嵌入层压缩
Among existing embedding compression approaches, low-rank factorization provides a simple and mathematically principled solution by approximating the embedding matrix with a reduced-rank decomposition. However, its practical effectiveness is often limited, as the resulting linear bottleneck constrains representational capacity. To overcome these limitations, we propose an enhanced factorization scheme that increases expressive power by introducing learnable non-linear projections within the compressed embedding layer. Our method is inspired by the Kolmogorov Arnold Networks (KAN) paradigm, which replaces fixed linear transformations with learnable univariate functions. Despite promising results in other domains, applications of KANs in NLP remain scarce and have so far been largely limited to final or task specific layers, rather than core representational components such as embedding layers. We evaluate the proposed approach by integrating the compressed embedding layer into the Skip-gram framework and comparing it against a standard low-rank baseline under matched parameter budgets on the Word Analogy and Semantic Similarity benchmarks. We further introduce an effective initialization strategy from a pre-trained, uncompressed embedding layer. The behavior of the model is analyzed both immediately after initialization and after a brief post-initialization refinement phase, considering training with the standard Skip-gram objective as well as variants augmented with distillation from the original embedding layer. Overall, our results show that augmenting low-rank factorization with learnable non-linear projections effectively mitigates the limitations of linear bottlenecks, yielding more faithful compressed embeddings under fixed dimensionality and suggesting a promising direction for embedding layer compression beyond purely linear methods.
中文翻译
在现有的嵌入压缩方法中，低秩分解通过用低秩分解近似嵌入矩阵，提供了一种简单且数学上合理的方法。然而，其实际效果常受限于线性瓶颈，限制了表达能力。为克服这些局限，我们提出了一种增强型分解方案，通过在压缩的嵌入层中引入可学习的非线性投影来提升表达能力。该方法受到Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）范式的启发，用可学习的一元函数替代固定的线性变换。尽管在其他领域表现出色，KAN在自然语言处理中的应用仍十分有限，且主要局限于最终层或特定任务层，而非核心表征组件如嵌入层。我们通过将压缩嵌入层集成到Skip-gram框架中，并在Word Analogy和语义相似性基准上与标准低秩基线进行对比，在匹配参数预算下评估了该方法。我们还引入了一种从预训练未压缩嵌入层出发的有效初始化策略。分析了模型在初始化后立即以及经过短暂后初始化优化阶段的行为，考虑了标准Skip-gram目标及结合原始嵌入层蒸馏的变体。总体而言，我们的结果表明，通过引入可学习的非线性投影来增强低秩分解，能有效缓解线性瓶颈的限制，在固定维度下产生更忠实的压缩嵌入，为超越纯线性方法的嵌入层压缩提供了有前景的方向。

[2610.29413]
Beyond Small Talk: Modeling Disclosure Trajectories in Human-AI Conversation
超越闲聊：建模人机对话中的披露轨迹
Recently, we see a rise in conversational agents as a form of social and emotional support, where disclosure will unfold over time. Yet, current evaluations of these agents typically examine isolated conversational states. In analyses of naturally occurring conversations, disclosure history and current disclosure are confounded which tends to make their effects difficult to isolate. We thus introduce Trajectory-based Disclosure Evaluation (TraDE), an evaluation framework grounded in Social Penetration Theory that utilizes predefined disclosure trajectories and evaluates how preceding disclosure history shapes model behavior under matched current disclosure. Through the use of TraDE, we demonstrate across multiple models and relational behavior benchmarks that responses to disclosures matched on depth can differ depending on the preceding trajectory, particularly in whether models accept or challenge the user's framing. We also observed that these differences track accumulated exposure to deeper disclosure rather than any single moment of vulnerability or the order of disclosures. It also tends to persists for several turns after disclosure returns to shallow levels. For several behaviors, we also demonstrate that models diverge most consistently from human supporters in the Emotional Support Conversation corpus (ESConv) not when users open up but rather when they step back in their disclosure. TraDE thus provides a systematic way to expose history-dependent relational behavior that evaluations of isolated conversational states would otherwise miss.
中文翻译
近年来，对话代理作为社会与情感支持形式日益普及，其中披露内容随时间逐步展开。然而，当前对这类代理的评估通常只考察孤立的对话状态。在自然发生的对话分析中，披露历史与当前披露相互混淆，使得二者的影响难以分离。为此，我们提出了基于披露轨迹的评估框架（TraDE），其理论基础为社会渗透理论，采用预定义的披露轨迹，评估先前披露历史如何在相同当前披露水平下塑造模型行为。通过TraDE，我们在多个模型和关系行为基准上证明，即使在披露深度匹配的情况下，响应也会因先前轨迹的不同而有所差异，尤其体现在模型是否接受或挑战用户的表述方式。我们还发现，这些差异更与深层披露的累积暴露相关，而非单一脆弱时刻或披露顺序。这种影响在披露回归浅层后仍持续数轮。对于某些行为，我们进一步证明，模型在情感支持对话语料库（ESConv）中与人类支持者最显著的偏离并非发生在用户敞开心扉时，而是当他们退回到较低披露水平时。因此，TraDE提供了一种系统化方法，揭示了传统孤立对话状态评估所忽略的历史依赖型关系行为。
👤 Keith Kwok, Wanting Ning, Tej Deep Pala, Desmond C. Ong, S. Supraja et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29410]
ECHO: Emergent Common-Mode Hidden Offsets Drive Vocabulary Collapse in Preference Optimization
ECHO：涌现的共模隐藏偏移驱动偏好优化中的词汇坍塌
Preference optimization can improve every ranking metric while building a hidden, shared representation shift that the relative loss barely sees, one that can eventually destroy generation. We trace this failure to the common-mode offset, a shared directional shift in the model's hidden states that appears across token positions, prompts, and both chosen and rejected responses. Because it moves chosen and rejected representations together, the relative ranking loss barely reacts to its accumulation. As this offset grows, it projects onto the output layer to form a vocabulary well, elevating a single token's probability until it dominates decoding and causes generation collapse. The isolated offset alone reconstructs the vocabulary well, injecting the learned offset into an untreated base model recreates the collapse where norm-matched random directions do not, and masking this common component from the loss prevents the well in our flagship setting. Under the per-token ranking loss we study, the same collapse appears in 14 of 17 base-model lineages and under full-parameter fine-tuning. Moreover, the offset is not merely an artifact of collapse, since it is already present in textbook DPO and SimPO runs that form no well. Textbook DPO keeps it weakly aligned with any single vocabulary direction even with three times our base budget. Trained from base models for that budget, however, full-parameter SimPO drives it into collapse on both Llama-2 and Llama-3.1, and the same kind of offset grows, without a well, from a public SFT checkpoint under the official SimPO recipe. Across 17 UltraFeedback runs, the better a run ranks held-out responses, the less often two public reward models prefer its samples to the base model's. These findings expose a blind spot in standard ranking metrics and introduce a representation-level signal that can warn of generation collapse before it occurs.
中文翻译
偏好优化可以在提升所有排名指标的同时，构建一个隐藏的、共享的表示空间偏移，而相对损失对此几乎无法察觉，这种偏移最终可能导致生成崩溃。我们将这一失败归因于共模偏移——一种出现在不同词位、提示和选择/拒绝响应中的共享方向性偏移。由于该偏移同时移动选择和拒绝的表示，相对排名损失对其积累反应极弱。随着该偏移的增长，它会投影到输出层形成词汇井，使单个词的概率不断上升直至主导解码并引发生成崩溃。仅靠孤立的偏移本身即可重建词汇井；将学习到的偏移注入未经处理的基础模型会重现崩溃现象，而使用范数匹配的随机方向则不会。在损失中屏蔽这一共模成分可防止旗舰设置下的词汇井形成。在我们研究的逐词排名损失下，同一崩溃现象出现在17个基础模型谱系中的14个，且在全参数微调下也出现。此外，该偏移并非崩溃的单纯产物，因为即使在不形成词汇井的教科书级DPO和SimPO运行中，它也已存在。教科书级DPO虽在三倍于我们基础预算的情况下仍保持弱对齐，但全参数SimPO在Llama-2和Llama-3.1上均导致崩溃。同样，公开的SFT检查点在官方SimPO配方下也出现了此类偏移但未形成词汇井。在17次UltraFeedback运行中，表现越好的运行在保留响应上的排名越高，但其样本被两个公开奖励模型优先选择的频率反而越低。这些发现揭示了标准排名指标中的盲点，并引入了一种表示层面的信号，可在生成崩溃发生前发出预警。
👤 Yun Li, Ehsan Javanmardi, Simon Thompson, Jiachen Gong, Qunli Zhang et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29350]
B-CARE: Blind Classwise Assessment of Relative Evidence for Stance Error Ranking
B-CARE：面向立场错误排序的盲式类间相对证据评估
Stance classifiers make errors that can be costly to identify under limited human-review budgets. Prioritizing potentially incorrect predictions remains challenging, particularly across heterogeneous base models. We introduce B-CARE (Blind Classwise Assessment of Relative Evidence for Stance Error Ranking), a prediction-blind framework for identifying high-risk stance predictions. Given only a target and text, a fixed LLM verifier assigns ordinal evidence scores to all candidate stance labels without observing the base model's prediction or confidence. B-CARE constructs a comparative risk score by contrasting the strongest alternative-class evidence with the evidence supporting the predicted class. The resulting classwise evidence profile can be reused across multiple base models without training a separate error detector or calibrating risk scores. We evaluate B-CARE on four stance benchmarks and three heterogeneous base LLMs, covering 12 dataset-model settings and 5,206 unique test instances. Evaluation includes error-ranking discrimination (AUROC and AUPRC), selective prediction (E-AURC), and review effectiveness (ErrorCapture at fixed review budgets). B-CARE achieves a mean AUROC of 0.735 and a mean AUPRC of 0.524, capturing 41.06% of errors within the highest-risk 20% of predictions. Although B-CARE does not uniformly outperform strong confidence-based baselines, it provides a reusable, complementary evidence signal for prioritizing potentially incorrect stance predictions across heterogeneous models.
中文翻译
立场分类器的错误可能因人力审查预算有限而难以识别。优先处理潜在错误预测仍具挑战性，尤其在异构基础模型之间。我们提出B-CARE（盲式类间相对证据评估），一种无需观察基础模型预测或置信度的预测盲框架，用于识别高风险立场预测。给定目标和文本，固定的大语言模型验证器为所有候选立场标签分配序数证据分值。B-CARE通过对比最强替代类证据与支持预测类的证据，构建类间风险评分。由此生成的类间证据分布可跨多个基础模型复用，无需为每个模型单独训练错误检测器或校准风险分数。我们在四个立场基准和三种异构基础LLM上评估B-CARE，覆盖12个数据集-模型组合和5,206个唯一测试实例。评估包括错误排序区分能力（AUROC与AUPRC）、选择性预测（E-AURC）和审查效率（固定审查预算下的ErrorCapture）。B-CARE平均AUROC达0.735，平均AUPRC为0.524，在最高风险20%的预测中捕获了41.06%的错误。尽管B-CARE并未在所有情况下优于强置信度基线，但它为异构模型中优先处理潜在错误预测提供了一种可复用、互补的证据信号。

[2610.29338]
Policy Central: Detecting Conflicts, Redundancy, and Similarity in Deployed Police Policy
Policy Central：检测已部署警察政策中的冲突、冗余与相似性
Policy Central is a deployed system that helps police departments find relationships between sections of their written policy: whether two sections conflict, are redundant, are merely similar, or are different. We describe the system and compare two architectures. Retrieve-Judge, a retrieve-then-classify pipeline, runs as a cheap library-wide health signal; Screen-Confirm, a screen-then-confirm cascade, is a selective audit of the documents a reviewer chooses, checking them exhaustively against one another. On a 302-pair benchmark drawn from one department's ~84 real policy documents, given each pair Screen-Confirm reaches 0.795 aggregate recall and 0.885 on Conflict, so current models judge well. Finding the pair is the hard part: Retrieve-Judge's composed recall falls to 0.457, and Screen-Confirm recovers 0.472 (0.615 on Conflict). Today these pairs are found by reading the whole library, nearly 20,000 sections, checked against one another and re-checked after every revision. A scan that returns half of them in minutes, at low cost, and can be re-run on demand changes the economics of policy review even before recall improves.
