[2610.29254]
Intergroup Bias Predicts Expressions of Social Judgment and Empathy in the r/AmITheAsshole Reddit Community
群体间偏见预测r/AmITheAsshole社区中的社会判断与共情表达
People share and understand others' experiences differently based on their social identities; often, people empathize more when the other person belongs to their ingroup compared to an outgroup. While this intergroup empathy gap is well-studied in social psychology, it remains unclear how it manifests in naturally occurring online social interactions. To this end, we measure how commenters express social judgments, as an indirect outcome of their understanding towards the posters, and empathy in text, based on whether commenters share the poster's gender or are of similar ages. We focus on posts where the poster self-reports these demographics, and analyze 126,716 comments associated with 36,163 posts from 18,111 commenters in r/AmItheAsshole, while controlling for commenter and poster group identities, post features (topic, length, and score), and both commenter- and post-level variance. Our results show that when commenters and posters are ingroup, in either gender or age, commenters are more likely to provide a Not the Asshole verdict and show greater text-based understanding as measured by LLMs. We also observe that older commenters behave differently from younger ones. Furthermore, in a subset of posts where the gender and age of the other party in the conflict are explicitly mentioned, we find that commenter-poster ingroup effects are moderated by the group relationships between the poster and the other party when commenters make social judgments. Our work highlights the importance of intergroup context and social group nuances in understanding online discourse.

人们基于自身社会身份，以不同方式分享和理解他人的经历；通常，当对方属于自己的内群体时，人们更容易产生共情，而非外群体。尽管群体间共情差距在社会心理学中已有广泛研究，但其在自然发生的在线社交互动中如何体现仍不明确。为此，我们测量评论者在文本中表达的社会判断和共情，作为其对发帖人理解程度的间接结果，并基于评论者是否与发帖人同性别或年龄相近进行分析。我们聚焦于发帖人自我报告相关人口统计信息的帖子，分析来自18,111名评论者的36,163篇帖子所关联的126,716条评论，同时控制评论者与发帖者群体身份、帖子特征（主题、长度、得分）以及评论者与帖子层面的方差。结果表明，当评论者与发帖者属于同一内群体（性别或年龄）时，评论者更可能给出“非施害者”结论，并表现出更强的基于文本的理解能力（通过LLM衡量）。我们还观察到年长评论者的行为与年轻评论者存在差异。此外，在部分冲突双方性别和年龄明确提及的帖子子集中，发现评论者-发帖者内群体效应会受到发帖者与其他方之间群体关系的调节，影响社会判断。本研究强调了群体背景和社会群体细微差异在理解网络话语中的重要性。

Carving Radiology Reports at Their Evidential Joints: Anatomical Region Sets as Generation Units
在证据关联处切分放射科报告：解剖区域集合作为生成单元
Organizing radiology reports into independently generated units offers a way to generate fine-grained clinical content and bind it to visual evidence. However, existing methods typically adopt generation-unit structures not directly specified in natural reports or their annotations, introducing additional modeling compromises. We propose RadCarve, which instead defines generation units directly from the anatomical region sets in existing statement-region correspondences, unifying unit formation with visual evidence selection. RadCarve's planner uses composition-specialized query banks to jointly predict unit existence and region composition from the image, iteratively refining both with regional evidence aggregated according to current membership predictions. The generator independently produces each unit's text conditioned on the joint visual evidence of its predicted region set. Compared with strong baselines, RadCarve improves CXRFEScore by 9.4% in unit generation given region sets and achieves 2.4 times the end-to-end binding F1. It also achieves leading report-generation results on Chest ImaGenome and PadChest-GR, using fewer than one-sixth as many parameters as large-scale baselines.

[2610.29162]
Why is AI-generated language so hard to understand?
为什么AI生成的语言如此难以理解？
There is a widespread sense that AI-generated text has recently become harder to understand. In this study, we investigate a set of psycholinguistic features that are known to correlate with human processing difficulty (e.g., object relative clauses, long syntactic dependencies, low-frequency n-grams). Using a novel corpus of AI-generated text across diverse domains, we find that AI models use these difficult features more often than paraphrases generated by round-trip translation. Compared with human writing, all nine frontier and near-frontier models use more inanimate subjects with animate verbs and more low-frequency n-grams, and most also use longer dependencies, more stacked nouns, and more reduced object relatives in conversation; we also demonstrate substantial variation across model families. We propose and test a hypothesis as to why: while human language is optimized for successful communication, AI-generated text is increasingly optimized for other goals like math, code, and reasoning, in which there is a pressure for \textit{information density} rather than successful communication. We provide a proof-of-concept for this hypothesis by fine-tuning a language model under a length penalty, which leads to a divergence from human-like linguistic behavior. Our findings highlight the axes of misalignment between linguistic processing demands in humans and LLMs, and point toward possible remedies.
中文翻译
目前普遍认为AI生成的文本近年来变得更难理解。本研究调查了一系列已知与人类语言处理难度相关的心理语言学特征（如宾语从句、长句法依赖、低频n-gram）。利用一个涵盖多个领域的新型AI生成文本语料库，我们发现AI模型比通过往返翻译生成的改写文本更频繁地使用这些困难特征。与人类写作相比，所有九个前沿及近前沿模型均使用更多无生命主语搭配有生命动词，以及更多低频n-gram；大多数模型在对话中还表现出更长的依赖关系、更多的嵌套名词和更多的省略宾语从句；我们还展示了不同模型家族之间的显著差异。我们提出并验证了一个假设：虽然人类语言优化于成功沟通，但AI生成文本正日益优化于其他目标，如数学、代码和推理，在这些任务中存在对信息密度的压力而非有效沟通。我们通过在长度惩罚下微调语言模型，提供了该假设的初步验证，导致模型行为偏离人类语言模式。我们的发现揭示了人类语言处理需求与大语言模型之间存在的错配轴线，并指出了可能的解决方案。
👤 Thomas Hikaru Clark, Matias B. Martin, Kyle Mahowald 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29136]
GRAFT: Restoring the Missing Sampling Interface in Continuous Diffusion Language Models
GRAFT：恢复连续扩散语言模型中缺失的采样接口
Continuous diffusion language models generate a whole sequence in parallel, never drawing a single word at any intermediate step; only the final step turns embeddings into text. Models such as ELF now report autoregressive-level quality at a fraction of the sampling steps of discrete-token diffusion models, and this design principle -- no step-wise discretization -- is what their own ablations credit for the gain. We show that this same principle carries a hidden cost: a collapse of the lexical long tail. Measured against held-out human text, ELF's output matches the human rate on common words almost exactly, yet its rarest words appear at only 0.30x the human rate, a gap that does not close from 105M to 652M parameters and that no sampler setting these models expose can reach. A same-size GPT-2, in contrast, spans zero to above the human rate by its decoding rule alone. We trace the difference to what continuous diffusion models lack: with no step that ever draws a discrete word, every step is instead driven by an average over the candidates still in play, and averaging a rare candidate together with the common word competing for its slot lands on the common word. An autoregressive model avoids this because its decoding rule samples one candidate rather than averaging over all of them. We propose GRAFT, a training-free method that grafts this missing choice back onto a frozen model: while the model is still undecided, GRAFT commits the rare words the average would otherwise discard, then regenerates the sequence from scratch with those commitments held in place. Across three ELF scales and on LangFlow, an independent continuous dLLM, GRAFT restores the rare-word share of generated text from 0.42-0.77x the human rate to 0.82-1.10x, a relative gain of 34-109%, and moves the entire word-frequency profile closer to human on every model tested, by 2.7x on ELF-B and 12x on LangFlow.
中文翻译
连续扩散语言模型以并行方式生成整个序列，从未在任何中间步骤抽取单个词；只有最终步骤将嵌入转换为文本。像ELF这样的模型在远少于离散token扩散模型采样步数的情况下实现了自回归级别的质量，这一设计原则——无逐步离散化——正是其自身消融实验所归因于性能提升的原因。我们表明，同样的原则也带来了隐藏代价：词汇长尾的崩溃。与保留的人类文本对比，ELF输出在常见词上的匹配率几乎完全一致，但最罕见词的出现频率仅为人类的0.30倍，这一差距在105M到652M参数规模间并未缩小，且任何暴露的采样设置都无法弥补。相比之下，同等规模的GPT-2仅凭解码规则即可覆盖从零到超过人类水平的范围。我们追溯差异根源在于连续扩散模型所缺乏的机制：由于没有任何步骤会抽取离散词，每一步都由仍在竞争的候选词平均驱动，而将罕见词与争夺其位置的常见词平均后，结果总是落在常见词上。自回归模型避免了这一点，因其解码规则是采样单一候选词而非对所有候选词求平均。我们提出GRAFT，一种无需训练的方法，将缺失的选择机制“嫁接”回冻结模型：当模型尚未决定时，GRAFT主动保留原本会被平均掉的罕见词，然后在保持这些承诺的前提下重新生成序列。在三个ELF规模和一个独立的连续dLLM LangFlow上，GRAFT将生成文本中罕见词的比例从0.42–0.77倍人类水平恢复至0.82–1.10倍，相对提升达34%–109%，并使所有测试模型的词频分布整体更接近人类水平，其中ELF-B提升2.7倍，LangFlow提升12倍。
👤 Haoning Yang, Lili Pan 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29097]
Multi-level Encoder with Global Topic for Task-oriented Dialogue Summarization
基于全局主题的多层级编码器用于面向任务的对话摘要
Task-oriented dialogue summarization aims to automatically extract key information to generate domain summaries to improve service efficiency and quality. Task-oriented dialogue is inherently logical and surrounds specific topic. How to effectively capture the dialogue topic and the most salient information becomes one of the major challenges of this task. In this paper, we propose a task-oriented dialogue summarization model utilizing a multi-level encoder with global topic (MLEGT). This model discovers temporal and spatial dependencies through a multi-level encoder, while simultaneously obtaining a simple yet effective global topic in the process of graph construction. It not only captures the crucial details but also breaks the long-range limit and discover the intrinsic structure across the dialogue. Then, a global topic guided pointer mechanism is added into the summrizer to capture ignored but important information, which is helpful to improve the accuracy of the summary. A comprehensive study of two public datasets, including a real-world large-scale Chinese police interrogation dataset and a medical reporting service dataset proves the superiority of our method on several strong baselines.
中文翻译
面向任务的对话摘要旨在自动提取关键信息以生成领域摘要，从而提升服务效率与质量。面向任务的对话本质上具有逻辑性且围绕特定主题展开。如何有效捕捉对话主题及最显著信息，成为该任务的主要挑战之一。本文提出一种面向任务的对话摘要模型——多层级编码器结合全局主题（MLEGT）。该模型通过多层级编码器发现时间与空间依赖关系，同时在图构建过程中获得简单而有效的全局主题。它不仅捕捉关键细节，还突破长距离限制，揭示对话中的内在结构。随后，在摘要器中引入全局主题引导的指针机制，以捕获被忽略但重要的信息，有助于提高摘要准确性。通过对两个公开数据集（包括一个真实世界的大规模中文警察审讯数据集和一个医疗报告服务数据集）的综合研究，证明了本方法在多个强基线上的优越性。
👤 Zhuoqi He, Peijie Huang, Yuhong Xu, Youming Peng, Mingzhi Xu et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28944]
Guilt by Association: Semantic Leakage Drives Associative Gender Bias
因关联而有罪：语义泄露驱动关联性别偏见
Semantic leakage occurs when a concept in the prompt steers model generation about an entity, even though the concept is irrelevant to it (e.g., a person who likes yellow is said to work as a school bus driver). It has mostly been studied in settings where the concept is directly attributed to the entity the model is asked about. This leaves open whether leakage is a flawed heuristic, where the model builds a profile of that entity, or a much broader associative tendency, e.g., working across seemingly unrelated entities. We argue for the latter: even after removing the attribution of the concept to the specific entity, leakage persists. Next, we extend this idea to gender bias. While work on gender bias in LMs typically looks at cases where the stereotyped concept is attributed to an entity, our findings suggest that a concept may steer outputs with its mere presence in the scene. We show this using gendered concepts under two conditions: when the subject engages with the concept, and when the concept just sits in the scene. We find that both shift model generation toward the stereotype to a similar extent. Together, our results suggest that semantic leakage drives a form of bias that is overlooked by current evaluations.
中文翻译
语义泄露是指提示中的某个概念即使与实体无关，也会引导模型关于该实体的生成（例如，喜欢黄色的人被说成是校车司机）。目前的研究大多集中在该概念直接归属于模型被询问的实体的情境下。这留下了疑问：泄露是一种错误启发式，即模型为特定实体构建画像，还是更广泛的联想倾向，例如跨越看似无关实体的关联？我们主张后者：即使移除了概念对特定实体的归属，泄露依然存在。接着，我们将这一观点扩展至性别偏见。尽管现有LM性别偏见研究多关注刻板印象概念被直接归因于实体的情形，我们的发现表明，只要概念出现在场景中，其存在本身即可引导输出趋向刻板印象。我们通过两种条件下的性别化概念进行了验证：一是主体与概念互动时，二是概念仅存在于场景中时。结果发现，两者对模型生成的刻板印象导向程度相似。综上，我们的研究结果表明，语义泄露驱动了一种当前评估体系所忽视的偏见形式。
👤 Hazel Chen, Peter West, Hila Gonen 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28897]
Is Refusal Direction Universal across Low-Resourced Languages?
拒绝指令在低资源语言中是否具有普适性？
Refusal directions have been shown to transfer cross-lingually with near-perfect effectiveness across safety-aligned languages (Wang et al., 2025), reportedly regardless of resource level. However, the only truly low-resourced language evaluated is Yoruba, which is strongly misaligned and shows limited transfer, leaving open how refusal directions behave for other low-resourced languages. We extend PolyRefuse to three additional low-resourced languages (Belarusian, Bashkir, and Tajik) and replicate the pipeline of (Wang et al., 2025) on Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct. We find that universality is graded rather than binary: directions from safety-aligned and partially-aligned languages transfer broadly, while those from strongly misaligned languages fail to reliably modulate refusal behavior on any target tested, including other misaligned languages, once ceiling effects are accounted for. Geometric alignment and behavioral transfer are further shown to be partially dissociated. These findings complicate the universality claim and suggest that users of the least-resourced, most strongly misaligned languages may face weaker safety guarantees.
中文翻译
拒绝指令在安全对齐语言之间已显示出近乎完美的跨语言迁移效果（Wang等，2025），据称与资源水平无关。然而，唯一被评估的真正低资源语言是约鲁巴语，其严重不对齐且迁移效果有限，因此其他低资源语言中的拒绝指令行为仍不明确。我们将PolyRefuse扩展至三种额外的低资源语言（白俄罗斯语、巴什基尔语和塔吉克语），并在Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct上复现了(Wang等，2025)的流程。我们发现普适性并非二元而是分级的：来自安全对齐和部分对齐语言的指令可广泛迁移，而来自严重不对齐语言的指令在任何目标语言上均无法可靠调节拒绝行为，即使在考虑上限效应后也是如此。几何对齐与行为迁移也被进一步证明存在部分分离。这些发现挑战了普适性主张，提示资源最少、最严重不对齐语言的用户可能面临更弱的安全保障。
👤 Anastasiia Ivanova 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28895]
CSD: Common-Support Distillation for Cross-Tokenizer Knowledge Transfer
CSD：面向跨分词器知识迁移的通用支持蒸馏
Cross-tokenizer logits distillation enables knowledge transfer across model families, allowing students to learn from stronger or more specialized teachers. However, differences in vocabulary and segmentation make teacher and student next-token distributions difficult to align. Existing logits-based methods are easy to integrate into standard distillation pipelines, but often rely on restrictive heuristics or tokenizer-pair-specific choices. They may restrict supervision to shared vocabulary subsets, require manually selecting different objectives for different tokenizer pairs, or match only the observed chunk and its complement rather than individual alternative continuations. We introduce Common-Support Distillation (CSD), a unified logits-based framework that constructs a shared support between the teacher and student at each aligned input chunk and matches the induced distributions with a single objective. For one-to-one alignments, CSD uses explicit common-token pairs as the shared support. For other alignments, it constructs a virtual support whose probabilities are computed using the autoregressive chain rule. A residual event captures probability mass outside the explicit support, ensuring that both induced distributions remain normalized. Across four teacher-student settings comprising Llama-3.2-1B and Gemma3-1B-PT students with Qwen and Phi teachers, CSD consistently achieves the highest average performance among the evaluated cross-tokenizer methods, outperforming the strongest alternative by up to 1.8 points and surpassing both continued pretraining and same-tokenizer distillation. For Llama-3.1-8B, CSD improves average accuracy over the strongest evaluated baseline by up to 4.2 points for the base student and 1.6 points for the instruction-tuned student.
中文翻译
跨分词器logits蒸馏实现了模型家族间知识迁移，使学生模型能够从更强或更专业的教师模型中学习。然而，词汇和分词差异使得教师与学生下一词分布难以对齐。现有基于logits的方法易于集成到标准蒸馏流程中，但常依赖于限制性启发式或分词器对特异性选择，可能仅将监督限制在共享词汇子集，需手动为不同分词器对选择不同目标，或仅匹配观察到的片段及其补集，而非单独的替代延续。我们提出通用支持蒸馏（CSD），一种统一的基于logits的框架，在每个对齐输入片段处构建教师与学生之间的共享支持，并使用单一目标匹配诱导分布。对于一对一映射，CSD使用显式的共同标记对作为共享支持；对于其他映射，则通过自回归链规则计算虚拟支持的概率。残差事件捕获显式支持之外的概率质量，确保两个诱导分布保持归一化。在包含Llama-3.2-1B和Gemma3-1B-PT学生模型与Qwen和Phi教师模型的四种设置中，CSD在所有评估的跨分词器方法中始终表现最佳，相比最强替代方案最高提升1.8分，超越持续预训练和同分词器蒸馏。对于Llama-3.1-8B，CSD在基础学生模型上平均准确率提升达4.2分，在指令微调学生模型上提升1.6分。
👤 Mingyu Yang, Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas, Adithyakrishna Venkatesh Hanasoge, Nima Tajbakhsh, Ashwath Aithal et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

KIRA: A Full-Stack Reading Screening System with Dual-Pathway Analysis for Children
KIRA：面向儿童的全栈阅读筛查系统，采用双通路分析
Early-grade reading screening is critical for identifying children at risk of reading difficulty. Existing AI-based screeners are often limited in the scope of their analyses and evaluation metrics, or are proprietary systems that lack transparent model specifications and evaluation. We present KIRA, a dual-pathway screening system that pairs word-level fluency scoring with detailed phoneme-level error detection. We adapt pretrained word and phoneme recognizers to children's speech, reducing word error rate from 13.88% to 8.84% and phoneme error rate from 41.66% to 23.00%. Powered by these child-adapted models, we implement Word Reading (WRE) and Oral Reading Fluency (ORF), two foundational tasks in K-2 screening. The system integrates these recognizers with a web-based teacher-facing interface presenting fluency scores alongside phoneme feedback for potential targeted intervention. KIRA brings modern child-speech recognition into a full-stack, deployable, classroom-ready reading screener.

早期年级阅读筛查对识别有阅读困难风险的儿童至关重要。现有的基于AI的筛查工具通常在分析范围和评估指标上受限，或为缺乏透明模型说明和评估的专有系统。我们提出KIRA，一种双通路筛查系统，将单词级流利度评分与详细的音素级错误检测相结合。我们对预训练的单词和音素识别器进行了儿童语音适配，将单词错误率从13.88%降至8.84%，音素错误率从41.66%降至23.00%。基于这些适配模型，我们实现了K-2年级筛查中的两项基础任务：单词阅读（WRE）和口语阅读流利度（ORF）。该系统将这些识别器与基于Web的教师界面集成，呈现流利度分数及音素反馈，以支持潜在的针对性干预。KIRA将现代儿童语音识别技术整合为一个完整的、可部署的、适合课堂使用的阅读筛查系统。

[2610.28838]
How to Use Parallel Data for Multilingual Pretraining
如何利用并行数据进行多语言预训练
High-quality human-translated parallel corpora can improve cross-lingual transfer, but are typically too small for large-scale pretraining. Machine-translated web corpora help overcome this limitation, making parallel data available at pretraining scale. Standard multilingual pretraining, however, samples source documents and their translations independently, without explicitly using their parallel structure. We investigate how to use the parallel structure of the data to improve multilingual pretraining by concatenating each document with its translation in the same training sequence. We compare this parallel pretraining (PPT) approach with standard pretraining (SPT), training models from scratch at matched token budgets. Surprisingly, despite the benefits of parallel data reported in prior cross-lingual work, PPT alone does not consistently improve language understanding over SPT. Our further analysis suggests that although PPT builds a strong cross-lingual foundation, as shown by its better use of shared information across languages, the mismatch between parallel training contexts and single-language task inputs limits its gains in general language understanding. This finding motivates a two-stage approach following the pretraining-fine-tuning paradigm: we first use PPT to build a cross-lingual foundation, then continue with SPT to adapt it to standard language modeling. In both high-resource and low-resource settings, the two-stage approach consistently improves general language understanding and cross-lingual task performance over standard pretraining alone at matched training budgets.
中文翻译
高质量的人工翻译并行语料库可以提升跨语言迁移效果，但通常规模太小，难以支持大规模预训练。机器翻译的网络语料库有助于克服这一限制，使并行数据在预训练规模上可用。然而，标准的多语言预训练方法独立采样源文档及其翻译，未显式利用其并行结构。我们研究了如何利用数据的并行结构来改进多语言预训练，通过将每个文档与其翻译在同一个训练序列中拼接。我们将这种并行预训练（PPT）方法与标准预训练（SPT）进行比较，在匹配的词元预算下从头训练模型。令人惊讶的是，尽管先前的跨语言研究报道了并行数据的优势，但仅使用PPT并不能持续优于SPT的语言理解能力。进一步分析表明，虽然PPT建立了强大的跨语言基础（体现在对跨语言共享信息更好的利用），但并行训练上下文与单语言任务输入之间的不匹配限制了其在通用语言理解上的收益。这一发现促使我们提出一种两阶段方法，遵循预训练-微调范式：首先使用PPT构建跨语言基础，然后继续使用SPT将其适配到标准语言建模任务。在高资源和低资源设置下，该两阶段方法在匹配训练预算下始终优于仅使用标准预训练的模型，在通用语言理解和跨语言任务性能上均有提升。
👤 Zhihong Huang, Yao Lu, Derek F. Wong, Hamid Alinejad-Rokny, Min Yang 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv

A Parallel Multi-Dialect Arabic Dependency Treebank
一种并行的多方言阿拉伯语依存句法树库
Dependency parsing resource development for Arabic has largely focused on Modern Standard Arabic, leaving dialectal Arabic comparatively under-resourced. We introduce MADARTB, the first large-scale parallel dependency treebank spanning five city dialects: Beirut, Cairo, Doha, Rabat and Tunis, and extending an existing parallel MSA version. In total MADARTB consists of 5,000 sentences annotated using the CATiB dependency scheme. We analyze syntactic similarity across all city dialects and MSA. Although global construction profiles exhibit high similarity across varieties, local syntactic structures frequently diverge, revealing substantial structural variation across dialects. We further establish a cross-dialect dependency parsing comparison using a supervised parser and four LLMs. The supervised parser achieves 87.5% LAS, while the best LLM setup trails at 85.7% LAS across all six varieties on the test set. We release our annotations and models publicly.

[2610.28799]
Where Do Legal Factors Live in LLMs?
法律因素在大语言模型中位于何处？
In case-based legal reasoning, legal professionals identify legally significant fact patterns in a new dispute and compare them with those of decided cases. In AI & Law, HYPO dimensions and CATO factors represent these patterns symbolically, organizing expert knowledge distilled from case narratives as explicit, structured, interpretable, and auditable legal concepts and relations that support case comparison, legal argument, and precedent-based reasoning. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for legal tasks, yet their internal organization of legal knowledge remains difficult to trace. We investigate whether LLMs form internal representations corresponding to all 26 CATO factors. Two research questions examine linear decodability and sparse localization with sparse autoencoders. Experiments cover three open models, Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-8B, and Gemma-2 2B, supported by cross-model comparison and human audit of controlled natural-language passages. Results show that all 26 factors are linearly decodable in all three models. Single-feature sparse readouts cover the complete factor catalog, with best-layer test AUCs ranging from 0.86 to 1.00. Five selected features approach full-vector readout performance. We report 78 model-specific layer-feature coordinates, one for each factor in each model. Together, these results show that information corresponding to expert-defined legal factors can be read from the internal activations of LLMs processing legal narratives. By locating these readouts, our study connects legal language and the symbolic abstractions of HYPO and CATO to learned internal representations.
中文翻译
在案例导向的法律推理中，法律专业人士会识别新争议中具有法律意义的事实模式，并将其与已决案件的事实模式进行比较。在人工智能与法律领域，HYPO维度和CATO因素以符号形式表示这些模式，将从案例叙述中提炼出的专家知识组织为明确、结构化、可解释且可审计的法律概念与关系，支持案例比较、法律论证和基于先例的推理。大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地应用于法律任务，但其内部法律知识的组织方式仍难以追踪。我们研究了LLMs是否形成了对应于全部26个CATO因素的内部表征。两项研究问题分别考察线性可解性和稀疏定位性，使用稀疏自编码器进行分析。实验涵盖三个开源模型：Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-8B和Gemma-2 2B，辅以跨模型比较和对受控自然语言段落的人工审计。结果表明，所有26个因素在三个模型中均可线性解码。单特征稀疏读出覆盖了完整的因素目录，最佳层测试AUC值在0.86至1.00之间。五个选定特征接近全向量读出性能。我们报告了78个模型特定的层-特征坐标（每个模型每个因素一个）。综合来看，这些结果表明，专家定义的法律因素信息可以从处理法律叙述的LLM内部激活中读取。通过定位这些读出点，我们的研究将法律语言与HYPO和CATO的符号抽象连接到了学习到的内部表征。

Scaling Therapy Supervision: Assessing Psychotherapy Fidelity Using Large Language Models
扩展心理治疗监督：使用大语言模型评估心理治疗的忠实度
Psychotherapy fidelity assessment measures how faithfully a therapist adheres to a treatment protocol, a critical determinant of positive patient outcomes. However, fidelity assessment is bottlenecked by manual expert review, rendering the process labor-intensive and unscalable. To scale therapy supervision and support clinician self-learning, we present an integrated application of large language models (LLMs) that automates psychotherapy fidelity workflows and generates actionable supervisory feedback from authentic, long-form clinical transcripts. We evaluate three open-weight models across multiple task decomposition configurations and reasoning strategies, demonstrating that evidence-first reasoning improves fidelity estimation. Furthermore, we establish the framework's external construct validity on an independent randomized controlled trial (RCT), where it reliably distinguishes Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) from non-CBT sessions across core therapeutic skills. Through a user study that evaluates the generated feedback, mental health experts indicate that the pipeline synthesizes actionable clinical feedback. Ultimately, our work offers a practical, scalable solution toward bridging the community mental health supervision gap.

Translation and data ethics
翻译与数据伦理
This chapter introduces data ethics for translation and discusses a number of ethical issues regarding data. These include issues of copyright, ownership and bias, which apply to all types of data, whereas there are further issues particular to minoritised language communities and those on either side of the digital divide, as data is implicated in power imbalances and dynamics. We close with a discussion of responsibility and regulation, and reflect on the growing attention on data ethics from translation studies researchers.

[2610.28673]
How Far Can the Gemma 4 Speech Encoder Go Without Changing Its Weights? Saudi and Gulf Arabic Speech Recognition with a Frozen and an Adapter-Augmented Encoder
Gemma 4 语音编码器在不更新权重的情况下能走多远？基于冻结与适配器增强编码器的沙特及海湾阿拉伯语语音识别
We study the 305M-parameter audio encoder shared by Gemma 4 and EmbeddingGemma 2 (identical except for its final projection) for Saudi and Gulf Arabic speech recognition, without changing any of its weights. A frozen system trains only a 22.6M-parameter CTC reader over a mix of encoder layers; an adapted system adds 3.1M low-rank adapter parameters, a 41.5M-parameter hybrid CTC/attention reader and joint decoding with a sub-word language model. Against Cohere Transcribe Arabic (2.07B parameters), re-run on identical audio, the frozen system trails in word error rate (WER) but is statistically indistinguishable in character error rate (CER) on five of six human-labelled conditions, while running 3.4 times faster on CPU with greedy decoding. The adapted system is statistically indistinguishable in WER on a public Saudi customer-service call (19.20% against 19.27%) and on clean Emirati speech, is significantly better on telephone-coded Emirati speech, has a significantly lower CER on all six conditions of these sets, and beats a 0.6B recogniser fine-tuned on SADA in WER on all six; it still trails Cohere in WER on long-form Saudi speech. On a blind, human-corrected public call-centre sample it ranks 4 of 15 systems, behind three commercial APIs (significantly only one) and ahead of the other self-hostable systems by point estimate. An optimised joint decoder runs it at 139 times real time on one GPU. We also show that references made by correcting machine drafts favour the drafting system. Code and public-set results are released.
中文翻译
我们研究了Gemma 4和EmbeddingGemma 2共用的305M参数音频编码器（仅最后一层投影不同），用于沙特及海湾阿拉伯语语音识别，且不更改任何权重。冻结系统仅训练一个22.6M参数的CTC读取器，覆盖编码器各层混合；适配系统则增加3.1M低秩适配器参数、一个41.5M参数的混合CTC/注意力读取器，并结合子词语言模型进行联合解码。与Cohere Transcribe Arabic（20.7亿参数）在相同音频上重新运行的结果相比，冻结系统在词错误率（WER）上落后，但在六组人类标注条件中的五组上字符错误率（CER）无统计显著差异，且在CPU上使用贪婪解码时速度提升3.4倍。适配系统在公开的沙特客户服务通话（WER 19.20% vs 19.27%）和干净的阿联酋语音上WER无显著差异，在电话编码的阿联酋语音上显著更优，所有六组条件下CER均显著更低，并在六组测试中均优于在SADA上微调的0.6B识别器的WER；但在长篇沙特语音上仍落后于Cohere。在盲测的人工校正公开呼叫中心样本中，其排名位列15个系统中的第4位，仅次于三个商业API（仅一个显著领先），优于其他自托管系统。优化后的联合解码器可在一块GPU上实现139倍实时运行。我们还发现，修正机器草稿时引用的参考更倾向于支持草稿系统。代码与公开数据集结果已发布。

[2610.29523]
pplx-embed-v2-late Technical Report
pplx-embed-v2-late 技术报告
In this report, we introduce pplx-embed-v2-late, a pair of 0.6B and 9B multilingual and multimodal late-interaction models. Both models achieve state-of-the-art results within their respective size classes across a range of text, image, and visual document retrieval benchmarks. On visual document retrieval tasks, pplx-embed-v2-late-0.6b is competitive with models several times larger than itself. The models are trained via representation distillation from an 18B teacher. Consequently, they share an embedding space that supports asymmetric deployment: documents can be indexed with the 9B model and queried with the low-latency 0.6B model. Both models are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/perplexity-ai/pplx-embed-v2.
中文翻译

[2610.29520]
Beyond the Vote: When Do LLMs Add Value to Retrieval-Based Tabular Classification?
超越投票：大语言模型何时为基于检索的表格分类带来价值？
Retrieval-based in-context learning (ICL) enables large language models (LLMs) to predict tabular labels from similar examples, but it remains unclear whether LLM inference improves on direct voting over those same examples. We address this question by comparing four general-purpose LLMs with distance-weighted nearest-neighbor voting (Distance-KNN) using identical retrieved neighbors across 19 tabular classification datasets. With the number of demonstrations fixed, enlarging the retrieval candidate pool improves accuracy for all predictors, yet Distance-KNN achieves the highest mean accuracy at every pool size. More detailed analyses reveal that LLM gains are concentrated where retrieved neighbors exhibit low label consensus and vary across models. These gains further depend on the average feature-space distance to the test query: under low label consensus, some LLMs outperform Distance-KNN in close and intermediate neighborhoods, but all fall behind on average in the most distant group. Complementary experiments on the number of demonstrations, prompt formulation, retrieval representation, and label-balanced demonstration selection largely preserve the overall findings. These results characterize when LLM inference adds predictive value beyond distance-weighted voting in retrieval-based tabular ICL.
中文翻译
基于检索的上下文学习（ICL）使大语言模型（LLMs）能够从相似示例中预测表格标签，但尚不清楚LLM推理是否优于对相同示例的直接投票。我们通过在19个表格分类数据集上比较四种通用LLM与距离加权最近邻投票（Distance-KNN），使用相同的检索邻居来回答这一问题。在演示数量固定的情况下，扩大检索候选池能提升所有预测器的准确率，但Distance-KNN在每个池大小下均达到最高平均准确率。更深入的分析表明，LLM的优势集中在检索邻居标签一致性较低的场景，且不同模型间存在差异。这些优势还依赖于测试查询与邻居的平均特征空间距离：在标签共识度低的情况下，某些LLM在近距离和中等距离邻域表现优于Distance-KNN，但在最远邻域则整体落后。关于演示数量、提示设计、检索表示和标签平衡的演示选择等补充实验基本保持了上述结论。这些结果刻画了在基于检索的表格ICL中，LLM推理相较于距离加权投票所能带来的额外预测价值的边界。
👤 Ziquan Wang, Yuxuan Nie, Jian Ma, Yingjie Li 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29415]
CCG Diffusion: Combinatory Categorial Grammar-Driven Compositional and Syntactically Disambiguated Text-to-Image Generation
CCG Diffusion：基于组合范畴语法的组合性与句法消歧文本到图像生成
Text-to-image generation models do not explicitly receive the syntactic structure of the prompt, i.e., which word modifies which and where each phrase attaches. Consequently, they often struggle to bind attributes to the correct objects, to depict spatial relations and counts correctly, and to render syntactically ambiguous prompts in the intended interpretation. Existing syntax-based control methods mainly address attribute binding and have limited ability to control spatial relations and counts or to specify the interpretation of syntactically ambiguous prompts. They rely on phrase-structure or dependency grammars and require additional rules or constraints on the tree structure to extract modification relations between words or to specify a syntactic interpretation. We propose CCG Diffusion, a training-free method that controls image generation using Combinatory Categorial Grammar (CCG). In CCG, the category of each word carries rich syntactic information: it specifies what the word combines with and what phrase it forms. Modification relations between words can therefore be read directly from a CCG parse. Moreover, prepositional phrase (PP) attachment ambiguity, a representative syntactic ambiguity, can be resolved simply by fixing the preposition's category. CCG Diffusion applies the same two CCG-based mechanisms, prompt rewriting and attention guidance, both to controlling attribute binding, spatial relations, and counts and to resolving PP ambiguity. On two architecturally different backbones, Stable Diffusion and Sana, CCG Diffusion achieves the highest scores among the compared methods in attribute binding and 2D spatial relations and significantly outperforms all of them in PP ambiguity resolution.
中文翻译
文本到图像生成模型并未显式接收提示的句法结构，即哪个词修饰哪个词、每个短语附着在何处。因此，它们常难以将属性正确绑定到对象、准确描绘空间关系与数量，也无法正确呈现句法歧义提示的预期解释。现有的基于句法的控制方法主要解决属性绑定问题，对控制空间关系与数量或指定句法歧义提示的解释能力有限。这些方法依赖短语结构或依存语法，并需额外规则或树结构约束来提取词之间的修饰关系或指定句法解释。我们提出 CCG Diffusion，一种无需训练的方法，利用组合范畴语法（CCG）控制图像生成。在CCG中，每个词的类别携带丰富的句法信息：它指明该词与何者组合并形成何种短语。因此，词之间的修饰关系可直接从CCG解析中读取。此外，介词短语（PP）附着歧义——一种典型的句法歧义——可通过固定介词的类别即可解决。CCG Diffusion 同时应用两种基于CCG的机制——提示重写与注意力引导——来控制属性绑定、空间关系与数量，以及解决PP歧义。在两个架构不同的骨干模型（Stable Diffusion 和 Sana）上，CCG Diffusion 在属性绑定和二维空间关系方面取得最高得分，并在PP歧义消解方面显著优于所有对比方法。

[2610.29351]
Closed-Loop Dual-Reference Guidance for Accent Control in Cross-Lingual Voice Cloning
Zero-shot cross-lingual voice cloning often carries the reference speaker's accent into the target language. We propose closed-loop dual-reference guidance, which pairs the original reference with a reusable native anchor constructed by voice conversion (VC). The references initialize separate language-model (LM) states that guide a shared acoustic trajectory; each completed patch then updates both states, keeping their generated histories aligned with the emitted speech. Anchor construction runs once per speaker, and online synthesis uses a frozen TTS model without per-utterance VC. On 20 held-out French speakers, the fixed setting $λ^{\!*}=0.33$ reduces residual French LID mass by 73\% versus direct cloning, at an ECAPA similarity cost of only $0.042$; relative to anchor-only cloning, it recovers $0.155$. The method achieves the lowest mean WER ($0.009$) and highest mean GOP ($4.750$) among the held-out test arms. In blinded preference tests, native-English listeners favor it over direct cloning on pronunciation naturalness, and over both anchor-only cloning and a TTS$\to$VC cascade on speaker identity. Online RTF is approximately 26\% lower than the cascade's ($0.59$ vs.\ $0.80$), and the same setting reduces Spanish LID mass by 56\% without re-tuning.
👤 Yuanzhong Zheng, Wei Huang, Ming Ma, Kailun Jin, Youhai Jiang 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29348]
Data Schedules and Learning Rate Schedules Should Be Designed Together
Mid-training (MT) is a training phase in LLM training pipelines that continues pretraining (PT) on smaller datasets of higher value. Taking the PT and MT phases as a whole, training usually starts with only PT data and then transitions completely to MT data. In this work, we view this complete transition from PT to MT data as a special case of a broader class of data mixing schedules that specify how the proportions of PT and MT tokens in each batch change over training. Under fixed total training compute and MT token budgets, we ask: Is this complete transition to MT data optimal for minimizing final MT loss? We show that the answer depends on the learning rate (LR) schedule: one data schedule may outperform another under one LR schedule, but changing only the LR schedule can reverse the outcome. Specifically, when the LR decays throughout continued training, introducing MT data earlier and mixing it with PT data lowers the final MT loss compared with placing all MT data at the end of training, but this result is reversed if we use constant LR. We therefore study how to jointly choose when to introduce MT data and when to decay the LR through ablation studies, and find that it is better to decay the LR only after MT data enters training. Finally, we develop a theoretical model that explains why the optimal data schedule for a fixed MT token budget depends on the LR schedule.
👤 Haodong Wen, Kairong Luo, Tingkai Yan, Wenguang Chen, Kaifeng Lyu 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29345]
Decodability Is Not Controllability: Local and Anisotropic Speaker Control in Continuous-Latent TTS
High linear decodability does not entail causal controllability in continuous-latent text-to-speech (TTS). In VoxCPM2, position-matched probes and equal-norm interventions across 30 speakers and 60 directed dyads expose this separation: reference-audio states at the final residual acoustic language model layer (RALM L7) retain 0.970 probe accuracy, yet aligned edits yield 0/60 target-dominant dyads. Edits at early reference-audio positions produce the opposite pattern. At L0, pair-aligned updates increase target similarity by 0.326 and achieve target dominance in all 60 dyads, against at most 1/60 under any equal-norm direction or position control. Independent speaker encoders, retrieval against held-out speaker prototypes, and double-blind listening confirm that the shifted voice is heard as the intended target. Cross-architecture interventions in dots.tts distinguish backbone leverage from direct acoustic control: backbone edits displace the source without target attraction, while x-vector ablation reduces source similarity from 0.849 to 0.006. These findings identify two requirements for training-free speaker control: geometric specificity and a computational route that carries the intended speaker condition into acoustic synthesis.

[2610.29342]
Uncensored Is Not Unbiased: Abliteration Removes Refusals but Not Script-Dependent Bias About Taiwan
"Abliterated" ("uncensored") builds of open-weight language models remove the activation direction that mediates refusal, and are popular partly on the assumption that a model that no longer refuses is also free of political censorship. We test this on two matched pairs of 2-bit models: NVIDIA's Nemotron 3.5 Bolt 74B-A9B and Qwen3.8-Flash-Next, each against a community abliteration. Each model judged 22 statement pairs (on the Republic of China (Taiwan), 1989 Tiananmen, control countries) in Traditional Chinese, Simplified Chinese (a character-level conversion) and English, and answered 27 open questions per script. Script mattered far more than abliteration: the original Bolt scored +0.58 on Index S (-1: People's Republic of China (PRC) position; +1: ROC position) in Traditional Chinese, +0.57 in English and +0.05 in Simplified Chinese, and affirmed "Taiwan is part of the PRC" with probability 0.91 in Simplified but 0.06 in Traditional characters; abliteration changed its scores by at most 0.06, mostly by raising "yes" on both sides. In Qwen, abliteration removed every refusal on 1989 and raised scores by 0.21 to 0.34, yet the abliterated Qwen still affirmed that Taiwan is part of the PRC in all three scripts. Naming the state the Republic of China (Taiwan) raised every model's score by 0.10 to 0.33 but left the script gap. Control pairs showed no script gap; each abliteration changed only its declared tensors. In 740,000 web documents, Simplified text on Taiwan contained PRC-framing terms 3.7 times as often as Traditional text. A script direction could not be steered away; a contrastive stance direction raised scores in every script, and its negation lowered them. A four-sentence system prompt raised the abliterated Qwen to +0.93 to +0.97. Keyword scoring rules failed the models closest to the ROC position mostly for attributing or denying the PRC claim. Uncensored is not unbiased.
👤 Ju-Chun Ko 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29340]
Reader--Writer Coupling in Transformer MLPs
A transformer MLP gives each hidden channel two roles: a reader that decides when the channel responds, and a writer that decides what it adds back to the residual stream. Read as a key--value memory, the layer can be measured one factor set at a time: the pairing of readers to writers is carried only by their shared index, invisible to any quantity computed from one factor set alone. Yet training couples the two factors through their joint update, so we ask what reading them together shows. The block supplies the joint instrument: the MLP writes back into the very stream it reads, so the exact Jacobian of its update is square, and its agreement with its own transpose resolves into reader--writer terms whose inner products give each channel, read as a couple, a signed pairing coordinate. Ranked by this coordinate, each block we measure has a small core of strongly coupled channels; where initialization gives either sign equally, the core is nearly all opposed: each writer points against the direction its reader responds to. This opposed core recurs from the smallest models trained from scratch to released checkpoints; its sign is in place within a few percent of training and holds while the coupling grows. Read as couples, most channels return their contribution against the reader that fired it, and the readers of later blocks take it up; few show in the logits. Training holds the couples in place: flipping the opposed writers moves the output toward common tokens, and a writer permuted onto another reader regrows to fit that reader while the reader stays.
👤 Giyeong Oh, Sunghee Ahn, Youngjae Yu 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29256]
Heard But Not Said: Prompting Understates Temporal Information of Musical Moments in Frozen Large Audio-Language Models
听到了却未说出：冻结的大规模音视频-语言模型中提示对音乐时刻时间信息的低估
Large audio-language models (LALMs) understand music, yet struggle to locate a described moment in time. We ask whether this reflects missing temporal information or a bottleneck in how the time span is extracted. Using a paired-query probe built from Slakh2100 stem activations, we attach a state query (e.g., ``only piano and bass are playing'') and a change query (e.g., ``the guitar drops out, leaving piano and bass'') to the same span and vary how much preceding audio the model is shown. Across frozen LALMs, three ways of extracting the span ask different questions: full-track prompting asking whether the model can say when; windowed yes/no scoring asking whether it verifies the moment when handed the segment, and whether it needs what came before; attention asking whether it can find the moment on its own. State queries score above baseline without context, while change queries improve with preceding context in most short-segment models; 30s padded encoders show no comparable recovery. Prompting and attention expose almost none of this. Prompting failures thus understate what LALMs can verify, though not what they can locate, by an amount set by query type and encoder.
中文翻译
大规模音视频-语言模型（LALMs）能够理解音乐，但在定位描述中的具体时间点上存在困难。我们探讨这种现象是由于缺乏时间信息，还是由于时间跨度提取过程中的瓶颈所致。通过使用Slakh2100音轨激活构建的成对查询探测器，我们将状态查询（例如“只有钢琴和贝斯在演奏”）与变化查询（例如“吉他停止了，只剩下钢琴和贝斯”）应用于同一时间段，并改变模型所见的前置音频量。在冻结的LALMs中，三种提取时间段的方法提出了不同的问题：全轨道提示询问模型是否能说出时间；窗口化是/否评分询问模型在给出片段时是否能验证该时刻，以及是否需要之前的内容；注意力机制则询问模型能否自主找到该时刻。状态查询在无上下文情况下得分高于基线，而大多数短段模型中，变化查询随着前置上下文增加而表现提升；30秒填充编码器则未表现出类似恢复。提示和注意力机制几乎未能揭示这些能力。因此，提示失败低估了LALMs可验证的能力，但并未低估其定位能力，这种低估程度由查询类型和编码器决定。
👤 Eunsik Shin 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29110]
GRAM: Group-wise Rank-Aware Model Merging via Subspace Alignment
GRAM：基于子空间对齐的分组秩感知模型合并
Merging different models into a single model is highly desirable for multi-task deployment, particularly in regulated domains like healthcare. In such settings, models are often locally fine-tuned due to strict data privacy policies, necessitating that aggregation operates exclusively on the fine-tuned weights. Existing methods apply a uniform merge across all tasks, ignoring the geometric relationships among their learned subspaces. When task updates occupy misaligned subspaces, projecting them onto a shared low-rank basis discards task-relevant directions, a failure mode named \emph{subspace interference}. Through this study, we show that the per-task projection error of a merged model is governed by the principal-angle alignment between each task and the shared subspace, so that grouping geometrically compatible tasks provably reduces this error within each group. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Group-wise Rank-Aware Model Merging (GRAM)}, which uses the principal-angle geometry of task vectors to decide which tasks should be merged together and then performs a standard SVD-based merge within each coalition; the pipeline requires only the fine-tuned weights at merge time. With only one additional model ($\nK{=}2$), GRAM significantly mitigates subspace interference and consistently outperforms prior merging methods across language understanding (GLUE with Flan-T5), 8-task CLIP vision, and medical instruction tuning (Qwen2-7B-Instruct with four English/Chinese medical LoRAs).
中文翻译
将不同模型合并为单一模型在多任务部署中极具吸引力，尤其是在医疗等受监管领域。由于严格的数据隐私政策，这些场景下的模型通常需要本地微调，因此聚合操作必须仅基于微调后的权重进行。现有方法对所有任务采用统一的合并策略，忽略了其学习子空间之间的几何关系。当任务更新占据未对齐的子空间时，将其投影到共享的低秩基上会丢失与任务相关的信息方向，这种失效模式被称为“子空间干扰”。本研究发现，合并模型的每个任务投影误差由该任务与共享子空间之间的主角对齐程度决定，因此将几何兼容的任务分组可证明地降低每组内的误差。受此启发，我们提出 extbf{分组秩感知模型合并（GRAM）}，利用任务向量的主角几何结构决定哪些任务应被合并，并在每个联盟内执行标准的SVD-based合并；该流程在合并时仅需微调后的权重。仅增加一个额外模型（$ K{=}2$），GRAM显著缓解了子空间干扰，并在语言理解（GLUE with Flan-T5）、8任务CLIP视觉和医学指令微调（Qwen2-7B-Instruct搭配四个英/中文医学LoRA）中持续优于先前的合并方法。
👤 Di Wu, Shuaidong Pan 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.29092]
Same Answer, Different Reasons: Trace-Level Divergence in Multilingual Large Language Models
相同答案，不同理由：多语言大语言模型中的追踪级差异
Multilingual large language models are increasingly deployed in settings where visible reasoning traces support verification, education, and safety auditing, yet standard evaluations typically measure only final-answer agreement or holistic response similarity. As a result, they establish outcome consistency without determining whether users across languages receive comparable supporting evidence. We introduce a trace-level divergence framework that compares visible reasoning traces as structured sequences, decomposing agreement into structural similarity, order consistency, and holistic similarity, with pair-specific calibration to enable comparison across languages. Across three reasoning benchmarks, seven languages, and six frontier LLMs, the natural baseline---embedding each whole response and comparing the two by cosine---systematically overstates agreement: $52.1\%$--$67.3\%$ of examples in the top quartile of holistic similarity are not in the top quartile of structural similarity. This dissociation is stable across sentence encoders and step segmentations, and blind bilingual annotation studies in two English-anchored language pairs show that human judgments align with structural similarity rather than with the holistic baseline. A same-language control shows that the level of divergence is language-specific: two English traces for paraphrases of the same question agree more closely than an English trace and its counterparts in other languages. Our results show that answer-level and holistic evaluations can substantially overestimate the consistency of multilingual explanatory artifacts, motivating trace-level evaluation for reliable multilingual deployment.
中文翻译
多语言大语言模型越来越多地应用于需要可见推理轨迹以支持验证、教育和安全审计的场景，但标准评估通常仅衡量最终答案的一致性或整体响应相似性。因此，它们建立了结果一致性，却无法确定跨语言用户是否获得可比的支持证据。我们引入了一种追踪级差异框架，将可见推理轨迹作为结构化序列进行比较，将一致性分解为结构相似性、顺序一致性和整体相似性，并通过成对校准实现跨语言比较。在三个推理基准、七种语言和六种前沿LLM上，自然基线——将整个响应嵌入并通过余弦相似度比较——系统性地高估了一致性：在整体相似性前四分位的52.1%–67.3%示例中，其结构相似性并不处于前四分位。这种分离现象在不同句子编码器和步骤分割下保持稳定，且在两个以英语为锚点的语言对上的盲法双语标注研究显示，人类判断与结构相似性一致，而非与整体基线一致。同语言对照表明，差异水平具有语言特异性：同一问题的两个英文轨迹在改写句中一致性高于其与其他语言对应轨迹的一致性。我们的结果表明，答案级和整体评估可能严重夸大多语言解释性产物的一致性，从而推动追踪级评估以实现可靠的多语言部署。
👤 Rui Sun, Peizhao Mei, Xiewei Cheng, Kaizhuo Chen, Taoran Li et al. 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28875]
To Manipulate or Not: Opinion Dynamics in LLM Interactions
操纵与否：LLM交互中的意见动态
Large language models increasingly take part in extended conversations where they can both shape and be shaped by another party's views, yet most persuasion research measures only one side of that exchange. We introduce a controlled LLM-LLM debate framework in which two language-model roles repeatedly discuss a contested topic (climate-change attribution): one is given an assigned stance and, in some conditions, an explicit instruction to persuade, while the other receives no assigned position. Across four ordered model combinations involving GPT-6 Luna and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite, we track both each role's explicitly reported position and a separate measure of its turn-by-turn behavior inside the conversation. Opinion dynamics differ substantially across model combinations: same-model interactions narrow the opinion gap on average, clearly for only one of the two models, while cross-model interactions widen it. Explicit reports and in-dialogue behavior capture distinct aspects of opinion dynamics, and a blanket instruction to persuade does not reliably increase influence. These findings suggest that conversational influence depends not only on assigned roles and initial positions but also on the participating models, with implications for how opinion change is measured in extended LLM interactions.
中文翻译
大型语言模型越来越多地参与长期对话，能够既影响他人观点，也被他人观点所影响，但大多数说服研究仅衡量交流的一方。我们引入一个受控的LLM-LLM辩论框架，其中两个语言模型角色反复讨论一个争议话题（气候变化归因）：一方被赋予指定立场，某些条件下还收到明确的说服指令，而另一方则无指定立场。在涉及GPT-6 Luna和Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite的四种有序模型组合中，我们同时追踪每个角色的显式报告立场和对话中逐轮行为的独立度量。意见动态在不同模型组合间存在显著差异：同模型交互平均缩小了意见差距，但仅对其中一个模型明显有效；而跨模型交互则扩大了差距。显式报告与对话内行为捕捉了意见动态的不同方面，而笼统的说服指令并未可靠提升影响力。这些发现表明，对话影响力不仅取决于分配的角色和初始立场，还取决于参与的模型本身，对如何在长期LLM交互中测量意见变化具有启示意义。

[2610.28843]
Beyond Correct Answers: How Compositional Cues Shape Visual Judgments
超越正确答案：组合线索如何塑造视觉判断
Visual question answering requires models to assign attributes and relations to the correct objects, a problem known as visual binding. Structured prompts make these assignments explicit through labeled fields. Yet an improvement from the full prompt does not reveal which labels help or how they interact. First, we isolate field-label effects across ten models and six tasks by holding the image and claim fixed. These comparisons reveal differences that overall accuracy can hide. With a relation label present, adding object-role labels helps Qwen3 but hurts Qwen2 on Spatial. Second, we compare auxiliary hints that preserve or swap the question's role assignments against a no-hint baseline. Across two image sets, Qwen3's advantage with preserving hints mainly reflects errors caused by swapped hints. Preserving hints remain near baseline. Field order further affects the harm. Third, we test clearer hint wording and direct selection of the relation shown in the image. Clearer hints improve text matching but do not consistently improve visual judgments. In contrast, relation selection partly reduces errors for some hints in Qwen3 and GPT-4.1-mini.
中文翻译
视觉问答要求模型将属性和关系准确分配给相应对象，这一问题被称为视觉绑定。结构化提示通过标注字段使这些分配变得明确。然而，完整提示的改进并不能揭示哪些标签起到了作用以及它们之间的交互方式。首先，我们通过固定图像和陈述，在十个模型和六个任务中隔离了字段-标签效应。这些比较揭示了总体准确率可能掩盖的差异。在存在关系标签的情况下，添加对象角色标签对Qwen3有帮助，但对Qwen2在空间任务上却有害。其次，我们将保留或交换问题角色分配的辅助提示与无提示基线进行对比。在两个图像集上，Qwen3在保留提示时的优势主要反映了因提示交换导致的错误。保留提示的表现仍接近基线水平。字段顺序也进一步影响了损害程度。第三，我们测试了更清晰的提示措辞以及直接选择图像中显示的关系。更清晰的提示改善了文本匹配，但并未一致提升视觉判断。相比之下，关系选择在某些提示下部分减少了Qwen3和GPT-4.1-mini的错误。

[2610.28797]
CALLIOPE: Private Insights into AI Use, Without Embeddings
CALLIOPE：无需嵌入即可洞察AI使用情况的私密视角
We introduce CALLIOPE, a top-down framework for discovering topic hierarchies in large corpora of unstructured data (e.g., LLM conversations), while supporting differential privacy (DP). Unlike traditional and even recent LLM-augmented approaches that ultimately rely on clustering vector embeddings, our framework leverages LLM capabilities directly for the entire pipeline. Experiments demonstrate that this approach provides better utility over existing (embedding-based) methods in the DP setting, especially in the high privacy regime. We evaluate the quality of CALLIOPE outputs using a variety of traditionally studied clustering metrics, as well as some novel utility metrics that we introduce.
中文翻译

[2610.28747]
A Reverse-Gold Theorem: Positive-Data Identification by Substrate-Bounded Contrastive Learners
逆黄金定理：基于底物受限对比学习者的正样本识别
Gold's theorem shows that no learner can identify an arbitrary language in a superfinite class from positive presentations alone. We prove a positive result of a different shape. Fix an innate \emph{recursive contrast operator} $R$ mapping any string to a finite set of structurally bounded perturbations of it, and a discriminative scorer of bounded capacity trained by InfoNCE-style contrastive estimation \citep{vandenoord2018,macollins2018} on self-generated perturbations. Under a faithfulness condition relating operator, scorer, and target, the trained scorer achieves \emph{PAC conditional identification} from positive data alone: with high probability it ranks all but an $\varepsilon$-fraction of positives above every out-of-language perturbation in their own orbit, with sample complexity polynomial in the capacity, the orbit size, the score bound, and $1/\varepsilon$. The negatives are constructed by the learner, not supplied by an oracle, and no global normalizing constant over strings is ever computed. The misranking bound follows from the geometry of the contrastive loss---per-example loss floors plus a definite loss increment under misranking---yielding an $\varepsilon^{-2}$ rate, together with an explicit approximation gap that quantifies the cost of in-language (``colliding'') perturbations and vanishes for orbit-pure operators. The ranking guarantee lifts to a global membership classifier under a margin condition, and the same operator composed with the learned scorer is a normalization-free Metropolis--Hastings generator. We instantiate the hypotheses for substitutable context-free languages \citep{clarkeyraud2007} and the subregular classes \citep{heinz2010,hayashikamigaito2025}, and state an open conjecture locating the construction in the mildly context-sensitive hierarchy.
中文翻译
Gold定理表明，没有任何学习者仅凭正样本展示就能识别任意超有限类中的语言。我们证明了一个不同形态的正面结果。固定一个内在的递归对比算子 $R$，它将任意字符串映射为结构上有界的扰动集合，并训练一个容量受限的判别评分器，通过InfoNCE风格的对比估计方法（​​citep{vandenoord2018,macollins2018}）在自生成扰动上进行学习。在算子、评分器与目标之间的忠实性条件下，训练后的评分器仅凭正样本即可实现PAC条件识别：以高概率将几乎所有正样本（除ε分数外）排在各自轨道中所有非语言扰动之上，样本复杂度为容量、轨道大小、得分上限和$1/ε$的多项式。负样本由学习者自行构造，无需来自预言机，且从不计算字符串上的全局归一化常数。错误排序的界限源于对比损失的几何结构——每个样本的损失下限加上错误排序时的确定性损失增量，从而得到$\varepsilon^{-2}$的收敛速率，并显式量化了同语言（“碰撞”）扰动带来的近似差距，对于轨道纯算子该差距趋于零。在边际条件下，该排序保证可提升为全局成员分类器，且同一算子与学习评分器组合可构成免归一化的Metropolis-Hastings生成器。我们在可替换上下文无关语言（​​citep{clarkeyraud2007}）和次正则类（​​citep{heinz2010,hayashikamigaito2025}）中实例化假设，并提出一个开放猜想，定位该构造在温和上下文敏感层次中的位置。
👤 Rakesh Sengupta 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28711]
Qwen-Audio-3.1-ASR Technical Report
Qwen-Audio-3.1-ASR 技术报告
We present Qwen-Audio-3.1-ASR, a family of audio-language models that builds upon its predecesor Qwen-Audio-3.0-ASR with improved speech recognition and expanded capabilities in speaker-diarized transcription and general audio understanding. The family retains support for 30 languages and 16 Chinese dialects, together with instruction-controlled capabilities including historical-context conditioning, hierarchical hotword customization, and native transcription polishing. Speaker-diarized ASR produces structured outputs containing speaker labels, timestamps, and recognized text, maintaining consistent speaker labels across turns and the representation of overlapping speech. The general audio understanding capabilities cover audio-, speech-, and music-captioning, temporal grounding and sound event detection, as well as audio question answering and multiple-choice reasoning. These tasks broaden the application scope from recognizing spoken words to interpreting speakers, acoustic events, and their temporal relations. To accommodate different deployment requirements, the family includes variants for asynchronous file transcription, low-latency streaming, interactive voice input, and non-streaming recognition, with speaker-diarized transcription and general audio understanding available in certain variants. Evaluations span conventional ASR, multilingual and dialectal speech, speaker-diarized transcription, and general audio understanding, demonstrating the capabilities of the Qwen-Audio-3.1-ASR family across transcription and broader audio analysis tasks.
中文翻译
我们介绍Qwen-Audio-3.1-ASR，一个音频-语言模型家族，基于其前代Qwen-Audio-3.0-ASR进行了语音识别能力的提升，并扩展了说话人区分转录和通用音频理解功能。该系列仍支持30种语言和16种汉语方言，具备指令控制功能，包括历史背景条件化、分层热词自定义和原生转录润色。说话人区分的自动语音识别（ASR）生成包含说话人标签、时间戳和识别文本的结构化输出，保持对话轮次间说话人标签的一致性，并表示重叠语音。通用音频理解能力涵盖音频、语音和音乐描述生成、时间定位与声音事件检测，以及音频问答和多选推理。这些任务将应用范围从识别口语词汇扩展到解释说话人、声学事件及其时间关系。为适应不同的部署需求，该系列包含异步文件转录、低延迟流式传输、交互式语音输入和非流式识别等多种变体，其中部分变体支持说话人区分转录和通用音频理解。评估覆盖传统ASR、多语言及方言语音、说话人区分转录和通用音频理解，展示了Qwen-Audio-3.1-ASR系列在转录和更广泛的音频分析任务中的能力。
👤 Chuanmeng Bian, Daren Chen, Peixin Chen, Zhigao Chen, Chong Deng et al. 📅 2026-10-20 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28663]
Beyond source attribution: Address authorship and comparability decisions in an LLM-built scholarly knowledge graph
超越来源归属：在LLM构建的学术知识图谱中处理作者身份与可比性决策
A knowledge graph needs a stable address for its evidence even where the literature offers competing classifications and agrees on none. Yet an address chosen for retrieval readily passes for the only classification available. This happens whenever records gathered under one name concern units that differ in boundaries, rank, or the criteria by which they are described or counted. In Sinitic dialectology the same localities recur in schemes whose units differ in membership and rank, and a unit may change its affiliation when the evidence for its diagnosis changes. Two tone counts, each quoted faithfully, may still count different things. The taxonomy of an entire field thus comes to rest on the unstable frame of a literature that contradicts itself. Neither verifying a quotation nor searching for conflicting values detects these differences. I distinguish the working address that gathers candidate evidence, the category that the source defines and the decision that admits a passage to a particular comparison, and argue that such decisions, together with the dependence among records, belong to the provenance of a comparison. On four re-runnable cases, the accompanying PaRAGraph Casebook shows where such differences arise among claims extracted by a language model and verified before entering the graph. The platform now stores the corresponding decisions as objects for one dimension of comparability, the address of a record: the placement of a locality in each source's scheme, the decision that a name in a given publication denotes a unit rather than a place, and the origin of every address. The working taxonomy can then be revised as new sources yield new claims, while the path from each comparison to its evidence stays fixed. Keywords: knowledge graphs; source attribution; classification; claim extraction; comparative concepts; Sinitic dialectology; Yue
中文翻译
知识图谱需要为其证据提供稳定地址，即使文献中存在相互竞争的分类且无一致意见。然而，为检索选择的地址很容易被视为唯一可用的分类。当以同一名称收集的记录所涉及的单元在边界、等级或描述与计数标准上存在差异时，这种情况就会发生。在汉藏语系方言学中，相同地域在不同方案中单位的成员资格和等级不同，且当诊断依据发生变化时，单位的归属也可能改变。两个忠实引用的声调计数可能实际上统计的是不同事物。因此，整个领域的分类体系最终建立在一个自我矛盾的文献不稳定框架之上。无论是验证引文还是搜索冲突值，都无法发现这些差异。我区分了用于收集候选证据的工作地址、源定义的类别以及决定某段文字是否可纳入特定比较的判断，并主张此类决策及其记录间的依赖关系属于比较的溯源范畴。在四个可重复案例中，配套的PaRAGraph案例集展示了语言模型提取的主张中此类差异如何出现，并在进入图谱前经过验证。该平台现在将相应的决策作为可比性的一个维度进行存储：记录的地址，即一个地点在各来源方案中的位置、某一出版物中名称指代单位而非地点的判断，以及每个地址的来源。因此，工作分类体系可随新来源带来新主张而更新，而每项比较与其证据之间的路径始终保持不变。
👤 Ksenia Kozha, PaRAGraph Research Project 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv
[2610.28636]
RESEARCHHUB: A Personalized Literature Discovery System for Biomedical Preprints
ResearchHub：面向生物医学预印本的个性化文献发现系统
We present ResearchHub, an interactive system for personalized literature discovery over biomedical preprints. Given a researcher profile or a free-form query, ResearchHub retrieves relevant papers from a domain-specific MedRxiv corpus and helps users move beyond near-duplicate recommendations through controllable novelty, diversity, and curriculum ordering. At the core of the system is prism, a bi-encoder retriever adapted to biomedical preprints using GPT-4o-generated natural language queries and a hybrid Multiple Negatives Ranking plus MarginMSE objective with cross-encoder soft labels. On 44,937 MedRxiv papers from 2023--2025, prism outperforms strong off-the-shelf dense retrievers, improving Recall@10 over SPECTER2-Search by 12.4 percentage points. ResearchHub further supports frontier retrieval based on a user's reading history, DPP-based diversity re-ranking, Local Intrinsic Dimensionality ordering from mainstream to specialized papers, and lightweight attribution cards for result explanation. The full pipeline runs with low latency, making the system suitable
中文翻译
我们提出了ResearchHub，一个针对生物医学预印本的交互式个性化文献发现系统。给定研究人员档案或自由格式查询，ResearchHub从领域特定的MedRxiv语料库中检索相关论文，并通过可控的新颖性、多样性以及课程排序帮助用户摆脱近似重复推荐的局限。该系统的核心是prism，一种使用GPT-4o生成的自然语言查询对生物医学预印本进行适配的双编码器检索器，结合了混合多重负例排名与MarginMSE目标函数，并采用交叉编码器软标签。在2023—2025年共44,937篇MedRxiv论文上，prism优于强大的现成密集检索模型，在Recall@10上比SPECTER2-Search高出12.4个百分点。ResearchHub还支持基于用户阅读历史的前沿检索、基于DPP的多样性重排序、从主流到专业论文的局部内在维度排序，以及轻量级的溯源卡片以解释结果。整个流程延迟低，使系统具备实际应用潜力。
👤 Viswonathan Manoranjan, Taeyoon Kim, Nicholas Do, Sang Woo Kim, Dawei Li et al. 📅 2026-08-05 🔗 arXiv
